# REVIEW-V3 — full record of the raw findings

Generated from the structured output of the review workflow (ten reviewers, ten independent
skeptics), with local paths made repository-relative. Nothing here is edited by hand. The
consolidated, lead-verified findings are in `../REVIEW-V3.md`; this file is the evidence behind
them: every raw finding with the reviewer's evidence and counterexample, the skeptic's verdict and
corrected claim, the proposed Lean checks, each reviewer's list of what was checked and found
correct, and what was not covered.

- Reviewed commit: `6481731`; five commits landed during the review (to `f2fe99b`). Citations
  under `Midfall/Instances/` and `docs/` may be pinned to either; unchanged files have the same
  line numbers at both.
- 115 reviewer findings (ids `A-1` … `J-14`) and 37 additions found by the skeptics (ids `A+1` …).
- A skeptic verdict of *confirmed* or *partially-correct* means the claim was re-derived from the
  source by a second agent. It is not a machine check. Where the corrected claim differs from the
  reviewer's claim, the corrected claim is the one to rely on.
- Low and informational findings were not re-verified.
- Paths are relative to the repository root unless they start with `formal/`-internal names such
  as `Midfall/…`.

## A — the headline statement (frozen surface)

**Reviewer summary.** Verdict: the three-conjunct shape of LoweringEquivalence is sound as a design (soundness under SoundSide, total completeness under CompleteSide + codec laws, and an unconditional two-outcome conjunct that correctly excludes STOP / fall-off-the-end). But the headline cannot be true non-vacuously at the deployment, and one frozen statement is false as written. All findings are by reading only (no Lean was run); please verify before relying on them.

New, beyond the project's recorded list:
1. (critical) The Lean verifier inside LeanAccepts is not Rust-faithful on the deployment's own VK. The port groups one-piece commitments by VALUE (R-7), Rust by pointer. The real poseidon vk.json has six identity fixed commitments, five queried at x, plus the identity committed-instance commitment at x. Lean therefore returns Error.opening for every proof, while Rust and the contract accept. No V2 plan item addresses this.
2. (high) At poseidonRender as instantiated, LeanAccepts is identically false (ofCompressed? is the constant none; all 27 VK commitments are the identity). The headline then reduces to "the contract never returns", block contracts are vacuous, and poseidon_rejects is false. The recorded framing ("blocks end_to_end") understates this.
3. (high) Frozen calldataOf repacks twice, so it never returns .ok; EndToEnd reduces to "verifyNative never accepts".
4. (high) No hypothesis gives the encoder-first compressed law that verifyMsgs needs; G1CodecLaws is too weak.
5. (high) Valid has no pin tying payload commitment words to R.vk's commitments, nor E.delta.
6. (high) rejects_of_not_accepts is false as stated; its docstring's rationale is wrong.

Also: SoundSide is prover-selectable with probability 1, not a negligible event (medium); completeness ignores forwarded precompile gas (medium).

Note: the working tree advanced from 6481731 to f2fe99b (5 commits, F.1) during the audit. Citations to Instances/Poseidon* and docs are pinned to 6481731 via git show; the facts also hold at f2fe99b.

### A-1 — Lean verifier in LeanAccepts rejects every proof at the real deployment VK (value-equality grouping, R-7, meets identity fixed commitments)

- **Severity (reviewer):** critical · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → critical · **Kind:** fidelity-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (docs/REVIEW.md:1720 (R-7, 'must be settled, not assumed', asks for a corpus with a repeated commitment) and Utils.lean:101-110. Not recorded: that the deployment fixture itself has repeated (identity) commitments, that the Lean verifier therefore rejects all its proofs, or any link to the V2 headlin […]) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:38-48, 257-259; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/Utils.lean:142-148, 295-299; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Verifier.lean:773-799; proofs/src/poly/query.rs:117-125; proofs/src/poly/kzg/utils.rs:54-58; proofs/src/plonk/verifier.rs:610-643; formal/fixtures/conformance/poseidon/vk.json; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:263-267
- **Claim (reviewer):** verifyMsgs (hence LeanAccepts) is not Rust-faithful whenever two distinct one-piece commitments in the multi-open query set are equal as group elements and queried at the same point. Rust compares OnePiece commitments by pointer, so they stay distinct. The Lean port compares by value and returns Error.opening (the DuplicatedQuery path). The deployment's own VK triggers this for every proof: fixed columns 8, 10, 11, 12, 16 have the identity commitment, are non-simple, and are queried at rotation 0; the committed-instance commitment is also the identity, queried at x. So at any faithful instantiation of the deployment, LeanAccepts is false for all calldata, while the Rust verifier and the contract accept valid proofs. The soundness conjunct is then false and completeness vacuous, even with every placeholder repaired and every sorry closed.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Rust query.rs:121-125: `(&CommitmentReference::OnePiece(self_com), &CommitmentReference::OnePiece(other_com)) => std::ptr::eq(self_com, other_com)`. Rust verifier.rs:616 `&committed_instances[column.index()]`, :640 `&vk.fixed_commitments[column.index()]` (distinct allocations). Lean MultiOpen.lean:43 `| .onePiece x, .onePiece y => decideBool (P.decEqG₁ x y)`; :257-259 `constructIntermediateSets ... (kzgCommitBeq ...) queries`. Utils.lean:142-148 `match cms.findIdx? (fun cd => eqC cd.commitment q.commitment) with | some pos => ... if cd.pointIndices.toList.contains pidx then .error (Fault.error Error.opening)`. In-tree KAT Utils.lean:295-299 confirms two equal-valued commitments at one point give Error.opening. Fixture vk.json: fixed_commitments_compressed_hex[8,10,11,12,14,16] all start `c000...` (identity); fixed_queries contains [8,0],[10,0],[11,0],[12,0],[16,0]; selector_flags marks 8,10,11,12,16 is_simple=false; instance_queries [[0,0],[1,0]], num_committed_instances 1. Conformance/Replay.lean:18-23 states the replay does NOT run verifyNative or the grouping on real bytes.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Any valid poseidon proof (for example the fixture proof.bin with instances.json). Rust: accept. Contract: returns true. Lean port with the real VK commitments: the query for fixed column 8 (identity, point x) matches the existing identity entry at point x (committed instance, or an earlier identity fixed column), so constructIntermediateSets returns Error.opening and verifyMsgs is not .ok. SoundSide holds (no logup panic), so conjunct 1 is violated.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed as stated, with one extension. verifyMsgs / LeanAccepts is not Rust-faithful whenever two distinct one-piece commitments in the multi-open query set are equal as group elements and queried at the same point: Rust keeps them apart (pointer equality), the Lean port merges them by value and returns Error.opening. The deployment's own VK triggers this for every proof: fixed columns 8, 10, 11, 12, 16 are identity commitments, non-simple, queried at rotation 0, and the committed-instance commitment is the identity at x. Rust accepts the fixture proof; the Lean port instantiated with the real commitments cannot. Extension: snapshotOf runs the same phaseA, so at a faithful VK it is never .ok either. BlockContractsAt is then vacuous and Rejects false at the repaired deployment, exactly as in A-2, until grouping is keyed by column identity. The contract side is not affected: the generator port groups by planned memory pointer.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Lean: formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:43 `| .onePiece x, .onePiece y => decideBool (P.decEqG₁ x y)`; :257-259 `constructIntermediateSets ... (kzgCommitBeq ...) queries`. Kzg/Utils.lean:142-148 `match cms.findIdx? (fun cd => eqC cd.commitment q.commitment) with | some pos => ... if cd.pointIndices.toList.contains pidx then .error (Fault.error Error.opening)`. KAT Utils.lean:295-299 (cisDupKats_ok) expects Error.opening for two equal values at one point. Verifier.lean:773-782 builds the committed-instance query; :792-799 builds fixed queries from `vk.fixedCommitments` filtered by `!vk.cs.hasSimpleSelectorCol`. Native.lean:229 and :280 pass committed `[[1]]`; Features.lean:31 `committedInstances := true`. Verifier.lean:1061-1066 maps the multiPrepare error to Error.opening. Rust: proofs/src/poly/query.rs:125 `) => std::ptr::eq(self_com, other_com),`; poly/kzg/utils.rs:55-58 (position by `==`, then DuplicatedQuery); plonk/verifier.rs:616 `&committed_instances[column.index()]`, :640 `&vk.fixed_commitments[column.index()]`. Fixture (I ran a read-only python parse of formal/fixtures/conformance/poseidon/vk.json): identity fixed indices [8, 10, 11, 12, 14, 16]; fixed_queries contain [8,0],[10,0],[11,0],[12,0],[16,0]; selector_flags is_simple=false for 8,10,11,12,16 (14 is simple); instance_queries [[0,0],[1,0]]; num_committed_instances 1. trace.json has `verdict accept`, `pairing_check True`. Contract side: Generator/Kzg.lean:776-789 says EcPoint equality is the planned memory pointer, so each fixed column is its own entry. Conformance/Replay.lean:18-30 confirms the replay does not run verifyNative or the grouping on real bytes. Recorded analysis that this finding shows to be wrong: docs/plans/P-SE-evidence/survey.md:401 ('matters only for Rust conformance claims') and docs/REVIEW.md:878 ('under Features.solidity the divergence is unreachable for honest proofs').
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
1) `#eval Midfall.Halo2.cisDupKats_ok` (expect true). 2) `open Midfall Midfall.Halo2 in #eval (haveI : Fact (Nat.Prime 17) := ⟨by decide⟩; match constructIntermediateSets (F := Fr 17) (C := Nat) eqNatRef #[vq17 1 0 2, vq17 1 7 3, vq17 1 0 4] with | .error (Fault.error Error.opening) => "Lean rejects" | _ => "other")` -- expect "Lean rejects"; this models committed instance = identity at x, a real commitment, an identity fixed column at x. 3) Strongest: run verifyNative in the ExponentModel instance on a toy VK with two equal fixed commitments queried at rotation 0 and an otherwise valid proof; expect Error.opening.
```
- **Suggested fix:** Make grouping follow Rust allocation identity: give each one-piece commitment a stable key (column kind + index, already present as CommitmentLabel, or an explicit allocation id) and have eqC compare keys, not group values. Add a KAT that runs constructIntermediateSets on the real fixture's query set (identity commitments at x) and expects success. Add this to the conformance corpus as R-7 asked. Until fixed, do not describe the Lean verifier as Rust-faithful.

### A-2 — At poseidonRender as instantiated, LeanAccepts is identically false, so the headline reduces to 'the contract never returns'

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** statement-vacuous · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (The placeholder is recorded as blocking check 3 and end_to_end only: PoseidonCoherence.lean header, docs/design.md@6481731:1820-1822, docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md@6481731:115, docs/STATUS.md@6481731:327-329. Not recorded: that LeanAccepts itself is unsatisfiable at the render, that block contracts are […]) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Instances/G1Agreement.lean:2002-2006; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean@6481731:345-350, 367-370, 379-380; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonCoherence.lean@6481731:439-440; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Native.lean:164-171, 269-292; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Transcript.lean:65-69, 145-151; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:278, 306; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:263-284, 298-311; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:116-139
- **Claim (reviewer):** verifyMsgs re-serialises each G1 message with toCompressed and re-reads it with ofCompressed?. The deployment codec has ofCompressed? := fun _ => none, and every successful run must read at least f_com and pi. So verifyMsgs poseidonE is never .ok, and LeanAccepts poseidonRender H cd is false for all H, cd. The all-identity VK commitments give a second independent reason via A-1. Consequences: (a) SoundSide and CompleteSide are trivially true; (b) conjunct 1 is equivalent to 'no permissive run from an entry frame returns', and conjunct 2 is vacuous; (c) BlockContractsAt poseidonRender product P is vacuously true for every P because snapshotOf is never .ok, so poseidon_quotient_block and blockContracts_of_valid at this render could be closed with no block proof; (d) poseidon_rejects says every run reverts, which is false if the modelled program returns on any calldata; (e) the proposed D-P45 gate (adds LeanAccepts) is refutable at this render, not merely unevaluated.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** G1Agreement.lean:2005-2006 `toCompressed := fun Q => bytesNatBE (g1zPair Q).1 fpBytes` / `ofCompressed? := fun _ => none`. PoseidonEnv.lean@6481731:380 `{ g1 := g1CodecZ, field := poseidonFc, delta := 2, fewerPointSetsRuntime := false }`; :346 `Array.replicate poseidonVkData.numFixedColumns (1 : G₁z)`. PoseidonCoherence.lean@6481731:439-440 `theorem check3_codec_decodes_no_compressed_point : ∀ b : Bytes, poseidonE.g1.ofCompressed? b = none := fun _ => rfl`. Native.lean:278-281 `let bytes := ms.foldl ... (fun acc m => ByteArray.append acc (msgWire E m))` then `(prepare ...).run (Transcript.initWithHash H bytes)`. Transcript.lean:69 `readRepr? := c.ofCompressed?`; :150-151 `match h.readRepr? repr with | none => .error (Fault.error (Error.transcript IoErrorKind.other))`. MultiOpen.lean:278 `let fCom ← tmRead (hashableG1 g1)`. Blocks.lean:123 hypothesis `snapshotOf R.E H R.vk inst ms none = .ok Snap`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Any calldata on which the modelled program returns, under a product satisfying Holds (HoldsFinal.holds_exhibited provides one). The run is permissive, isReturn holds, SoundSide holds trivially, and LeanAccepts is false, so conjunct 1 fails. If no such run exists in the model, the headline is true only vacuously.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed. At poseidonRender as instantiated, verifyMsgs and snapshotOf are never .ok, because every run reaches phaseB's `tmRead (hashableG1 g1)` and `g1CodecZ.ofCompressed? = fun _ => none`. So: LeanAccepts poseidonRender H cd is false for all H, cd; SoundSide and CompleteSide are trivially true; conjunct 2 is vacuous; conjunct 1 says only that no permissive run from a deployed entry frame returns; BlockContractsAt poseidonRender product P holds vacuously for every P, so poseidon_quotient_block is closable with no block proof; poseidon_rejects says every such run is a non-committing halt; and the D-P45 gate (adds LeanAccepts) is refutable at this render. Whether conjunct 1 and poseidon_rejects are false or vacuously true depends on whether the modelled program has a returning run, which I did not establish by reading.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Instances/G1Agreement.lean:2002-2006 `noncomputable def g1CodecZ ... toCompressed := fun Q => bytesNatBE (g1zPair Q).1 fpBytes` / `ofCompressed? := fun _ => none`. Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:229-230 (HEAD f2fe99b) `poseidonE ... { g1 := g1CodecZ, field := poseidonFc, delta := 2, ... }`; :196 and :200 `Array.replicate ... (1 : G₁z)`. Instances/PoseidonV2.lean:152-163 `poseidonRender ... E := poseidonE ... vk := poseidonVkExec`. PoseidonCoherence.lean:432-433 `theorem check3_codec_decodes_no_compressed_point : ∀ b : Bytes, poseidonE.g1.ofCompressed? b = none := fun _ => rfl`. Halo2/Exec/Transcript.lean:69 `readRepr? := c.ofCompressed?`; :147-151 read returns an error on `none`. MultiOpen.lean:278 `let fCom ← tmRead (hashableG1 g1)` is unconditional in phaseB; Pcs.lean:80-81 and Verifier.lean:1061 always call multiPrepare. Native.lean:278-283 and :291-292: verifyMsgs needs prepareMsgs .ok. Lowering/Snapshot.lean:255-262 and :349-355: snapshotOf runs phaseB. LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:123 hypothesis `snapshotOf R.E H R.vk inst ms none = .ok Snap`. PoseidonV2.lean:208-210 poseidon_quotient_block; :222-223 poseidon_rejects. SoundSide is trivial because the first G1 read fails in parseTrace, before linearize can reach the logup.rs:460 site. docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:52 'so it will be proved, not evaluated'. The project records only the narrower fact: docs/REVIEW.md:3012-3014 'no accepted calldata at any model in the tree' and DECISIONS-PENDING.md:111 'blocks `end_to_end`'.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
`example : ∀ b, poseidonRender.E.g1.ofCompressed? b = none := fun _ => rfl`; then prove `theorem snap_never_ok (H inst ms Snap) : Midfall.Lowering.snapshotOf poseidonRender.E H poseidonRender.vk inst ms none ≠ .ok Snap` and `example (H cd) : ¬ LoweringV2.LeanAccepts poseidonRender H cd`; then `example (product) : poseidonRender.BlockContractsAt product (fun _ => True) := fun _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ h => absurd h (snap_never_ok ..)`.
```
- **Suggested fix:** Treat a real G1 codec (both compressed directions, with flag bits) and real VK commitments as preconditions of stating the headline at the deployment. Add a gate that fails if `∀ cd, ¬ LeanAccepts R H cd` is provable; D-P45 with LeanAccepts does this, and its discharge must be a planned item. Do not accept any per-deployment block row as progress while snapshotOf is never .ok at the render.

### A-3 — Frozen calldataOf repacks the proof twice; EndToEnd reduces to 'verifyNative never accepts'

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** statement-false · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:335-340, 347-361; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Calldata.lean:186-197, 241-250, 1636-1646; formal/Midfall/Generator/Quotient/VmTypes.lean:739-750; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:643-653
- **Claim (reviewer):** calldataOf first calls Lowering.repackProof on π to get π', then calls the five-argument Lowering.encodeCalldata on π'. That function takes the COMPRESSED proof and calls repackProof again. Under G1CodecLaws.eipWidth, π'.size = repackedLen = compressedLen + 80*(prefixG1Count+2), which never equals compressedLen, so the second repack throws lengthMismatch. Hence calldataOf R π inst = .ok cd is unsatisfiable. The right side of EndToEnd is then false, hside is vacuous, and end_to_end asserts that verifyNative rejects every (inst, π) of the right instance count. That is false for any render with a valid proof where the hypotheses hold. The statement is in the hashed frozen section 9.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Statements.lean:337-340 `(repackProof (p := p) (RepackedProofLayoutPlan.fromProofLayout R.plan.proofLayout) R.E.g1 π).mapError ... >>= fun π' => encodeCalldata (p := p) R.cfg.numInstances (RepackedProofLayoutPlan.fromProofLayout R.plan.proofLayout) R.E.g1 π' inst`. Only Midfall.Lowering.encodeCalldata has this arity; Generator.encodeCalldata is `(proof : Bytes) (instances : List Word256) : Bytes`. Calldata.lean:248-250 `match repackProof (p := p) plan c π with | .error e => throw ... | .ok proof => pure (encodeCalldataRaw proof (inst.toList.map frWord))`. Calldata.lean:188 `if π.size ≠ plan.compressedLen then throw (RepackError.lengthMismatch ...)`. KAT Calldata.lean:1637 feeds the compressed proof (`katCompressed`) to encodeCalldata; :1644-1646 shows a wrong-size input yields `lengthMismatch 176 32 1 1 0`. VmTypes.lean:739-750 gives compressedLen with 48-byte G1 and repackedLen with 128-byte G1.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Any render, any π with repackProof π = .ok π', any inst with inst.size = numInstances: calldataOf returns .error (repack lengthMismatch). So `verifyNative ... = .ok () ↔ False`; with an accepted (inst, π) the iff is false. The D.3 diagnostic in PoseidonCoherence.lean uses `Midfall.Generator.encodeCalldata rp [w]` (the raw encoder), i.e. the intended composition differs from the frozen text.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed. The frozen calldataOf binds Lowering.repackProof's output π' into the five-argument Lowering.encodeCalldata, which repacks its proof argument again. Under G1CodecLaws.eipWidth (present in EndToEnd as hcodec), π'.size = compressedLen + 80*(prefixG1Count+2), so the second repack throws lengthMismatch and calldataOf never returns .ok. EndToEnd therefore reduces to `verifyNative ... ≠ .ok ()` with hside vacuous, and the sorried end_to_end is unprovable for any valid render that has an accepted proof. Without eipWidth the claim does not hold for arbitrary codecs, but EndToEnd always carries it. The v1 end_to_end_noAcc has the same text. Not recorded in STATUS, QUEUE, DECISIONS-PENDING, PLAN-V2 or SORRY.md.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** LoweringV2/Statements.lean:335-340 `(repackProof (p := p) (RepackedProofLayoutPlan.fromProofLayout R.plan.proofLayout) R.E.g1 π).mapError ... >>= fun π' => encodeCalldata (p := p) R.cfg.numInstances (...) R.E.g1 π' inst`. Overload resolution: Generator/Evm.lean:100 `def encodeCalldata (proof : Bytes) (instances : List Word256) : Bytes` has no `p` and the wrong arity; Generator/Calldata.lean:208 `repackProof (c) (plan) (compressed) : Except ApiFault Bytes` has the codec first and no `p`. So both names resolve to Midfall.Lowering. Lowering/Calldata.lean:241-250: `match repackProof (p := p) plan c π with | .error e => throw ... | .ok proof => pure (encodeCalldataRaw proof ...)`. Calldata.lean:188 `if π.size ≠ plan.compressedLen then throw (RepackError.lengthMismatch ...`. Calldata.lean:164 a g1 slot emits `c.toEip Q`; :173 an fr slot emits `frToInputBE s`; :114-118 slots = prefixG1Count g1, numEvals fr, 1 g1, numPointSets fr, 1 g1. VmTypes.lean:739-741 compressedLen uses 48-byte G1; :748-750 repackedLen uses 128-byte G1. Assumptions/Codec.lean:90 eipWidth. KAT Calldata.lean:1637 feeds `katCompressed` to encodeCalldata; :1644-1646 a wrong-size input gives `lengthMismatch 176 32 1 1 0`. The intended composition exists and is unused: Calldata.lean:257-266 repackThenEncode. Statements.lean:351 `(hcodec : G1CodecLaws R.E.g1)`, :356-361 the iff. Blocks.lean:643-653 end_to_end is `sorry`. v1: Lowering/Obligations.lean:361-366 same composition.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
`open Midfall Midfall.Lowering Midfall.Lowering.CdKats in #eval (haveI : Fact (Nat.Prime 17) := ⟨by decide⟩; match Midfall.Lowering.repackProof (p := 17) katPlan toyCodec katCompressed with | .ok π' => (Midfall.Lowering.encodeCalldata (p := 17) 1 katPlan toyCodec π' #[(5 : Midfall.Fr 17)]).isOk | .error _ => true)` -- expect false. General form: `theorem calldataOf_never_ok (R) (π inst cd) (h : G1CodecLaws R.E.g1) : LoweringV2.calldataOf R π inst ≠ .ok cd`.
```
- **Suggested fix:** With sign-off and a re-hash of Frozen.sha256, change calldataOf to `Lowering.repackThenEncode R.cfg.numInstances plan R.E.g1 π inst` (Calldata.lean:257-266), or to repackProof followed by encodeCalldataRaw and the instance-length check. Add a KAT that calldataOf is .ok on a toy plan and decodes back. The v1 statement at Lowering/Obligations.lean:361-366 has the same defect.

### A-4 — No hypothesis supplies the encoder-first compressed codec law that verifyMsgs needs; G1CodecLaws is too weak

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Native.lean:164-171, 278-281; formal/Midfall/Assumptions/Codec.lean:75-90; formal/Midfall/Common/Codec.lean:113-114; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:302-308; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:92-127, 688-698; formal/Midfall/Instances/G1Agreement.lean:2005
- **Claim (reviewer):** The contract absorbs the calldata EIP bytes b of P = ofEip? b. verifyMsgs absorbs toEip P' where ofCompressed? (toCompressed P) = some P'. Agreement needs P' = P, i.e. `∀ Q, ofCompressed? (toCompressed Q) = some Q` or injectivity of toCompressed. The soundness conjunct and BlockSound carry no codec hypothesis. G1CodecLaws (completeness only) has only the decoder-first compressedRoundtrip. Valid and Eip2537.Holds mention only toEip / ofEip?. So the generic rows (BlockRow, blockContracts_of_valid) are false for a codec with non-injective toCompressed even when every stated hypothesis holds, and for a codec whose ofCompressed? rejects everything the block contracts are vacuous while Rejects is false. The deployment's toCompressed is the x-coordinate only (Q and Q⁻¹ collide), so repairing ofCompressed? by Classical.choose on the image, the pattern DECISIONS-PENDING section 8(a) recommends for G2 'at the same time', would satisfy every stated law and still make the Lean verifier read back the wrong point.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Codec.lean:79 `compressedRoundtrip : c.compressedRoundtrip` with Common/Codec.lean:113-114 `∀ b P, c.ofCompressed? b = some P → c.toCompressed P = b`; the only encoder-first law is :82 `ofEip_toEip : ∀ Q : G₁, c.ofEip? (c.toEip Q) = some Q`. Native.lean:170 `| Msg.g1 P => E.g1.toCompressed P`. Statements.lean:302-304: conjunct 1 has no G1CodecLaws; :305 only conjunct 2 does. Blocks.lean:125 BlockSound ungated; :126 only BlockProgress is gated on G1CodecLaws. G1Agreement.lean:2005 `toCompressed := fun Q => bytesNatBE (g1zPair Q).1 fpBytes`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take a real codec c and distinct points A, B. Define c' with the same toEip / ofEip?, toCompressed' Q := c.toCompressed (if Q = A then B else Q), and ofCompressed?' b := (c.ofCompressed? b).filter (· ≠ A). All seven G1CodecLaws fields and Holds still hold. For calldata cd_A obtained from a valid proof whose first commitment is B by substituting A's EIP bytes: verifyMsgs reads back B and accepts, so LeanAccepts cd_A holds; the contract absorbs A's bytes and rejects. Completeness fails. BlockSound at .transcriptParser fails too: the run exits normally with success = 1 and challenges different from Snap's, so it is neither Clean nor Poisoned nor reverted.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed, with scope made precise. No hypothesis of conjunct 1, BlockSound, Rejects, Valid or Eip2537.Holds mentions the compressed half of the codec, and G1CodecLaws (conjunct 2 and BlockProgress only) has only the decoder-first compressedRoundtrip. verifyMsgs and snapshotOf re-read each point through ofCompressed? (toCompressed P), so nothing forces the read-back point to be P. The reviewer's codec c' satisfies all seven G1CodecLaws fields and Holds. The generic sorried obligation blockContracts_of_valid (in the headline cone) is false at such a render, and the reason is hash-independent: Inv at .transcript pins the advice-commitment memory to toEip of the snapshot's point B, while the contract copied A's calldata bytes. The frozen per-deployment headline is not made false by this, because a concrete codec's laws are facts there. But the deployment's toCompressed is x-only, so a Classical.choose repair of ofCompressed? would satisfy every stated law and still read back Q⁻¹ for Q.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Assumptions/Codec.lean:75-90: fields eipRoundtrip, compressedRoundtrip, ofEip_toEip, eipShape, toEip_one, compressedWidth, eipWidth; there is no `∀ Q, ofCompressed? (toCompressed Q) = some Q`. Common/Codec.lean:113-114 `∀ b P, c.ofCompressed? b = some P → c.toCompressed P = b`. Native.lean:170 `| Msg.g1 P => E.g1.toCompressed P`; :278-281 the transcript is rebuilt from msgWire. Transcript.lean:150-154 reads through readRepr? and absorbs toInput of the decoded value. Statements.lean:302-304 (conjunct 1 has no codec law), :305 (only conjunct 2 has G1CodecLaws). Blocks.lean:125 BlockSound ungated, :126 BlockProgress gated; :152-154 blockContracts_of_valid takes only hR. Assumptions/Eip2537.lean:130-164: every Holds field uses c.toEip / c.ofEip? only. Statements.lean:182-217: no Valid clause mentions toCompressed / ofCompressed? (vkPoints uses toEip; EnvAgrees, Inv.lean:533-579, has no codec field). Lowering/Inv.lean:770-771 `holdsG1s E st (ptrAddr M.adviceCommsMptrBase) (S.trace.adviceCommitments.getD 0 #[])`. Lowering/Calldata.lean:309-310: decodeSlots yields the point whose EIP bytes are in calldata. G1Agreement.lean:2005 x-only toCompressed. DECISIONS-PENDING.md:103-111 recommends Classical.choose for G2 and says to decide ofCompressed? 'at the same time'. I re-checked c' field by field: compressedRoundtrip' holds because the filter removes A and toCompressed' agrees with c.toCompressed off A; compressedWidth' is inherited.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
`example : ¬ ∀ Q, g1CodecZ.ofCompressed? (g1CodecZ.toCompressed Q) = some Q := fun h => by simpa [g1CodecZ] using h 1`. Abstract check: for a codec c with `c.ofCompressed? (c.toCompressed B) = some B` and A ≠ B, define c' as in the finding and prove `G1CodecLaws c → G1CodecLaws c'` and `c'.ofCompressed? (c'.toCompressed A) = some B`.
```
- **Suggested fix:** Either add `compressedEncoderFirst : ∀ Q, c.ofCompressed? (c.toCompressed Q) = some Q` to G1CodecLaws and make the laws a hypothesis of the soundness side too (conjunct 1, BlockSound, Rejects), or better, make the Lean side of LeanAccepts independent of the compressed codec by driving the transcript from messages directly (absorb toEip Q). The deployment codec then needs a real ZCash-compressed toCompressed (with flag bits) and a decoder proved against it.

### A-5 — Render.Valid does not tie the VK payload's commitment words to R.vk's commitments, nor E.delta to the emitted DELTA literal

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (delta: v1 only (Inv.lean:473, docs/plans/P-DELTA-GAP.md); not carried into PLAN-V2, QUEUE, STATUS or DECISIONS-PENDING. Commitment pin: not recorded anywhere. Recorded item 5 (G2 slot pins, Gap B) is the analogous gap for G2 only.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:124-125, 182-217; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:473-489, 533-579; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:68-72; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonValid.lean@6481731:412-415; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean@6481731:345-350, 380; proofs/solidity-verifier/templates/partials/quotient_numerator/QuotientNumeratorBlock.yul:1407

The only Valid clause mentioning the latter is vkPoints (readability, already implied by Holds.g1Valid). EnvAgrees pins domain data, the digest word and the two G2 params, not commitments. Inv does not take R.vk at all. So Valid cannot distinguish the real VK from one with arbitrary commitments, and the PCS and FinalPairing rows cannot be derived from Valid. E.delta is likewise unpinned ('STILL DROPPED' in Inv.lean) although the permutation identity uses it and the contract hard-codes Fr::DELTA; the deployment sets delta := 2. Neither gap appears in the V2 plan documents; D-P46 covers only the three base-slot pins.

PoseidonValid.lean@6481731:414-415 discharges it with `∀ x ∈ poseidonVkExec.fixedCommitments.toList, x = (1 : G₁z)` by native_decide, i.e. at an all-identity VK. PoseidonEnv.lean@6481731:380 `delta := 2`. QuotientNumeratorBlock.yul:1407 `let delta := {{ fr_delta }} // BLS12-381 Fr::DELTA`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take any R satisfying Valid and replace R.vk.fixedCommitments by an all-identity array (the current deployment does exactly this). No clause of Valid changes value, but the Lean DualMSM is now built from identity points while the contract uses the payload's 27 real points, so the PCS / FinalPairing BlockSound and BlockProgress are false. The same holds when E.delta is replaced by 2 for a circuit with a permutation argument.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed. The only Valid clause that mentions R.vk.fixedCommitments or R.vk.permutation.commitments is SupportedV2.vkPoints, which is readability and is literally the conclusion of Holds.g1Valid. EnvAgrees has ten fields, none about commitments or delta. Inv takes no VK; its .vk clause pins memory to the plan view's payload, not to R.vk. So Valid cannot distinguish R.vk from a VK with other commitments, or E.delta from any other value. The pcs and finalPairing rows therefore cannot follow from R.Valid, nor can the quotient row for a circuit with a permutation argument. At the deployment the mismatch is actual: R.vk carries 27 identity points and delta := 2, while the payload carries the real commitments and the template hard-codes Fr::DELTA. Neither pin is in PLAN-V2, QUEUE, STATUS or DECISIONS-PENDING; D-P46 covers only the three base slots. The delta gap is recorded for v1 only.

Inv.lean:473 `* delta : E.delta = (Nat.cast blsDeltaLiteral : Fr p). STILL DROPPED`. Inv.lean:741 `(bytesAt st (ptrAddr M.vkMptr) PV.vk.len == PV.vk.bytes)`; :824-827 `def Inv (E) (params) (k) (PV) (S) (V) (st)`. Blocks.lean:71 Clean uses `Inv ... R.E R.params k R.pv Snap V st`. `grep fixedCommitments|permutation.commitments` over Midfall/LoweringV2 hits only Statements.lean:124; `grep delta` over LoweringV2 hits only the phrase 'up to delta'. Instances/PoseidonValid.lean:442-445 discharges vkPoints with `∀ x ∈ poseidonVkExec.fixedCommitments.toList, x = (1 : G₁z)` by native_decide. PoseidonEnv.lean:196, :200, :230. Template QuotientNumeratorBlock.yul:1407 `let delta := {{ fr_delta }} // BLS12-381 Fr::DELTA`; proofs/src/plonk/permutation.rs:262, :276 use F::DELTA. DECISIONS-PENDING.md:56-73 (D-P46 draft has baseSlots only). docs/plans/P-DELTA-GAP.md is a v1 plan.
- **Suggested fix:** Add decidable Valid clauses: payload fixed-commitment section equals the concatenation of R.E.g1.toEip over R.vk.fixedCommitments, the same for permutation commitments, and a delta pin against a generator-side constant computed the way fr_delta_literal() is. Extend D-P46 accordingly and discharge them at the deployment with real commitments.

### A-6 — rejects_of_not_accepts is false as stated; its docstring's justification for dropping SoundSide is wrong

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (Recorded item 4; docs/STATUS.md@6481731:27-32; docs/design.md@6481731:1816-1819 ('Investigate first; do not weaken a hypothesis to move a number'). Not recorded: that the statement is false, that the docstring's reasoning is wrong, and that conjunct 3 can be proved without any rejection lemma.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:344-363, 606-608; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:698-706; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:269-276, 309-311
- **Claim (reviewer):** The obligation says: if not LeanAccepts then every permissive run is isRevertOrHalt, with no SoundSide. On the logup bad set the Lean verifier panics (not LeanAccepts) while the contract computes the prefix/suffix helper identity and can return ABI true, so the conclusion fails there. The docstring's reason ('SoundSide ... is a COMPLETENESS side condition: nothing in the Lean-rejects-so-the-contract-reverts direction needs it') contradicts the project's own Inv.lean and is the opposite of the truth: SoundSide exists precisely for that direction. Conjunct 3 does not need rejection in that branch at all, because its left disjunct (returns abiTrue with the world unchanged) covers an accepting run.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Blocks.lean:350-352 `SoundSide excludes the logup-panic bad set, which is a COMPLETENESS side condition: nothing in the "Lean rejects, so the contract reverts" direction needs it, so the hypothesis is spurious for this use.` Blocks.lean:360-362 `(hna : ¬ LeanAccepts R H cd) (hobs : RunBody ...) : obs.isRevertOrHalt`. Inv.lean:701-703 `Lean Logup.expressions panics when f_j(x) + beta = 0 ... On that set the contract accepts an honest proof and Lean rejects, so verifier_sound needs this for every H.`
- **Counterexample / witness:** H is universally quantified, so choose it. Take an honest prover on a satisfiable circuit with a lookup. After the advice commitments, fix a point x0 with x0^n ≠ 1 and define the digest at the beta squeeze so that beta = -f_j(x0); let the honest prover continue; define the digest at the x squeeze to give x0 (these are different transcript states from the single input pinned by DeploymentV2.vkHash). The proof is honest and valid, so the contract returns true under honest calls; Lean panics at logup.rs:460, so not LeanAccepts. The run is not isRevertOrHalt.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed, and the obligation is false for a second, more elementary reason. (1) As the reviewer says: it omits SoundSide, and on the logup bad set an honest proof makes Lean panic while the contract, which never inverts, returns true; H is universally quantified, so the bad point can be hit by choosing H. The docstring's rationale that SoundSide is 'a COMPLETENESS side condition' is wrong and contradicts Inv.lean:698-706. (2) New: it also omits `hR : R.Valid`. R.prog, R.consts, DeploymentV2, EntryFrame and Holds do not depend on R.cfg.numInstances, R.vk or R.params, while LeanAccepts does. Take any render R0 with a returning run on cd and set R1 := {R0 with cfg.numInstances := n+1}. Every hypothesis still holds, decodeCalldata fails its count and size guards so ¬LeanAccepts R1 H cd, and the same run returns. Conjunct 3 needs no rejection lemma in the ¬LeanAccepts branch, because its left disjunct already admits a run that returns ABI true.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Blocks.lean:356-363: binders are R, product, W, Lyt, H, vkAddr, cd, st₀, g, obs, hdep, hentry, hpc, `(hna : ¬ LeanAccepts R H cd)`, hobs; conclusion `obs.isRevertOrHalt`; there is no hR and no SoundSide. Blocks.lean:350-352 'SoundSide excludes the logup-panic bad set, which is a COMPLETENESS side condition: nothing in the "Lean rejects, so the contract reverts" direction needs it'. Inv.lean:701-703 'On that set the contract accepts an honest proof and Lean rejects, so verifier_sound needs this for every H.' Halo2/Exec/Logup.lean:277-279 `match invert f with | none => .error (Fault.panic "proofs/src/plonk/logup.rs:460")`; proofs/src/plonk/logup.rs:~458-462 `.map(|f| product * f.invert().unwrap())`; proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/quotient.rs:1815-1865 prefix/suffix products, no inversion. For (2): Statements.lean:93 `pv := planViewOf R.plan R.model R.cfg.accumulator`; :96 `consts := constTableOf R.model`; :99 `prog := asmOf R.pv R.model vkAddr qAddr`; :224-231 DeploymentV2 reads only R.plan.vk.runtimeBytes; :240-247 EntryFrame reads only R.consts; :263-267 LeanAccepts reads R.cfg.numInstances, R.vk, R.params, R.E. Lowering/Calldata.lean:340-341 `guard ((cdWord cd numInstCptr).toNat == numInstances)` and `guard (cd.size == instCptr + 32 * numInstances)`. Use site: Blocks.lean:606-608 (conjunct 3, ¬LeanAccepts branch) with hR in scope at :494. Statements.lean:309-311 conjunct 3.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
For (2): `example (R0 : LoweringV2.Render p G₁ G₂ Gₜ) : let R1 := { R0 with cfg := { R0.cfg with numInstances := R0.cfg.numInstances + 1 } }; R1.pv = R0.pv ∧ R1.consts = R0.consts ∧ (∀ a b, R1.prog a b = R0.prog a b) := ⟨rfl, rfl, fun _ _ => rfl⟩`, and `example (H cd) : LeanAccepts R0 H cd → ¬ LeanAccepts R1 H cd` from the size guard of decodeCalldata.
```
- **Suggested fix:** Delete rejects_of_not_accepts. Prove conjunct 3 from an invariant-free control-flow lemma: from any state satisfying only Frame, every run of each slice reverts/halts non-committing or exits normally, and traceReturn reverts or returns abiTrue (it ends in `mstore(RETURN_MPTR, 1) return(RETURN_MPTR, 0x20)`); combine with worldEq_of_runBody. Keep SoundSide on Rejects for conjunct 1.

### A-7 — SoundSide is selectable by the prover with probability 1; 'excludes a negligible bad set' describes acceptance, not membership

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → medium · **Kind:** statement-weaker-than-claimed · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (v1 finding 5 and Statements.lean:269-270 record the side condition and that it must appear in the English headline; docs/plans/P-SE-evidence/design1.md:73 and design2.md:58 discuss it. The adversarial selectability and the resulting gap in the soundness chain are not stated.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:269-276, 302-304; proofs/src/plonk/logup.rs:420-466; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Logup.lean:274-280; proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/quotient.rs:1826-1860
- **Claim (reviewer):** f_j(x) + beta is computed from advice / fixed / instance evaluations that the prover writes into the proof after beta and x are fixed. A prover can therefore make SoundSide false for any proof by setting one evaluation so that some f_j(x) = -beta; no grinding is needed. On that set the theorem says only conjunct 3 (returns true or reverts): it does not say the contract rejects, and it does not relate acceptance to the Lean verifier. That acceptance there is negligible is a separate cryptographic argument (KZG evaluation binding plus Schwartz-Zippel on honestly opened evaluations) which is not part of the formal chain and cannot be inherited from the Lean verifier's soundness, since that verifier aborts there. The English claim 'returns ABI true iff the Lean verifier accepts' is therefore stronger than the theorem.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** logup.rs:455-460 `chunk.iter().map(|input| compress_expressions(input) + beta)` ... `.map(|f| product * f.invert().unwrap())`, with `&|query| advice_evals[query.index.unwrap()]` at :424. Logup.lean:278-279 `match invert f with | none => .error (Fault.panic "proofs/src/plonk/logup.rs:460")`. Contract: quotient.rs:1826-1860 builds q_lookup_prefix / q_lookup_suffix products and sums them with no inversion. Statements.lean:269-270 `Excludes a negligible bad set; it MUST appear in the English headline.`
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take any accepted calldata and overwrite one advice evaluation word that occurs linearly in a lookup input so that the compressed input equals -beta. SoundSide is now false for this calldata by construction. The contract then proceeds to the pairing check; nothing in the theorem states its verdict.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed. SoundSide is a predicate on prover-supplied calldata: the lookup inputs f_j(x) are computed from the advice, fixed and instance evaluation words the prover writes after beta and x are fixed. A prover can make SoundSide false at will by choosing one evaluation so that a compressed input equals -beta. On that set the theorem gives only conjunct 3 (returns true or reverts), so 'the contract accepts implies the Lean verifier accepts' has no content there, and the contract's soundness on that set cannot be inherited from the Lean or Rust verifier. The docstring 'Excludes a negligible bad set' is true only of honest transcripts. This is a gap in the formal chain rather than a known attack: the contract checks the inversion-free identity, and a tampered evaluation should fail the KZG opening, but that argument is outside the formalisation.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Statements.lean:269-276 SoundSide (docstring 'Excludes a negligible bad set; it MUST appear in the English headline'); :302-304 conjunct 1 is guarded by SoundSide; :309-311 conjunct 3 is the only unconditional statement. proofs/src/plonk/logup.rs:420-430 evaluates inputs with `&|query| advice_evals[query.index.unwrap()]` (also fixed_evals, instance_evals), then `compress_expressions(input) + beta` and `f.invert().unwrap()`. Halo2/Exec/Logup.lean:274-280 is the port with the panic string. Prover control: Halo2/Exec/Verifier.lean readAlgebra reads the evaluation arrays from the transcript after x (AlgebraRead fields instanceEvals / adviceEvals / fixedEvals). Contract: proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/quotient.rs:1815-1865, no inversion.
- **Suggested fix:** State the English headline with both side conditions and say explicitly that SoundSide is a predicate on prover-chosen calldata. To close the gap formally, prove equivalence against a total reference verifier that uses the inversion-free helper identity (which is what the contract computes), and relate verifyMsgs to that reference: verifyMsgs accepts implies the reference accepts; the reference accepts and SoundSide imply verifyMsgs accepts.

### A-8 — Completeness is blind to forwarded precompile gas: the honest call relation ignores req.gas

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** partially-correct → low · **Kind:** trusted-base-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Yul/Externals.lean:779-805; formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:705-723; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:251-252, 305-308; proofs/solidity-verifier/templates/partials/verifier/AssemblyHelpers.yul:33, 294; proofs/solidity-verifier/templates/partials/verifier/Constants.sol:193-203
- **Claim (reviewer):** The gas oracle models only the value of the GAS opcode, and the in-scope program never executes gas() (it occurs only in trace, gas-checkpoint and external-quotient renders, all excluded by SupportedV2). So quantifying over g in soundness and fixing g to 0 in completeness are both immaterial and harmless. The real gas gap is elsewhere: every precompile call forwards a generator-emitted constant (MODEXP_GAS, G1ADD_GAS, G1MSM_GAS_1PAIR, PAIRING_GAS_2PAIR, the PCS MSM gas), and the honest relation answers success for any valid input regardless of the gas in the request. Completeness would therefore still be provable if the generator emitted a constant that is too low (even 0), while the real contract would reject every proof. Top-level out-of-gas is likewise unmodelled.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Externals.lean:799-805 `def ExternalCallRelation.respond (r) (req) : Option CallOutcome := if req.target != r.address || ! (r.validInput req.input) then none else match r.output req.input with | some out => some { success := true, returndata := out } ...` (neither req.gas nor r.gas is read). Externals.lean:786-787 `/-- The scheduled gas cost of the call ... -/ gas : Bytes -> Nat` is unused by respond. AssemblyHelpers.yul:33 `staticcall(MODEXP_GAS, ...)`. Gas constants are checked only by the #eval KAT gasScheduleKats_ok (Externals.lean:1238).
- **Counterexample / witness:** A render whose constant table has G1ADD_GAS = 0: the Lean completeness conjunct is unaffected (honest G1ADD still succeeds), but on chain every G1ADD staticcall fails and the contract reverts PrecompileFailed for every proof.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** The technical core is correct: the honest call relation never reads the forwarded gas, the gas oracle models only the GAS opcode, and gas() occurs only in trace, gas-checkpoint and external-quotient template branches, all excluded by SupportedV2. So the completeness conjunct would still be provable if the generator emitted too-low precompile gas constants, and no Valid clause pins MODEXP_GAS, G1ADD_GAS, G1MSM_GAS_1PAIR, PAIRING_GAS_2PAIR or the PCS MSM gas. The correction is to 'already recorded: no'. That gas is unmodelled is a recorded scope exclusion that must appear in the English headline. What is new is narrower: the forwarded-gas constants are generator output, so the exclusion leaves part of the generated code unverified for liveness. Soundness is unaffected, since the permissive relation allows any failure.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Yul/Externals.lean:799-805 `def ExternalCallRelation.respond (r) (req) : Option CallOutcome := if req.target != r.address || ! (r.validInput req.input) then none else match r.output req.input with | some out => some { success := true, returndata := out } ...`; neither req.gas nor r.gas is read. Externals.lean:786-787 the `gas` field is set at :838, :897, :962, :1000 and used only by the KAT gasScheduleKats_ok (:1238, :1315). Yul/Bridge.lean:705-712 honestOf uses respond; :718-723 honestCalls. Templates: AssemblyHelpers.yul:33 `staticcall(MODEXP_GAS, ...`, :294 `staticcall(PAIRING_GAS_2PAIR, ...`; Constants.sol:193-196 the four constants. gas() appears only at TraceAndGasHelpers.yul:21, QuotientAndLinearization.yul:28 and :35 (quotientEvaluator call), and :153 (inside `{%- if self.trace %}` opened at :129). `grep` for the gas constant names over Midfall/LoweringV2, Lowering/Inv.lean and Instances/PoseidonPins.lean returns nothing. Recorded: Bridge.lean:726-727 'any FAILURE is permitted (gas is unmodelled, ...'; docs/PLAN.md:80 'Out of scope: exact gas'; docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:356-359 the English statement must name 'that gas is not modelled'.
- **Suggested fix:** Either make respond fail when req.gas.toNat < r.gas req.input (so the constants become part of what completeness proves), or add decidable Valid clauses pinning each gas constant to at least the EIP schedule for the frame it is used with, and list 'transaction gas suffices' as an explicit side condition of completeness in the English headline.

### A-9 — EntryFrame.zeroAbove is stronger than the on-chain guard supports (zero from 0x80 rather than from TRANSCRIPT_MPTR)

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** unsound-or-unrealistic-assumption · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** low
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Shared.lean:86; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:240-247; proofs/solidity-verifier/templates/contracts/Halo2Verifier.sol:3-7, 173-176
- **Claim (reviewer):** The nominal solc frame is an instance of EntryFrame: ZeroAbove only constrains addresses at or above 0x80, so the free-memory-pointer word at 0x40..0x5f and dirty scratch below 0x40 are allowed. But the pinned compiler reserves stack-spill slots in [0x80, fmp) (measured 0x8c0 / 0x8e0), and the theorem requires those to be zero at body entry. If the optimised code spills any value before the first statement of the modelled program, the real state is outside EntryFrame and the theorem is silent. Only the weaker fact 'memory is zero from TRANSCRIPT_MPTR up, and fmp <= TRANSCRIPT_MPTR' is what the generated layout relies on and what the deployed guard enforces.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Shared.lean:86 `def ZeroAbove (mem₀ : Nat → UInt8) : Prop := ∀ a, 0x80 ≤ a → mem₀ a = 0`. Halo2Verifier.sol:4-7 `solc's stack-spill reservation stays below it -- measured 0x8c0 on 0.8.24 and 0x8e0 on 0.8.26+`. Halo2Verifier.sol guard `if gt(mload(0x40), TRANSCRIPT_MPTR) { ... revert }`. The only IR in the tree is unoptimised (`mstore(64, memoryguard(128))`, Midfall/Yul/solc-ir-Halo2Verifier.yul:288), so pre-body spills cannot be checked from the repository.
- **Suggested fix:** Weaken zeroAbove to `∀ a, t ≤ a → st.memory a = 0` for the t of the fmp clause (TRANSCRIPT_MPTR). This strictly enlarges the set of covered entry states and matches the guard the contract actually executes; Inv's g1_identity window clause still follows provided that window lies at or above TRANSCRIPT_MPTR.

### A-10 — permissive does not cover a call to a missing precompile (success with empty returndata)

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** doc-inaccuracy · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:725-732
- **Claim (reviewer):** The docstring says a failure is permitted because 'a precompile may be missing on a different deployment'. On the EVM a call to an address without code succeeds with empty returndata; it does not fail. permissive requires every success to be the honest answer, so executions on a chain without EIP-2537 are outside the modelled behaviours and the soundness conjunct does not formally apply to them. The contract's own returndatasize checks make it revert in that case, but that is not what the theorem says. This is a trusted-base boundary: Eip2537.Holds plus callsOf assume the four precompiles exist.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Bridge.lean:725-732 `any FAILURE is permitted (gas is unmodelled, a precompile may be missing on a different deployment), a SUCCESS must be the honest one` / `⟨fun req st resp => resp.world = CallWorld.ofState st ∧ (resp.success = true -> c.Call req st resp)⟩`. Contract check: AssemblyHelpers.yul:34 `if iszero(eq(returndatasize(), ...)) { fail(ERR_PRECOMPILE_FAILED) }`.
- **Suggested fix:** Correct the docstring and list 'the target chain implements EIP-2537 and modexp at the four addresses' in the trusted-base table; optionally add the empty-success response to permissive and prove the returndatasize guards reject it.

### A+1 — rejects_of_not_accepts has no R.Valid hypothesis, so it is false independently of the logup bad set (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:356-363 (statement), :606-608 (use); formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:93-99, 224-231, 240-247, 263-267; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Calldata.lean:340-341
- **Claim:** The sorried obligation quantifies over every Render with no validity hypothesis. Its conclusion is about R.prog, a function of R.plan, R.model and R.cfg.accumulator only. Its key hypothesis, ¬LeanAccepts, also depends on R.cfg.numInstances, R.vk, R.params and R.E. These are unrelated without Valid (vkPins.numInst and circuitMeta are the clauses that tie them). Counterexample: take any R0 with a returning permissive run on cd from an entry frame, and let R1 be R0 with cfg.numInstances increased by one. R1.prog, R1.consts, DeploymentV2, EntryFrame and Holds are unchanged; decodeCalldata fails its count and size guards, so ¬LeanAccepts R1 H cd; the run still returns. The statement is therefore false whenever any render has a returning run. Adding hR (it is in scope at the single use site) removes this defect but not the SoundSide one in A-6.
- **Evidence:** Blocks.lean:356-363 binder list ends `(hpc ...) (hna : ¬ LeanAccepts R H cd) (hobs : RunBody ...) : obs.isRevertOrHalt` with no `hR`. Statements.lean:93 `def pv : PlanView := planViewOf R.plan R.model R.cfg.accumulator`; :99 `def prog (vkAddr qAddr : U256) : YulBlock := asmOf R.pv R.model vkAddr qAddr`. By contrast prefix_run_clean (Blocks.lean:246) does carry `(hR : R.Valid)`.

### A — errors the skeptic found in the reviewer's report

The suggested fix (delete it and use a control-flow lemma) remains sound.
- A-1 'recorded_where' is incomplete. R-7 is also discussed at formal/docs/plans/P-SE-evidence/survey.md:401 and formal/docs/REVIEW.md:876-881, and both record a position ('matters only for Rust conformance claims'; 'unreachable for honest proofs') that the finding shows to be wrong. The conclusion is unchanged.

- Line citations to Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean, PoseidonCoherence.lean and PoseidonValid.lean are valid only at 6481731 (disclosed by the reviewer). At HEAD f2fe99b the same text is at PoseidonEnv.lean:196, 200, 229-230; PoseidonCoherence.lean:432-433; PoseidonValid.lean:442-445.

### A — checked and found correct

- Conjunct structure: STOP and falling off the end are neither isReturn nor isRevertOrHalt, and conjunct 3 excludes them (formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:540, 552-553; formal/vendor/yul-semantics/YulSemantics/Dialect/EVMExec.lean:262-264, 274-279). Conjunct 1's 'returnData = abiTrue' is implied by conjunct 3.
- obs.view = returnView st0 pins exactly: outcome = .halt, halted = some (.ret, abiTrue) with abiTrue the 32-byte big-endian 1, and all eleven WorldEq components equal to st0's; memory, activeWords and the returndata buffer are deliberately not pinned (Bridge.lean:510-537, 557-562, 660-664).
- For a revert the worldUnchanged half of conjunct 3 is automatic from committedState rollback; the meaningful world claim is the return case (Bridge.lean:668-670; Blocks.lean:399-414).
- Hash abstraction is consistent: extcodehash reads projectedCodeHash through env.keccakOf (EVMExec.lean:307-310, 859), which initState sets to hashOfBytes H (formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Shared.lean:62-72). H is pinned to keccak256 at exactly one input by DeploymentV2.vkHash (Statements.lean:229), matching Valid.vkProgressPins.vkHash (Statements.lean:167-168). In the production no-accumulator render the only keccak256 call is the transcript squeeze (AssemblyHelpers.yul:116); VK digest enters via vk.transcriptRepr pinned by EnvAgrees.vkDigestWord (Inv.lean:579).
- No hypothesis of LoweringEquivalence quantifies over programs or mentions the program; RunBody runs the fixed R.prog vkAddr 0 (Statements.lean:255-257, 298-311). EntryFrame.fmp mentions only the constant table.
- Gas oracle: gas() occurs only in trace, gas-checkpoint and external-quotient renders (TraceAndGasHelpers.yul:13-22; QuotientAndLinearization.yul:28, 35, 153), all excluded by SupportedV2 (Statements.lean:127, 132). So the universal g in soundness and the fixed g = 0 in completeness are immaterial for in-scope renders.
- DeploymentV2, EntryFrame and Eip2537.Holds are independent and jointly satisfiable; entryFrame_inhabited is proved without sorry (Blocks.lean:624-635). The nominal solc frame (fmp word at 0x40, zero above 0x80, zero callvalue, verifyProof selector) is an instance of EntryFrame; ZeroAbove does not contradict a nonzero free-memory pointer because that word lies below 0x80 (Shared.lean:86; Statements.lean:240-247).
- The ABI-shape guard block and the `let vk := <addr>` wrapper local are part of the modelled program (formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Program.lean:66-76, 105-116), so only solc's dispatcher, callvalue check and ABI decoder are trusted through EntryFrame.
- permissive over-approximates precompile failures and forces every success to be honest; honest runs are permissive runs (Bridge.lean:731-740).
- Logup divergence is as described: Rust panics at logup.rs:460, Lean ports the panic (formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Logup.lean:277-280), the contract uses prefix/suffix products (quotient.rs:1826-1860). The x3-in-point-set case is rejected on both sides: Lean panics (MultiOpen.lean:156-160) and scalar_inv reverts on zero (AssemblyHelpers.yul:22-23). The x^n - 1 inversion at verifier.rs:450 is trace-feature only.
- decodeCalldata's guards match the template checks I read: selector, head words, proof length, instance count, exact calldatasize, canonical scalars, and g1WordsOk against common_uncompressed_g1 (formal/Midfall/Lowering/Calldata.lean:288-346; Halo2Verifier.sol guard; VkLoading.yul; AssemblyHelpers.yul common_uncompressed_g1).
- lowering_equivalence_of_blocks (Blocks.lean:493-608) is sorry-free plumbing whose case analysis matches the three conjuncts, given its named obligations.
- EndToEnd: both directions are derivable in principle from LoweringEquivalence plus round-trip lemmas once calldataOf is repaired (A-3); hcomp is redundant with hcodec (G1CodecLaws.compressedRoundtrip), and hside is stronger than either direction needs.
- Features.solidity (committedInstances, truncatedChallenges on; outerFewerPointSets off) makes the dummy-query branch dead, so E.fewerPointSetsRuntime is irrelevant (formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Features.lean; MultiOpen.lean:249-252).

## B — the program and `Render.Valid`

**Reviewer summary.** The SHAPE of R.prog matches the template (ABI guard, vk local, MF-2 guard, helpers, `let r`/`let success`, seven partials, terminal return). The token comparator is adequate for this corpus, the inliner is correct on solc-compilable text, and NoWriters is sound in fuel and coverage. Render.Valid is not yet the right predicate, in three independent ways the project has not, or only partly, recorded.

1. Fidelity. The frozen clauses circuitMeta and layout mis-port plan.rs. Rust's plan meta is ConstraintSystemMeta::new post-processed by set_num_point_sets; Lean demands equality with the raw `new` (numPointSets = 0). Every faithful generator plan fails clause 1, and clauses 1 and 2 together force a proof layout without q_evals (4224 bytes against the deployed 4320). poseidon_valid is unprovable for the deployed contract even after all data fixes, and porting LoweringPlan::new would re-refute clause 1.

2. Weakness. Valid ties the payload to the Lean VK only through word 0. The header words, N_INV/OMEGA/OMEGA_INV_TO_L, the G1 base, the fixed and permutation commitments and plan.data are free. The generic hinge blockContracts_of_valid and vk_progress are therefore false as stated (counter-renders given). D-P46 covers only the base slots.

3. Structure. The frozen Render.scope assumes every funDef is a helper. The real template defines q_program_fail and q_pow5 inside the quotient partial, so after WP-G1 prog_slices_decomp, runBody_iff_slices and the quotient block contracts break.

Also: token equality plus `closed` does not pin builtin-versus-call or call resolution, and NoWriters inherits that blind spot. prog_slices_decomp is false for arbitrary R. Constant read-back by string parsing drives the recurring pin gaps.

HEAD moved from 6481731 to f2fe99b during the audit. Only Instances, docs, fixtures and a Rust test changed; the Instances and status evidence was re-read at f2fe99b. Nothing was run in Lean; all findings come from reading, so please verify them before relying on them.

### B-1 — Frozen Valid.circuitMeta + Valid.layout mis-port plan.rs: every faithful generator plan fails clause 1 (numPointSets)

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** fidelity-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonValid.lean:17-21 and :155-161 (f2fe99b) record the 4224-vs-4320 gap and attribute it to 'LoweringPlan::new's post-constructor sizing', as a clause-2 data/port gap. Not recorded: clauses 1 and 2 are incompatible at any faithful plan, the frozen clause 1 itself is wron […]) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:186-190; formal/Midfall/Generator/Encoding.lean:473-474; formal/Midfall/Generator/Vk.lean:1037-1038; proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/vk.rs:504-519; proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/plan.rs:238-244; proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/encoding/mod.rs:160-161
- **Claim (reviewer):** Valid.circuitMeta requires plan.meta_ to EQUAL the raw output of ConstraintSystemMeta.new, whose numPointSets is 0. In Rust the plan's meta is that value post-processed by meta_data_for_vk (set_num_dummy_evals, then set_num_point_sets(kzg::num_point_sets(&meta,&data))), and the proof layout is built from the post-processed num_point_sets. So at the generator's real plan (numPointSets >= 1 for any circuit with an opening) clause 1 is false. Clause 1 and clause 2 can hold together only for a plan whose proofLayout has ZERO q_eval words, which is not the deployed layout. poseidon_valid is therefore unprovable for the deployed contract regardless of fixture data, and WP-G4 (port LoweringPlan::new, then 'restate circuitMeta/layout as lemmas of the constructor') would refute clause 1 again.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Statements.lean:186-187 `circuitMeta : ConstraintSystemMeta.new Features.solidity R.vk.cs R.cfg.numCommittedInstances = .ok R.plan.meta_`; :189-190 `layout : ProofCalldataLayout.fromProtocol R.plan.meta_.protocol abiVerifyProofProofCptr R.plan.meta_.numEvals R.plan.meta_.numPointSets = .ok R.plan.proofLayout`. Encoding.lean:473-474 `numDummyEvals := 0` / `numPointSets := 0` (faithful to encoding/mod.rs:160-161). vk.rs:519 `meta.set_num_point_sets(kzg::num_point_sets(&meta, &data));`. plan.rs:238 `let (vk_mptr, meta, data, _) = inputs.meta_data_for_stable_static_layout(&vk);` and :239-244 `ProofCalldataLayout::from_protocol(&meta.protocol, ..., meta.num_evals, meta.num_point_sets)`. The Lean port of that step exists but Valid does not use it: Vk.lean:1037-1038 `let nSets ← numPointSets meta1 data` / `let meta2 := meta1.setNumPointSets nSets`. Abi.lean:392 `let (qEvals, cursor9) := ProofCalldataLayout.wordSection cursor8 numPointSets`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Deployment (f2fe99b): ConstraintSystemMeta.new on poseidonVkExec.cs gives numPointSets = 0, so fromProtocol yields proofLen 4224. The deployed layout is 4320 bytes with 3 q_eval words (PoseidonCoherence.lean:198 `repackedPlan.numPointSets == 3`; 96 bytes = 3 words). Faithful plan: meta_.numPointSets = 3, so clause 1 is false. Raw meta (the current poseidonPlanV2, clause 1 PROVED since F.1): clause 2 forces a 4224-byte layout, which differs from the deployed contract's PROOF length and q_evals loop.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Frozen Valid.circuitMeta demands R.plan.meta_ EQUAL the raw output of ConstraintSystemMeta.new, whose numPointSets is 0. Rust's plan meta is that value after meta_data_for_vk (set_num_dummy_evals, then set_num_point_sets), and the proof layout is built from the post-processed count. So a faithful plan fails clause 1 whenever numPointSets >= 1. Clauses 1 and 2 together force a proofLayout with zero q_eval words (4224 bytes at the deployment against the deployed 4320). poseidon_valid is therefore unprovable for the deployed contract whatever the fixture data, and PLAN-V2's WP-G4 step 'restate Valid.circuitMeta/layout/invariants as lemmas of the constructor' cannot succeed for circuitMeta. The project records the 96-byte gap as a clause-2 data/port gap only; the defect in frozen clause 1 is not recorded.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:186-190 `circuitMeta : ConstraintSystemMeta.new Features.solidity R.vk.cs R.cfg.numCommittedInstances = .ok R.plan.meta_` and `layout : ProofCalldataLayout.fromProtocol R.plan.meta_.protocol abiVerifyProofProofCptr R.plan.meta_.numEvals R.plan.meta_.numPointSets = .ok R.plan.proofLayout`. formal/Midfall/Generator/Encoding.lean:473-474 `numDummyEvals := 0` / `numPointSets := 0` inside ConstraintSystemMeta.new (matches proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/encoding/mod.rs:160-161). Rust vk.rs:504-519: `let mut meta = (*self.meta).clone(); ... meta.set_num_dummy_evals(n_dummy); ... meta.set_num_point_sets(kzg::num_point_sets(&meta, &data));`. plan.rs:238-244: `let (vk_mptr, meta, data, _) = inputs.meta_data_for_stable_static_layout(&vk); let proof_layout = ProofCalldataLayout::from_protocol(&meta.protocol, ..., meta.num_evals, meta.num_point_sets);` and that `meta` is stored in the plan. The Lean port of the step exists and is unused by Valid: Generator/Vk.lean:1037-1038 `let nSets ← numPointSets meta1 data` / `let meta2 := meta1.setNumPointSets nSets`. Abi.lean fromProtocol: `let (qEvals, cursor9) := ProofCalldataLayout.wordSection cursor8 numPointSets`. Deployment: Instances/PoseidonValid.lean:117-120 proves `ConstraintSystemMeta.new ... == poseidonPlanV2.meta_` (so the plan carries the raw meta, numPointSets 0); :17-21 records derived proofLen 4224 against 4320; PoseidonCoherence.lean:198 `repackedPlan.numPointSets == 3` (3 words = 96 bytes). Features.solidity has `outerFewerPointSets := false` (Halo2/Exec/Features.lean:33), so only numPointSets differs. docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:251-252: 'Then restate `Valid.circuitMeta/layout/invariants` as lemmas of the constructor.' I grepped the generator port: numPointSets is consumed only through fromProtocol, so no other clause rescues the pair. No doc (STATUS, QUEUE, DECISIONS-PENDING, design, REVIEW) says clause 1 is mis-stated.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
In Instances scope: `#eval (match Midfall.Generator.numPointSets poseidonPlanV2.meta_ poseidonPlanV2.data with | .ok n => n | .error _ => 999)` (expect 3) and `#eval (match ProofCalldataLayout.fromProtocol poseidonPlanV2.meta_.protocol abiVerifyProofProofCptr poseidonPlanV2.meta_.numEvals 3 with | .ok l => l.proofLen | .error _ => 0)` (expect 4320). These confirm the repaired clause pair would hold at the deployment; the defect itself is settled by reading.
```
- **Suggested fix:** Frozen-surface change (sign-off): replace clause 1 by the generator's actual derivation, e.g. `∃ m0 n, ConstraintSystemMeta.new Features.solidity R.vk.cs R.cfg.numCommittedInstances = .ok m0 ∧ numPointSets m0 R.plan.data = .ok n ∧ R.plan.meta_ = m0.setNumPointSets n` (Features.solidity has outerFewerPointSets off, so nDummy = 0). Alternatively state it through metaDataForVk / metaDataForStableStaticLayout (Vk.lean:1011, :1049). Then set poseidonPlanV2.meta_ to the post-processed meta and re-run the clause table.

Not recorded: the header words need […]) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:533-579, :741; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:71-86, :182-217; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:56-65, :152-154; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks/VkLoading.lean:118-122, :152-160, :751-756; formal/Midfall/Generator/LoweringPlan.lean:213-240, :534-618; proofs/solidity-verifier/templates/partials/verifier/VkLoading.yul:82-88; Lagrange.yul:17,30,43; proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/kzg/mod.rs:1867
- **Claim (reviewer):** In Rust, plan.vk, vk_mptr, data, memory and quotient are all derived from the halo2 VK (plan.rs:237-307). In Lean they are free Render fields, checked only for internal consistency. EnvAgrees (Valid.slots) constrains exactly ONE payload word (constants[0], the digest). Nothing relates to R.vk, R.E or R.params any of: (a) header words 1, 2, 7-10, which the contract cross-checks against rendered literals; (b) N_INV, OMEGA, OMEGA_INV_TO_L (words 3, 4, 6), read by Lagrange and PCS; (c) the G1 base (words 11-14), read by PCS; (d) fixed and permutation commitments; (e) plan.data (commitment and eval pointers; a Lean port Data.new exists at Encoding.lean:623 and is not used); (f) the quotient program and constants. Hence `blockContracts_of_valid (hR : R.Valid)` and the registered sorry `vk_progress` are false for some Valid renders, as soon as any Valid render with an accepting proof exists. D-P50's six pins and D-P46's base-slot pins do not close this.

Inv.lean:741 `(bytesAt st (ptrAddr M.vkMptr) PV.vk.len == PV.vk.bytes)` pins memory to the payload, not to the VK. (hR : R.Valid) : R.BlockContractsAt product (· ≠ .quotientLin)`.

Let R' be R with plan.vk.constants[1] (num_instances) changed and model regenerated; only EXPECTED_VK_CODEHASH_WORD and BUILD_ID change. All 13 clauses still hold. circuitMeta, layout, inputs and supported do not read plan.vk. invariants reads only lengths and a seed. The assembly text carries no payload words in separate-VK mode, so codegen, closed and noWriters are unchanged. slots reads constants[0]. vkPins and vkProgressPins hold (same length, recomputed hash). DeploymentV2 R' is satisfiable. From Clean .start the vkLoading slice copies the payload, the header check gives success = 0 and `fail(ERR_VK_MISMATCH)` reverts. So BlockProgress R' .vkLoading and conjunct 2 of LoweringEquivalence R' are false while R'.Valid holds. Soundness variant: perturb constants[4] (omega); the Lagrange slice then exits normal with success = 1 and wrong l_0/l_last/l_blind, which is neither Clean .lagrange, nor Poisoned, nor reverted, so BlockSound R' .lagrange is false. Data variant: plan.data := shiftedFixedComms (GenAstDispatch.lean:237) moves AST and text together and makes PCS read commitments one word off.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Valid relates the VK payload (R.plan.vk) to the Lean VK only through constants[0]. Header words, domain words, the G1 base, fixed and permutation commitments, plan.data and the quotient plan are free Render data checked only for internal consistency. The counter-render R' (R with payload word 1 changed and model regenerated) keeps all 13 clauses and reverts at ERR_VK_MISMATCH, so BlockProgress R' .vkLoading, blockContracts_of_valid at R' and conjunct 2 of LoweringEquivalence R' are false while R'.Valid holds. Two points go beyond the reviewer. (1) vk_progress is false at a genuine Valid render too, because it drops the decodeCalldata hypothesis (see additional finding 1). (2) The same freedom plausibly breaks headline SOUNDNESS generically: a payload carrying another circuit's fixed commitments with the same shape keeps Valid, and the contract then accepts proofs the Lean verifier rejects. I could not machine-check that last step. The project records the general 'hinge may be unprovable' concern; the specific untied items and the existing ports (Data.new, generateVk) that could state the tie are not recorded.

No Inv or Valid clause mentions `R.vk.fixedCommitments` except SupportedV2.vkPoints (readability of the Lean VK's own points, Statements.lean:124). Rust derives all of these from the halo2 VK (plan.rs:237-307). Data.new exists at Generator/Encoding.lean:623 and generateVk at Vk.lean:1093; neither is used by Valid. Note P currently fails invariants (clause 3), so equality of verdicts is the most this check can show today.
- **Suggested fix:** Stop adding pins one at a time. Add ONE payload clause, `payloadOf R.E R.vk R.params R.cfg = R.plan.vk` (header words; G1/G2 bases; fixedComms = vk.fixedCommitments.map toEip; permutationComms likewise), using Vk.lean's generateBaseVk/generateVk. Add `Data.new R.plan.meta_ R.plan.vk R.plan.memory = .ok R.plan.data`. Or add the single clause `R.plan = LoweringPlan.new ...` once ported. Add these rows to F.2's deployment table before further block rows are stated against `R.Valid`.
