### B-4 — CodegenAgrees + closed do not make the AST 'the parse of the text': builtin-vs-call and unresolved calls are invisible, and NoWriters inherits the blind spot

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** partially-correct → medium · **Kind:** statement-weaker-than-claimed · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (YulGolden.lean header and docs/REVIEW.md:1098, :1136 record the token relation's builtin/call blindness for the v1 golden gates (stmtsBeq is 'authoritative'). V2's Program.lean:186-189 and D-V2 clause 1 nevertheless present token equality as the link, and no V2 clause or card adds call resolution.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Program.lean:128-134, :186-194; formal/Midfall/Generator/YulGolden.lean:11-21, :76-120; formal/vendor/yul-semantics/YulSemantics/PrettyPrint.lean:41-45, :61-70; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Shared.lean:152-156, :192-193; formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:1073; formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAstDispatch.lean:289-297
- **Claim (reviewer):** Valid.codegen is token equality of EVM.print(AST) with the text. The printer is not injective. `Expr.builtin op args` and `Expr.call name args` print identically; identifiers print verbatim; `[letDecl [x] none, assign [] e]` prints as `let x := e`; `Expr.var "true"` prints as the literal. No other Valid clause repairs the first case. mainClosed's refE ignores call names, and noWritersScan treats `Expr.call "sstore" ..` as a non-writer. So a Valid render may carry an AST with `.call "mstore"` (or `.call "sstore"`, `.call "log1"`, `.call "call"`) where the text, and the compiled contract, has the builtin. In Lean that statement is stuck (no lookupFun), so every forall-runs conjunct (soundness, two outcomes, world unchanged) holds vacuously on paths through it. Only accept-paths are protected, by completeness. mainClosed is also a whole-block approximation ('bound anywhere' counts) and is computed on mainAsmRaw only, not on abiGuardBlock. It does not detect a helper body reading an outer local (it explicitly allows `vk` anywhere), out-of-scope uses, or arity errors.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Program.lean:190-192 `yulTokens (YulSemantics.EVM.print (abiGuardBlock m)) == abiGuardTokens m && yulTokens (YulSemantics.EVM.print (mainAsmRaw pv)) == mainAssemblyTokens m`. YulGolden.lean:13-18: 'PrettyPrint prints Expr.builtin and Expr.call with the same shape ... A port that emits .call where the Rust means the built-in passes every token gate'. GenAstDispatch.lean:290-294: the project's own gate proves `yulTokens (EVM.print good) == yulTokens (EVM.print bad) && ! SyntaxEq.stmtsBeq good bad` for staticcall spelled as a user call. Shared.lean:155 `| Expr.call _ args => args.flatMap refE`. Bridge.lean:1073 `| .e (.call _ args), rec => rec (.es args)`. Program.lean:133-134: mainClosed ranges over `mainAsmRaw pv` only and accepts `x == "vk" || x == "quotientEvaluator"` unconditionally.
- **Counterexample / witness:** P' := mainAsmRaw pv with one `Expr.builtin Op.mstore a` on a rejection-only branch replaced by `Expr.call "mstore" a`. codegenAgrees, mainClosed and noWritersFuel return the same values as for P. The Lean program has no run through that branch; the compiled contract does. The same holds with `Expr.call "sstore" [..]`: NoWriters holds while the text writes storage.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** The core holds: the frozen `codegen` clause is token equality of EVM.print(AST) with the text, the printer does not distinguish Expr.builtin from Expr.call, mainClosed ignores call names and counts a name bound anywhere as bound, and noWritersScan treats a user call named after a writer opcode as clean. So Valid does not establish that the AST is the parse of the text; what it leaves trusted is the emitter's builtin/call spelling, call resolution, arity and lexical scoping. The framing must be corrected: R.prog is not a Render field. It is `asmOf R.pv R.model` over the fixed emitter `mainAsmRaw pv`, so no Valid render can 'carry' a different AST and the reviewer's P' is not a Render. This is an emitter-fidelity (trusted-base and gate) gap, not a counter-render. It is latent today: every opcode-named user call in the emitter files (14 hits) sits in a negative-control mutation gate, none in an emission path. Exposure is limited to paths taken only by rejected inputs, since a stuck node on the accept path falsifies completeness. Related weakness worth stating in the headline's English: conjuncts 1 and 3 are forall-runs, so nothing proves a run exists (a revert happens) on rejected calldata.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Program.lean:190-192 `yulTokens (YulSemantics.EVM.print (abiGuardBlock m)) == abiGuardTokens m && yulTokens (YulSemantics.EVM.print (mainAsmRaw pv)) == mainAssemblyTokens m`. vendor/yul-semantics/YulSemantics/PrettyPrint.lean ppExpr: `| .builtin op args => pOp op ++ "(" ++ ppExprs pOp args ++ ")"` and `| .call fn args => fn ++ "(" ++ ppExprs pOp args ++ ")"`; `.var x => x` and `.bool true => "true"` print alike. Generator/YulGolden.lean:11-18: the token relation 'is STRICTLY WEAKER ... A port that emits .call where the Rust means the built-in passes every token gate'. Lowering/GenAstDispatch.lean:289-294 is the project's own gate showing equal tokens and unequal stmtsBeq for staticcall spelled as a user call. Shared.lean:155 `| Expr.call _ args => args.flatMap refE`; :192-193 freeNames filters refs against boundSs of the WHOLE block. Program.lean:132-134 mainClosed ranges over `mainAsmRaw pv` only and accepts `x == "vk" || x == "quotientEvaluator"`. Yul/Bridge.lean noWritersStep: `| .e (.call _ args), rec => rec (.es args)`. Against the reviewer's framing: Statements.lean:99 `def prog ... := asmOf R.pv R.model vkAddr qAddr`. My grep of `(ycall|Expr.call) "<opcode>"` in Lowering/GenAst*.lean and LoweringV2: GenAst.lean:1454, 1686, 1699, 1891, 2022, 2023, 2349, 2784, 2793; GenAstPcs.lean:953; GenAstDispatch.lean:291; GenAstQuotient.lean:828, 869 - each is a `good -> bad` rewrite or a `_wrongQuotation` used as a negative control.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
`example : yulTokens (YulSemantics.EVM.print [Stmt.exprStmt (Expr.builtin Op.sstore [Expr.lit (.number 0), Expr.lit (.number 1)])]) = yulTokens (YulSemantics.EVM.print [Stmt.exprStmt (Expr.call "sstore" [Expr.lit (.number 0), Expr.lit (.number 1)])]) := by native_decide` and `example : Midfall.Yul.Bridge.NoWriters [Stmt.exprStmt (Expr.call "sstore" [Expr.lit (.number 0), Expr.lit (.number 1)])] := ⟨8, by decide⟩`.
```
- **Suggested fix:** Add one decidable Valid clause on `R.prog 0 0`: every `Expr.call f args` has f among the block's funDef names with matching arity; no funDef name is in the image of EVM.opName; binder lists are non-empty; identifiers are lexically valid. With it, token equality on this string-literal-free corpus does determine the AST. Alternatively compare with stmtsBeq against per-slice `yul%` quotations.

### B-5 — prog_slices_decomp (and what depends on it) is stated for every R with no hypothesis and is false when a constant name collides with a top-level binder; Valid gives no 'no shadowing' fact

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → medium · **Kind:** statement-false · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:220-225, :276-285; formal/Midfall/Lowering/InlineConstants.lean:263-267, :361-369; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Program.lean:58-60, :88-90; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/ProgramLemmas.lean:27-31; formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAst.lean:537, :694
- **Claim (reviewer):** The inliner threads a shadow list through a block: each let or assign target, and each funDef's parameters and returns, shadow all LATER siblings. `asmOf` inlines the whole program at once; `R.slice q` inlines each slice from an empty shadow list. The two agree only if no top-level binder of the prefix (vk, helper parameters such as `success`, `r`, `sel`, the main locals `r` / `success`, earlier slices' lets) is a constant name. That is the side condition of inline_append. prog_slices_decomp, runBody_iff_slices and prefix_run_clean omit it and quantify over all R, so they are false as stated. Under Valid the condition is true at genuine renders (constants are upper-case) but is not derivable from any clause: constTableOf filters only "vk" and "success". Future rows for slices that read `r` (lagrange, transcriptParser, pcs, quotientLin) will need `R.consts.lookup "r" = none`, which is one more missing pin.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** InlineConstants.lean:264-266 `| Stmt.funDef _ ps rs _ => ps ++ rs | Stmt.letDecl xs _ => xs | Stmt.assign xs _ => xs`; :366 `sh := acc.sh ++ boundOf s`. Program.lean:60 `.filter fun c => c.1 != "vk" && c.1 != "success"`; :89 `Stmt.letDecl ["r"] (some (Expr.var "FR_MODULUS"))`. GenAst.lean:694 `[yset "x_n" (yop Op.mulmod [yvar "x_n", yvar "x_n", yvar "r"])]`. Blocks.lean:220-223: no hypothesis on R. ProgramLemmas.lean:28 carries the hypothesis `(h : ∀ x ∈ topBinders a, x ∉ consts.map Prod.fst)`. VkLoading.lean:102-104 documents the per-slice 'unshadowed' site for `success`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take any R whose model has `quotientDirect := some { constants := ["uint256 internal constant r = 5;"], functions := [], block := [] }` (Models.lean:697-703; printed by renderConstants at VerifierPartials.lean:2158-2160; parsed by solConstantLine?). Then R.consts contains ("r", 5). In R.prog the Lagrange uses of `r` stay `Expr.var "r"` (shadowed by `let r`); in R.slice .lagrange they become `lit 5`. So R.prog ≠ preSlice ++ helpers ++ mainLocalsInlined ++ slices.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** prog_slices_decomp (and runBody_iff_slices, which rewrites by it) is stated for every R with no hypothesis. It is false for a Render whose constant table binds a name that a top-level binder of the prefix also binds, because asmOf inlines the whole program with a threaded shadow list while R.slice and R.helpers inline from an empty one. The reviewer's witness works: a model with quotientDirect constants line `uint256 internal constant r = 5;` puts ("r", 5) in R.consts; Lagrange's `r` stays a variable in R.prog and becomes `lit 5` in R.slice .lagrange. For genuine VM renders the side condition is true but no Valid clause yields it (constTableOf filters only "vk" and "success"), so the generic composition cannot discharge inline_append's hypothesis from hR. The repair need not touch the frozen surface: the lemmas and lowering_equivalence_of_blocks live in refinable Blocks.lean and can take a decidable no-shadow hypothesis discharged per deployment.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Blocks.lean:220-225 prog_slices_decomp has binders `(R) (vkAddr qAddr) (c) (g)` only. Program.lean:114-116 `asmOf ... := Inline.inlineContractConstants (constTableOf m) (abiGuardBlock m ++ wrapperLocals m vkAddr qAddr ++ mainAsmRaw pv)`; :125-126 sliceOf inlines `GenAst.emitPartial pv q` alone. Lowering/InlineConstants.lean boundOf: `| Stmt.funDef _ ps rs _ => ps ++ rs | Stmt.letDecl xs _ => xs | Stmt.assign xs _ => xs`; inlineStep: `sh := acc.sh ++ boundOf s`; inExpr leaves `Expr.var x` when `shadowed sh x`. Program.lean:89 `Stmt.letDecl ["r"] (some (Expr.var "FR_MODULUS"))`; GenAst.lean:694 `[yset "x_n" (yop Op.mulmod [yvar "x_n", yvar "x_n", yvar "r"])]` with no binder for r inside the slice. Program.lean:58-60 constTableOf filters `c.1 != "vk" && c.1 != "success"` only. Generator/Models.lean:697-703 DirectQuotientRendering.constants : List String; VerifierPartials.lean renderConstants ends with `(match ctx.quotientDirect with | some qdirect => (jinjaFor qdirect.constants (fun line loop => "\n    " ++ line))`. ProgramLemmas.lean:27-31 inline_append carries exactly the missing hypothesis `(h : ∀ x ∈ topBinders a, x ∉ consts.map Prod.fst)`.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
`example : Inline.inlineContractConstants [("r", 5)] (mainLocals ++ [Stmt.exprStmt (Expr.var "r")]) ≠ Inline.inlineContractConstants [("r", 5)] mainLocals ++ Inline.inlineContractConstants [("r", 5)] [Stmt.exprStmt (Expr.var "r")] := by decide` (open Midfall.LoweringV2 Midfall.Lowering YulSemantics).
```
- **Suggested fix:** Add a decidable clause `noShadow` to Valid: no binder (let or assign target, funDef parameter or return) of abiGuardBlock ++ wrapperLocals ++ mainAsmRaw is in `R.consts.map Prod.fst`. solc rejects such shadowing anyway, so this also aligns the model with Solidity. Give prog_slices_decomp, runBody_iff_slices and the block rows `hR : R.Valid`. Drop the ad-hoc vk/success filter once noShadow exists.

### B-6 — constTableOf reads the constant table back by string-parsing the rendered Solidity; the promised KAT and 'WP-G2' do not exist, and this is the root of the recurring per-constant pin gaps

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** partially-correct → medium · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (docs/STATUS.md 'STRUCTURAL FINDING' records the symptom (pins found one card at a time); the Program.lean docstring records the intent. The missing KAT, the missing WP-G2 plan entry and the parser's silent mis-parse are not recorded.) · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Program.lean:51-60; formal/Midfall/Lowering/InlineConstants.lean:96-112; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:147-168, :212; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks/TraceReturn.lean:217-218; formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md §5; formal/docs/QUEUE.md:1101
- **Claim (reviewer):** R.consts is `filterMap solConstantLine?` over `renderConstants ... |>.splitOn "\n"`. (1) The parser is permissive. natOfLiteral drops non-digit characters in the decimal branch and maps non-hex characters to 0 in the hex branch, so an expression RHS (`2**8`, `A + 1`, `1e3`) parses silently to a wrong number. Only `uint256 internal constant` lines are read; a trailing comment containing `=` or `;` drops the line. Program.lean says a KAT 'must check' literal-only RHS until WP-G2; neither that KAT (no Instances/CodegenAgreement.lean; G.1 not run) nor a WP-G2 entry in PLAN-V2 or QUEUE exists. (2) Any GENERIC fact `R.consts.lookup X = some (f R.pv)` must be proved through String.splitOn / startsWith on a symbolic string. That is why TRANSCRIPT_MPTR, RETURN_MPTR and the six vk pins each became separate Valid clauses or row hypotheses. prefix_run_clean alone still needs NUM_INSTANCE_CPTR = proofLayout.proofEnd, ERR_BAD_CALLDATA_SHAPE, ERR_MEMORY_LAYOUT_VIOLATED and FR_MODULUS = p. Every remaining row needs its *_MPTR constants equal to pv.memory fields.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Program.lean:55-57 'WP-G2 replaces the read-back by a data-level table from which `renderConstants` is printed; until then a KAT must check that every `uint256 internal constant` line of `renderConstants` parses to a literal'. InlineConstants.lean:100 `s.toList.foldl (fun acc c => if c.isDigit then acc * 10 + (c.toNat - '0'.toNat) else acc) 0`; :104-106 prefix `"uint256 internal constant "`. `grep -rn WP-G2 docs Midfall` returns only Program.lean:55. Statements.lean:212 pins TRANSCRIPT_MPTR only; TraceReturn.lean:217-218 `def trRetMptr ... := ∃ m, R.consts.lookup "RETURN_MPTR" = some m` remains a row hypothesis. Checked: every `uint256 internal constant` RHS in templates/partials/verifier/Constants.sol renders to a literal; the two expression-valued constants are `bytes32` (:12, :216) and are not used in assembly, so today's table is right for the deployed profile.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Hypothetical line `uint256 internal constant X = 2**8;` gives ("X", 28). No Valid clause or gate rejects it.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed: R.consts is a string parse of the rendered Constants text; the parser silently mis-parses non-literal right-hand sides; the KAT that Program.lean says 'must check' this does not exist (no Instances/CodegenAgreement.lean, QUEUE G.1 not run); and 'WP-G2' is named only in Program.lean:55, with no entry in PLAN-V2 or QUEUE. Generic facts of the form `R.consts.lookup X = some (f R.pv)` therefore go through String.splitOn/startsWith on a symbolic string, which is the common cause of the per-constant pins. Two corrections. (1) prefix_run_clean does not need ERR_BAD_CALLDATA_SHAPE or ERR_MEMORY_LAYOUT_VIOLATED: they occur only inside guard bodies the accepting run does not execute; it does need NUM_INSTANCE_CPTR = proofLayout.proofEnd, TRANSCRIPT_MPTR (already pinned) and FR_MODULUS = p. (2) The mis-parse is not only hypothetical within the port's own printer: printValue renders a symbolic or negative pointer as `add(name, 0x..)` / `sub(0, 0x..)`, which the decimal branch reads as 20 for `sub(0, 0x20)`. Today's deployed table is right: every uint256 constant line of the fixture is a literal.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Program.lean:55-60: 'WP-G2 replaces the read-back by a data-level table ...; until then a KAT must check that every uint256 internal constant line of renderConstants parses to a literal' and `(((renderConstants (partialCtxOfVerifier m)).splitOn "\n").filterMap Inline.solConstantLine?).filter ...`. Lowering/InlineConstants.lean natOfLiteral: decimal branch `s.toList.foldl (fun acc c => if c.isDigit then acc * 10 + (c.toNat - '0'.toNat) else acc) 0`; hexDigitVal ends `else 0`; solConstantLine? requires the prefix `"uint256 internal constant "` and exactly `[nm, v]` from splitOn "=" and `[val, _]` from splitOn ";". `grep -rn WP-G2 docs Midfall` returns only Program.lean:55; PLAN-V2 lists WP-G1, G3, G4. docs/QUEUE.md:1101 G.1 names `Instances/CodegenAgreement.lean`, which is absent from Midfall/Instances/. Statements.lean:212 pins TRANSCRIPT_MPTR only; Blocks/TraceReturn.lean:217-218 `def trRetMptr ... := ∃ m, R.consts.lookup "RETURN_MPTR" = some m` is still a row hypothesis. Against sub-claim (1): Program.lean:74-76 and :83-85 place the two ERR_ names inside the `Stmt.cond` bodies; prefix_run_clean (Blocks.lean:246-260) asserts one `.normal` run under LeanAccepts. For (2): Generator/Filters.lean:87-93 printValue `| .integer i => if i >= 0 then fmtHex i else "sub(0, " ++ fmtHex (-i) ++ ")"` and `"add(" ++ name ++ ", " ++ fmtHex off ++ ")"`. Template check: all 93 `uint256 internal constant` lines of Constants.sol render to literals; four (:102, :105-:107) carry a trailing `// 4 words` comment the parser tolerates; fixture lines 167-172 agree.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
`#eval Midfall.Lowering.Inline.solConstantLine? "    uint256 internal constant X = 2**8;"` (expect `some ("X", 28)`) and `#eval Midfall.Lowering.Inline.solConstantLine? "    uint256 internal constant Y = sub(0, 0x20);"` (expect `some ("Y", 20)`).
```
- **Suggested fix:** Create the WP-G2 card: a data-level `constsOfModel : Model → List (String × Nat)` from which renderConstants is printed, plus a per-render decidable clause or KAT `constTableOf m = constsOfModel m`. All pins then become structural lookups provable for every R. Until then land G.1 with the literal-RHS check.

### B-7 — Valid admits renders the Rust generator refuses: Halo2Verifier.validate_layout is not a clause, and SupportedV2 does not pin VM lowering

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** fidelity-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:116-134, :194; formal/Midfall/Generator/Artifacts.lean:351-423, :492-504; proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/artifacts.rs:338; formal/Midfall/Generator/LoweringPlan.lean:553-575
- **Claim (reviewer):** (1) Rust's generate_verifier_from_plan panics unless `validate_layout()` passes (artifacts.rs:338). The Lean port deliberately splits the validator out, and Valid.model only asks `generateVerifierFromPlan ... = .ok R.model`, so layout violations the generator would abort on are Valid. (2) The SupportedV2 docstring states the scope 'embedded quotient VM', but the only quotient clause is `flags.externalQuotient = false ∧ flags.expectedQuotient = none`, which also admits Direct lowering. There plan.direct.rendering (constants, functions, block: free string lists) feeds the model, and validateGeneratorInvariants returns early without any quotient-program check.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Artifacts.lean:495-498 'the ART:337-339 `validate_layout().unwrap_or_else(panic)` is NOT folded in here ... the port keeps the two steps separate'. Statements.lean:194 `model : generateVerifierFromPlan (fun _ _ => R.qblocks) R.inputs R.flags R.plan = .ok R.model`. Statements.lean:131-132 `embeddedQuotient : R.flags.externalQuotient = false ∧ R.flags.expectedQuotient = none`. LoweringPlan.lean:553-558: `| some d => ... .ok (do ...)` is the Direct arm, 'returns Ok early and never reaches the VM checks'.
- **Counterexample / witness:** A plan with lowering = direct and direct.rendering = { constants := [], functions := [], block := [] } has no quotient-numerator code at all, yet passes the invariants arm (table = [], quotientProgramWords = 0).
- **Suggested fix:** Add `R.model.validateLayout = .ok ()` to Valid. Add `R.plan.lowering = QuotientLowering.vm ∧ R.plan.direct = none` to SupportedV2 (first-headline scope).

### B-8 — WP-G1 cannot land inside the frozen Program.lean: mainAsmRaw takes only a PlanView, which carries neither the constraint-system expressions nor qblocks

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (PLAN-V2 WP-G1 mentions extending mainAsmRaw; QUEUE G.4 splits the region into 'template / model fields / compact-block lines'. Neither records the frozen-surface conflict or that PlanView lacks the inputs.) · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Program.lean:46-49, :98-99, :151-152; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:135-225; formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAstQuotient.lean:162, :355-374; formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:94-97, :232-237; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:66-70
- **Claim (reviewer):** The quotient-numerator text is the model's strings, which are R.qblocks verbatim plus the VM template. `qblocks` is 'CHECKED, not trusted' only if an AST emitter independently produces the same tokens. The emitter's sole input is `pv : PlanView` (meta_, dat, memory, counts); it holds no gate, lookup or permutation expressions and no qblocks. The existing AST twins in GenAstQuotient consume `Expression F`. So G.3/G.4 must change the signature of the frozen `mainAsmRaw` / `planViewOf` (or PlanView) and will probably need a new Valid clause. PLAN-V2 says 'Extend mainAsmRaw accordingly' without flagging that Program.lean §2 is frozen. Until then qblocks is checked by nothing: Valid.codegen is simply false.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Program.lean:98-99 `def mainAsmRaw (pv : PlanView) : YulBlock := mainGuardBlock ++ GenAst.emitHelpers pv ++ mainLocals ++ GenAst.emitSlices pv`; :151-152 `quotientAndLinearization := renderQuotientAndLinearization b (renderQuotientHelpers b) (renderQuotientNumeratorBlock b)`. Statements.lean:68-70 '`qblocks` ... is CHECKED, not trusted'. PLAN-V2.md:94-95 'Frozen surface ... `Program.lean` §2–§4'; :235 'Extend `mainAsmRaw` accordingly'. GenAstQuotient.lean:162 `def evaluateBasicCoreA {F : Type} [BEq F] (expression : Expression F) : E F (Block Op × String)`.
- **Suggested fix:** Decide now, with sign-off, what feeds the numerator AST (R.vk.cs expressions via GenAstQuotient, or a port of QRS:2000 that emits AST). Change Render.pv / mainAsmRaw once, together with the scope change of B-3, so the frozen surface is re-hashed a single time.

### B-9 — Two Solidity-side trust items the one-block model relies on are not in the trusted-base row

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** trusted-base-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:54-59; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:233-247; proofs/solidity-verifier/templates/contracts/Halo2Verifier.sol:136-139, :171-191
- **Claim (reviewer):** (1) Both assembly blocks are annotated `("memory-safe")`, which licenses solc's stack-to-memory mover to keep spilled Yul variables in [0x80, initial fmp). The Yul-level model (variables in VEnv, ZeroAbove memory) matches the bytecode only if R.prog never reads or writes [0x40, TRANSCRIPT_MPTR) apart from reading 0x40. That footprint property is neither proved nor listed as assumed; the MF-2 guard checks only that fmp ≤ TRANSCRIPT_MPTR. (2) PLAN-V2 argues that solc's pre-assembly code 'can only revert, so soundness and two-outcomes do not depend on it'. Completeness does depend on it: solc's ABI decoder for (bytes calldata, uint256[] calldata) must not revert on any calldata that decodeCalldata accepts. That is also unlisted. Minor: the row says '`view` ⇒ zero call value'; the callvalue check comes from non-payability, not from `view`.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Halo2Verifier.sol:172-175 'The `memory-safe` annotation above is what enables solc's stack-to-memory mover, which reserves spill slots upward from 0x80. The generated layout below writes absolute addresses from TRANSCRIPT_MPTR upward'. PLAN-V2.md:56-59 trusted row: 'selector routing, `view` ⇒ zero call value, ABI decoding only reads calldata, solc's free-memory prologue leaves `mload(0x40) ≤ TRANSCRIPT_MPTR`, fresh frame ... Everything solc adds before the assembly can only revert'. Statements.lean:244 `zeroAbove : ZeroAbove st.memory`.
- **Suggested fix:** Add both items to the trusted row and to the English headline. Or prove the footprint as a block-level invariant (every memory access of R.prog is below 0x40, or at or above the TRANSCRIPT_MPTR constant) and state decoder acceptance as an explicit EntryFrame-existence assumption.

### B+1 — vk_progress (registered sorry) is false as stated: it drops the decodeCalldata hypothesis, so it claims progress for wrong-shape calldata (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks/VkLoading.lean:751-756 and :766-771; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:106-111; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:741-749; formal/SORRY.md:66
- **Claim:** OBLIGATION FALSE AS STATED. vk_progress quantifies over an arbitrary cd and an unrelated Snap and concludes BlockProgress R .vkLoading Snap H vkAddr product cd. BlockProgress is total: from every Clean .start state a run must exist and end `.normal ∧ Clean R .vk`. Clean .start puts no constraint on the calldata beyond `st.env.calldata = cd`, while Clean .vk requires `st.env.calldata.length == proofEnd + 32 + 32 * numInstances`, and the slice itself reverts with ERR_BAD_CALLDATA_SHAPE on a wrong shape. Witness: any Valid render with one accepting snapshot (so ChallengesGood and DualMSM.check hold for that Snap), a cd of the wrong length, and the obvious Clean .start state (r, vk, success = 1 bound; env.calldata = cd; VK runtime deployed). No run can satisfy the post. The caller vk_blockRow has hdec/hinst/hsnap in scope and does not pass them. Repair: add the three decode hypotheses to vk_progress (refinable file, no frozen change). vk_sound_clean is not falsified by this, but without hdec it needs an INSTANCE_CPTR = proofEnd + 32 table fact that vkPins does not carry.
- **Evidence:** VkLoading.lean:751-755 `theorem vk_progress (R) (product) (hR : R.Valid) (hpins : vkPins R) (hppins : vkProgressPins R) (Snap) (H) (vkAddr) (cd : Bytes) : G1CodecLaws R.E.g1 → ChallengesGood R.vk Snap → DualMSM.check Snap.c.dual R.params = true → BlockProgress R Partial.vkLoading Snap H vkAddr product cd`. :768-771 `intro hR _hpc H vkAddr cd ms instW inst Snap hdec hinst hsnap` then `vk_progress R product hR hR.vkPins hR.vkProgressPins Snap H vkAddr cd _hcodec _hcg _hdual` (hdec unused). Blocks.lean:106-111 BlockProgress := StmtsTotal ... `(fun V st => Clean R q.pre Snap H vkAddr cd V st) (fun _ _ V' st' o => exitOk R q Snap H vkAddr cd V' st' o)`; :48-52 StmtsTotal = StmtsContract ∧ StmtsContractAll. Inv.lean:740-749: the `.vk` block, guarded by `if Checkpoint.vk ≤ k`, contains `(st.env.calldata.length == PV.proofLayout.proofEnd + 32 + 32 * PV.numInstances)`; Checkpoint.idx .start = 0 < 1. VkLoading.lean:141-148 vkS12 `success := and(success, eq(calldatasize(), add(INSTANCE_CPTR, n*32)))` and vkS13 `if iszero(success) { fail(ERR_BAD_CALLDATA_SHAPE) }`. The file's own header (:706-708) says the calldata-length clause comes 'via decodeCalldata's own guard', which the statement does not take.

### B+2 — VerifyingKey.WellFormed.permutationCommitmentsMatch is mis-stated against Rust, so SupportedV2.wf (hence Render.Valid) is unsatisfiable for real circuits; recorded as a refuted clause but no repair card exists (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/VerifyingKey.lean:99-102, :114-116; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Permutation.lean:203-210; proofs/src/plonk/permutation/keygen.rs:244-250; proofs/src/plonk/permutation/verifier.rs:41-46; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:128; formal/Midfall/Statements.lean:93; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonValid.lean:38-43, :399-413; formal/docs/STATUS.md:279
- **Claim:** ASSUMPTION UNSATISFIABLE / FIDELITY GAP, partly recorded at f2fe99b. The clause demands `vk.permutation.commitments.size = divCeil columns.size (csDegree - 2)`, i.e. one VK commitment per CHUNK. In Rust the VK holds one sigma commitment per permutation COLUMN; the per-chunk objects are the product commitments read from the proof. The Lean verifier itself reads `vk.commitments.size` permutation evals and documents 'the number of sigma polynomials'. So WellFormed is false for every circuit with more permutation columns than chunks (8 against 3 at the deployment), independent of any fixture data. Consequences: Valid clause 9 can never hold at the deployment, so poseidon_valid is unprovable until this v1 definition changes; and the v1 frozen theorem verifyMsgs_iff_spec, which takes `hwf : vk.WellFormed F`, has an unsatisfiable hypothesis there. The project records the refutation and calls it 'a v1 Exec/VerifyingKey defect', but QUEUE.md and DECISIONS-PENDING.md contain no item to repair it (grep for WellFormed, sigma, clause 9 returns nothing relevant). Repair: `vk.permutation.commitments.size = vk.cs.permutation.columns.size`, with sign-off because the v1 frozen statements consume WellFormed.
- **Evidence:** VerifyingKey.lean:114-116 `permutationCommitmentsMatch : vk.permutation.commitments.size = divCeil vk.cs.permutation.columns.size (vk.csDegree - 2)`; its docstring :99-102 justifies it by 'read_product_commitments reads one commitment per CHUNK'. keygen.rs:244-250 `let mut commitments = Vec::with_capacity(p.columns.len()); for permutation in &permutations { commitments.push(CS::commit_lagrange(params, permutation)); } VerifyingKey { commitments }`. verifier.rs:41-46 `let chunk_len = vk.cs_degree - 2; let permutation_product_commitments = self.columns.chunks(chunk_len).map(|_| transcript.read())` (read from the transcript, not the VK). Permutation.lean:203-209 'One scalar is read per commitment in the VK: the number of sigma polynomials ...' / `tmReadN hashableFr vk.commitments.size`. PoseidonValid.lean:399-402 `poseidonVkExec.permutation.commitments.size ≠ divCeil poseidonVkExec.cs.permutation.columns.size (poseidonVkExec.csDegree - 2)` and :411-413 `¬ SupportedV2 poseidonRender` via `h.wf.permutationCommitmentsMatch`. Statements.lean:128 `wf : R.vk.WellFormed Features.solidity`. Midfall/Statements.lean:93 `theorem verifyMsgs_iff_spec (hwf : vk.WellFormed F)`.

### B+3 — SupportedV2 says 'embedded quotient VM' but pins neither plan.lowering nor plan.direct; a Direct-lowering plan with free rendering text passes supported, invariants and model (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** low
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:32-33, :131-132; formal/Midfall/Generator/LoweringPlan.lean:167-184, :555-575, :640-642; formal/Midfall/Generator/Models.lean:697-703
- **Claim:** STATEMENT WEAKER THAN ITS DOCSTRING. The first-headline scope is described as 'embedded quotient VM'. The clause only excludes the external evaluator. R.plan.direct is a free field whose `rendering` carries three free string lists (constants, functions, block). validateGeneratorInvariants accepts a Direct plan after light size and table checks, and generateVerifierFromPlan copies the strings into the model, the constant table and the text. Only `codegen` stands between such a render and Valid, and that depends on what the still-incomplete emitter does for a Direct plan. Impact today is bounded because codegen is false for every render; it matters for the planned generic statement and feeds B-5/B-6 (free constant lines). Repair: add `R.plan.direct = none ∧ R.plan.lowering = .vm` to SupportedV2 (frozen, sign-off), or say in the scope text that Direct is admitted.
- **Evidence:** Statements.lean:32-33 'First-headline scope (SupportedV2): no accumulator, embedded quotient VM, separate VK contract, production render'; :131-132 `embeddedQuotient : R.flags.externalQuotient = false ∧ R.flags.expectedQuotient = none`. LoweringPlan.lean:640-642 `match L.direct with | some d => .ok (QuotientRendering.direct (rendering := if trace then d.traceRendering else d.rendering))`; :555-575 direct branch of validateGeneratorInvariants checks only `L.quotient.isSome || L.lowering != .direct`, `quotientProgramWords`, `quotientConstWords` and the table words. Models.lean:697-703 `structure DirectQuotientRendering where constants : List String; functions : List String; block : List String`. GenAst.lean has no Direct arm (grep for direct/Direct finds only comments).

### B+5 — Coverage of this skeptic pass: what was reproduced, what was found correct, and what was not covered (found by the skeptic)

### B — errors the skeptic found in the reviewer's report

- B-4 overclaims with 'a Valid render may carry an AST with .call "mstore"' and gives P' as a counterexample. R.prog is not a Render field: Statements.lean:99 defines it as `asmOf R.pv R.model ...` over the fixed emitter `mainAsmRaw pv` (Program.lean:98-99), so P' is not a Render. The defect is an emitter-fidelity and gate gap. The reviewer also did not check the current emitter: all 14 opcode-named user calls in GenAst*.lean are negative-control mutation gates, so the issue is latent.
- B-6 says prefix_run_clean 'still needs' ERR_BAD_CALLDATA_SHAPE and ERR_MEMORY_LAYOUT_VIOLATED. Both names occur only inside the guard bodies (Program.lean:74-76, :83-85), which the accepting prefix run does not execute; prefix_run_clean asserts one normal run under LeanAccepts. Only NUM_INSTANCE_CPTR, TRANSCRIPT_MPTR and FR_MODULUS are needed there.
- B-6 line references: the two expression-valued bytes32 constants are Constants.sol:12 and :26 (EXPECTED_VK_CODEHASH, EXPECTED_QUOTIENT_CODEHASH). Line :216 is BUILD_ID, a literal. The reviewer also missed that printValue (Generator/Filters.lean:87-93) can itself print `add(name, 0x..)` / `sub(0, 0x..)`, a non-hypothetical source of expression right-hand sides.
- B-3's suggested fix states that frozen Statements.lean section 1 must be edited. Render.scope is referenced only from Blocks.lean and Blocks/*.lean (refinable), so the block scope can be redefined there without re-hashing the frozen file.
- B-2 understates vk_progress: it is false at a genuine Valid render with an accepting proof, not only at the perturbed R', because the lemma takes no decodeCalldata hypothesis (VkLoading.lean:751-756). B-2 also describes the other nine EnvAgrees fields as 'facts about vk.domain, params or p'; domainN reads PV.k, negLagranges reads PV.numNegLagranges and truncWidth reads E.field. This is immaterial to the finding.
- B-5's 'already_recorded: no' is right, but its fix says to add a `noShadow` clause to Valid. A frozen change is not required: prog_slices_decomp, runBody_iff_slices and lowering_equivalence_of_blocks are all in refinable Blocks.lean and can take a decidable hypothesis discharged per deployment.
- The reviewer's summary treats the three top items as the full set of reasons Valid is 'not yet the right predicate'. It omits that SupportedV2.wf is unsatisfiable for real circuits through VerifyingKey.WellFormed.permutationCommitmentsMatch (per-column sigma commitments compared with a per-chunk count), which blocks poseidon_valid independently of B-1.

### B — checked and found correct

- Program shape versus template: asmOf's order (ABI guard; `let vk`; MF-2 guard; helpers; `let r := FR_MODULUS`, `let success := true`; seven partials in include order) matches proofs/solidity-verifier/templates/contracts/Halo2Verifier.sol:136-231. verifyProof is `external view returns (bool)` with no modifiers. Nothing sits between or after the assembly blocks except the two address locals. Production TraceReturn ends in `mstore(RETURN_MPTR, 1) return(RETURN_MPTR, 0x20)` (TraceReturn.yul:69-71). (formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Program.lean:66-116)
- Merging the two assembly blocks into one Yul block is harmless here: the first block declares no variable or function (Halo2Verifier.sol:147-154), `vk` is a Solidity value-type local readable from assembly, and no Yul partial uses `proof.offset` / `instances.length` style accessors (grep over templates/partials).
- wrapperLocals' conditions (`m.embeddedVk.isNone`, `m.quotientExternal.isSome`) match the template's two match arms (Halo2Verifier.sol:156-170; formal/Midfall/Generator/Contracts.lean:315-320).
- mainAssemblyText and abiGuardText are exact copies of the corresponding tail of renderHalo2Verifier / verifierNoticeSection (Contracts.lean:311-314, :340-348 against Program.lean:159-173). The offsets `drop 6` and `drop 7` are correct for vhMainAssemblyOpen / vhGuardOpen (`assembly ( "memory - safe" )` is six tokens, plus the function-body brace), and the appended `}` stands for the assembly block's closing brace in vhFooter.
- Tokenizer on this corpus: `//` comments are cut per line; `:=` and `->` are split identically on both sides; 0x literals reduce to values; decimals are kept verbatim. The Yul templates contain no string literals, no `/* */` comments and no `//` inside code (grep), so yulTokens is not lossy for the generator's output. (formal/Midfall/Generator/YulGolden.lean:76-120)
- The comparison is made BEFORE inlining (on mainAsmRaw and abiGuardBlock). The running program is tied to it by definition: asmOf = inlineContractConstants (constTableOf m) (abiGuardBlock ++ wrapperLocals ++ mainAsmRaw). (Program.lean:114-116, :190-192)
- inlineContractConstants: descends into funDef bodies; shadows parameters, returns, let and assign targets and for-init binders; nested blocks do not leak binders. Fuel exhaustion returns the block unmodified with `exhausted := true`, so under-substitution leaves free names that mainClosed rejects (fail-safe). (formal/Midfall/Lowering/InlineConstants.lean:287-390)
- inline_append is TRUE as stated (its side condition on topBinders is exactly what the shadow-list fold needs), although still sorried. hoist_prog_addr_indep is true (addresses occur only as let right-hand-side literals). emitSlices_eq is proved. (formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/ProgramLemmas.lean:27-41; Blocks.lean:145-148)
- Solidity constants: every `uint256 internal constant` in Constants.sol renders to a literal RHS. The only expression-valued constants are `bytes32` (EXPECTED_VK_CODEHASH, BUILD_ID), which are not parsed and not referenced from assembly; the Yul uses EXPECTED_VK_CODEHASH_WORD (VkLoading.yul:71). solc forbids Yul identifiers that shadow Solidity declarations, so the inliner's shadow rule is never exercised on compilable text.
- NoWriters: writers are sstore, tstore, log0-4, selfdestruct, create, create2, call, callcode, delegatecall. funDef bodies, all three for-loop blocks, every switch case and the default are scanned. Fuel 0 answers false, so exhaustion is never 'clean'. staticcall is correctly excluded. The scan ignores literal values, so noWriters_prog (R.prog 0 0 to R.prog vkAddr qAddr) is plausible. (formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:1039-1108)
- Halo2VerifyingKey.bytes / len / runtimeBytes are functions of the payload model (0xfe prefix ++ payload), not free fields. EXPECTED_VK_CODEHASH is derived as keccak256 of plan.vk.runtimeBytes, so vkProgressPins is consistent by construction. (formal/Midfall/Generator/Models.lean:462-484; Artifacts.lean:180, :524)
- Vendored semantics match Yul scoping where the model depends on it: callOk gives the callee only its parameters and zeroed returns; Step.block hoists the block's funDefs; an unbound variable has no Step rule (stuck). (formal/vendor/yul-semantics/YulSemantics/BigStep.lean:115-127, :171-193, :223-227)
- The Lean ConstraintSystemMeta.new is a faithful port of the Rust constructor (both initialise num_dummy_evals and num_point_sets to 0). The defect in B-1 is in what Valid compares it against, not in the port. (formal/Midfall/Generator/Encoding.lean:454-478 against proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/encoding/mod.rs:141-168)
- Program.lean is generic in (plan, model): no definition reads the pinned poseidon table (constTableOf is used instead of Inline.contractConstants). The literals 224, 0, 4, 0x40 in abiGuardBlock / mainGuardBlock are fixed template text. The only fixture leftovers are the inert `vk` / `success` filter in constTableOf and the unconditional allow-list in mainClosed.
- Joint satisfiability in the abstract: no pair of the 13 clauses is contradictory for every render. The defect is at faithful generator outputs (B-1), not a logical contradiction.

## C — Yul/EVM semantics and the Bridge

**Reviewer summary.** Read-only review; no Lean was run, so every needs_lean_check item is unverified and should be checked before relying on it.

What holds: the vendored big-step Yul semantics and the EVM dialect are faithful for every builtin the production verifier uses (each stepOp arm was compared with EVM behaviour). The staticcall step (finishCall / copyReturn) matches the EVM. Observations are sane: a normal fall-through, STOP or SELFDESTRUCT is not classified as revert-or-halt, so conjunct 3 genuinely excludes them. Three of the four Bridge sorries are true as stated, including Run_worldEq_of_noWriters, whose scan suffices for all eleven WorldEq components. Soundness of the English claim survives out-of-gas, the 63/64 rule, call depth and memory-expansion cost, because permissive admits every call failure and production templates contain no gas().

Main defects:
1. Nothing (no theorem, Valid clause or plan item) shows the modelled program is never stuck. Conjuncts 1 and 3 are forall-runs, so they are vacuous wherever the AST has no derivation, and the frozen token-level CodegenAgrees cannot tell a builtin from a same-named user call. Adequacy and determinism exist only for the closed dialect.
2. permissive forbids success with empty returndata, which is what the real EVM returns at a codeless precompile address, although its docstring says missing precompiles are covered. The generated returndatasize defences are dead code in the model.
3. runBody_iff_slices needs determinism of the prefix under a non-functional call relation; no lemma provides it and the plan prices it at zero.
4. rejects_of_not_accepts (in the headline cone; outside my dimension) is stated without R.Valid and without SoundSide. It is contradicted by any accepting run, and the recorded argument that SoundSide is spurious is wrong.

Minor: the honest relation ignores forwarded gas; 160-bit address aliasing is mishandled; rel_worldLocal is provably false yet still registered as a pending proof.

### C-1 — No non-stuckness (totality) guarantee for the modelled program; conjuncts 1 and 3 are vacuous wherever the AST is stuck, and token-level CodegenAgrees cannot rule that out

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** statement-weaker-than-claimed · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (REVIEW.md R-72 (headline_of_no_runs) and PLAN-V2 §1 (stuck_satisfies_every_post, for v1). The D-P45 repair recommended in DECISIONS-PENDING §3 covers only the accepting side and explicitly declines a run-existence conjunct; reject-side and permissive-path stuckness is not recorded.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:302-311; formal/vendor/yul-semantics/YulSemantics/BigStep.lean:56-58,185-203; formal/vendor/yul-semantics/YulSemantics/Adequacy.lean:772-789; formal/vendor/yul-semantics/DESIGN.md:322-332; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Program.lean:132-134,190-194; formal/vendor/yul-semantics/YulSemantics/PrettyPrint.lean:44-45; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Shared.lean:128,155; formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAst.lean:1691-1702
- **Claim (reviewer):** No theorem, Render.Valid clause or planned obligation establishes that R.prog has a run for every entry frame and calldata under permissive calls, nor that no reachable configuration is stuck. Conjuncts 1 and 3 of LoweringEquivalence are forall-runs statements; conjunct 2 asserts existence only under LeanAccepts with honest calls. In the big-step semantics an unresolved Expr.call, an arity mismatch or an unbound variable has no derivation, so on any path taken only by rejecting inputs (or only after a permitted precompile failure) a stuck AST satisfies conjuncts 1 and 3 vacuously. The only link from the AST to the Solidity text, CodegenAgrees, compares token streams of EVM.print, which prints Expr.builtin op and Expr.call (opName op) identically. mainClosed checks variable names only: function names are ignored and assignment targets count as binders. run_adequacy and determinism are proved only for the closed dialect EVM.evm (gas and all calls stuck there), and adequacy is not a totality statement in any case. So the headline does not imply the English 'otherwise reverts', and even a perfectly trusted solc (a forward simulation from Yul runs) transfers nothing for inputs on which the Lean AST has no run.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Statements.lean:302-304 '(∀ (g : ExternalGas) (obs : Obs), RunBody R vkAddr 0 (permissive (callsOf product)) g st₀ obs → obs.isReturn → SoundSide R H cd → obs.returnData = abiTrue ∧ LeanAccepts R H cd)'; :305-306 'LeanAccepts R H cd → CompleteSide R H cd → G1CodecLaws R.E.g1 → (∃ obs, RunBody R vkAddr 0 (callsOf product) ...' (the only existence claim). BigStep.lean:56-58 'Ill-formed situations (unbound variable, arity mismatch, `break` outside a loop, …) simply have no applicable rule — the program is *stuck* (no derivation).' Adequacy.lean:786-788 'theorem EVM.run_adequacy {prog st0 V' st' o} : Run EVM.evm prog st0 V' st' o ↔ ∃ n, Interp.run EVM.exec n prog st0 = .ok (V', st', o)'. DESIGN.md:324-326 'The determinism lemma, the fuel-indexed interpreter, and the adequacy theorem are all stated for the **closed-world local dialect `EVM.evm`**. They do **not** apply to the **open-world `EVM.evmWithExternal`'. Program.lean:190-192 'def codegenAgrees (pv : PlanView) (m : Model) : Bool := yulTokens (YulSemantics.EVM.print (abiGuardBlock m)) == abiGuardTokens m && yulTokens (YulSemantics.EVM.print (mainAsmRaw pv)) == mainAssemblyTokens m'. PrettyPrint.lean:44-45 '| .builtin op args => pOp op ++ "(" ++ ppExprs pOp args ++ ")"' and '| .call fn args => fn ++ "(" ++ ppExprs pOp args ++ ")"'. Shared.lean:155 '| Expr.call _ args => args.flatMap refE'; :128 '| Stmt.assign xs e => xs ++ boundE e'. GenAst.lean:1691-1693 'The terminal 'return' spelled as a USER call named 'return' keeps the token stream and breaks stmtsBeq.' PLAN-V2.md:192 'There is no falsifier registry, and the token gates are not extended to V2 (W6).'
- **Counterexample / witness:** P := [ if iszero(calldataload(0)) { exprStmt (Expr.call "pop" [lit 0]) }, mstore(0, 1), return(0, 32) ] with Expr.call rather than Expr.builtin. For calldata whose first word is 0 there is no Step derivation in any Bridge.Dialect c g (lookupFun finds no 'pop'), so 'every returning run has calldataload(0) ≠ 0' holds. EVM.print P is token-identical to 'if iszero(calldataload(0)) { pop(0) } mstore(0,1) return(0,32)', whose compiled bytecode returns ABI true for every calldata. Transposed to the verifier: a GenAst emitter that spells one builtin inside a rejection-only branch as a user call passes Valid.codegen, Valid.closed and Valid.noWriters, leaves conjunct 2 untouched, and makes conjuncts 1 and 3 vacuous on exactly those inputs. The project's own gates 19/20 exhibit this substitution keeping the token gate green. Partial mitigation outside the theorem: v1 #eval gates (emit*_beq_ok, emittedProgramClosure_ok in Lowering/Partials.lean:1836) check AST equality and call closure at fixture plan views, but they are not clauses of Render.Valid and not in the V2 cone.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed as a statement-weaker-than-claimed and assurance gap, with one correction. Conjuncts 1 and 3 of LoweringEquivalence are forall-runs statements; the only run-existence claim is conjunct 2, under LeanAccepts, CompleteSide, G1CodecLaws and honest calls. No theorem, Render.Valid clause or queued card asserts that R.prog is non-stuck on rejecting inputs or after a permitted precompile failure: BlockProgress is total only from a Clean boundary under honest calls; prefix_run_clean needs LeanAccepts; Render.Rejects and the staged-rejection card C.3 are forall-runs; the recommended D-P45 variant declines a run-existence conjunct. The big-step semantics has no rule for an unresolved Expr.call, an unbound variable, or a valued expression statement. The frozen link CodegenAgrees compares tokens of EVM.print, which prints Expr.builtin op and Expr.call (opName op) identically. mainClosed ignores call names, counts any binder anywhere as binding, and does not scan the ABI guard block. So the headline gives 'every modelled run reverts or returns true', not 'the contract reverts', and transfers nothing through a forward-simulation reading of solc trust on inputs where the AST has no run. CORRECTION: determinism is NOT closed-dialect only. Bridge.dialect_deterministic, Run.det and RunCommitted.det cover the open dialect under functional oracles, and honestCalls_functional makes the honest relation functional. What is missing is determinism under permissive or an arbitrary gas oracle, and any adequacy or totality result for the open dialect. SCOPE NOTE: R.prog is a fixed function of (plan, model) through GenAst, so the 'transposed' counterexample needs a defect in the emitter itself. A grep of literal ycall / Expr.call names in the emitters found only helper names; builtin-named calls occur only in falsifier gates. That grep is not exhaustive, and nothing in Valid or the V2 cone enforces it.

- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
open YulSemantics YulSemantics.EVM in
example (c : ExternalCalls) (g : ExternalGas) (st : EvmState) (r : Res (Midfall.Yul.Bridge.Dialect c g)) :
    ¬ Step (Midfall.Yul.Bridge.Dialect c g) [[]] [] st (.stmt (.exprStmt (.call "pop" [.lit (.number 0)]))) r := by
  intro h
  cases h with
  | exprStmt he => cases he with | callOk _ hl _ _ _ => simp [lookupFun] at hl
  | exprStmtHalt he => cases he with
    | callHalt _ hl _ _ => simp [lookupFun] at hl
    | callArgsHalt ha => cases ha with
      | argsRestHalt hr => cases hr
      | argsHeadHalt _ hh => cases hh
-- expected true:
#eval Midfall.Generator.yulTokens (YulSemantics.EVM.print [.exprStmt (.call "pop" [.lit (.number 0)])]) == Midfall.Generator.yulTokens (YulSemantics.EVM.print [.exprStmt (.builtin .pop [.lit (.number 0)])])
```
- **Suggested fix:** (a) Add a decidable well-formedness clause to Render.Valid: every Expr.call resolves in scope with matching arity and result count; no user function carries a builtin name; expression statements are zero-valued; break/continue/leave are well placed. Prove once, generically, that a well-formed program cannot be stuck (e.g. an oracle-parameterised fuel interpreter never answers .stuck). (b) Add the missing headline conjunct (frozen surface, needs sign-off): for every functional, total c' included in permissive (callsOf product) and every functional, total g, ∃ obs, RunBody R vkAddr 0 c' g st₀ obs. (c) Replace or supplement token-level CodegenAgrees with an AST-level equality (stmtsBeq against the parsed text), as v1 gates 15/17 did. (d) Until then, STATUS should describe the reject side as 'every modelled run reverts', not 'the contract reverts'.

### C-2 — permissive excludes 'success with empty returndata' (codeless precompile address); the docstring claims missing precompiles are covered, and the generated returndatasize defences are dead code in the model

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → medium · **Kind:** trusted-base-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:725-732,1430-1435; formal/Midfall/Yul/Externals.lean:799-805; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:251-252,302-311; proofs/solidity-verifier/templates/partials/verifier/AssemblyHelpers.yul:33-34,187-188,238-239,294-296; proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/kzg/mod.rs:1826-1909
- **Claim (reviewer):** permissive c admits any failure (with arbitrary returndata) and only honest successes. It therefore excludes the response the real EVM gives when the target has no code (a chain or fork without EIP-2537 at 0x0b/0x0c/0x0f): success = 1, empty returndata, output memory untouched. The docstrings nevertheless say a missing precompile is covered as a failure. In the model a successful call to a generated frame always carries returndata of the honest length (32 or 128 bytes), so every 'eq(returndatasize(), N)' guard the generator emits is unreachable on the success arm. The theorem would hold unchanged if the generator dropped those guards, although the real contract would then read stale scratch memory as a precompile result. The actual trusted assumption is 'EIP-2537 and modexp are present and honest at the four addresses', which is stronger than the documented 'may fail but never lie'.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Bridge.lean:725-727 'The soundness relation: any FAILURE is permitted (gas is unmodelled, a precompile may be missing on a different deployment), a SUCCESS must be the honest one, and no world change is ever reported.' Bridge.lean:731-732 'def permissive (c : ExternalCalls) : ExternalCalls := ⟨fun req st resp => resp.world = CallWorld.ofState st ∧ (resp.success = true -> c.Call req st resp)⟩'. Bridge.lean:1430-1432 'a failure needs no justification (gas is unmodelled, a precompile may be missing on another deployment)'. Externals.lean:801-802 'else match r.output req.input with | some out => some { success := true, returndata := out }'. AssemblyHelpers.yul:294-296 'ret := staticcall(PAIRING_GAS_2PAIR, ...)' then 'ret := and(ret, eq(returndatasize(), {{ template_constants.word_bytes|hex() }}))' then 'if iszero(ret) { fail(ERR_PRECOMPILE_FAILED) }'.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Request {kind := .staticcall, target := 0x0f, input := anything}, response {success := true, returndata := [], world := CallWorld.ofState st}. This is the real EVM's answer on a chain without EIP-2537, and it does not satisfy (permissive (callsOf product)).Call: honestOf at 0x0f returns either failResp (success = false) or a 32-byte word. Variant generator that omits the returndatasize conjunct in ec_pairing: all three conjuncts still hold in the model, while on such a chain mload(scratch) reads the first word of the G1 point that mcopy just placed there. The shipped generator does defend every site (templates and kzg/mod.rs all check returndatasize); the point is that the theorem does not verify the defence.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed as stated. permissive c admits a response iff its world is unchanged and, when success = true, it is the honest response. For the four generated frame shapes the honest success carries non-empty returndata (128 bytes at 0x0b / 0x0c, 32 bytes at 0x0f, modLen = 32 bytes at 0x05). So 'success = 1 with empty returndata', which is the real EVM's answer to a STATICCALL to a codeless address (a chain without EIP-2537), is not a permissive response, although two docstrings say a missing precompile is covered as a failure. In the model every generated 'eq(returndatasize(), N)' conjunct is therefore true whenever the call flag is 1, so the theorem cannot distinguish the generator from a variant without those guards. The effective trusted assumption is 'the four precompiles exist and every success is the honest one', stronger than the documented 'may fail but never lie'. One precision: honest modexp can return success with empty returndata when modsize = 0, but no generated frame uses that. Practical impact is bounded: the Rust templates do emit the guards at every site, and the constructor smoke probes (not modelled) refuse deployment where the precompiles are missing.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** - formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:731-732 'def permissive (c : ExternalCalls) : ExternalCalls := ⟨fun req st resp => resp.world = CallWorld.ofState st ∧ (resp.success = true -> c.Call req st resp)⟩'.
- Bridge.lean:725-727 'any FAILURE is permitted (gas is unmodelled, a precompile may be missing on a different deployment), a SUCCESS must be the honest one'; :1430-1432 repeats 'a precompile may be missing on another deployment'.
- Bridge.lean:705-711 honestOf returns 'ofOutcome st o' or 'failResp st' (success := false, :694-695). formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:251-252 'callsOf product := honestCalls product 0 (fun _ _ _ => False)'.
- formal/Midfall/Yul/Externals.lean:799-805 respond: 'some out => some { success := true, returndata := out }'; :948-952 pairing output is 'bytesNatBE (if b then 1 else 0) wordBytes'; :824-827 and :879-886 G1 outputs are g1PointBytes; :972-981 modexp output length is modsize ('a modsize of 0 returns 0 bytes').
- formal/vendor/yul-semantics/YulSemantics/Dialect/EVMExec.lean:614-632: copyReturn copies 'min size data.length' bytes and finishCall sets 'returndata := response.returndata', so the model could express the codeless response; only permissive excludes it.
- proofs/solidity-verifier/templates/partials/verifier/AssemblyHelpers.yul:294-296 'ret := staticcall(PAIRING_GAS_2PAIR, ...)', 'ret := and(ret, eq(returndatasize(), ...))', 'if iszero(ret) { fail(ERR_PRECOMPILE_FAILED) }'; the comment at :288-293 names 'a missing, repriced, or short-returning precompile'. Same guard at :33-34, :187-188, :238-239, FinalPairing.yul:39-59 and src/lowering/kzg/mod.rs:1826-1909.
- PrecompileSmoke.sol:66-70 'This catches chains where the precompile is missing or returns a non-standard success shape'.
- EVM rule relied on: a call to an account with empty code executes no code and returns success with empty returndata; a precompile address not active in the fork is such an account.
- No record found: a grep of formal/docs and SORRY.md for 'missing precompile', 'codeless' or 'empty returndata' finds nothing on this.

- **Suggested fix:** Extend the soundness relation with the codeless-account behaviour, e.g. permissive' c := fun req st resp => resp.world = CallWorld.ofState st ∧ (resp.success = true → c.Call req st resp ∨ resp.returndata = []), use it in conjuncts 1 and 3 (frozen surface, needs sign-off), and let the block contracts prove the returndatasize guards. Otherwise correct the two docstrings and add an explicit trusted-base row: the four precompiles exist and behave per EIP-2537 / EIP-198 on the target chain, and the missing-precompile defence is not covered by the theorem.

### C-3 — runBody_iff_slices (forward direction) needs determinism of the prefix under the non-functional permissive relation; no such lemma exists and the plan budgets none

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** partially-correct → low · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:246-260,276-285,545-549,592-596; formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:229-231; formal/vendor/yul-semantics/YulSemantics/Determinism.lean:22-24; formal/docs/QUEUE.md:385; formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:275
- **Claim (reviewer):** runBody_iff_slices takes ONE prefix run hp ending in (V₁, st₁) and claims every RunBody factors through that same (V₁, st₁). The forward direction is true only because the prefix (ABI guard, wrapper lets, MF-2 guard, main locals) is deterministic for every (c, g). The composition instantiates it at permissive (callsOf product) and an arbitrary gas oracle, where neither Step.det (needs every op deterministic) nor dialect_deterministic (needs c.Functional and g.Functional) applies: permissive is not functional and g is arbitrary. A determinism lemma restricted to code free of gas and call-family builtins does not exist in the tree. The vendored Step.det is about 370 lines of case analysis, so this is not the '30-60 lines, no semantic content' the queue records. Related: PLAN-V2 WP-T item 4 plans proofs through EVM.run_adequacy, which is about the closed dialect EVM.evm; no lemma transfers Step between EVM.evm and Bridge.Dialect c g in either direction.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Blocks.lean:279-284 '(hp : ExecStmts (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (R.scope c g) [] st₀ (R.preSlice vkAddr 0 ++ R.mainLocalsInlined) V₁ st₁ .normal) (obs : Obs) : RunBody R vkAddr 0 c g st₀ obs ↔ ∃ (V' : VEnv (Bridge.Dialect c g)) (st' : EvmState) (o : Outcome), obs = ⟨committedState st₀ st', o⟩ ∧ ExecStmts (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (R.scope c g) V₁ st₁ R.slices V' st' o'. Blocks.lean:548-549 '(runBody_iff_slices R vkAddr (permissive (callsOf product)) gg st₀ V₁ st₁ hpre obs).1 hobs'. Bridge.lean:229-231 'theorem dialect_deterministic {c : ExternalCalls} {g : ExternalGas} (hc : c.Functional) (hg : g.Functional) (op : Op) : (Dialect c g).Deterministic op'. Determinism.lean:22 'theorem Step.det (hdet : ∀ op, D.Deterministic op)'. QUEUE.md:385 '`30-60 lines`. Evidence: `Step.stmts_append_inv` 10 proof lines ... the rest is `Step.block` / `Step.funDef` bookkeeping (BigStep.lean:222-227). No semantic content.' PLAN-V2.md:275 'For closed-dialect fragments, proof by evaluation through the sorry-free `YulSemantics.EVM.run_adequacy`.'
- **Counterexample / witness:** Not a falsity: the statement is true if prog_slices_decomp holds. Witness that the cited tools cannot close it: (permissive ExternalCalls.none) admits both failResp st and {success := false, returndata := [0], world := CallWorld.ofState st} for the same request, so it is not Functional, and (Dialect (permissive c) g).Deterministic Op.staticcall is false. Step.stmts_append_inv applied to a RunBody yields some prefix endpoint (V₁', st₁'), not the given (V₁, st₁); equating them is exactly prefix determinism.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** The core is right; the severity and one inference need correcting. runBody_iff_slices fixes ONE prefix endpoint (V₁, st₁) from hp and claims every RunBody factors through it. Its forward direction therefore needs uniqueness of the prefix run and exclusion of a halting prefix run, i.e. determinism of the prefix. The composition applies it at permissive (callsOf product) with an arbitrary gas oracle, where neither Step.det nor dialect_deterministic applies (permissive is not functional; g is arbitrary). No determinism lemma restricted to call-free and gas-free code exists in the tree, and no lemma transfers Step between EVM.evm and Bridge.Dialect, so PLAN-V2 WP-T item 4 ('by evaluation through run_adequacy') has no route into the open dialect either. QUEUE.md prices the lemma at 30-60 lines with 'No semantic content', which is wrong. CORRECTIONS: (i) the statement is true (given prog_slices_decomp) and lives in the refinable layer, so this is a cost and plan-accuracy defect, not a correctness one. (ii) A generalisation of the 370-line Step.det is not required: the prefix is about six statements of local builtins, and a direct inversion suffices. The only precedent is in the retired Instances/RunShellInversion.lean, where one guard cost about 80 lines; a few hundred lines is a fair estimate. Restating prefix_run_clean in total form, as PLAN-V2 WP-M0 item 3 words it, avoids determinism but costs the same forall-runs inversion of the prefix.

### C-4 — rejects_of_not_accepts (headline cone) is stated for every Render with neither R.Valid nor SoundSide; it is contradicted by any accepting run, and the recorded 'SoundSide is spurious' argument is wrong (outside dimension C, found while reading Blocks.lean)

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (Recorded item 4 and DECISIONS-PENDING 'Also open' (C.2/C.3) note only that Rejects demands SoundSide and that rejects_of_not_accepts is a separate sorry. The missing R.Valid and the incorrect justification for dropping SoundSide are not recorded.) · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:344-363,606-608; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:263-276; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Program.lean:46-49; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Calldata.lean:340; proofs/src/plonk/logup.rs:458-461
- **Claim (reviewer):** (1) rejects_of_not_accepts has no hR : R.Valid although its caller has one. LeanAccepts decodes with R.cfg.numInstances, while R.prog depends on cfg only through cfg.accumulator and checks the instance count of R.model. For a render whose cfg.numInstances differs from the model's, no calldata the program accepts decodes, so the lemma asserts that R.prog never returns. Together with conjunct 2 of the headline at the genuine render and one accepted calldata this yields False. (2) Even with hR, dropping SoundSide is unjustified. SoundSide exists because Lean returns a panic error where f_j(x) + β = 0 while the contract does not invert there. On that set ¬LeanAccepts holds and the contract may still return ABI true, which conjunct 3 permits through its left disjunct but this lemma forbids. H is universally quantified, so the set is not empty by a negligibility argument. The docstring's statement that SoundSide is 'a COMPLETENESS side condition ... spurious for this use' contradicts SoundSide's own definition.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Blocks.lean:356-362 'theorem rejects_of_not_accepts (R : Render p G₁ G₂ Gₜ) (product : PairingProduct) (W : ExecEnv) ... (hdep : DeploymentV2 R W H vkAddr) (hentry : EntryFrame R W Lyt H cd st₀) (hpc : Eip2537.Holds R.E.g1 ⟨R.encG2⟩ product) (hna : ¬ LeanAccepts R H cd) (hobs : RunBody R vkAddr 0 (permissive (callsOf product)) g st₀ obs) : obs.isRevertOrHalt' (no Valid, no SoundSide). Blocks.lean:350-352 '`SoundSide` excludes the logup-panic bad set, which is a COMPLETENESS side condition: nothing in the "Lean rejects, so the contract reverts" direction needs it, so the hypothesis is spurious for this use.' Statements.lean:269-270 'Soundness side condition (v1 finding 5): Lean panics where `f_j(x) + β = 0`, the contract never inverts there.' Statements.lean:265 'decodeCalldata (p := p) R.plan.proofLayout R.cfg.numInstances R.E.g1 cd = some (ms, instW)'. Program.lean:48 'planViewWithAccumulator L.vk L.vkMptr L.memory L.proofLayout m.numInstances m.k'. Calldata.lean:340 'guard ((cdWord cd numInstCptr).toNat == numInstances)'. logup.rs:458-460 '.map(|f| product * f.invert().unwrap())'.
- **Counterexample / witness:** R' := { poseidonRender with cfg := { cfg with numInstances := cfg.numInstances + 1 } }. Then R'.prog = poseidonRender.prog, and DeploymentV2, EntryFrame and Eip2537.Holds are unchanged because they read plan, consts, E.g1 and encG2 only. Any calldata on which the program returns has calldataload(NUM_INSTANCE_CPTR) equal to the model's count, so decodeCalldata with the incremented count is none and ¬LeanAccepts R' holds. rejects_of_not_accepts R' then says the returning run is a revert. So the lemma is refuted by any accepting run of the real verifier; honest runs are permissive runs by honest_le_permissive. This is conditional on one accepting calldata existing, which the tree has not yet exhibited but the completeness claim presupposes. For (2): a circuit with logup lookups, an H chosen so that β = -f_j(x) at an honestly generated proof; the contract's multiplied-out helper identity still holds, Rust and Lean panic.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Both parts are confirmed, and part (1) is stronger than the reviewer stated.
(1) rejects_of_not_accepts is stated for every Render with no R.Valid. R.prog reads cfg only through cfg.accumulator, while LeanAccepts decodes with cfg.numInstances. For ANY render R and ANY calldata cd, the render R'' := { R with cfg.numInstances := cd.size + 1 } has the same program, the same DeploymentV2 / EntryFrame / Eip2537.Holds, and ¬LeanAccepts R'' H cd, because decodeCalldata's size guard fails. So the lemma is equivalent to 'no R.prog ever returns from an entry frame'. It is refuted by any returning run of any render and is inconsistent with headline conjunct 2 wherever that conjunct is non-vacuous. No accepting run is exhibited in the tree yet, so the refutation is conditional on the project's own completeness claim. Adding hR : R.Valid (the caller has it; vkPins.numInst ties pv.numInstances to cfg.numInstances) repairs this part.
(2) Even with hR, dropping SoundSide is unjustified. Rust and the Lean port fault at logup.rs:460 when some f_j(x) + β = 0, so prepareMsgs errors and ¬LeanAccepts holds. The generated contract computes the partial products by prefix × suffix with no inversion, so an honestly generated proof at such a challenge passes the contract. H is universally quantified and constrained at one input only, so such (H, cd) are not excluded. I did not construct a witness; the argument rests on those two code facts. The docstring's claim that SoundSide is 'a COMPLETENESS side condition ... spurious for this use' contradicts SoundSide's own frozen docstring.
CONSEQUENCE not stated by the reviewer: on that bad set snapshotOf is not ok (snapshotOf_prepareMsgs equates the isOk flags), so BlockContractsAt and prefix_run_clean give nothing there, and card C.3 excludes the set. Conjunct 3 on SoundSide-violating calldata therefore has no true planned obligation behind it; it needs either a frozen-surface change or a new snapshot-free two-outcomes obligation.
RECORDING: more is recorded than the reviewer says. design.md already warns that deleting SoundSide from Rejects 'would also assert rejection inside the logup-panic bad set, which has not been checked'. What is not recorded: the SoundSide-free statement already sits in the headline cone under that name, its docstring and QUEUE.md assert the opposite, and R.Valid is missing.
