### C-5 — The honest precompile relation ignores the forwarded gas; completeness holds even for staticcall(0, ...)

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** fidelity-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (PLAN.md §1 'Out of scope: exact gas' and PLAN-V2 §6 'gas is not modelled'. That the honest relation ignores the gas argument of program-chosen constants is not recorded.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Yul/Externals.lean:755-757,786-787,799-805; formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:705-711; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:305-308
- **Claim (reviewer):** ExternalCallRelation carries a gas schedule (field gas) and the request carries the forwarded amount, but respond and honestOf consult neither. Under callsOf product a call with a valid frame succeeds whatever gas was forwarded. Conjunct 2 therefore does not cover the generator's fixed gas constants (MODEXP_GAS, G1ADD_GAS, G1MSM_GAS_*, PAIRING_GAS_2PAIR): a constant below the chain price makes the real contract revert PrecompileFailed on every valid proof while the theorem still holds. The constants are pinned only by #eval KATs (gasScheduleKats_ok, modexpGasKats_ok). The real-EVM condition also includes the 63/64 rule: the caller must hold enough gas for each constant to be forwarded in full.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Externals.lean:786-787 '/-- The scheduled gas cost of the call, as a function of the frame. -/ gas : Bytes -> Nat'. Externals.lean:799-802 'def ExternalCallRelation.respond (r : ExternalCallRelation) (req : CallRequest) : Option CallOutcome := if req.target != r.address || ! (r.validInput req.input) then none else match r.output req.input with | some out => some { success := true, returndata := out }' (no use of req.gas or r.gas). A grep finds no consumer of the gas field outside the Externals KATs. proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/tests.rs:1561-1592 treats a bound below the chain price as a real failure mode.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Request {kind := .staticcall, gas := 0, target := 0x05, input := modexpInvFrame 2}: honestOf returns a success with the 32-byte inverse. On the real EVM a zero-gas modexp call fails.
- **Lean check proposed (reviewer):**

```lean
#eval (Midfall.Yul.Bridge.honestOf (Midfall.Yul.honestRelations (fun _ => true))
  { kind := .staticcall, gas := 0, target := 0x05, value := 0, input := (Midfall.Yul.modexpInvFrame 2).data.toList }
  YulSemantics.EVM.EvmState.init).map (·.success)   -- expected: some true
```
- **Suggested fix:** Either make respond fail when req.gas.toNat < r.gas req.input, so that conjunct 2 covers the forwarded constants (the 63/64 rule stays outside), or state in the English headline that completeness assumes every precompile call receives at least its scheduled gas.

### C-6 — 160-bit address aliasing: the honest fold fails an aliased precompile call that the real EVM would execute, and extcode*/balance reads are not masked; docstring and KAT claim otherwise

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** fidelity-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Yul/Externals.lean:799-800; formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:683-685,700-711,1500-1522; formal/vendor/yul-semantics/YulSemantics/Dialect/EVMExec.lean:852-859
- **Claim (reviewer):** (a) honestOf selects the relation by the 160-bit accountKey (hits) but then calls respond on the unmasked request, and respond compares the full 256-bit target with the precompile address. A request to 2^160 + 0x0b therefore hits G1ADD and is answered with failResp. Under permissive a success is impossible for that target, whereas the real EVM truncates the address and the call succeeds, so for aliased targets the relation is not an over-approximation of real behaviour. The docstring and KAT honestFoldKats_ok say the aliased request 'still reaches G1ADD', but the KAT tests only hits. (b) extcodesize, extcodecopy, extcodehash and balance read the environment maps at the full word with no accountKey reduction, so they agree with the EVM only for clean addresses or alias-invariant environments. Neither is reachable from the generated verifier: precompile targets are literals and vk is a literal bound from an address-typed immutable. The headline's quantification over vkAddr ≥ 2^160 adds only non-physical instances.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Externals.lean:800 'if req.target != r.address || ! (r.validInput req.input) then none'. Bridge.lean:700-701 'def hits (r : Midfall.Yul.ExternalCallRelation) (req : YulSemantics.EVM.CallRequest) : Bool := accountKey req.target == accountKey r.address'. Bridge.lean:708-711 '(rels.find? (fun r => hits r req)).map fun r => match r.respond (toYulReq req) with | some o => ofOutcome st o | none => failResp st'. Bridge.lean:1500-1501 'The honest fold dispatches on the 160-bit account key, not the full word: a request to 2^160 + 0x0b still reaches G1ADD'. EVMExec.lean:853 '| .extcodesize => rd1 (fun a => BitVec.ofNat 256 (st.env.extCodeOf a).length) args st'; :859 '| .extcodehash => rd1 (fun a => projectedCodeHash st.env st.env.balanceOf a) args st'.
- **Counterexample / witness:** req := {kind := .staticcall, gas := 375, target := BitVec.ofNat 256 (2^160 + 11), value := 0, input := G ++ G}. The model's honest answer is failResp (success = false, no returndata); the real EVM returns success with 2G.
- **Lean check proposed (reviewer):**

```lean
#eval (Midfall.Yul.Bridge.honestOf (Midfall.Yul.honestRelations (fun _ => true))
  { kind := .staticcall, gas := 375, target := BitVec.ofNat 256 (2^160 + 11), value := 0,
    input := (let g := Midfall.Yul.bytesOfWords Midfall.Yul.katG1GenWords; g.data.toList ++ g.data.toList) }
  YulSemantics.EVM.EvmState.init).map (·.success)   -- expected: some false (real EVM: success)
```
- **Suggested fix:** Mask the target in toYulReq (or compare accountKey in respond) and extend honestFoldKats_ok to assert the response, not only hits. Either constrain vkAddr < 2^160 in DeploymentV2 or note that extcode* reads are unmasked.

### C-7 — Evaluator.rel_worldLocal is provably false as stated yet remains a sorried theorem registered as a pending proof obligation

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (Bridge.lean docstring at :985-1005 and the exhibit world_projection_misses_address_and_keccak. SORRY.md:72 does not flag the falsity, and the false theorem is still declared.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:985-1009,1018-1027; formal/SORRY.md:72; formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:330
- **Claim (reviewer):** rel_worldLocal quantifies over every runtime block rt and claims the evaluator response depends on the caller only through CallWorld.ofState. calleeState reads st.env.address (as caller) and st.env.keccakOf, which are not CallWorld components, so for rt := { mstore(0, caller()) return(0, 32) } and two states differing only in env.address the two sides of the iff differ (by EVM.run_det). The theorem's own docstring says 'NOT TRUE AS STATED', but it is still 'theorem ... := by sorry', and SORRY.md lists it as an ordinary proof obligation. Any proof citing it could derive False. It is not in the V2 headline cone (callsOf uses an empty evaluator relation), but PLAN-V2 X2 names it as needed. The other three Bridge sorries are true as stated (see verified_correct).
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Bridge.lean:985 'NOT MECHANICAL, AND NOT TRUE AS STATED - the obstruction, named (P-BRIDGE step 3 audit finding).' Bridge.lean:1006-1009 'theorem rel_worldLocal (W Lyt addr rt) : ∀ req st st' r, CallWorld.ofState st = CallWorld.ofState st' -> (rel W Lyt addr rt req st r <-> rel W Lyt addr rt req st' r) := by sorry'. Bridge.lean:939 'address := addr, caller := st.env.address, callvalue := 0, static := true'. SORRY.md:72 '| Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean | 1009 | `sorry` | P-SE stage 1-2: Yul run/contracts layer (semantic equivalence) |'. PLAN-V2.md:330 'X2 externalised quotient evaluator: needs world-locality of `Evaluator.rel`.'
- **Counterexample / witness:** rt := yul% { mstore(0, caller()) return(0, 32) }, st := { EvmState.init with env.address := 1 }, st' := { EvmState.init with env.address := 2 }, r := the response returning the 32-byte word 1. CallWorld.ofState st = CallWorld.ofState st' by rfl; rel ... st r holds; rel ... st' r fails because the unique run from calleeState ... st' returns the word 2.
- **Lean check proposed (reviewer):**

```lean
Prove: ¬ (∀ (W : ExecEnv) (Lyt : Layout) (addr : U256) (rt : Block Op) req st st' r, CallWorld.ofState st = CallWorld.ofState st' → (Evaluator.rel W Lyt addr rt req st r ↔ Evaluator.rel W Lyt addr rt req st' r)), instantiating rt := yul% { mstore(0, caller()) return(0, 32) } and the two states above.
```
- **Suggested fix:** Delete the theorem, or replace it with its proved negation and a restatement over a projection that includes env.address and env.keccakOf. Mark the SORRY.md row as 'false as stated, do not cite' until then.

### C-8 — Real-EVM behaviours outside the model: soundness is unaffected, but the English completeness claim needs explicit gas qualifications

- **Severity (reviewer):** info · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** statement-weaker-than-claimed · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (PLAN.md §1 ('Out of scope: exact gas') and PLAN-V2 §6 ('gas is not modelled'). The per-behaviour analysis and the exact completeness qualification are not written down.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/vendor/yul-semantics/DESIGN.md:353-354; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:302-311; formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:731-732
- **Claim (reviewer):** Out-of-gas at an arbitrary point, the 63/64 rule, memory-expansion cost and call depth have no counterpart in the semantics. An out-of-gas real run is not a modelled run (HaltKind has no such kind). None of them can make a real run return true without a modelled run doing so. Frame-level out-of-gas is an exceptional halt, which is consistent in spirit with conjunct 3's revert-or-halt disjunct. Sub-call starvation (63/64, depth 1024, low forwarded gas) surfaces as a failed call with empty returndata, which permissive admits. gas() is absent from production templates and g is universally quantified in conjuncts 1 and 3. Memory accesses at huge offsets run out of gas for real, while the unbounded Nat-indexed memory lets the model continue, which errs toward more modelled runs. What these behaviours do falsify is unqualified completeness: conjunct 2's 'every honest run returns true' is a gas-free idealisation. The real statement is 'returns true given enough gas at entry for each fixed-gas precompile call to be forwarded in full, and given that the fixed constants cover the chain's prices'.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** DESIGN.md:353-354 '**Gas.** Not modeled by design (§1). Within-frame out-of-gas is not expressible; out-of-gas in a callee is subsumed by the open-world call relation.' Statements.lean:307-308 '∀ obs, RunBody R vkAddr 0 (callsOf product) (ExternalGas.ofFun fun _ => 0) st₀ obs → obs.view = returnView st₀'. Template grep: gas() occurs only in TraceAndGasHelpers.yul:21, QuotientAndLinearization.yul:28,35,153 (trace, gas-checkpoint and external-quotient renders) and PrecompileSmoke.sol comments.
- **Counterexample / witness:** A valid proof submitted with too little gas for 63/64 of the remainder to cover PAIRING_GAS_2PAIR: the real pairing call fails and the contract reverts PrecompileFailed. The model's honest run returns true.
- **Suggested fix:** Put the qualification verbatim in the English headline in STATUS.md: completeness is conditional on sufficient gas, including the 63/64 rule at each fixed-gas staticcall and adequacy of the constants. State that out-of-gas, depth and starvation runs are outside the modelled run set and revert by EVM semantics, not by the theorem.

### C-9 — Yul-core deviations from the specification that the verifier does not hit

- **Severity (reviewer):** info · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** fidelity-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/vendor/yul-semantics/YulSemantics/BigStep.lean:95-100,269-275,306-320; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Shared.lean:128
- **Claim (reviewer):** (1) 'leave' inside a for-loop init or post block has no rule: forLoop and forInitHalt require the init outcome to be .normal or .halt, and loopStep and loopPostHalt require the post outcome to be .normal or .halt. Yul allows 'leave' there inside a function. The templates use empty or single-assignment post blocks and 'let' init blocks, so this is not reachable. (2) VEnv.set on an unbound name is a silent no-op, so an assignment to an undeclared variable has a run where Yul rejects the program. freeNames counts assignment targets as binders, so Valid.closed does not detect assigned-but-never-declared names. (3) Shadowing and redeclaration are not rejected (innermost binding wins). All three are irrelevant for solc-accepted programs but confirm that Valid.closed is not a well-formedness guarantee (see C-1). Everything else checked matches the Yul specification.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** BigStep.lean:311 'Step D funs Vb stb (.stmt (.block post)) (.sres Vp stp .normal) →' and :319 '(.sres Vp stp .halt) →' (no .leave arm). BigStep.lean:270 'Step D (hoist D init :: funs) V st (.stmts init) (.sres Vinit stinit .normal) →'. BigStep.lean:95-99 'Update the innermost binding of `x` to `v` (no-op if unbound; well-formed programs never hit that case). ... | [] => []'. Shared.lean:128 '| Stmt.assign xs e => xs ++ boundE e'.
- **Counterexample / witness:** function f() { for { } 1 { leave } { } } followed by f(): valid Yul (returns immediately after the first body iteration), no derivation in the model.
- **Suggested fix:** None needed for the current templates. If the well-formedness clause of C-1 is added, include 'no leave in for-init or for-post' and 'assignment targets are declared' so the model's stuck and no-op cases are excluded syntactically.

### C+1 — Conjunct 3 has no true planned obligation on SoundSide-violating calldata (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:309-311; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:116-127, 246-260, 587-608; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Snapshot.lean:381-386; formal/docs/QUEUE.md:1050-1057; formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:283
- **Claim:** PLAN gap, the constructive complement of C-4. Frozen conjunct 3 quantifies over every calldata with no SoundSide. The composition proves it by a LeanAccepts case split; in the ¬LeanAccepts branch the only tool is rejects_of_not_accepts, which is false on the logup-panic set (C-4). No other route exists in the architecture: every block contract is conditioned on 'snapshotOf ... = .ok Snap', prefix_run_clean on LeanAccepts, and where prepareMsgs panics snapshotOf is not ok. Staged rejection (C.3 and WP-M0 item 2) explicitly excludes that set. So closing conjunct 3 needs either a frozen-surface change (SoundSide as a premise of the reverting branch) or a new snapshot-free obligation ('every run of R.prog reverts or ends in TraceReturn's single return of ABI true with the world unchanged') that no card owns. Conjunct 3 itself is probably true as frozen; the plan to prove it is what is broken.
- **Evidence:** Blocks.lean:606-608 'right; exact ⟨rejects_of_not_accepts R product W Lyt H vkAddr cd st₀ gg obs hdep hentry hpc hacc hobs, hw⟩'. Blocks.lean:121-123: BlockContractsAt hypotheses are decodeCalldata = some, instOfWords? = some and 'snapshotOf R.E H R.vk inst ms none = .ok Snap'. Snapshot.lean:385-386 '(snapshotOf E H vk inst ms acc).isOk = (prepareMsgs (Gₜ := Gₜ) E H Features.solidity vk inst ms).isOk' (itself a registered sorry). PLAN-V2.md:283 'Exclude only the `logup.rs:460` panic (the `SoundSide` bad set)'. QUEUE.md:1055-1056 says the same for C.3.

### C+2 — Valid.closed does not scan the ABI guard block and is scope-insensitive, so it is not a closure check on R.prog (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** low
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Program.lean:66-76, 114-116, 132-134; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Shared.lean:114-119, 192-193; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:201-202
- **Claim:** The frozen clause 'closed : mainClosed R.pv R.model = true' is documented as 'After inlining, the main block's only free names are the two wrapper locals'. It scans the inlined mainAsmRaw only. asmOf also runs abiGuardBlock, whose two names NUM_INSTANCE_CPTR and ERR_BAD_CALLDATA_SHAPE are resolved only if constTableOf happens to contain them; no Valid clause pins either. If ERR_BAD_CALLDATA_SHAPE were absent, only the guard's revert branch would be stuck, so conjuncts 1 and 3 would hold vacuously on ABI-malformed calldata and nothing in the headline would notice. That is a concrete in-surface instance of C-1. In addition freeNames treats a name bound anywhere (any let, assignment target or function parameter) as bound everywhere, so mainClosed = true does not rule out an out-of-scope read such as a helper body reading a main-block local, which is stuck because callees see only parameters and return slots. At a real render both constants are declared in Constants.sol, so this is a robustness gap in the clause, not a defect of the deployment.
- **Evidence:** Program.lean:132-134 'def mainClosed (pv : PlanView) (m : Model) : Bool := (freeNames (Inline.inlineContractConstants (constTableOf m) (mainAsmRaw pv))).all fun x => x == "vk" || x == "quotientEvaluator"'. Program.lean:114-116 'Inline.inlineContractConstants (constTableOf m) (abiGuardBlock m ++ wrapperLocals m vkAddr qAddr ++ mainAsmRaw pv)'. Program.lean:73-75: the guard reads 'Expr.var "NUM_INSTANCE_CPTR"' and 'Expr.var "ERR_BAD_CALLDATA_SHAPE"'. Shared.lean:115-117 'Deliberately generous to the program - a name bound anywhere at all counts as bound'. BigStep.lean:189: the callee environment is '(decl.params.zip argvals) ++ bindZeros D decl.rets'. proofs/solidity-verifier/templates/partials/verifier/Constants.sol:37 and :225 declare both constants.

### C — errors the skeptic found in the reviewer's report

- Summary and C-1 say determinism exists only for the closed dialect EVM.evm. That is wrong: formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:229-231 (dialect_deterministic) and :297-313 (Run.det, RunCommitted.det) prove determinism for the open dialect under c.Functional and g.Functional, and :869-881 (honestCalls_functional) makes the honest relation functional, so conjunct 2's dialect is deterministic. The reviewer's own C-3 cites dialect_deterministic. Only adequacy and the interpreter are closed-dialect only; what is missing for the open dialect is determinism under permissive or an arbitrary gas oracle.
- C-4 'recorded_where' is incomplete. formal/docs/design.md:1814-1819 (under D-P47, 'Not decided, deliberately') already says that dropping SoundSide from Rejects 'would also assert rejection inside the logup-panic bad set, which has not been checked. Investigate first', and DECISIONS-PENDING.md 'Also open' keeps it as investigation C.3. The risk is therefore recorded as open. What is new is that the SoundSide-free statement already exists in the cone as rejects_of_not_accepts with a docstring asserting the opposite, plus the missing R.Valid.
- C-4 counterexample (1) is narrower than needed and silently assumes the contract constant NUM_INSTANCE_CPTR equals plan.proofLayout.proofEnd. Setting cfg.numInstances := cd.size + 1 fails decodeCalldata's size guard (Calldata.lean:341) for every calldata and every render, with no such assumption.
- C-3 infers from the 370-line Step.det that the glue lemma is of that order. A generalised Step.det is not required; a direct inversion of the six-statement prefix suffices (precedent: Instances/RunShellInversion.lean:323-403, about 80 lines for one guard). Medium overstates a cost-estimate defect on a true lemma in the refinable layer; low is the better fit.
- Summary says 'production templates contain no gas()'. The templates do contain gas(): QuotientAndLinearization.yul:28, :35 and :153, and TraceAndGasHelpers.yul:21. They are rendered only in the trace, gas-checkpoint and external-quotient arms, which SupportedV2.production and embeddedQuotient (Statements.lean:127, :132) exclude. The statement holds for the first-headline scope only; the external-quotient production render (WP-X2) forwards gas().
- C-2 says a successful call in the model always carries 32 or 128 bytes. That holds for the generated frames, but the honest modexp relation returns success with zero bytes when modsize = 0 (Externals.lean:972-981), so the general statement is slightly too strong. It does not affect the finding.

### C — checked and found correct

- Builtin fidelity (formal/vendor/yul-semantics/YulSemantics/Dialect/EVMExec.lean:711-885), checked for every opcode in the production templates (add, sub, mul, div, mod, addmod, mulmod, lt, gt, eq, iszero, and, or, xor, byte, shl, shr, mload, mstore, mcopy, keccak256, pop, calldataload, calldatasize, calldatacopy, returndatasize, extcodesize, extcodecopy, extcodehash, staticcall, return, revert). Wrap-around is BitVec 256; div and mod by zero give 0 (:717,:719); addmod and mulmod compute in Nat with modulus 0 giving 0 (:721-722); shl/shr take the shift first and give 0 for shifts of 256 or more (:739-740); byte is big-endian indexed with 0 for index 32 or more (:738); calldataload and calldatacopy zero-pad (wordFrom/byteFrom, :329-338,:791-797); mload/mstore are big-endian (loadWord/storeWord, :332-342); mcopy reads the pre-state (copyWithin, :357-358); returndatacopy out of bounds is a non-committing halt (:805-811); extcodehash of an empty account is 0 (projectedCodeHash, :307-310); return and revert expose readBytes of memory (:877-884).
- The staticcall step (EVMExec.lean:614-631, 889-899): the input is read from pre-call memory; only min(outSize, returndata length) bytes are written (copyReturn); the returndata buffer is replaced; the flag is 1 or 0; a staticcall never installs the response world. Under honestCalls a failure is failResp (returndata = []), so output memory is untouched and returndatasize is 0 (Bridge.lean:694-695, 705-711), as for a failing real precompile. Under permissive a failure may carry arbitrary returndata, which is a sound over-approximation.
- permissive is total: failResp st satisfies it for every request (Bridge.lean:1433-1435), so no staticcall is stuck under permissive; and a success must equal the honest response (Bridge.lean:731-732, 718-723).
- Observations (Bridge.lean:486-564, 660-670): isRevertOrHalt requires halted = some (k, d) with k.commits = false, so a normal fall-through (halted = none), stop and selfdestruct satisfy neither disjunct of conjunct 3 and would refute it. A successful-but-empty run cannot be hidden as a revert. returnView fixes outcome = .halt, halted = some (.ret, abiTrue) and all eleven world components. committedState rolls back exactly the non-committing halts (EVMExec.lean:262-279). abiTrue is the 32-byte big-endian word 1.
- Bridge sorries: keccakOf_invariant (Bridge.lean:469-472) and RunCommitted_keccakOf (:476-479) are true as stated; no stepOp arm, finishCall, CallWorld.install or finishSelfdestruct touches env.keccakOf. Run_worldEq_of_noWriters (:1221-1223) is true as stated for every call relation: the only writers of the eleven WorldEq components (storage, transient, logs, selfdestructs, selfBalance, balanceOf, extCodeOf, extCodeHashOf, nonceOf, storageOf, transientOf) are sstore, tstore, log0-4, selfdestruct, create, create2 and non-static call/callcode/delegatecall, all flagged by Op.isWriter (:1039-1042). staticcall preserves the world whatever the response; function bodies are scanned at their funDef; Run starts from an empty function environment. setimmutable changes only env.immutable, which is outside WorldEq.
- Determinism: dialect_deterministic (Bridge.lean:229-292) is proved without sorry and its case analysis is correct. callsOf product is functional via honestCalls_functional (:869-881): accountKey 0 is not a precompile key and the evaluator slot is empty, so the honest dialect with a functional gas oracle is deterministic. twoResponses_nondet and gas_nondet_with_any correctly show both hypotheses are necessary. Nondeterminism enters only through the call family and gas().
- Yul core (BigStep.lean): right-to-left argument evaluation with source-order values (:207-219); callee environment is params and returns only, with the function environment of the definition site (:185-193); function hoisting per block and for-init (:114-118, 225-227, 269-272); block scope restore; for-loop init scope extending over cond/post/body; break, continue and leave propagation; switch through selectSwitch; if with no else. These match the Yul specification for all constructs the templates use.
- Production templates (proofs/solidity-verifier/templates and src/lowering/kzg/mod.rs:1826-1909): no msize; no gas(), call, log, sstore, create, selfdestruct or returndatacopy under production flags; every staticcall targets a literal address 0x05/0x0b/0x0c/0x0f and is followed by a success check and an eq(returndatasize(), N) check; the only return is TraceReturn.yul:72 after mstore(RETURN_MPTR, 1).
- Adequacy and determinism statements: Interp.run_adequacy (Adequacy.lean:772-774) is stated for any Lawful ExecDialect and EVM.run_adequacy (:786-789) for the closed dialect only. The vendored tree contains no sorry and no axiom; keccakBytes is the only opaque constant (EVMExec.lean:98).
- Midfall/Yul/Contracts.lean: StmtsContractAll and the composition lemmas (cons_split, append, block, cond, switch, callUser, of_det, ExecLoopAll.countdown_or_halt) are proved without sorry and state what they claim; the non-vacuity example at :94-95 is valid.
- Soundness of the English claim is not threatened by out-of-gas, the 63/64 rule, call depth or memory-expansion cost: frame-level out-of-gas reverts, and every sub-call starvation is a call failure that permissive admits.

### C — not covered

- No Lean, lake or cargo was run: every needs_lean_check snippet is unverified, and no #print axioms or cone output was re-measured.
- Proof bodies of Adequacy.lean (lines 140-440, 500-730) and Determinism.lean (lines 80-380) were skimmed, not read line by line; their statements were read in full.
- formal/Midfall/Yul/ShellModel.lean, IRCheck.lean and ImportSolcIR.lean (retired v1 track) were not read.
- The byte-level precompile arithmetic in Externals.lean (affine addition, MSM, subgroup checks, modexp, gas tables) was read but not independently recomputed against EIP-2537 vectors; formal/Midfall/Assumptions/Eip2537.lean (Holds) was only outlined.
- Whether the actual GenAst output at poseidonRender is free of stuck constructs (C-1 is a gap in the statement and plan, not a demonstrated stuck path).
- EntryFrame realism belongs to another dimension. One lead for that reviewer: EntryFrame.zeroAbove demands zero memory from 0x80, while the deployed via-IR code sets the free pointer to 0x8e0 (Midfall/Yul/Builtins.lean:32-34) and solc's stack-to-memory mover may write spill slots in [0x80, mload(0x40)) before and during the body; the modelled program does not read that region, so the stronger assumption is probably harmless but is not what solc guarantees.
- Inline.inlineContractConstants, CodegenAgrees and the GenAst emitters were read only as far as needed for C-1; the PCS call sites in the Lean port (GenAstPcs) were not checked against kzg/mod.rs.
- C-4 depends on the existence of at least one accepting calldata for the real verifier and on the contract accepting inside the logup-panic set; neither was established mechanically.
- No differential evidence (revm or solc Yul interpreter) for the vendored stepOp was examined; fidelity of the builtins rests on my manual comparison.


## D — block contracts and composition

**Reviewer summary.** Verdict: `lowering_equivalence_of_blocks` is sound plumbing, but several sorried obligations it rests on are false as stated, and the architecture does not yet deliver the headline.

1. All soundness content sits in the single program-level `Rejects` sorry. Block contracts are only spent on Lean-accepted calldata, `prefix_run_clean` demands `LeanAccepts`, and `Inv` is typed over the full `Snapshot`, so it cannot carry staged rejection.
2. `rejects_of_not_accepts` is false (lead a confirmed). On the logup bad set the contract uses prefix/suffix products, never inverts, and returns true while Lean panics. Conjunct 3 itself stays true but needs a snapshot-free exit analysis.
3. The frozen headline and `Rejects` are false for calldata of 2^256 bytes or more (`calldatasize` truncation versus a size guard in ℕ).
4. `Render.scope` omits the QuotientHelpers functions the real template defines inside the quotient region. `prog_slices_decomp`, `runBody_iff_slices` and quotient progress fail at any codegen-agreeing render.
5. `Inv` does not pin what the PCS reads, and `Valid` does not tie the VK payload or codec laws to the Lean verifier. The hinge is false as stated beyond the recorded pins.
6. `ec_pairing_contract`, `vk_progress` and `vk_sound_clean` are false as stated; all are cheaply fixable.
7. At today's `poseidonRender` the headline proposition is itself false (empty PCS, so the pairing runs on two identity points). The cone counts citations of a placeholder.

The sequencing lemmas, `inline_append`, `fp_pairing_algebra`, the TraceReturn rows and the contract combinators check out.

`R.Valid` is uninhabited today, so "false as stated" verdicts on Valid-conditional lemmas refer to any render where `Valid` can hold. This was a read-only review and no Lean was run; please verify before relying on it.

### D-1 — Frozen headline and Rejects are false for calldata of length >= 2^256 (calldatasize truncates, decodeCalldata compares in Nat)

- **Severity (reviewer):** critical · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → critical · **Kind:** statement-false · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:240-247,298-311; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Calldata.lean:341; formal/vendor/yul-semantics/YulSemantics/Dialect/EVMExec.lean:791-792; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:134-139; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks/VkLoading.lean:718-728
- **Claim (reviewer):** LoweringEquivalence conjunct 1 quantifies over every cd : Bytes, and EntryFrame puts no bound on cd.size. The EVM model computes calldatasize modulo 2^256, while decodeCalldata requires an exact size equality in Nat. Padding an accepted calldata with 2^256 zero bytes gives a calldata on which the program runs identically and returns ABI true, yet LeanAccepts is false and SoundSide holds vacuously. So conjunct 1, Render.Rejects, poseidon_rejects and the standalone vk_sound_clean (which has no decode hypothesis) are false at any render that accepts at least one proof. The counterexample is not realisable on a real EVM, but the frozen statement is unprovable as written.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** EVMExec.lean:792 `| .calldatasize => rd0 (BitVec.ofNat 256 st.env.calldata.length) args st`; :791 `wordFrom st.env.calldata p.toNat` (zero-padded). Calldata.lean:341 `guard (cd.size == instCptr + 32 * numInstances)`. GenAst.lean:963-965 `eq(calldatasize(), add(INSTANCE_CPTR, numInstances*32))`. Statements.lean:240-247 EntryFrame fields are shape, zeroAbove, fmp, callvalue, selector only. Statements.lean:302-304 `RunBody ... -> obs.isReturn -> SoundSide R H cd -> obs.returnData = abiTrue ∧ LeanAccepts R H cd`. Bytes is `abbrev Bytes := ByteArray` (Common/Bytes.lean:27), unbounded in the logic.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take cd0 with LeanAccepts R H cd0 and a returning run. Let cd' := cd0 ++ (2^256 zero bytes). Then calldatasize(cd') = calldatasize(cd0), every calldataload/calldatacopy reads the same bytes, and the same EntryFrame memory works, so the run returns abiTrue. decodeCalldata ... cd' = none by the size guard, hence ¬LeanAccepts R H cd' and SoundSide R H cd' is vacuous.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** The frozen LoweringEquivalence conjunct 1, Render.Rejects and the standalone vk_sound_clean are false at any render that has one Lean-accepted calldata cd0 with a returning run. Padding cd0 with 2^256 zero bytes gives the same run (calldatasize wraps, loads and copies zero-pad) while decodeCalldata fails its exact size guard in Nat. Two corrections: the counterexample cannot be instantiated at today's poseidonRender, because LeanAccepts is uninhabited there (see the first additional finding), so poseidon_rejects is false for D-11's reason, not this one. It is unrealisable on a real EVM and the repair is one bound (cd.size < 2^256) in the trusted EntryFrame, at the cost of a frozen re-hash.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Reproduced step by step. EVMExec.lean:792 `| .calldatasize   => rd0 (BitVec.ofNat 256 st.env.calldata.length) args st`; :791 `wordFrom st.env.calldata p.toNat`; :329 `byteFrom data i := data.getD i 0`; :353-354 copyInto reads `byteFrom data (src + (a - dst))`. Calldata.lean:341 `guard (cd.size == instCptr + 32 * numInstances)`. Bytes.lean:27 `abbrev Bytes := ByteArray` (unbounded in the logic). Statements.lean:240-247: EntryFrame has only shape, zeroAbove, fmp, callvalue, selector; no size bound. Statements.lean:302-304 conjunct 1 demands `LeanAccepts R H cd` for every returning permissive run with SoundSide; SoundSide (271-276) is vacuous when decodeCalldata = none. An honest run is a permissive run (Bridge.lean:737-740 honest_le_permissive) and conjunct 1 quantifies every gas oracle. CallWorld (EVMExec.lean:445-461) has no calldata field, so call responses are unaffected. Inv .vk also compares in Nat (Inv.lean:748-749), so vk_sound_clean (VkLoading.lean:718-728, no decode hypothesis) fails on the same input: normal exit, success = 1, not Clean .vk, not Poisoned. Severity is critical by the letter of the rubric (the frozen headline is false as written); practical exploitability is nil.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
example (S : Nat) : BitVec.ofNat 256 (S + 2^256) = BitVec.ofNat 256 S := by apply BitVec.eq_of_toNat_eq; simp [BitVec.toNat_ofNat]
-- and: from h : cd.size ≠ L.proofEnd + 32 + 32 * n, prove decodeCalldata (p := p) L n c cd = none by unfolding decodeCalldata and its seventh guard.
```
- **Suggested fix:** Add `cd.size < 2^256` (or a realistic bound) to the trusted EntryFrame, or as a headline hypothesis; this needs a frozen-surface re-hash. Pass hdec into vk_sound_clean and vk_progress.

### D-2 — rejects_of_not_accepts is false: on the logup-panic set the contract returns true while Lean rejects

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (Item 4 of the brief; STATUS.md:25-32; QUEUE.md:414-423; DECISIONS-PENDING.md:138. They record the hypothesis mismatch but assert the opposite conclusion (SoundSide is spurious).) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:344-363,587-608; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Logup.lean:278-280; proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/quotient.rs:1826-1864; proofs/src/plonk/logup.rs:458-461; formal/docs/STATUS.md:27-32; formal/docs/QUEUE.md:414-423
- **Claim (reviewer):** The lead auditor's hypothesis (a) is right and the Blocks.lean docstring is wrong. SoundSide is needed exactly in the direction 'Lean rejects, so the contract reverts'. Lean and Rust abort when some f_j(x)+beta = 0. The generated Yul computes the helper identity as h*prod − sum(prefix*suffix) with no inversion, so an honest proof whose challenges land on that set passes the contract. rejects_of_not_accepts has no SoundSide hypothesis and quantifies over every H, so it is false at any render with a lookup. The decision floated in STATUS.md (delete SoundSide from Rejects) would make Rejects false too. Headline conjunct 3 is still true there (the run returns abiTrue), but its current proof goes through this false lemma.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Blocks.lean:349-352 "`SoundSide` excludes the logup-panic bad set, which is a COMPLETENESS side condition: nothing in the \"Lean rejects, so the contract reverts\" direction needs it, so the hypothesis is spurious for this use." Blocks.lean:606-608 conjunct 3, ¬LeanAccepts branch: `right; exact ⟨rejects_of_not_accepts ...` (it concludes revert). Logup.lean:278-280 `match invert f with | none => .error (Fault.panic "proofs/src/plonk/logup.rs:460")`. quotient.rs:1826-1860 emits `mstore(q_lookup_prefix, 1)` and prefix/suffix mulmod loops; :1861-1864 `q_lookup_eval := addmod(mulmod(h, q_lookup_product, r), sub(r, q_lookup_sum), r)`. STATUS.md:28-29 "Deleting `SoundSide` from `Rejects` makes `rejects_of_not_accepts` a one-liner".
- **Counterexample / witness:** Use a circuit with one lookup (the deployed poseidon has numLookups = 1). Fix an honest witness and commitments. Define H so that theta = theta0, beta = −f_j(x0) and the x-squeeze returns x0, for a chosen x0 outside the domain; H is only pinned at the VK runtime bytes. The helper identity still vanishes on the domain, the quotient exists, and the contract's check at x0 passes, so it returns abiTrue. Lean's prepareMsgs returns the logup.rs:460 panic, so ¬LeanAccepts.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** rejects_of_not_accepts is false as stated, for two independent reasons. (a) As the reviewer says: it has no SoundSide hypothesis, and on the logup bad set (some f_j(x)+beta = 0) Lean and Rust abort while the generated Yul uses prefix/suffix products and accepts an honest proof; H is universally quantified, so the set is reachable. (b) Not noted by the reviewer: the lemma also has no R.Valid hypothesis, so it is claimed for renders whose program is unrelated to R.vk or R.E. The Blocks.lean, QUEUE.md and STATUS.md text calling SoundSide a completeness condition is wrong; the frozen SoundSide docstring itself calls it a soundness condition. Deleting SoundSide from Rejects would make Rejects false. Conjunct 3 stays true on the bad set but must be proved without this lemma.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Blocks.lean:356-363: hypotheses are hdep, hentry, hpc, hna, hobs only; no hR, no SoundSide. Blocks.lean:350-352 "`SoundSide` excludes the logup-panic bad set, which is a COMPLETENESS side condition"; contrast Statements.lean:269-270 "Soundness side condition (v1 finding 5): Lean panics where `f_j(x) + β = 0`, the contract never inverts there." Lean: Logup.lean:278-280 `match invert f with | none => .error (Fault.panic "proofs/src/plonk/logup.rs:460")`. Rust: logup.rs:460 `.map(|f| product * f.invert().unwrap())`. Generator: quotient.rs:1826 `mstore(q_lookup_prefix, 1)`, prefix/suffix mulmod loops, :1862 `let q_lookup_eval := addmod(mulmod({}, q_lookup_product, r), sub(r, q_lookup_sum), r)`; no inversion. Use site: Blocks.lean:606-608 concludes revert in conjunct 3's ¬LeanAccepts branch. STATUS.md:27-30 "Deleting `SoundSide` from `Rejects` makes `rejects_of_not_accepts` a one-liner"; QUEUE.md:415-422 repeats the completeness mislabel. The protocol-level counterexample (honest witness, H chosen so beta = −f_j(x0) at the squeezed x0) is sound: the helper identity vanishes on the domain, so the non-inverting check at x0 passes.
- **Suggested fix:** Delete rejects_of_not_accepts and keep SoundSide in Rejects. Prove conjunct 3 without LeanAccepts: a snapshot-free exit-discipline contract per slice (every run ends normal or halts with a revert; traceReturn ends in a revert or `return` of the word 1), composed with StmtsContractAll.append, plus NoWriters.

### D-3 — All soundness content sits in the monolithic Rejects sorry; no workable architecture for the rejecting side

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (Blocks.lean:129-133 and PoseidonV2.lean:211-219 say WP-M0 will decompose Rejects. The three obstacles above are not recorded.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:129-139,246-260,541-560; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:757-776,816-819; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Snapshot.lean:363-370; formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:281-286
- **Claim (reviewer):** Lead (d) is confirmed. In conjunct 1 the LeanAccepts branch returns hacc itself and the other branch is discharged by hrej. The seven BlockSound contracts are only used on calldata Lean already accepts, to obtain returnData = abiTrue. Three structural obstacles block a staged rejection design. (i) prefix_run_clean takes LeanAccepts as a hypothesis, whereas PLAN-V2 WP-M0.3 asked for 'either reverts or reaches Clean .start'. So even the case 'snapshot exists but DualMSM.check = false', which BlockSound .finalPairing plus BlockSound .traceReturn already force to revert, is unreachable. (ii) Inv takes a full Snapshot at every checkpoint; its .transcript arm needs x1, x2, x3, x4, fCom and pi, which exist only after phaseC. When Lean faults in linearize or phaseB (for example the fEval panic when x3 lands in a point set), there is no Lean value to state the parser, Lagrange or quotient posts against. (iii) BlockContractsAt is conditional on snapshotOf = .ok, so it says nothing on parse-time rejects.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Blocks.lean:543-557 `by_cases hacc : LeanAccepts R H cd` ... `exact ⟨returnData_of_committed_ret st₀ st' hh, hacc⟩`; :559 `exact absurd (hrej W Lyt H vkAddr cd st₀ gg obs hdep hentry hpc hside hacc hobs)`. Blocks.lean:250 `(c : ExternalCalls) (g : ExternalGas) (hacc : LeanAccepts R H cd)`. PLAN-V2.md:284 "from an `EntryFrame`, either reverts or reaches `Clean .start`". Inv.lean:764-774 uses `S.a.x1`, `S.a.x2`, `S.b.x3`, `S.c.x4`, `S.b.fCom`, `S.c.pi` under `Checkpoint.transcript ≤ k`. Snapshot.lean:366 `| .transcript => "snapshotOfPcs"`. Inv.lean:816-819 the .pairing arm demands `DualMSM.check S.c.dual params`.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** The whole direction 'contract returns, so Lean accepts' is carried by the single sorried Rejects hypothesis. In conjunct 1 the accept branch returns hacc itself, and the seven BlockSound rows are spent only to get returnData = abiTrue. The three obstacles are real. One softening: the plan does list staged rejection (PLAN-V2 WP-M0.2) and prefix records exist (snapshotOfTrace to snapshotOfPcs, Checkpoint.prefixNeeds), so the accurate wording is 'no architecture exists yet, and the current §6/§7 shapes cannot host it without restructuring', not 'no workable architecture'.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Blocks.lean:543-557 `by_cases hacc : LeanAccepts R H cd` ... `exact ⟨returnData_of_committed_ret st₀ st' hh, hacc⟩`; :559 `exact absurd (hrej W Lyt H vkAddr cd st₀ gg obs hdep hentry hpc hside hacc hobs)`. (i) Blocks.lean:250 prefix_run_clean takes `(hacc : LeanAccepts R H cd)`; PLAN-V2.md:284 asks for "from an `EntryFrame`, either reverts or reaches `Clean .start`". I re-derived that BlockSound .finalPairing plus .traceReturn force revert when DualMSM.check is false (Clean .pairing is unsatisfiable by Inv.lean:816-818), but the composition never reaches them without LeanAccepts. (ii) Inv.lean:764-774 uses `S.a.x1`, `S.a.x2`, `S.b.x3`, `S.c.x4`, `S.b.fCom`, `S.c.pi` under `Checkpoint.transcript ≤ k`; these are SnapPcs fields (Snapshot.lean:141-146). Snapshot.lean:366 `| .transcript => "snapshotOfPcs"`. Clean (Blocks.lean:68-72) takes a full Snapshot. (iii) Blocks.lean:121-123: BlockContractsAt is conditional on decodeCalldata, instOfWords? and `snapshotOf ... = .ok Snap`.
- **Suggested fix:** Split prefix_run_clean into (a) a decode-only prefix lemma (the prefix reverts or reaches Clean .start) and (b) the snapshot/DualMSM facts. Wire 'snapshot exists' soundness through the existing BlockSound chain. Re-index Inv by prefix records (SnapTrace through SnapPcs) and move x1..x4/fCom/pi to a transcript-level record that does not depend on linearize, before writing any rejection row.

- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:676-680,757-776,792-802; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Snapshot.lean:249; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:92-101,152-154; proofs/solidity-verifier/templates/partials/verifier/TranscriptProofParser.yul:146,176,202-203,246,311; proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/kzg/mod.rs:287,1373; proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/encoding/mod.rs:359-360
- **Claim (reviewer):** BlockSound quantifies over every (V, st) satisfying Clean at q.pre. Clean .quotient does not constrain what the real PCS block reads: (i) the REVERSED_EVALS window, where the parser spills every eval and the PCS and quotient blocks read them; (ii) QUOTIENT_EVAL_MPTR and the SELECTOR_ACC buckets, because the .quotient arm is `match S.lin with | none => true` and snapshotOf always sets `lin := none`; (iii) the lookup-m, perm-z, lookup-helper, lookup-z and trashcan commitment regions, since Inv .transcript pins only advice and quotient-limb commitments, fCom and pi. So for a correct, fully emitted PCS slice there are Clean .quotient states from which the block exits normally with success = 1 and pairing inputs different from Snap's. That is neither reverted, Clean .pcs, nor Poisoned. The recorded S.2 issue (empty pcs slice) is a special case; filling in the PCS code does not repair it. The quotient row has the mirror problem: its post says nothing about its real outputs.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Inv.lean:770-774 only `holdsG1s ... adviceCommsMptrBase`, `quotientLimbCommsMptrBase`, `fComMptr`, `piMptr`. Inv.lean:795-801 `(match S.lin with | none => true | some L => holdsFr st (ptrAddr M.quotientEvalMptr) ...`. Snapshot.lean:249 `pure { toSnapLagrange := s3, queries := queries, lin := none }`. Inv.lean:676-680 gap_reversedEvalsWindow: "Inv .transcript does not pin the REVERSED_EVALS window". TranscriptProofParser.yul:146 `let lookup_m_walk := LOOKUP_M_COMMS_MPTR_BASE`, :176 `PERM_Z_COMMS_MPTR_BASE`, :311 `let eval_buf := REVERSED_EVALS_MPTR`. kzg/mod.rs:1373 `mload(add(SELECTOR_ACC_MPTR, ..))`, :287 `Query::new(data.computed_quotient_comm, 0, data.computed_quotient_eval)`. encoding/mod.rs:359-360 `let eval_cptr = reversed_evals_mptr`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take the honest state at .quotient for an accepted proof and overwrite one word of REVERSED_EVALS (or QUOTIENT_EVAL_MPTR, or one byte-valid point in PERM_Z_COMMS). Clean R .quotient still holds. The PCS block runs normally, all MSMs succeed, and PAIRING_RHS ≠ toEip Snap.c.dual.right.eval, so none of the three post arms holds.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** As stated. Clean R .quotient leaves unpinned everything a faithful PCS slice reads besides the challenge slots, advice and quotient-limb commitments, fCom and pi: the REVERSED_EVALS window, QUOTIENT_EVAL and the selector buckets (inert because snapshotOf always sets lin := none), and the lookup-m, perm-z, lookup-helper, lookup-z and trashcan commitment regions. So BlockSound .pcs is false for a faithful PCS program: perturb an unpinned word, Clean still holds, the block exits normally with success = 1 and pairing inputs different from Snap's. The empty-slice refutation the project records is a special case, and the quotient row's post says nothing about its real outputs.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Inv.lean:757-776: the .transcript arm pins theta, beta, gamma, trash, y, x, x1..x4, `holdsG1s ... adviceCommsMptrBase`, `quotientLimbCommsMptrBase`, fComMptr, piMptr, qEvalCptrMptr and nothing else. Inv.lean:795-801 `(match S.lin with | none => true | some L => ...)`; Snapshot.lean:249 `pure { toSnapLagrange := s3, queries := queries, lin := none }`. Inv.lean:676-680 gap_reversedEvalsWindow. Parser writes: TranscriptProofParser.yul:146 `let lookup_m_walk := LOOKUP_M_COMMS_MPTR_BASE` with calldatacopy at :151; :176 `PERM_Z_COMMS_MPTR_BASE`; :202-203 lookup helper and z bases; :311-321 `let eval_buf := REVERSED_EVALS_MPTR` ... `mstore(eval_buf, eval)`. PCS reads: encoding/mod.rs:359-360 `let eval_cptr = reversed_evals_mptr;`; kzg/mod.rs:1373 `mload(add(SELECTOR_ACC_MPTR, ..))`; :287 `Query::new(data.computed_quotient_comm, 0, data.computed_quotient_eval)`. BlockSound quantifies every (V, st) with Clean at q.pre (Blocks.lean:94-97). PLAN-V2.md:282 (WP-M0.1) plans this check but it has not been done; QUEUE.md:855-857 records only the empty-slice case.
- **Suggested fix:** Before writing rows S.5 to S.7 and pcs, extend Inv with a reversed-evals clause (needs the EvalRead to Fr bridge), the five commitment regions, and QUOTIENT_EVAL/SELECTOR_ACC (fill Snapshot.lin, or state them against a recomputed linearization). Alternatively merge quotientLin and pcs into one contract whose pre is Inv .lagrange plus those pins.

### D-6 — Valid does not tie the VK payload's key material (domain words, fixed and permutation commitments, G1 base) to R.vk / R.E; the hinge is false at a Valid render whose payload disagrees

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** trusted-base-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (STATUS.md:279-290 (general 'under-pins' pattern), D-P46 (three base slots), Inv.lean:473-490 (delta). Domain words and the fixed/permutation commitments are not recorded.) · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:182-217 (slots at 203-205); formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:533-579,741; formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAst.lean:695,700,717; formal/docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:57-72
- **Claim (reviewer):** The `slots` docstring says the payload words (VK header, field constants, domain, G1/G2 bases) agree with the Lean VK. EnvAgrees has ten fields; only vkDigestWord mentions the payload, and the others are facts about R.vk alone. Nothing in the 13 clauses equates the payload's OMEGA, OMEGA_INV_TO_L and N_INV words, G1 base, fixed commitments or permutation commitments with R.vk.domain, R.vk.fixedCommitments or R.vk.permutation.commitments. Inv .vk pins memory to PV.vk.bytes, a plan field, not to R.vk. Nothing pins E.delta either. The contract reads omega and n_inv from the payload in Lagrange and the commitments in the PCS, so `blockContracts_of_valid` (from R.Valid alone) is false at any Valid render whose payload encodes different key material. At the deployment these will surface one pin at a time. D-P46 proposes only the three base slots.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Statements.lean:203-205 "The payload words the contract loads (VK header, field constants, domain, G1/G2 bases) agree with the environment and VK the Lean verifier uses. -/ slots : EnvAgrees R.E R.vk R.pv R.params R.sG2gen". Inv.lean:533-579 fields: modulus, truncWidth, domainN, baryWeight, omegaInvMul, omegaOrder, negLagranges, nG2, sG2, vkDigestWord. Inv.lean:548 `baryWeight : vk.domain.barycentricWeight = ((2 ^ vk.domain.k : ℕ) : Fr p)⁻¹` (no payload term). GenAst.lean:695 `ylet "omega" (yop Op.mload [yvar "OMEGA_MPTR"])`, :717 `yop Op.mload [yvar "N_INV_MPTR"]`. Inv.lean:473 "delta ... STILL DROPPED".
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take a render R with all 13 clauses true and form R' by replacing R.vk.fixedCommitments[0] with another readable point, or R.vk.domain.omega with another primitive n-th root (adjusting omegaInv), keeping transcriptRepr. Every Valid clause is unchanged, but the contract (payload) and Lean (R'.vk) now verify against different keys, so BlockProgress .pcs/.lagrange and the headline's completeness fail at R'.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** As stated, and wider. Valid ties the payload to the Lean VK only through word 0 (vkDigestWord). plan.vk (constants, fixedComms, permutationComms) is a free plan field that validateGeneratorInvariants and generateVerifierFromPlan never compare with R.vk. Besides the domain words, G1 base and commitments, the Lean-side fields E.delta, E.fewerPointSetsRuntime and E.field (beyond numBits) are also unpinned. So for any Valid R, replacing R.vk.fixedCommitments[0] by another readable point, or changing E.delta, gives a Valid R' whose contract and Lean verifier disagree, and the generic hinge blockContracts_of_valid is false at R or R'. The `slots` docstring overstates what EnvAgrees says.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Statements.lean:203-205 docstring "The payload words the contract loads (VK header, field constants, domain, G1/G2 bases) agree with the environment and VK" versus Inv.lean:533-579: ten fields, only `vkDigestWord : (PV.vk.constants.getD 0 ("", 0)).2 = frWord vk.transcriptRepr` mentions the payload; e.g. :548 `baryWeight : vk.domain.barycentricWeight = ((2 ^ vk.domain.k : ℕ) : Fr p)⁻¹`. LoweringPlan.lean:213-215 `vk : Halo2VerifyingKey` is a plan field; :534-630 validateGeneratorInvariants checks meta, memory, G1 coverage, PCS sizing and quotient reservation only. VerifyingKey.lean:109-119 WellFormed constrains sizes and degrees, not commitment values; :57 transcriptRepr is an imported field. The contract reads the payload: GenAst.lean:695 `ylet "omega" (yop Op.mload [yvar "OMEGA_MPTR"])`, :717 `yop Op.mload [yvar "N_INV_MPTR"]`; Constants.sol:54-57 place these at `memory.vk_mptr + vk_header.*`. Lean side: Snapshot.lean:245 `Verifier.linearize (p := p) delta vk ...` with delta = E.delta, and :258-259 `E.fewerPointSetsRuntime`; Inv.lean:473 "delta ... STILL DROPPED". DECISIONS-PENDING.md:57-72 (D-P46) proposes only the three base slots.
- **Suggested fix:** Add a decidable payload-agreement clause family to Valid (or to a per-deployment pins structure consumed by the rows): header words equal frWord of vk.domain.{omega, omegaInv^…, (2^k)⁻¹}, payload commitments equal toEip of the VK commitments, G1/G2 bases, and E.delta equal to the generator literal. Fix the slots docstring.

### D-7 — ec_pairing_contract is false as stated in three ways, and FinalPairing.lean misdescribes the helper

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → medium · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks/FinalPairing.lean:30-37,185-227,314-328; formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAst.lean:265-292; proofs/solidity-verifier/templates/partials/verifier/AssemblyHelpers.yul:301-308
- **Claim (reviewer):** The production helper is: `ret := and(ret, eq(mload(scratch), 1)); if iszero(ret) { fail(ERR_PROOF_REJECTED) }; ret := 1`. It reverts on a false pairing and never returns 0 from a nonzero input. Three defects follow. (i) Honest half: fpPostHonest demands `o = .normal` with no revert arm, but for a Snap with fpAnsBit = false every honest run from Clean .pcs reverts; the lemma has no DualMSM.check hypothesis. (ii) Conjunct 1: fpPre does not constrain `success`. With success = 3 and a yes answer the helper writes 1, while the post demands fpWord(fpInBit V && ans) = 0. (iii) Conjunct 1 quantifies over an arbitrary ExternalCalls c, but the post pins success to `product (fpFrame ..)`; a c that always answers 1 refutes it. The header claim that the helper 'otherwise returns and(success, eq(mload(scratch), 1))', and the claim that a normal exit with success = 0 is the intended handoff, are wrong for the production render.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** GenAst.lean:287-291 `yset "ret" (yop Op.and [yvar "ret", yop Op.eq [yop Op.mload [yvar "scratch"], ylit 1]])`, `yif (yop Op.iszero [yvar "ret"]) [yexpr (ycall "fail" [yvar "ERR_PROOF_REJECTED"])]`, `yset "ret" (ylit 1)`. AssemblyHelpers.yul:307-308 `if iszero(ret) { fail(ERR_PROOF_REJECTED) }` / `ret := 1`. FinalPairing.lean:223 `o = .normal ∧ Frame R H vkAddr cd V' st' ∧`; :211 `VEnv.get V' "success" = some (fpWord (fpOutBit R product Snap V))`; :318-320 `(c : ExternalCalls) (g : ExternalGas) : StmtsContractAll (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) ... (fpPre R Snap H vkAddr cd) (fpPost ...)`; :33-34 "and otherwise returns `and(success, eq(mload(scratch), 1))`".
- **Counterexample / witness:** (i) Snap with dual.left.eval = g and dual.right.eval = 1 (check false), plus any Clean .pcs state for it: the honest run halts with a revert. (ii) The same state with V.success := 3 and a check-true Snap: success' = 1, but fpOutBit = false. (iii) c := the relation answering {success := true, returndata := word 1} to every request, with a check-false Snap.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** As stated: ec_pairing_contract is false in three ways, all at inputs its only consumer never uses. (i) The honest half has no revert arm and no check hypothesis, but the production helper reverts on a false pairing. (ii) fpPre leaves `success` unconstrained; with success = 3 and a yes answer the slice writes 1 while the post demands 0. (iii) Conjunct 1 quantifies an arbitrary ExternalCalls but pins the result to `product`. The file header and the fpOutBit docstring describe the trace-render tail, not the production one; consequently the normal-and-poisoned exit from Clean .pcs is unreachable in production.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Production helper: GenAst.lean:287-291 `yset "ret" (yop Op.and [yvar "ret", yop Op.eq [yop Op.mload [yvar "scratch"], ylit 1]])`, `yif (yop Op.iszero [yvar "ret"]) [yexpr (ycall "fail" [yvar "ERR_PROOF_REJECTED"])]`, `yset "ret" (ylit 1)`; AssemblyHelpers.yul:306-308 the same under `{%- else %}`. Because `ret := staticcall(...)` (AssemblyHelpers.yul:294) overwrites the incoming value, any nonzero success yields 1. FinalPairing.lean:185-189 fpPre = Frame ∧ Inv .pcs; Frame (Blocks.lean:56-65) and Inv .pcs (Inv.lean:807-813) do not mention success. :156-157 `fpInBit V := VEnv.get V "success" == some (1 : U256)`; :211 post `some (fpWord (fpOutBit R product Snap V))`. :223 fpPostHonest `o = .normal ∧ ...`. :314-320 `(c : ExternalCalls) (g : ExternalGas) : StmtsContractAll (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) ... (fpPre R Snap H vkAddr cd) (fpPost ...)` for every c. Header :33-34 "otherwise returns `and(success, eq(mload(scratch), 1))`"; :165 "of the helper's last line". The consumer applies conjunct 1 only at permissive (callsOf product) with Clean .pcs (:388-390) and the honest half only under hdual (:431-432, :444-445).
- **Suggested fix:** State conjunct 1 at c = permissive (callsOf product), with pre Clean .pcs or Poisoned (success in {0,1}). Add `fpAnsBit R product Snap = true` to the honest half, or give it a revert arm. Drop the unreachable normal-and-poisoned arm from the description. Conclusions that hold only vacuously today are listed under D-12.

### D-8 — vk_progress and vk_sound_clean are stated without the decode hypothesis; vk_progress also needs pins beyond D-P50's six

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → medium · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (STATUS.md:279-290 and VkLoading.lean §5 record the six pins. The missing hdec and the header and calldata-constant pins are not recorded.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks/VkLoading.lean:718-728,751-756,766-771; formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAst.lean:941-967; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:147-168
- **Claim (reviewer):** (1) vk_progress claims BlockProgress R .vkLoading for an arbitrary cd and Snap; vk_blockRow has hdec/hinst/hsnap in scope but does not pass them. For a cd of the wrong shape the block reverts at `fail(ERR_BAD_CALLDATA_SHAPE)`, so the total contract is false. vk_sound_clean has the same omission; it fails only through D-1's oversize calldata and otherwise needs an INSTANCE_CPTR table pin. (2) Even with hdec, the honest run must pass the six header cross-checks and the three calldata checks. That needs the table addresses NUM_INSTANCES_MPTR, K_MPTR, HAS_ACCUMULATOR_MPTR, ACC_OFFSET_MPTR, NUM_ACC_LIMBS_MPTR, NUM_ACC_LIMB_BITS_MPTR, PROOF_LEN_CPTR, NUM_INSTANCE_CPTR and INSTANCE_CPTR to match the plan view, and the payload header words to equal pv.numInstances, pv.k and the four accumulator words. None of these is in vkPins or vkProgressPins, which cover VK_MPTR, payload length, numInst, disjointness, vkLen and vkHash. So the vkLoading row is not derivable from R.Valid as D-P50 assumes.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** VkLoading.lean:751-755 `theorem vk_progress (R) (product) (hR : R.Valid) (hpins : vkPins R) (hppins : vkProgressPins R) (Snap) (H) (vkAddr : U256) (cd : Bytes) : G1CodecLaws R.E.g1 → ChallengesGood R.vk Snap → DualMSM.check Snap.c.dual R.params = true → BlockProgress R Partial.vkLoading Snap H vkAddr product cd`. :768 `intro hR _hpc H vkAddr cd ms instW inst Snap hdec hinst hsnap` (not passed on). GenAst.lean:941-942 `success := and(success, eq(mload(NUM_INSTANCES_MPTR), numInstances))`; :958-967 the three calldata checks and `fail(ERR_BAD_CALLDATA_SHAPE)`. Statements.lean:165-168 vkProgressPins has vkLen and vkHash only.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Any cd of the wrong length, a Snap with DualMSM.check true, and a state with Frame (calldata = cd), success = 1 and zero memory: Clean R .start holds, and the unique honest run reverts at statement 13. exitOk fails.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** As stated. (1) vk_progress is false for a wrong-shape cd: exitOk needs Clean .vk, whose calldata-length clause is then unsatisfiable, so no run can meet the post. vk_sound_clean without hdec fails on D-1's oversize calldata. vk_blockRow has hdec, hinst and hsnap in scope and drops them. (2) Even with hdec, progress needs table-address pins for the six header slots and three calldata constants, and payload-header content pins; none is in vkPins or vkProgressPins. Sharper than the reviewer's wording: the payload header words are free plan fields that Valid does not check, so at a Valid render whose header word differs from the literal the block always reverts and vk_progress is false, not merely underivable.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** VkLoading.lean:751-755 vk_progress binds `(Snap) (H) (vkAddr : U256) (cd : Bytes)` with only G1CodecLaws, ChallengesGood and DualMSM.check as premises. :768-771 `intro hR _hpc H vkAddr cd ms instW inst Snap hdec hinst hsnap` then calls the three lemmas without hdec. Inv.lean:748-749 `(st.env.calldata.length == PV.proofLayout.proofEnd + 32 + 32 * PV.numInstances)` is part of Inv .vk; Blocks.lean:59 Frame.calldata pins the state's calldata to cd. GenAst.lean:941-952 six `success := and(success, eq(mload(<SLOT>_MPTR), lit))`; :958-967 three calldata checks then `fail(ERR_BAD_CALLDATA_SHAPE)`. Statements.lean:147-168: vkPins = vkMptr, payloadLen, numInst, disjoint; vkProgressPins = vkLen, vkHash. The payload is `plan.vk.constants` (Models.lean:389, 474-480), a plan field no Valid clause compares with pv.numInstances or pv.k. I checked that the VK guard itself is passable from Frame alone: extcodehash is `projectedCodeHash` = keccakOf (extCodeOf a) for non-empty code (EVMExec.lean:307-310, :859) and runtimeBytes always carries the 0xfe prefix (Models.lean:484-485).
- **Suggested fix:** Add hdec/hinst/hsnap to the three §6 lemmas. Extend the pin bundle with the nine table pins and the payload-header content pins, and decide them at the deployment before attempting vk_progress.

### D-9 — prefix_run_clean is not derivable from the 13 Valid clauses, and runBody_iff_slices needs a determinism lemma the tree does not have

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → medium · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (QUEUE.md:384-385 (cost rows) and STATUS.md:279-290 (the pattern). The specific pins and the determinism lemma are not listed.) · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:56-65,246-285,444-446; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:460-463,738; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Program.lean:66-90; formal/vendor/yul-semantics/YulSemantics/Determinism.lean:22,405; formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:731-732
- **Claim (reviewer):** Leads (b) and (c). The statements are true in substance at a real deployment. decodeCalldata's guards do mirror the ABI guard and the VkLoading shape checks; EntryFrame.fmp makes the MF-2 guard pass; and the prefix contains only local stepOp builtins, so every run factors through one (V1, st1). But prefix_run_clean needs facts no Valid clause supplies except through Valid.model, which is string-level and not dischargeable: (1) table FR_MODULUS equals p mod 2^256 (Frame.r and LocalsOk both constrain `r`); (2) table NUM_INSTANCE_CPTR equals plan.proofLayout.proofEnd, and the model's abi fields are 4, 0x40, 0x24; (3) model.embeddedVk = none, so `let vk` exists; (4) 0x80 ≤ ptrAddr g1IdentityMptr, so ZeroAbove gives the zero window; (5) TRANSCRIPT_MPTR < 2^256. Separately, runBody_iff_slices' forward direction in the permissive dialect with an arbitrary gas oracle cannot use ExecStmts.det, which needs every op deterministic. A fragment-restricted determinism lemma, or a direct inversion of the prefix, is needed and is missing from the 30-60 line estimate.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Blocks.lean:64 `r : ∃ rv, R.consts.lookup "FR_MODULUS" = some rv ∧ VEnv.get V "r" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 rv)`. Inv.lean:460-463 LocalsOk: `some w => w == BitVec.ofNat 256 p`. Program.lean:72-73 `Expr.builtin Op.sub [Expr.var "NUM_INSTANCE_CPTR", lit m.abiSelectorBytes]`. Calldata.lean:337-338 `(cdWord cd 4).toNat == 0x40 && (cdWord cd 0x24).toNat == numInstCptr - 4`. Determinism.lean:405 `theorem ExecStmts.det (hdet : ∀ op, D.Deterministic op)`. Bridge.lean:731-732 permissive admits any failed response, so it is not Functional. Blocks.lean:446 "No determinism hypothesis is used anywhere in this file."
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** As stated, with one precision: the five facts are consequences of Valid.model in principle, so prefix_run_clean is true at a Valid render but not provable without reasoning through renderConstants and the string parser on a symbolic model, the same situation that forced D-P50's pins. I verified items 1, 2 and 5 against the definitions; items 3 and 4 are plausible and unverified. The forward direction of runBody_iff_slices needs prefix determinism in a dialect where the vendored determinism theorems do not apply, and no fragment-level lemma exists in LoweringV2.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Blocks.lean:64 Frame.r `∃ rv, R.consts.lookup "FR_MODULUS" = some rv ∧ VEnv.get V "r" = some (BitVec.ofNat 256 rv)`; Inv.lean:460-463 LocalsOk `some w => w == BitVec.ofNat 256 p`; the only link is EnvAgrees.modulus `p = frModulusConstant` (Inv.lean:537), which does not mention the table. Program.lean:66-76 abiGuardBlock reads `m.abiSelectorBytes`, `m.abiProofHeadOffset`, `m.abiInstancesHeadCptr` and `Expr.var "NUM_INSTANCE_CPTR"`; Calldata.lean:337-338 `(cdWord cd 4).toNat == 0x40 && (cdWord cd 0x24).toNat == numInstCptr - 4`. Program.lean:80-82 MF-2 guard `gt(mload(0x40), TRANSCRIPT_MPTR)` versus Statements.lean:245 `(loadWord st.memory 0x40).toNat ≤ t` with t an unbounded Nat. Program.lean:58-60 constTableOf parses `renderConstants ... splitOn "\n" ... filterMap Inline.solConstantLine?`. Determinism.lean:405 `theorem ExecStmts.det (hdet : ∀ op, D.Deterministic op)`; Bridge.lean:731-732 permissive admits any failed response; Blocks.lean:446 "No determinism hypothesis is used anywhere in this file." QUEUE.md:385 estimates runBody_iff_slices at "30-60 lines ... No semantic content"; runBody_iff_slices (Blocks.lean:276-285) is stated for a given (V₁, st₁), so the forward direction must identify the run's own prefix state with it.
- **Suggested fix:** Add a prefix-pins bundle (items 1 to 5), decided at the deployment as for D-P50. Prove a syntactic-fragment determinism lemma for call-free and gas-free code, or invert the seven prefix statements directly.

### D-10 — Soundness half of BlockContractsAt has no codec-law hypothesis, but Inv compares memory with toEip of decoded points

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → medium · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:116-127; formal/Midfall/Assumptions/Codec.lean:75-82; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Calldata.lean:304-312; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:401-402,770-774; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:302-311
- **Claim (reviewer):** BlockContractsAt gates only progress on G1CodecLaws, and headline conjuncts 1 and 3 carry no codec hypothesis. After the parser block, memory holds the calldata bytes b, while Inv .transcript demands `bytesAt = E.g1.toEip Q` for Q read back by Lean from `ofEip? b` re-serialised with toCompressed. That needs eipRoundtrip and compressedRoundtrip, which are laws, not fields of G1Codec, and are not in Valid. Eip2537.Holds.g1Decodes gives the roundtrip only for precompile-valid frames, and the parser does not validate on-curve. So BlockSound .transcriptParser, and hence blockContracts_of_valid's soundness half, is unprovable or false for a codec whose ofEip? accepts some invalid frame or whose toCompressed is not injective.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Blocks.lean:125-127 `(∀ g : ExternalGas, BlockSound R q Snap H vkAddr (permissive (callsOf product)) g cd) ∧ (G1CodecLaws R.E.g1 → ChallengesGood R.vk Snap → DualMSM.check ... → BlockProgress ...)`. Codec.lean:75-77 `structure G1CodecLaws ... eipRoundtrip : c.eipRoundtrip`. Calldata.lean:309-310 `let b ← bytesTake cd cur g1EipWidth; let Q ← c.ofEip? b`. Inv.lean:401-402 `holdsG1 ... := bytesAt st a 128 == E.g1.toEip Q`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take a codec equal to the real one on valid frames, but with ofEip? b0 = some 1 for one range-canonical off-curve frame b0, and calldata carrying b0 as an advice commitment. Holds can still hold. The parser exits normally with success = 1 and memory = b0 ≠ toEip 1, so the state is neither Clean .transcript nor Poisoned.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** As stated, and the suggested fix is insufficient. The soundness half of BlockContractsAt and headline conjuncts 1 and 3 carry no codec hypothesis, while Inv .transcript compares memory (raw calldata bytes) with toEip of the point Lean reads back through ofCompressed? (toCompressed (ofEip? b)). Holds.g1Decodes covers only precompile-valid frames. Beyond the reviewer: G1CodecLaws itself has only the decoder-first compressed law, so it does not give ofCompressed? (toCompressed Q) = some Q nor injectivity of toCompressed. A codec satisfying every law in the bundle can still make Lean read a different point than the contract absorbs, so the gated progress half and the generic conjunct 2 also fail at such a codec. The fix needs an encoder-first compressed law, not just G1CodecLaws in Valid.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Blocks.lean:125-127: only the progress half is gated on `G1CodecLaws R.E.g1`. Statements.lean:302-304 and 309-311: conjuncts 1 and 3 have no codec premise. Calldata.lean:309-310 `let b ← bytesTake cd cur g1EipWidth; let Q ← c.ofEip? b`. Native.lean:170 `| Msg.g1 P => E.g1.toCompressed P`; Transcript.lean:69 `readRepr? := c.ofCompressed?`; Native.lean:277-281 prepareMsgs re-serialises and reruns the byte pipeline. Inv.lean:401-402 `holdsG1 ... := bytesAt st a 128 == E.g1.toEip Q`. Codec.lean (Common):110-111 eipRoundtrip `c.ofEip? b = some P → c.toEip P = b`; :117-118 compressedRoundtrip `c.ofCompressed? b = some P → c.toCompressed P = b` (decoder-first only). Assumptions/Codec.lean:75-90: the bundle adds ofEip_toEip, eipShape, toEip_one and widths; a repo-wide search found no law of the form ofCompressed? (toCompressed Q) = some Q. Eip2537.lean:162-164 g1Decodes is restricted to frames the validators accept. AssemblyHelpers.yul:80-84: the parser "does not run an independent curve/subgroup check". Witness for the extension: real EIP codec, toCompressed Q2 := enc(Q1), ofCompressed? enc(Q2) := none; every G1CodecLaws field and Holds still hold.
- **Suggested fix:** Add G1CodecLaws (at least eipRoundtrip and the compressed agreement) to Render.Valid; it is a theorem at the real codec (g1CodecZ_eipRoundtrip). Alternatively gate the soundness half on it and supply it per deployment.

### D-13 — prog_slices_decomp and runBody_iff_slices are stated for all renders without R.Valid; a constant named like a binder breaks them

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** low
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:220-225,276-285; formal/Midfall/Lowering/InlineConstants.lean:263-267,361-369; formal/Midfall/Generator/VerifierPartials.lean:2158-2160
- **Claim (reviewer):** Both statements quantify over an arbitrary Render with no Valid hypothesis. The inliner threads a shadow list along the top level, so in R.prog every slice is inlined under the shadow of `r`, `success`, `vk` and all helper parameters, while R.slice q is inlined with an empty shadow. The two agree only if no top-level binder is a constant name. renderConstants interpolates `qdirect.constants` lines taken from the model, so an arbitrary model can put, say, `r` into constTableOf. Then `r` is substituted inside the standalone slices but not in R.prog, and the decomposition and the iff fail. inline_append itself is fine, because it carries the side condition.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Blocks.lean:220-221 `theorem prog_slices_decomp (R : Render p G₁ G₂ Gₜ) (vkAddr qAddr : U256) (c : ExternalCalls) (g : ExternalGas) :` (no hR). VerifierPartials.lean:2158-2160 `(match ctx.quotientDirect with | some qdirect => (jinjaFor qdirect.constants (fun line loop => ...`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take R.model with quotientDirect = some { constants := ["uint256 internal constant r = 5;"], .. }. Then `(R.slice .quotientLin)` contains `lit 5` where `R.prog` keeps `var "r"`.
- **Lean check proposed (reviewer):**

```lean
Check whether partialCtxOfVerifier copies the model's quotientDirect into ctx.quotientDirect; if it does not, this finding is void.
```
- **Suggested fix:** Add `hR : R.Valid`, or a decidable `∀ x ∈ topBinders (prefix ++ helpers ++ mainLocals ++ slices), x ∉ R.consts.map Prod.fst` hypothesis, to both statements.

### D+1 — At the deployment instance LeanAccepts is identically false: the Lean verifier's G1 codec decodes no compressed point, and the VK and delta are placeholders (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** critical
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Instances/G1Agreement.lean:2002-2006; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:196-200,229-230; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonV2.lean:152-163; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Transcript.lean:65-69,145-154; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Native.lean:268-292; formal/docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:50-53,111; formal/docs/design.md:1820-1823
- **Claim:** poseidonRender.E is poseidonE, whose codec is g1CodecZ with `ofCompressed? := fun _ => none`. verifyMsgs re-serialises the decoded messages and reruns the byte-level pipeline, which reads every G1 point through ofCompressed?. So prepareMsgs fails at the first G1 read for every H and cd, and LeanAccepts poseidonRender H cd is uninhabited. Consequences for the per-deployment headline, whatever happens to Valid, the emitter or the block proofs: conjunct 2 (completeness) is vacuous; SoundSide always holds (the fault is a transcript error, not the logup panic); conjunct 1 therefore reduces to 'no permissive run ever returns', which is false for any working verifier contract and false today (D-11). poseidon_rejects cannot be closed at this environment even with a correct program. The proposed D-P45 non-vacuity gate (which adds LeanAccepts) cannot be discharged here either, contrary to its 'it will be proved, not evaluated' note. In the same environment the Lean VK's fixed and permutation commitments are all the identity, delta is 2, toCompressed is the x-coordinate only (not injective), and sG2 is g2 (Gap B). The Lean side of the deployment is therefore not the Rust verifier for the deployed key. Recorded only partially: the docs treat the placeholder decoder as a blocker for end_to_end and WP-I2, not as making the message-level LeanAccepts and the headline's soundness conjunct degenerate.
- **Evidence:** G1Agreement.lean:2005-2006 `toCompressed := fun Q => bytesNatBE (g1zPair Q).1 fpBytes` / `ofCompressed? := fun _ => none`. PoseidonEnv.lean:230 `{ g1 := g1CodecZ, field := poseidonFc, delta := 2, fewerPointSetsRuntime := false }`; :196 `Array.replicate poseidonVkData.numFixedColumns (1 : G₁z)`; :199-200 permutation commitments likewise; header :23-24 "The compressed decoder is a PLACEHOLDER THAT DECODES NOTHING". PoseidonV2.lean:153 `E := poseidonE`. Transcript.lean:69 `readRepr? := c.ofCompressed?`; :150-151 `match h.readRepr? repr with | none => .error (Fault.error (Error.transcript IoErrorKind.other))`. Native.lean:279-281 prepareMsgs runs `(prepare ...).run (Transcript.initWithHash H bytes)`; :288-292 verifyMsgs binds prepareMsgs first. Statements.lean:263-267 LeanAccepts requires `verifyMsgs ... = .ok ()`. DECISIONS-PENDING.md:50-52 "Discharging the gate at the deployment needs an accepting calldata ... so it will be proved, not evaluated"; :111 "Decide `g1CodecZ.ofCompressed?` (same discrete-log class, blocks `end_to_end`)". design.md:1820-1823 asks only whether end_to_end is dischargeable. Lean check: `example (b : Bytes) : poseidonRender.E.g1.ofCompressed? b = none := rfl`.
