### D — errors the skeptic found in the reviewer's report

- D-7 ends with 'Conclusions that hold only vacuously today are listed under D-12', but no D-12 exists in the findings handed over.
- D-4 overstates its scope: 'the only SupportedV2 scope' and 'any codegen-agreeing render' should read 'compact-VM renders'. SupportedV2.embeddedQuotient (Statements.lean:132) also admits direct-lowering renders, where QuotientHelpers.yul is not included (QuotientAndLinearization.yul:53-60) and the functions render in the helper region (quotient_direct.rs:60-62).
- D-4 states the stuck-call consequences (b) and (c) unconditionally. They hold only on runs that reach a call to q_pow5 / q_program_fail; q_program_fail is called only on failing paths (QuotientNumeratorBlock.yul:42-54 and the opcode defaults). Only the hoist inequality (a) is unconditional.
- D-1 says poseidon_rejects is false 'at any render that accepts at least one proof'. poseidon_rejects is about poseidonRender, where no calldata is Lean-accepted (g1CodecZ.ofCompressed? is `fun _ => none`), so the D-1 mechanism does not apply there; it is false for D-11's reason.
- D-2 and the summary miss that rejects_of_not_accepts also lacks R.Valid (Blocks.lean:356-361), which makes it false independently of the logup set.
- D-10's suggested fix (add G1CodecLaws, 'at least eipRoundtrip and the compressed agreement', to Valid) is insufficient: the bundle has only the decoder-first compressed law (Common/Codec.lean:117-118), which does not imply ofCompressed? (toCompressed Q) = some Q.
- D-11 calls poseidon_quotient_block 'plausibly true ... because the slice is the stub'. With the placeholder codec no snapshot can exist at poseidonRender, so BlockContractsAt is more likely vacuously true for every predicate there. The reviewer did not notice the placeholder codec at all, although it is the stronger reason the headline is false at this render.
- D-3's title 'no workable architecture' overstates: PLAN-V2.md:283 (WP-M0.2) plans staged rejection over snapshotOfTrace..snapshotOfPcs and the prefix records exist (Snapshot.lean:112-153, 363-370). The accurate claim is that nothing is stated yet and the current shapes obstruct it.
- Doc line references have drifted from the current tree: G.3 is at QUEUE.md:1103 (cited 1044); the 'pcs row REFUTABLE' note is at QUEUE.md:855-857 (cited 797-800). All Lean, Rust and template line references I checked were accurate.

### D — checked and found correct

- Composition plumbing: lowering_equivalence_of_blocks (formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:493-608) follows from its named lemmas; each branch and the eleven proved transport facts (:370-471) were read and are correct. Conjunct 2 uses both halves of StmtsTotal.
- Checkpoint chain: Partial.pre and Checkpoint.next line up (formal/Midfall/Lowering/Checkpoint.lean:87-105) with exitOk (Blocks.lean:82-87). Clean at checkpoint k produced by block k is exactly the pre of block k+1; finalPairing exits at .pairing = traceReturn.pre.
- slices_seq_sound and slices_seq_progress are true and derivable from seven BlockSound/BlockProgress after the D-P49 gate, using StmtsContractAll.append with mid = Clean k ∨ Poisoned (Blocks.lean:92-101, 307-332; formal/Midfall/Yul/Contracts.lean:156-170).
- Contract combinators (question 5): StmtsContractAll, cons_split, append and Step.stmts_append_inv are sound, including a halting or non-normal first block (Contracts.lean:102-170). StmtsTotal = existential ∧ universal is the right total-correctness notion (Blocks.lean:49-52; vendor Contract.lean:94-99). Posts constrain the outcome explicitly, so leave/break/continue are excluded by obligation.
- inline_append is true as stated: the shadow list only matters for names that are constant keys, and fuel is per nesting level (formal/Midfall/Lowering/InlineConstants.lean:287-374; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/ProgramLemmas.lean:27-31).
- hoist_prog_addr_indep and noWriters_prog are true: the addresses occur only as let-literals (Program.lean:105-108), and hoist and the writer scan ignore literal values.
- fp_pairing_algebra is true: Holds.pairing2 at (right, nG2⁻¹), (left, sG2⁻¹) is the negation of DualMSM.check's sum (formal/Midfall/Assumptions/Eip2537.lean:137-140; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/Msm.lean:222-233; FinalPairing.lean:148-151, 278-281).
- fp_stmt_steps and vk_poison_walk are true: from success = 0 the first iszero(success) guard reverts, and the and-chains keep 0 (GenAst.lean:651-652, 941-954; VkLoading.lean:631-649).
- TraceReturn rows are meaningful and sorry-free: tr_blockSound holds for every R, tr_blockProgress under trRetMptr. The emitted slice equals the production template (GenAst.lean:786-790 vs templates/partials/verifier/TraceReturn.yul:70-72); the walks invert real Step derivations, and the existence half constructs a run (formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks/TraceReturn.lean:972-1051).
- Lead (b): the prefix contains only local stepOp builtins (Program.lean:66-90), which are function graphs in every Bridge dialect (formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:174-179). Factoring every RunBody through one (V1, st1) is semantically sound.
- Lead (c): decodeCalldata's guards mirror the ABI guard and VkLoading's three shape checks (formal/Midfall/Lowering/Calldata.lean:334-345 vs Program.lean:66-76 and GenAst.lean:958-967), for cd.size < 2^256. EntryFrame.fmp implies the MF-2 guard passes for TRANSCRIPT_MPTR < 2^256.
- Lead (g): for a snapshot with DualMSM.check = false, BlockSound .finalPairing cannot take the clean arm (Inv.lean:816-819, 881-890) and BlockSound .traceReturn conjunct 2 forces the revert. The contracts are adequate there; only the composition fails to reach them (D-3).
- Lead (f), variable environment: letVal prepends, so a redeclaration shadows rather than sticking (vendor BigStep.lean:230-233). Leaked top-level locals such as lagrange_precompile_failed are harmless, and Clean/Frame read only r, vk and success.
- Poisoned without Inv is the right design: real code keeps a poisoned success at 0 (`if success { success := staticcall..}` in kzg/mod.rs:1820-1832, 1873-1909; batch_invert leaves on success = 0 and Lagrange then reverts). Precompile calls forward constant gas, and the honest relation ignores gas (formal/Midfall/Yul/Externals.lean:799-805).
- VK guard is passable from DeploymentV2: extcodehash reads projectedCodeHash = keccakOf(extCodeOf) for non-empty code (vendor EVMExec.lean:307-310, 859; Statements.lean:224-231).
- Headline conjunct 3 is true on the logup bad set (it does not mention Lean); only its proof route is wrong (D-2).
- snapshotOf mirrors prepare's phase order (formal/Midfall/Lowering/Snapshot.lean:267-355 vs formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Verifier.lean:298, 1016-1086 and Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:330-340), so snapshotOf_prepareMsgs is plausibly true. Reasoned only, not checked in detail.

### D — not covered

- No Lean, lake or cargo was run. Every verdict is from reading source, and the needs_lean_check snippets are untested.
- 'False as stated' verdicts for lemmas with hR : R.Valid are relative to a hypothetical Valid render; none exists in the tree (D-12), so no unconditional refutation can be exhibited.
- formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAst.lean was read only for the helpers, FinalPairing, Lagrange, TraceReturn, QuotientLin and VkLoading. The transcript-parser emitter, GenAstPcs.lean, GenAstQuotient.lean and most of GenAstDispatch.lean were not read.
- formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean lines 1075-1592 (noWriters scan, Run_worldEq_of_noWriters, the remaining view lemmas) and ImportSolcIR/ShellModel were not read.
- Inv satisfiability for a real honest run at the deployed layout was not checked: whether every Inv-pinned region is permanent through the PCS and pairing phases, and Instances/InvFixture.lean.
- snapshotOf_prepareMsgs and end_to_end were only reasoned about at a high level. Native.prepareWith, the calldata round-trip sorries (Calldata.lean:731-795, 1288) and verifyNative_iff_msgs_codec were not examined.
- Instances/PoseidonValid.lean, PoseidonPins.lean, PoseidonEnv.lean and PoseidonInputs.lean were not read. D-11 relies on the M0.1/M.1 measurements quoted in QUEUE.md and on the emitter definitions.
- Whether the generator's coverage validator guarantees that every proof G1 slot reaches a subgroup-checking precompile (needed by Rejects; points_subgroup_checked_generator) was not examined.
- The fidelity of emitted slices to the templates beyond the pieces quoted, and the multi-pair G1MSM bridge from Holds.g1Msm1/g1Add to the PCS's fused MSM, were not examined.


## E — fidelity of the Lean verifier to Rust

**Reviewer summary.** Read line by line, the Lean port (Halo2/Exec/*) follows the Rust verifier closely. The transcript, the read/squeeze schedule, the logup, permutation and trash identities, linearization, the multi-open with truncation, the DualMSM check and the panic sites are transcribed faithfully. `logup.rs:460` is accurate. `Features.solidity` describes real Rust code. `verifyNative_iff_msgs` has no sorry in its dependencies (by reading; nothing was built or run).

The right-hand side of the headline is still not the Rust verifier where it matters:

1. **Commitment grouping (R-7).** Lean groups multi-open queries by commitment value; Rust groups by pointer. The deployed poseidon key has five queried fixed commitments plus the committed instance equal to the identity. Lean therefore returns `Error.opening` on every proof for that key, including the Rust-accepted fixture. The recorded "unreachable for honest proofs" analysis is refuted, and no plan item covers it.
2. **Placeholder codec.** At `poseidonRender`, `ofCompressed?` is `fun _ => none`, so `LeanAccepts` is uninhabited. `poseidon_rejects` is then false as stated, the block contracts are vacuous, and the proposed D-P45 gate is unsatisfiable.
3. **Permutation delta.** `E.delta` is unpinned and `poseidonE.delta` is 2, not `Fq::DELTA`. The recorded reason for not pinning it misreads `Fq` as the base field.
4. **`WellFormed.permutationCommitmentsMatch`** is false for real keys (8 sigma commitments against 3 chunks).
5. **Rotation equality.** `Rotation.cur ≠ Rotation.value 0` makes an imported key panic in the permutation identity.
6. **Conformance.** The replay checks transcript primitives along the exporter's schedule only. No Rust-generated proof has been accepted by Lean `verifyNative`.

The working tree advanced from 6481731 to f2fe99b during the audit. The in-scope Exec, LoweringV2 and Rust files are unchanged; line references for the changed instance and doc files are at 6481731.

Review the accuracy and completeness of these findings before relying on them.

### E-1 — Lean groups multi-open queries by commitment value, Rust by allocation: the Lean verifier rejects every proof for the deployed poseidon key

- **Severity (reviewer):** critical · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → critical · **Kind:** fidelity-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (formal/docs/REVIEW.md R-7 (:1720-1737) and R-32 (:876-881) record the pointer-vs-value divergence, but conclude it is unreachable for honest proofs and leave it open. Nothing in PLAN-V2, QUEUE, STATUS or DECISIONS-PENDING.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:42-48,257-259; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/Utils.lean:142-149; proofs/src/poly/query.rs:117-125; proofs/src/poly/kzg/utils.rs:54-58; formal/fixtures/conformance/poseidon/vk.json:70-78; formal/fixtures/conformance/poseidon/trace.json:38,83,108,248,1617; formal/docs/REVIEW.md:878-880
- **Claim (reviewer):** Rust compares OnePiece commitments with `std::ptr::eq`, so equal-valued commitments in different slots are distinct entries. Lean's `kzgCommitBeq` compares by value, so they merge, and a second query at the same point returns DuplicatedQuery (`Error.opening`). The deployed poseidon key has five queried, non-selector fixed columns (8, 10, 11, 12, 16) whose commitment is the identity, and the committed instance is also the identity; all are opened at x. So `verifyNative` / `verifyMsgs` return `.error (Fault.error Error.opening)` for every proof at a faithful key, while Rust and the contract accept. The recorded R-7 conclusion that this is unreachable for honest proofs is wrong, and no V2 plan, queue or decision item addresses it.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** MultiOpen.lean:43 `| .onePiece x, .onePiece y => decideBool (P.decEqG₁ x y)`. Utils.lean:142-148 `match cms.findIdx? (fun cd => eqC cd.commitment q.commitment) with | some pos => ... if cd.pointIndices.toList.contains pidx then .error (Fault.error Error.opening)`. query.rs:125 `) => std::ptr::eq(self_com, other_com),`. vk.json:70,72,73,74,76,78 are six `"c000…00"` entries (fixed_commitments[8,10,11,12,14,16]); `fixed_queries` contains [8,0],[10,0],[11,0],[12,0],[16,0]; `selector_flags` marks only 13,14,15,17 simple. trace.json `dual_msm.right` has separate terms `instance_0` (:38), `fixed_8` (:83), `fixed_10` (:108), `fixed_11`, `fixed_12`, `fixed_16`, each with `point_compressed_hex` c000…; :248 `"pairing_check": true`; :1617 `"verdict": "accept"`. REVIEW.md:878-879 "under `Features.solidity` the divergence is unreachable for honest proofs because every commitment is read once from the transcript into its own slot".
- **Counterexample / witness:** Key: the deployed poseidon key. Committed instance: [[identity]], which `verifyNative` hard-codes as `[[1]]`. Proof: formal/fixtures/conformance/poseidon/proof.bin. Rust accepts it, and BASELINE.md:228 records Solidity ACCEPT for the same shape. In Lean, `collectQueries` emits (x, instance_ 0, 1) and later (x, fixed 8, 1); `pass1` finds the existing identity entry already containing x's index and returns `Error.opening`. Hence `snapshotOf` is never `.ok`, `LeanAccepts` is false for the accepting calldata, `SoundSide` holds (the error is not the logup panic), and headline conjunct 1 fails. The current placeholder key (all 27 commitments = 1) behaves the same way.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed; I could not break it. Rust compares OnePiece commitments by address, Lean's kzgCommitBeq by value, and constructIntermediateSets.pass1 returns Error.opening when an equal-valued commitment is queried twice at one point. The deployed poseidon key has six identity fixed commitments, five of them (columns 8, 10, 11, 12, 16) queried at rotation 0 and not simple selectors. verifyNative/prepareMsgs hard-code the committed instance as the identity [[1]], also opened at x. So at a faithful import of that key, every proof that reaches multi_prepare gets Fault.error Error.opening, LeanAccepts is uninhabited, and headline conjunct 1 is false at the Rust-accepted fixture. No Valid clause excludes equal or identity commitments. Three precisions: (1) In the tree as built at f2fe99b this point is never reached. E-2 (codec) fails at the first advice read, and E-6 (rotation panic in Permutation.expressions) runs before collectQueries. E-1 is therefore the third independent reason LeanAccepts is uninhabited at the deployment, and the only one that is a defect of the generic Exec verifier rather than of instance data. (2) The weaker form is not key-specific: any proof in which two one-piece commitments opened at a common point coincide (for example two equal advice commitments) is accepted by Rust and rejected by Lean. I did not exhibit such a proof. (3) The recorded 'unreachable for honest proofs' analysis is wrong because VK fixed commitments and the committed instance are not read from the transcript. No PLAN-V2, QUEUE, STATUS or DECISIONS-PENDING item mentions R-7 (grep).
- **Evidence (skeptic):** proofs/src/poly/query.rs:121-125 `(&CommitmentReference::OnePiece(self_com), &CommitmentReference::OnePiece(other_com)) => std::ptr::eq(self_com, other_com)`. proofs/src/poly/kzg/utils.rs:54-58 `position(|comm| comm.commitment == query.get_commitment())` then `return Err(Error::DuplicatedQuery)`. proofs/src/plonk/verifier.rs:616 `&committed_instances[column.index()]`, :640 `&vk.fixed_commitments[column.index()]` (distinct Vec slots, so distinct addresses). formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:43 `| .onePiece x, .onePiece y => decideBool (P.decEqG₁ x y)`; :257-259 passes kzgCommitBeq to constructIntermediateSets. Kzg/Utils.lean:142-148 `match cms.findIdx? (fun cd => eqC cd.commitment q.commitment) with | some pos => ... if cd.pointIndices.toList.contains pidx then .error (Fault.error Error.opening)`. The in-tree KAT Utils.lean:295-299 (cisDupKats_ok) already pins exactly this outcome for two equal-valued commitments at one point. Verifier.lean:773-779 (committed instance query at rotateOmega x rot), :792-799 (fixed queries, filtered by `!vk.cs.hasSimpleSelectorCol`), :1066 `| .error (Fault.error _) => throw (Fault.error Error.opening)`. Native.lean:229 and :280 `prepare ... [[1]] [[inst.toList]]`. Domain.lean:232-237: rotateOmega at rotation 0 is `value * powVartime omega 0`, so instance_0 and fixed_8 share one point. fixtures/conformance/poseidon/vk.json:70,72,73,74,76,78 are `c000...00`; fixed_queries contain [8,0],[10,0],[11,0],[12,0],[16,0]; selector_flags are simple only at 13,14,15,17; instance_queries [[0,0],[1,0]]; num_committed_instances 1. instances.json committed = c000...00. trace.json:38 `instance_0`, :83 `fixed_8`, :108 `fixed_10` are separate identity-point terms; :248 `"pairing_check": true`; :1617 `"verdict": "accept"`. LoweringV2/Statements.lean:124-125 vkPoints is the only Valid clause about VK commitments (readability only; G1Agreement.lean:2022 g1CodecZ_g1Valid covers the identity). docs/REVIEW.md:878-880 `under Features.solidity the divergence is unreachable for honest proofs because every commitment is read once from the transcript into its own slot`.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
Confirmatory only (cisDupKats_ok already pins the mechanism). Scratch file:
import Midfall.Halo2.Exec.Kzg.Utils
open Midfall Midfall.Halo2
instance : Fact (Nat.Prime 17) := ⟨by decide⟩
```

- **Suggested fix:** Model allocation identity: give `CommitmentReference.onePiece` a slot token (advice i / instance i / fixed i / permutation i / proof-read ordinal) and group on the token, as the second external audit proposed. ### E-2 — At poseidonRender the Lean verifier cannot read any G1 point, so LeanAccepts is uninhabited and poseidon_rejects is false as stated

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (The placeholder is recorded (design.md:1820, DECISIONS-PENDING.md:115, PoseidonEnv.lean header, PoseidonCoherence.lean check 3), but only as blocking `end_to_end` and `calldataOf`. Its effect on `LeanAccepts`, `Rejects`, the block contracts and the D-P45 gate is not.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Instances/G1Agreement.lean:2002-2006; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:379-380 (at 6481731; :229-230 at f2fe99b); formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Native.lean:269-292; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Transcript.lean:145-154; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Verifier.lean:155-156; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:263-267,298-311; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:116-139; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonV2.lean:207-221; formal/Midfall/Assumptions/Codec.lean:75-95; formal/docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:49-51,115
- **Claim (reviewer):** `verifyMsgs` re-serialises each G1 message with `E.g1.toCompressed` and re-reads it with `E.g1.ofCompressed?`. `poseidonE.g1 = g1CodecZ`, whose `ofCompressed?` is `fun _ => none`, and the key has 8 advice columns, so `prepareMsgs` fails at the first advice read for every input. Consequences at `poseidonRender`:
(a) `LeanAccepts poseidonRender H cd` is false for all H, cd.
(b) `SoundSide` holds for all cd, so `poseidon_rejects` asserts every permissive run reverts or halts for every calldata — false if the contract accepts anything.
(c) Headline conjunct 2, `poseidon_quotient_block` and `blockContracts_of_valid` are vacuous here, because `snapshotOf` is never `.ok`.
(d) The D-P45 gate (adding `LeanAccepts`) is unsatisfiable at this render, contrary to the recorded "it will be proved, not evaluated".
(e) `EndToEnd poseidonRender` is vacuously true (False ↔ False), not "blocked".
Generically, neither `Valid`, `Eip2537.Holds` nor `G1CodecLaws` requires `ofCompressed? (toCompressed P) = some P`, which `verifyMsgs` needs.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** G1Agreement.lean:2005-2006 `toCompressed := fun Q => bytesNatBE (g1zPair Q).1 fpBytes` / `ofCompressed? := fun _ => none`. PoseidonEnv.lean:380 `{ g1 := g1CodecZ, field := poseidonFc, delta := 2, fewerPointSetsRuntime := false }`. Native.lean:278-281 `let bytes := ms.foldl ... (msgWire E m)` then `(prepare ...).run (Transcript.initWithHash H bytes)`. Transcript.lean:150-151 `match h.readRepr? repr with | none => .error (Fault.error (Error.transcript IoErrorKind.other))`. Verifier.lean:156 `let reads ← tmReadN (hashableG1 g1) cols.length`. Blocks.lean:138-139 `SoundSide R H cd → ¬ LeanAccepts R H cd → RunBody ... → obs.isRevertOrHalt`. Blocks.lean:123 `snapshotOf R.E H R.vk inst ms none = .ok Snap →`. Codec.lean:75-95: `G1CodecLaws` has `compressedRoundtrip` (decoder-first) and `ofEip_toEip`, but no encoder-first compressed law. DECISIONS-PENDING.md:51 "so it will be proved, not evaluated"; :115 "blocks `end_to_end`".
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take cd = the repacked calldata of the Rust- and Solidity-accepted poseidon fixture proof, with honest precompiles satisfying `Holds`. The real contract returns ABI true. `LeanAccepts` is false: `Transcript.read (hashableG1 g1CodecZ)` is never `.ok`. `SoundSide` is true: the fault is `Error.transcript`, not `Fault.panic "…logup.rs:460"`. So `poseidon_rejects` would force that run to revert.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed, and the generic half is stronger than stated. (a) At poseidonRender, E.g1 = g1CodecZ with `ofCompressed? := fun _ => none`. Every G1 read goes through Transcript.read (hashableG1 g1), whose readRepr? is ofCompressed?. The key has 8 phase-0 advice columns, and f_com and pi are read unconditionally, so prepare, prepareMsgs, verifyMsgs, verifyNative and snapshotOf are never `.ok`. LeanAccepts poseidonRender H cd is therefore provably False for all H and cd. The tree records only that no accepting calldata has been exhibited, and frames check 3 as 'BLOCKED rather than FALSE'. (b) SoundSide holds for every cd, because the fault is Error.transcript, never the logup.rs:460 panic. poseidon_rejects therefore states that every permissive run of the poseidon program is a non-committing halt for every calldata. That is unprovable, and false as soon as one accepting run exists in the Lean EVM model. The existence of such a run is the project's own premise but is not exhibited in Lean, so the falsity is conditional. The same argument makes the current lowering_equivalence_poseidon false rather than vacuous: conjuncts 1 and 3 together force 'no run ever returns'. (c) Every BlockContractsAt / BlockRow at poseidonRender is vacuously true because snapshotOf is never .ok. poseidon_quotient_block could be closed by a trivial proof, so the cone-count metric can drop with no content. (d) The D-P45 gate with the added `LeanAccepts R H cd` conjunct is unsatisfiable at this render; it cannot be 'proved, not evaluated'. (e) calldataOf is never .ok (repackProof pushes f_com and pi through ofCompressed? unconditionally) and verifyNative is never .ok, so EndToEnd at this render is False ↔ False. (f) Generic: no clause of Valid, Eip2537.Holds or G1CodecLaws constrains ofCompressed? beyond the decoder-first compressedRoundtrip, which a none-decoder satisfies vacuously. For any Valid R, the render R1 that differs only by `ofCompressed? := fun _ => none` is still Valid, has the same program, DeploymentV2, EntryFrame and Holds, and has LeanAccepts identically False. So the sorried generic rejects_of_not_accepts and the planned generic `∀ R, R.Valid → LoweringEquivalence R` are false unless no Valid render's program ever returns.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Instances/G1Agreement.lean:2002-2006 `noncomputable def g1CodecZ ... toCompressed := fun Q => bytesNatBE (g1zPair Q).1 fpBytes / ofCompressed? := fun _ => none`. Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:229-230 (f2fe99b; :379-380 at 6481731) `poseidonE ... { g1 := g1CodecZ, field := poseidonFc, delta := 2, fewerPointSetsRuntime := false }`. Instances/PoseidonV2.lean:153 `E := poseidonE`, :169 `poseidonRender.E = poseidonE := rfl`. Instances/PoseidonCoherence.lean:432-433 `theorem check3_codec_decodes_no_compressed_point : ∀ b : Bytes, poseidonE.g1.ofCompressed? b = none := fun _ => rfl`; :450-452 `BLOCKED rather than FALSE`. Exec/Transcript.lean:65-69 `readRepr? := c.ofCompressed?`; :147-151 `match bytesTake ... | none => .error (... unexpectedEof) | some repr => match h.readRepr? repr with | none => .error (Fault.error (Error.transcript IoErrorKind.other))`. Exec/Verifier.lean:152-156 `let reads ← tmReadN (hashableG1 g1) cols.length`; Circuit.lean:1528-1530 phases = [0]; PoseidonEnv gate 4 pins numAdviceColumns 8. MultiOpen.lean:278 and :306 (f_com and pi reads, unconditional). Native.lean:278-283 (prepareMsgs re-serialises with msgWire = toCompressed, then runs prepare). Lowering/Snapshot.lean:179-185 and 349-355 (snapshotOf runs parseTrace with E.g1). LoweringV2/Statements.lean:263-267 (LeanAccepts), :271-276 (SoundSide), :302-311 (conjuncts). Blocks.lean:119-123 (BlockContractsAt is guarded by `snapshotOf ... = .ok Snap`), :134-139 (Rejects), :356-363 (rejects_of_not_accepts has neither R.Valid nor SoundSide), :618-620 (the planned generic theorem). Yul/Bridge.lean:552-553 `isRevertOrHalt := ∃ k d, halted = some (k, d) ∧ k.commits = false`. Generator/Calldata.lean:156-167 and :228, :234 (repackPushG1 for f_com and pi). Assumptions/Codec.lean:75-90: G1CodecLaws has compressedRoundtrip (decoder-first), ofEip_toEip and compressedWidth, and no `ofCompressed? (toCompressed Q) = some Q`. Assumptions/Eip2537.lean contains no occurrence of 'compressed'. docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:38-50 (D-P45 text adds `LeanAccepts R H cd ∧ G1CodecLaws R.E.g1`; `so it will be proved, not evaluated`), :111 `blocks end_to_end`. docs/design.md:1820-1823 records the placeholder only as a question about end_to_end. docs/REVIEW.md:3017-3019 records `no accepted calldata at any model in the tree`.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
No doubt remains on (a). Optional formalisation: `example (H : Hash) (cd : Bytes) : ¬ LeanAccepts poseidonRender H cd`, by unfolding verifyMsgs/prepareMsgs/prepare/parseTrace down to Transcript.read and rewriting with PoseidonCoherence.check3_codec_decodes_no_compressed_point. For (f), the Valid-independence example given under E-4 with `{ R with E := { R.E with g1 := { R.E.g1 with ofCompressed? := fun _ => none } } }` in place of the delta update.
```

- **Suggested fix:** Either define the message-level verifier over `Msg` values directly, with no `toCompressed`/`ofCompressed?` round trip, or add the encoder-first law `∀ Q, ofCompressed? (toCompressed Q) = some Q` to the hypotheses and install a real compressed codec at the deployment. Correct DECISIONS-PENDING #3 and #8: with the placeholder the D-P45 gate is refutable, not merely expensive.

### E-3 — Conformance replay never executes the Lean verifier; no Rust-generated proof has been accepted by verifyNative

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** tooling-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (The Replay.lean header states the replay is not `verifyNative`, and REVIEW.md R-56/R-57 cover fixture provenance. Not recorded: that the port's schedule and algebra are unexercised by any Rust artefact, that the docstring claim is wrong, and that step 2 would currently fail.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Conformance/Replay.lean:16-35,150-196,199-239; formal/Conformance.lean:63-93; formal/docs/conformance-schema.md:109-115; formal/Midfall/Instances/NativeE2E.lean:36-41; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonCoherence.lean:1-25
- **Claim (reviewer):** `Conformance.replay` folds over the exporter's `transcript_log` events and, per event, performs one Lean read, absorb or squeeze. The schedule comes from trace.json, not from the port. `parseTrace`, `readAlgebra`, `linearize`, `multiPrepare` and `DualMSM.check` are not imported or run. DualMSM terms are checked only for canonical encodings, not against Lean-computed terms. So the replay tests keccak, the codecs, challenge reduction and x3 truncation only. The Replay.lean docstring claim that R-30/R-32-class schedule bugs "are caught" does not hold for the port's own schedule. Acceptance step 2 of conformance-schema.md has never been met. The only `verifyNative = .ok` runs are hand-built Fr 17 exponent-model fixtures whose π was found by a 17-way search. E-1 shows that a faithful run of the real fixture would be rejected.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Replay.lean:228 `let st := b.trace.events.foldl (fun acc e => step b acc e) st0`. Replay.lean:16-24 "WHY NOT `verifyNative` ITSELF ... the full pipeline cannot be executed on real bytes yet". Replay.lean:31-35 "R-30 / R-32 (a read schedule paraphrased differently in two places) are caught because the runner's cursor advances by what IT decoded". Replay.lean:199-208: `checkTerm` checks scalar < R, label non-empty and point decodes. Imports at Replay.lean:37-40: Bundle, Exec.Arith, Exec.Transcript, Instances.Bls12_381 only. conformance-schema.md:113-114 "`verifyNative` accepts the poseidon proof and rejects each single-bit mutation". NativeE2E.lean:36-40 "exactly one of the seventeen candidates balances it".
- **Counterexample / witness:** Swap two reads of the same width in `Verifier.readAlgebra` (for example read the permutation common evaluations before the fixed evaluations). `lake exe midfall-conformance` still exits 0, because `readAlgebra` is never called.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed. Conformance.replay folds over the exporter's event list (b.trace.events). Per event it performs one Lean Transcript.read / common / squeeze, choosing the read width from the trace event (wire.size 48 or 32). The port's own schedule and algebra are never executed on a Rust artefact: Verifier.parseTrace, readAlgebra, linearize, collectQueries, multiPrepare and DualMSM.check are not imported. The absorb prefix is a second hand-written paraphrase of parse_trace (derivePrefix), which is the R-30 pattern the docstring says it avoids. MSM terms are checked only for canonical scalar, non-empty label and decodable point. The replay reads three fields of vk.json (transcript_repr, truncated_challenges, scalar_num_bits). PLAN-V2 WP-I1's 'validate [the VK term] with the conformance replay' therefore validates nothing about commitments, queries, the permutation or the domain. The only verifyNative = .ok runs are Fr 17 exponent-model fixtures, which have no queried non-simple fixed column, so they cannot expose E-1. Acceptance step 2 of conformance-schema.md is unmet, has no V2 QUEUE card, and would currently fail three ways (E-2, E-6, E-1). The reviewer's Replay.lean line numbers are slightly off; see reviewer_errors.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Midfall/Conformance/Replay.lean:38-41 imports only `Midfall.Conformance.Bundle`, `Midfall.Halo2.Exec.Arith`, `Midfall.Halo2.Exec.Transcript`, `Midfall.Instances.Bls12_381`. :18-29 `WHY NOT verifyNative ITSELF ... the full pipeline cannot be executed on real bytes yet`. :31-36 `R-30 / R-32 (a read schedule paraphrased differently in two places) are caught because the runner's cursor advances by what IT decoded`. :131-151 derivePrefix (vk_repr, committed instance, instance_len, values) written independently of Verifier.parseTrace. :177-200 `| Ev.read name wire absorbed => if wire.size == 48 then match st.t.read hG1 ... else if wire.size == 32 then match st.t.read hFr`. :208 uses `b.vk.truncatedChallenges` and `b.vk.scalarNumBits`. :218-224 checkTerm (`scalarOk`, label non-empty, `hG1.readRepr? t.point`). :232 `let st := b.trace.events.foldl (fun acc e => step b acc e) st0`. Conformance.lean:62-92 (the exit code depends on the replay report only). docs/conformance-schema.md:111-115 `2. verifyNative accepts the poseidon proof and rejects each single-bit mutation the mutation corpus produces`. docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:239-243 (WP-I1). `grep -rl g1AffineCodec Midfall` returns only Bls12_381.lean, Replay.lean and PoseidonCoherence.lean, and none of them calls Verifier.parseTrace, readAlgebra or prepare. Instances/NativeE2E.lean:24-41 (the fixture has one advice column, one fixed column that is a simple selector, and pi found by a 17-way search).
- **Suggested fix:** Add an executable replay that runs `Verifier.prepare` at a computable BLS12-381 codec with a pairing-free guard. Compare the port's own read log tag by tag with trace.json, compare the DualMSM (scalar, point, label) lists and all intermediate values in `solidity_trace` (137 entries are already exported), and add a mutation corpus.

### E-4 — E.delta is unconstrained by Valid/EnvAgrees and is 2 at the deployment; the recorded reason for not pinning it is wrong

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** unsound-or-unrealistic-assumption · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (Inv.lean:473-490 and docs/plans/P-DELTA-GAP.md record that the clause is dropped and that fixtures use 2. The base-field claim is not recorded as wrong; no QUEUE or DECISIONS-PENDING item.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:379-380 (6481731); formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:473-490,533-579; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Permutation.lean:407-412; proofs/src/plonk/permutation.rs:262,276; curves/src/bls12_381/fq.rs:22-23,490-503; formal/Midfall/Generator/VerifierPartials.lean:1108; formal/docs/plans/P-DELTA-GAP.md
- **Claim (reviewer):** The Rust permutation identity uses `F::DELTA` for the BLS12-381 scalar field, 7^(2^32) mod r = 0x08634d0a…89d7. The Lean verifier takes δ from `VerifierEnv.delta`, which no clause of `EnvAgrees`, `SupportedV2` or `Valid` constrains, and `poseidonE.delta := 2`. At f2fe99b `Valid.slots` is reported PROVED at this render with δ = 2. Once the key carries its 8 permutation columns (chunks of 3), Lean's grand-product term differs from Rust's and the contract's for every column after the first, so the Lean verifier rejects honest proofs. The recorded justification for leaving the clause out — that the constant is a base-field DELTA so the cast would cross fields — is false: `midnight_curves::Fq` is the scalar field.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** PoseidonEnv.lean:380 `delta := 2`. Inv.lean:473 "delta : E.delta = (Nat.cast blsDeltaLiteral : Fr p). STILL DROPPED"; Inv.lean:484-486 "the constant is a BASE-field DELTA (Fq::DELTA) while E.delta : Fr p, so the sketch's Nat.cast into Fr crosses fields". fq.rs:22 "Represents an element of the scalar field Fq of the BLS12-381 elliptic curve construction"; fq.rs:502-503 MODULUS "0x73eda753299d7d483339d80809a1d80553bda402fffe5bfeffffffff00000001". permutation.rs:262 `* &(<F as PrimeField>::DELTA .pow_vartime([(chunk_index * chunk_len) as u64]))`, :276 `current_delta *= &F::DELTA;`. Permutation.lean:408 `(beta * x) * powVartime delta (chunkIndex * chunkLen)`, :412 `currentDelta := currentDelta * delta`. VerifierPartials.lean:1108 `frDelta := "0x8634d0aa021aaf843cab354fabb0062f6502437c6a09c006c083479590189d7"`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take R' = R with `E.delta := 2`, for any Valid R whose permutation argument has at least 2 columns. `Valid R'` still holds, since no clause reads `E.delta`, and the program is identical. For an honest proof the contract accepts, while Lean's `right` product uses βx·2 instead of βx·DELTA for the second column, so the linearization check fails and `LeanAccepts R'` is false. `poseidonRender` is such an R'.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed. The Rust permutation identity uses F::DELTA for midnight_curves::Fq, which is the BLS12-381 scalar field. Its value is 7^(2^32) mod r = 0x08634d0aa021aaf843cab354fabb0062f6502437c6a09c006c083479590189d7; I recomputed this independently and decoded the Montgomery limbs at fq.rs:492-499 to the same number. The Lean verifier threads VerifierEnv.delta into Permutation.expressions. No clause of EnvAgrees, SupportedV2 or Valid reads E.delta, and poseidonE.delta := 2. Valid.slots is reported PROVED at f2fe99b with that value. With the 8 permutation columns now imported (chunk_len 3), every right-product factor except column 0 of chunk 0 uses a different delta power than Rust and the contract. Lean therefore rejects honest proofs except with negligible probability, once E-2 and E-6 no longer pre-empt it. The recorded reason for not pinning it ('the constant is a BASE-field DELTA (Fq::DELTA) ... crosses fields') is false. Generic form: for any Valid R, changing only E.delta keeps Valid and the program, so the sorried deployment obligations (poseidon_rejects, poseidon_quotient_block) and the planned generic theorem cannot hold for both values. There is no QUEUE, STATUS or DECISIONS-PENDING item; docs/plans/P-DELTA-GAP.md records the gap but was never executed.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** curves/src/bls12_381/fq.rs:22-23 `Represents an element of the scalar field Fq of the BLS12-381 elliptic curve construction`; :492-499 `const DELTA: Fq = Fq(blst_fr { l: [0x70e3_10d3_d146_f96a, ...] })`; :502-503 MODULUS `0x73eda753299d7d483339d80809a1d80553bda402fffe5bfeffffffff00000001`. Python: pow(7, 2**32, r) = 0x8634d0aa021aaf843cab354fabb0062f6502437c6a09c006c083479590189d7, equal to limbs * 2^-256 mod r. proofs/src/plonk/permutation.rs:261-263 `let mut current_delta = (beta * &x) * &(<F as PrimeField>::DELTA.pow_vartime([(chunk_index * chunk_len) as u64]));`, :276 `current_delta *= &F::DELTA;`. formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Permutation.lean:408 `let mut currentDelta := (beta * x) * powVartime delta (chunkIndex * chunkLen)`, :412 `currentDelta := currentDelta * delta`. Exec/Native.lean:182 `Verifier.prepare ... E.g1 E.delta F`. Lowering/Inv.lean:473 `delta : E.delta = (Nat.cast blsDeltaLiteral : Fr p). STILL DROPPED`; :485-487 `the constant is a BASE-field DELTA (Fq::DELTA) while E.delta : Fr p, so the sketch's Nat.cast into Fr crosses fields`; :533-579 EnvAgrees has ten fields (modulus, truncWidth, domainN, baryWeight, omegaInvMul, omegaOrder, negLagranges, nG2, sG2, vkDigestWord), and the only one reading E is truncWidth (E.field.numBits). `grep E.delta Midfall/LoweringV2 Midfall/Lowering Midfall/Assumptions` finds only Inv.lean comments, LogupNoPanic (Inv.lean:709) and Snapshot.lean:309. Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:230 `delta := 2`. Instances/PoseidonValid.lean:35-37 `8 slots PROVED FLIPPED ... All ten EnvAgrees clauses now hold`. Generator/VerifierPartials.lean:1108 `frDelta := "0x8634d0aa021aaf843cab354fabb0062f6502437c6a09c006c083479590189d7"`. cs.json permutation_columns has 8 entries; vk.json cs_degree 5.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

### E-5 — VerifyingKey.WellFormed.permutationCommitmentsMatch compares per-column sigma commitments with the per-chunk product count, so Valid is unsatisfiable for real keys

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (At 6481731, PoseidonValid.lean:41 attributes the failure to missing columns ("8 against 0"). At f2fe99b (landed during this audit) PoseidonEnv.lean:485-487, PoseidonValid.lean:38-43 and STATUS.md:279 identify it as a clause defect. No repair is planned yet.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/VerifyingKey.lean:99-102,114-116; proofs/src/plonk/permutation.rs:123; proofs/src/plonk/permutation/verifier.rs:41-47,74-78; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:128; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Spec/Degrees.lean:99-103
- **Claim (reviewer):** Rust's `permutation::VerifyingKey.commitments` holds one sigma commitment per permutation column. The per-chunk objects are the proof's product commitments. `WellFormed` requires `commitments.size = divCeil columns.size (csDegree - 2)`, which is false whenever columns ≠ chunks; for poseidon it is 8 against 3. `SupportedV2.wf` puts `WellFormed` inside `Valid`, so `poseidon_valid` is unprovable with a faithful key, and the frozen v1 `verifyMsgs_iff_spec` has an unsatisfiable hypothesis for such keys.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** VerifyingKey.lean:114-116 `permutationCommitmentsMatch : vk.permutation.commitments.size = divCeil vk.cs.permutation.columns.size (vk.csDegree - 2)`. Its docstring at :99-102: "read_product_commitments reads one commitment per CHUNK of permutation columns, and Committed::evaluate then indexes the vector by chunk" — this describes the proof's product commitments, not the key's. permutation.rs:123 `let commitments = (0..argument.columns.len())`. trace.json has 8 `proof_permutation_common_eval` reads and labels `vk_perm_0..7`, against 3 `proof_permutation_product_commitment`. Statements.lean:128 `wf : R.vk.WellFormed Features.solidity`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** The deployed poseidon key has 8 permutation columns, cs_degree 5 and 8 sigma commitments: 8 ≠ divCeil 8 3 = 3.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed, with a narrower scope and an update on recording. VerifyingKey.WellFormed.permutationCommitmentsMatch equates the VK's sigma-commitment count (one per permutation column in Rust) with the per-chunk count divCeil columns (cs_degree - 2), which is the number of proof-side product commitments. The clause is false exactly when there are at least 2 permutation columns and cs_degree >= 4. It holds when cs_degree = 3 or there is at most one column, so 'unsatisfiable for real keys' over-generalises. For the deployed key it is 8 against 3. Conversely, any key that satisfies the clause in that regime is not a Rust key, because Lean would then read 3 common permutation evaluations where Rust reads 8. SupportedV2.wf puts the clause inside the frozen Valid, and the frozen v1 verifyMsgs_iff_spec takes WellFormed as its only hypothesis. At f2fe99b the project records this itself as a v1 clause defect; at 6481731 it was attributed to missing data. No repair is scheduled: nothing in QUEUE or DECISIONS-PENDING, and STATUS.md:66 still carries the stale 'D.2 gap A' attribution. The repair edits Exec/VerifyingKey.lean, which is outside Frozen.sha256 (that file hashes only LoweringV2/Program.lean, Shared.lean and Statements.lean). It therefore changes the meaning of the frozen Valid without changing any hashed text, and should be recorded as a statement repair.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/VerifyingKey.lean:114-116 `permutationCommitmentsMatch : vk.permutation.commitments.size = divCeil vk.cs.permutation.columns.size (vk.csDegree - 2)`; docstring :99-102 `read_product_commitments reads one commitment per CHUNK of permutation columns`. The port's own Exec/Permutation.lean:125-131 says the opposite of that clause: `the sigma commitments of one permutation argument - one per *column*, not per set`; :207-210 VerifyingKey.evaluate reads `vk.commitments.size` evaluations. proofs/src/plonk/permutation.rs:123-125 `let commitments = (0..argument.columns.len()).map(|_| CS::Commitment::read(reader, format))`; permutation/keygen.rs:244 `Vec::with_capacity(p.columns.len())`; permutation/verifier.rs:74-78 (one read per commitment). trace.json tag counts: 8 `proof_permutation_common_eval`, 3 `proof_permutation_product_commitment`. vk.json: 8 permutation commitments, cs_degree 5. LoweringV2/Statements.lean:128 `wf : R.vk.WellFormed Features.solidity`. Midfall/Statements.lean:93 `theorem verifyMsgs_iff_spec (hwf : vk.WellFormed F)`. Recorded at f2fe99b: Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:485-487 `permutationCommitmentsMatch STILL FAILS, 8 sigma commitments against divCeil 8 3 = 3 z-chunks. That second half is a v1 clause defect, not a data gap`; PoseidonValid.lean:38-43; docs/STATUS.md:279 against the stale :66 `clause 9 is D.2 gap A (no gates in vk.json)`. Midfall/LoweringV2/Frozen.sha256 lists three files.
- **Suggested fix:** State `vk.permutation.commitments.size = vk.cs.permutation.columns.size`. Keep the `divCeil` form only for the proof's product commitments (Degrees.numPermSets). Record the change as a statement repair with a both-halves witness.

### E-6 — Rotation equality is structural in Lean: an imported key (all rotations as Rotation.value) panics in the permutation identity where Rust succeeds

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** fidelity-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (The hazard is noted at Circuit.lean:120-129, with the claim that it cannot bite the verifier. Not recorded: the deployment importer triggers it.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Circuit.lean:120-132,1292-1296,1892; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Permutation.lean:316-323; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:297-311 (6481731); formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonFixture.lean:144-158,184-189,394-400 (f2fe99b)
- **Claim (reviewer):** `Permutation.columnEval` looks up `(col, Rotation.cur)` with the derived `BEq`, and `Rotation.cur == Rotation.value 0` is false. In Rust `Rotation::cur()` is `Rotation(0)`. The deployment importer deliberately encodes every rotation as `Rotation.value r`. For any imported key with a non-empty permutation argument — which is the case at f2fe99b, with 8 columns from cs.json — `Permutation.expressions` returns `Fault.panic "proofs/src/plonk/circuit.rs:2074"` (or :2084 / :2094) for every proof. At 6481731 this was masked only because `permutation.columns` was empty.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Circuit.lean:130-132 `inductive Rotation where | cur | next | prev | value (v : Int) deriving BEq`. Circuit.lean:1892 KAT `(Rotation.cur == Rotation.value 0) == false`. Circuit.lean:125-129 "it cannot bite the verifier itself ... but it can bite a hand-written `ConstraintSystem`, which is exactly what the conformance exporter will feed us". Permutation.lean:319 `let idx ← cs.getAnyQueryIndex col Rotation.cur`. Circuit.lean:1294 `cs.adviceQueries.toList.findIdx? (fun q => q == (column, at'))`. PoseidonEnv.lean:300 (6481731) `def poseidonRotation (r : Int) : Rotation := Rotation.value r`, with the comment "it becomes Rotation.value and never Rotation.cur".
- **Counterexample / witness:** At f2fe99b: `poseidonCs.getAnyQueryIndex (advice column 0) Rotation.cur` returns `.error (Fault.panic "proofs/src/plonk/circuit.rs:2074")`, although `adviceQueries` contains `(advice 0, Rotation.value 0)`. Rust's `get_any_query_index(col, Rotation::cur())` returns index 0.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed, with the severity raised to match E-4, which has the same impact class. Rust's Rotation::cur() is Rotation(0). Lean's Rotation is a four-constructor inductive with derived structural BEq, and the tree's own KAT pins `(Rotation.cur == Rotation.value 0) == false`. Permutation.columnEval looks up `(col, Rotation.cur)` by that BEq. The deployment importer builds every query rotation as Rotation.value r (poseidonRotation, jRotationOf). At f2fe99b poseidonCs.permutation.columns has 8 columns: fixed 9, advice 0-4, instance 0-1. For every proof that reaches the permutation identity, Permutation.expressions returns Fault.panic at the first column. That is fixed 9, so the site is circuit.rs:2084; advice columns would give :2074 and instance columns :2094. Rust succeeds there. This happens inside partiallyEvaluateIdentities, before collectQueries, so it pre-empts E-1. The panic string is not logup.rs:460, so SoundSide does not exclude it. No Valid clause requires canonical rotations: noRotInst is stated through `.int`, and circuitMeta is PROVED at this cs. LeanAccepts poseidonRender is therefore uninhabited for a second, independent reason, and poseidon_rejects would remain false after the codec is repaired. The in-source claim 'it cannot bite the verifier itself' is refuted by the importer, which that same comment anticipated. At 6481731 this was masked because permutation.columns was the default empty array.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** proofs/src/poly/mod.rs:509-515 `pub struct Rotation(pub i32); ... pub fn cur() -> Rotation { Rotation(0) }`; proofs/src/plonk/permutation.rs:246 `advice_evals[vk.cs.get_any_query_index(column, Rotation::cur())]`. formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Circuit.lean:130-132 `inductive Rotation where | cur | next | prev | value (v : Int) deriving BEq`; :125-129 `it cannot bite the verifier itself ... but it can bite a hand-written ConstraintSystem, which is exactly what the conformance exporter will feed us`; :1892 `(Rotation.cur == Rotation.value 0) == false`; :1300-1304 `match cs.fixedQueries.toList.findIdx? (fun q => q == (column, at')) with | some i => .ok i | none => .error (Fault.panic "proofs/src/plonk/circuit.rs:2084")`; :1319-1324 getAnyQueryIndex. Exec/Permutation.lean:319 `let idx ← cs.getAnyQueryIndex col Rotation.cur`; :403-405 (called for each zipped column of each chunk). Exec/Verifier.lean:318-319 (Permutation.expressions inside partiallyEvaluateIdentities), :965-984 (linearize runs it before collectQueries). Instances/PoseidonFixture.lean:147 `def poseidonRotation (r : Int) : Rotation := Rotation.value r` with the comment `it becomes Rotation.value and never Rotation.cur`; :156-158 poseidonFixedQueries; :187-189 jRotationOf; :398 `permutation := { columns := poseidonCsData.permutationColumns }`. cs.json permutation_columns = [fixed 9, advice 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, instance 0, 1]; vk.json fixed_queries[0] = [9, 0]. LoweringV2/Statements.lean:121 `noRotInst : ∀ q ∈ R.vk.cs.instanceQueries.toList, q.2.int = 0`. Exec/Domain.lean:90-94 (Rotation.int maps cur to 0 and value v to v). `git show 6481731:formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean` :320-333: poseidonCs had no permutation field.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
import Midfall.Instances.PoseidonFixture
open Midfall Midfall.Halo2 Midfall.Instances.PoseidonFixture in
#eval poseidonCs.permutation.columns.toList.map fun c => match poseidonCs.getAnyQueryIndex c Rotation.cur with | .ok i => s!"ok {i}" | .error (Fault.panic s) => s | .error _ => "error"
-- expect ["proofs/src/plonk/circuit.rs:2084", five times "...:2074", two times "...:2094"]
-- control: the same with (Rotation.value 0) in place of Rotation.cur; expect eight "ok _"
```
- **Suggested fix:** Make `Rotation` equality semantic: define `BEq` by `Rotation.int`, or collapse the type to a structure over `Int`. Add a KAT that runs `Permutation.expressions` on the imported key.

### E-7 — The assumed pairing's generators g₁/g₂ are opaque, so pinning the Lean verifier's SRS bases to the deployed bytes is not derivable from the five axioms

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → medium · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (DECISIONS-PENDING.md #8 (Gap B) records that `poseidonSG2` is g₂ rather than s·G₂. It does not record that `P.g₁`/`P.g₂` themselves are unconstrained axiom fields.) · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Assumptions/CurvePairing.lean:69-103; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:310-312; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:573-576; formal/docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:56-70,102-115 (6481731)
- **Claim (reviewer):** The Lean verifier's `-G` term is `P.g₁⁻¹`, and `EnvAgrees.nG2` requires `params.nG2Prepared = P.g₂⁻¹`. At the deployment P is the axiom `bls12_381Pairing`, and nothing states which elements its `g₁` and `g₂` are. No theorem or axiom relates them to `g1zGen` or to exponent 1. Therefore D-P46's draft pin `vkG1Words … = R.E.g1.toEip P.g₁` and Gap B option (a) — naming the G2 bases from their bytes while keeping `EnvAgrees.nG2` — are independent of the axioms and cannot be proved at `poseidonRender` without a further axiom.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** CurvePairing.lean:101 `axiom bls12_381Pairing : AdmissiblePairing G₁z G2fold blsGT`, with the comment "Nothing here says ... that its generators are the published ones". MultiOpen.lean:311 `{ scalars := #[b.x3, v], bases := #[pi, P.g₁⁻¹], ...`. Inv.lean:573 `nG2 : params.nG2Prepared = P.g₂⁻¹`. DECISIONS-PENDING.md:68 `baseSlots : vkG1Words R.pv vkG1BaseSlot = R.E.g1.toEip P.g₁ ∧`. A grep for `bls12_381Pairing.g₁ =`, `= g1zGen` finds no pin.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Two models of the five axioms: one with `bls12_381Pairing.g₁ = g1zGen`, one with `g₁ = g1zGen ^ 2` (still a generator, still non-degenerate). The payload's G1 base word equals `toEip g₁` in the first and not in the second, so the pin is neither provable nor refutable.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed. bls12_381Pairing is an axiom of type AdmissiblePairing G₁z G2fold blsGT. Its g₁ and g₂ fields are constrained only by hG₁, hG₂ and hNonDeg, and no declaration relates them to g1zGen or to any byte string. Two refinements. (1) G1 half: the Lean verifier's -G term is hard-wired to P.g₁⁻¹, and the payload's G1 base is the standard generator, whose coordinates equal g1zPair g1zGen (PoseidonEnv gate 11). The D-P46 draft pin `vkG1Words R.pv vkG1BaseSlot = R.E.g1.toEip P.g₁` is therefore equivalent to bls12_381Pairing.g₁ = g1zGen. That is independent of the five axioms: replacing g₁ by g₁² gives another admissible pairing because r is an odd prime. There is no escape short of a sixth axiom or re-typing the generator as an env/params field. (2) G2 half: DualMSM.check reads only params.sG2Prepared and params.nG2Prepared, never P.g₂. Under Gap B option (a) the slot pins would follow from choose_spec as the document says. What becomes unprovable is EnvAgrees.nG2 (`params.nG2Prepared = P.g₂⁻¹`), which Valid.slots carries and which is currently PROVED by rfl only because poseidonParams is built from bls12_381Pairing.g₂. So option (a)'s 'No new axiom' is true only if that clause is dropped or restated, and EnvAgrees lives in a v1 file reached from the frozen Valid. The project records Gap B (s·G₂ modelled as g₂) but not that P.g₁ and P.g₂ are opaque axiom fields.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Assumptions/CurvePairing.lean:101 `axiom bls12_381Pairing : AdmissiblePairing G₁z G2fold blsGT`; :97-100 `Nothing here says ... that its generators are the published ones`. Common/Curve.lean:44-55: fields e, g₁, g₂, `hG₁ : g₁ ≠ 1`, `hG₂ : g₂ ≠ 1`, `hNonDeg : e (Additive.ofMul g₁) (Additive.ofMul g₂) ≠ 0`. Exec/Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:310-312 `{ scalars := #[b.x3, v], bases := #[pi, P.g₁⁻¹], labels := #[.custom "π", .custom "-G"] }`. Exec/Kzg/Msm.lean:227-233 (DualMSM.check uses `params.sG2Prepared` and `params.nG2Prepared` only). Lowering/Inv.lean:573 `nG2 : params.nG2Prepared = P.g₂⁻¹`; :617 v1 `g1BaseWords : vkG1Words PV vkG1BaseSlot = E.g1.toEip P.g₁`. Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:236 `poseidonSG2 : G2fold := bls12_381Pairing.g₂`; :241-242 poseidonParams; :284-286 (both generator clauses by rfl); :461 `decompressed poseidonParamsData.g1Hex == some (g1zPair g1zGen)`. docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:67 `baseSlots : vkG1Words R.pv vkG1BaseSlot = R.E.g1.toEip P.g₁ ∧`; :99-112 (Gap B option (a), `No new axiom`). `grep -rn 'bls12_381Pairing\.g₁\|g₁ = g1zGen' Midfall` finds only uses in HoldsFinal.lean, PoseidonEnv.lean, PoseidonValid.lean and CurveSurface.lean, and no equation with g1zGen or exponent 1.
- **Suggested fix:** Strengthen D-P36a to assume a pairing with named generators (for example a sixth axiom `bls12_381Pairing.g₁ = g1zGen` and a G2 analogue), and list it in the trusted base. Alternatively make the verifier's G1 generator a `VerifierEnv`/`ParamsVerifierKZG` field pinned by bytes rather than `P.g₁`. Decide this together with Gap B.

### E-8 — i32Abs models i32::MIN.abs() as a panic in the release profile; Rust release returns i32::MIN without panicking

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** fidelity-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Verifier.lean:667-675,877; proofs/src/plonk/verifier.rs:348
- **Claim (reviewer):** `i32::abs` carries `#[rustc_inherit_overflow_checks]`. In the reference (release) profile `i32::MIN.abs()` wraps to `i32::MIN`; only debug builds panic. The port's comment claims the opposite and returns `Fault.panic "…verifier.rs:348"`. The case is reachable only with an instance query at rotation i32::MIN, which `SupportedV2.noRotInst` excludes.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Verifier.lean:668-671 "`i32::MIN.abs()` overflows, and because the check is inside `abs` itself rather than in an arithmetic operator it fires in the release profile too (BASELINE.md), so it is a `Fault.panic` and not a wrap". Rust core int_macros.rs (1.84.1, :3325-3329 and :3346-3356): "code in debug mode will trigger a panic on this case and optimized code will return `i32::MIN` without a panic", `#[rustc_inherit_overflow_checks] pub const fn abs(self) -> Self { if self.is_negative() { -self } else { self } }`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** A constraint system with an instance query at `Rotation(i32::MIN)`: Rust release continues with |min| = i32::MIN, Lean returns a panic fault.
- **Suggested fix:** Return `wrapI32 (-x)` for negative x (release semantics) and correct the comment, or state explicitly that the debug reading is modelled.

### E-9 — Features.solidity documentation misstates the fewer-point-sets configuration

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** doc-inaccuracy · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Features.lean:21-34; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:243-252; aggregation/Cargo.toml:55-58; proofs/src/poly/kzg/mod.rs:31-41,331-359; proofs/solidity-verifier/tests/ivc_keccak_solidity.rs:1038,1060
- **Claim (reviewer):** In the recorded production profile (`evm,truncated-challenges,in-circuit-fewer-point-sets`), feature unification compiles `midnight-proofs/fewer-point-sets` in, and its thread-local switch defaults to ON. The outer proof is laid out without dummy queries only because callers wrap it in `scoped_fewer_point_sets(OUTER_FEWER_POINT_SETS_ENABLED)`. The Lean comments say the feature "is NOT among them" and the branch "is dead there". Lean's `feat.outerFewerPointSets && fewerPointSetsRuntime` agrees with Rust only under that caller discipline; a bare `prepare` in the production binary reads dummy evaluations. In the fixture profile (`evm,rust-verifier-trace`) the branch really is compiled out.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** aggregation/Cargo.toml:55-56 `in-circuit-fewer-point-sets = [ "midnight-proofs/fewer-point-sets", ...`. kzg/mod.rs:36 `static ENABLED: Cell<bool> = const { Cell::new(true) };`. ivc_keccak_solidity.rs:1060 `let _outer_proof_layout = scoped_fewer_point_sets(outer_fewer_point_sets);`. MultiOpen.lean:246-248 "plain fewer-point-sets is NOT among them, so feat.outerFewerPointSets is false for the deployed profile and this whole branch is dead there". Features.lean:33 `TODO(BASELINE): confirm against the frozen baseline's deploy command line`.
- **Suggested fix:** Document that the Lean flag models (cfg ∧ runtime switch) and that the modelled Rust verifier is `prepare` under `scoped_fewer_point_sets(false)`. Resolve the TODO.

### E-10 — On malformed keys Lean is stricter than Rust or reports a different fault site

- **Severity (reviewer):** info · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** fidelity-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (Comments at the cited Lean sites describe (1) and (2) as deliberate.) · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Verifier.lean:85-92,168-173,704-711,782-786,323-324; proofs/src/plonk/verifier.rs:108-146,398-401; proofs/src/plonk/permutation.rs:202-240
- **Claim (reviewer):** Four divergences, all confined to keys excluded by `WellFormed`:
(1) `scatterIndexed` faults on an unwritten advice or challenge slot, where Rust keeps `Commitment::default()` / `F::ZERO` and continues.
(2) `readFixedEvals` returns UnexpectedEof immediately when simple selectors exceed fixed columns, where Rust would read scalars until EOF and could surface `io::Error::other` first.
(3) `partiallyEvaluateIdentities` chunks all lookups eagerly, where Rust chunks lazily per zipped pair.
(4) Rust evaluates `permutation::expressions`' `chunks(chunk_len)` (permutation.rs:237) and `permutation.queries`' `blinding_factors()` eagerly when the iterator chain is built, so a combined-fault key can report a different panic site.
None changes accept/reject on well-formed keys. `SoundSide` matches only the logup.rs:460 string, whose relative order (gates, permutation, logup, trash) is the same on both sides.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Verifier.lean:90-92 `match placed.mapM (fun x => x) with | some xs => .ok xs.toArray | none => .error (Fault.panic site)` against verifier.rs:108-110 `vec![vec![CS::Commitment::default(); ...]]`. Verifier.lean:709-710 `if nSimple > nRead then throw (Fault.error (Error.transcript IoErrorKind.unexpectedEof))`. Verifier.lean:782-784 "observed here, after this proof's advice and instance queries have been built".
- **Suggested fix:** No code change needed. Record these as accepted divergences outside `WellFormed`.

### E+1 — Valid does not tie the Lean verifier's VK commitment values or domain omega to the payload the contract loads (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:124-125,182-217; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:533-579,807-813; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:195-200,217-223; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonValid.lean:35-37; formal/Midfall/Generator/Vk.lean:454-459
- **Claim:** Statement defect of the same class as the recorded fpG2Slots / RETURN_MPTR under-pinning, but for different data, and not in D-P46, D-P48 or any QUEUE item (grep). Valid ties R.vk to the plan only through circuitMeta (constraint-system shape), inputs.k, EnvAgrees.domainN (k) and EnvAgrees.vkDigestWord (the imported, unchecked transcriptRepr). No clause relates R.vk.fixedCommitments, R.vk.permutation.commitments, or R.vk.domain.omega / omegaInv to the payload words (header slots Omega = 4, OmegaInv = 5, OmegaInvToL = 6, and the commitment sections). Inv at .pcs pins PAIRING_RHS to Snap.c.dual.right.eval, which Lean builds from R.vk's commitments; Inv at .lagrange pins l_last and l_blind, which Lean computes from R.vk.domain.omega. So for a Valid R whose Exec key carries other commitments, or another primitive n-th root, the block that computes those values cannot satisfy BlockSound. The generic blockContracts_of_valid is therefore false as stated for such R, wherever a snapshot exists. This is live at the deployment: poseidonVkExec carries 27 identity placeholders against the payload's real points, and no Valid clause is refuted by that (slots is reported PROVED). This is the v1 defect PLAN-V2:43 says V2 removed ('plan and VK described different circuits with every pin green'), surviving for VK values rather than shape.
- **Evidence:** Statements.lean:124-125 `vkPoints : ∀ C ∈ R.vk.fixedCommitments.toList ++ R.vk.permutation.commitments.toList, ptReadable (R.E.g1.toEip C)` is the only occurrence of fixedCommitments or permutation.commitments in Midfall/LoweringV2 and Lowering/Inv.lean (grep). Inv.lean:533-579: the ten EnvAgrees fields are modulus, truncWidth, domainN, baryWeight, omegaInvMul, omegaOrder, negLagranges, nG2, sG2, vkDigestWord. omegaInvMul and omegaOrder are internal to vk.domain; none mentions a payload omega word or a commitment. Inv.lean:810-812 `holdsG1 E st (ptrAddr M.finalComMptr) S.c.finalCom.eval ... holdsG1 E st (ptrAddr M.pairingRhsMptr) S.c.dual.right.eval`; :780-783 lLast, lBlind, l0. PoseidonEnv.lean:195-196 `def poseidonFixedCommitments : Array G₁z := Array.replicate poseidonVkData.numFixedColumns (1 : G₁z)`; :199-200 the same for the permutation commitments. PoseidonValid.lean:35-37 `8 slots PROVED ... All ten EnvAgrees clauses now hold`. Generator/Vk.lean:454-459 header slots `omega`, `omegaInv`, `omegaInvToL`. Confidence medium-high: derived by reading; the falsity of the block row presupposes a reachable Clean pre-state and a snapshot.

### E+2 — rejects_of_not_accepts is stated for every Render with no Valid hypothesis, and is false wherever the program accepts but the unpinned Lean side rejects (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:356-363,606-608,618-620
- **Claim:** rejects_of_not_accepts is one of the sorried obligations in the headline cone. It has neither `hR : R.Valid` nor SoundSide, although its only call site (Blocks.lean:607) has hR in scope. DeploymentV2, EntryFrame, Holds and RunBody read only R.plan, R.consts, R.E.g1.toEip / ofEip?, R.encG2 and R.prog; LeanAccepts reads R.E, R.params and R.vk. Take any render R0 at which the headline is meant to hold non-vacuously, and let R1 be R0 with `ofCompressed? := fun _ => none` (or with E.delta changed, or with vk.transcriptRepr changed). The hypotheses and runs are identical and LeanAccepts R1 is False, so the statement claims that no run of R0's program ever returns. That contradicts R0's own completeness conjunct. Adding hR does not rescue the compressed-decoder and delta variants, because Valid is independent of both (E-2, E-4). The planned generic `∀ R, R.Valid → LoweringEquivalence R` is false for the same reason until Valid pins those fields. The project's recorded discussion of this lemma concerns only the SoundSide / logup-panic question.
- **Evidence:** Blocks.lean:356-363 `theorem rejects_of_not_accepts (R : Render p G₁ G₂ Gₜ) (product : PairingProduct) ... (hdep : DeploymentV2 R W H vkAddr) (hentry : EntryFrame R W Lyt H cd st₀) (hpc : Eip2537.Holds R.E.g1 ⟨R.encG2⟩ product) (hna : ¬ LeanAccepts R H cd) (hobs : RunBody R vkAddr 0 (permissive (callsOf product)) g st₀ obs) : obs.isRevertOrHalt := by sorry`. Statements.lean:224-231 (DeploymentV2 reads R.plan.vk.runtimeBytes only), :240-247 (EntryFrame reads R.consts), :255-257 (RunBody reads R.prog), :263-267 (LeanAccepts reads R.E, R.params, R.vk). Blocks.lean:618-620 `The generic ∀ R, R.Valid → LoweringEquivalence R becomes provable once WP-G3 ports QRS:2000 and Valid gains the generator fact`. docs/QUEUE.md:389 and :421-422 discuss only deleting SoundSide. Conditional on an accepting run existing for some render in the model.

(2) collectQueries matches verifier.rs:580-647 in chain order and in the simple-selector filter. (3) The right-product delta ladder in Permutation.expressions matches permutation.rs:261-277. (4) VerifyingKey.evaluate reads one evaluation per sigma commitment, as in Rust. (5) The logup.rs:460 site string is the actual `f.invert().unwrap()` line. (6) `git diff --stat 6481731..HEAD` shows no change under Midfall/Halo2, LoweringV2, Lowering, Conformance, Assumptions or the Rust src trees; only Instances, docs, cs.json and the exporter test changed. 'verifyNative_iff_msgs has no sorry in its dependencies' was not verified beyond seeing its proof body. The existence of an accepting run in the Lean EVM model, on which the falsity (as opposed to unprovability) of poseidon_rejects rests, is not established. ### E — errors the skeptic found in the reviewer's report

- E-3: line references into Midfall/Conformance/Replay.lean are off. The fold `let st := b.trace.events.foldl ...` is at :232 (not :228); checkTerm is at :218-224 (not :199-208); the 'WHY NOT verifyNative ITSELF' paragraph is :18-29 (not :16-24); the imports are :38-41 (not :37-40). The content quoted is accurate.
- E-6: severity understated. The finding has the same impact class as E-4 (an instance value that Valid does not exclude makes the Lean verifier reject every proof at the deployed render), yet E-4 is rated high and E-6 medium. Also, at the deployment the first permutation column is fixed 9, so the first panic site is circuit.rs:2084; the counterexample uses advice column 0 (:2074), which is correct for that column but is not the first fault.
- E-5: the title 'Valid is unsatisfiable for real keys' over-generalises. The clause holds when cs_degree = 3 (chunk_len 1) or there is at most one permutation column; it fails for the deployed key (8 columns, cs_degree 5). At f2fe99b the project already records it as a clause defect (PoseidonValid.lean:38-43, PoseidonEnv.lean:485-487, STATUS.md:279), so the residual finding is the missing repair item and the stale STATUS.md:66.
- E-1: 'verifyNative / verifyMsgs return Error.opening for every proof at a faithful key' holds only for proofs that reach multi_prepare, and only with canonical rotations. With the f2fe99b importer the E-6 panic fires earlier (partiallyEvaluateIdentities runs before collectQueries), and with the placeholder codec E-2 fires earlier still. 'The current placeholder key behaves the same way' is counterfactual on a working codec. 'BASELINE.md:228' is the row at :227, and it concerns fixtures/poseidon/proof.bin, which trace.json's fixture_match marks as a different proof from the conformance fixture; the Rust accept for the conformance proof is trace.json:248 and :1617.
- E-2: the title states 'poseidon_rejects is false as stated' unconditionally. What reading establishes is that it is unprovable and equivalent to 'no permissive run ever returns'; falsity needs an accepting run in the Lean EVM model, which is the project's premise but is not exhibited (the claim body does hedge). The finding also understates its generic consequence: the same argument falsifies the sorried generic rejects_of_not_accepts and the planned `∀ R, R.Valid → LoweringEquivalence R`, and makes the current lowering_equivalence_poseidon false rather than merely vacuous in conjunct 2.
- E-7: 'Gap B option (a) ... cannot be proved at poseidonRender without a further axiom' needs a split. The G2 slot pins themselves would follow from choose_spec; what becomes unprovable is EnvAgrees.nG2 (params.nG2Prepared = P.g₂⁻¹), which DualMSM.check never reads, so the G2 half can also be repaired by dropping or restating that clause. Only the G1 half (-G hard-wired to P.g₁⁻¹ at MultiOpen.lean:311) strictly needs a new axiom or a re-typed generator.
- E-4: 'the Lean verifier rejects honest proofs' is true up to negligible probability (a mismatch in a field product) and only for keys with at least 2 permutation columns; and at the current render it is pre-empted by E-2 and E-6. The substance is unaffected.
