- verifyNative_iff_msgs: the statement is the intended byte-level / message-level equivalence under `compressedRoundtrip`. Its proof, `Lowering.verifyNative_iff_msgs_codec` (formal/Midfall/Lowering/Obligations.lean:832-900), uses only lemmas in formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/TranscriptLog.lean, which contains no `sorry` and imports only Exec.Native and Assumptions.Codec (also sorry-free). Checked by reading; `#print axioms` was not run. Caveat: both sides run the same pipeline, so it gives no Rust-fidelity assurance, and at g1CodecZ both sides are False.
- Sorried definitions: exactly one of the 60 registered sorries is a definition, `Midfall.Halo2.Spec.accepts : Prop := sorry` (formal/Midfall/Halo2/Spec/Piop.lean:64-67). It is used only by the sorried v1 `verifyMsgs_iff_spec` and not by LoweringV2. The other 59 are theorem bodies. Security/SRS.lean and Security/Challenges.lean contain definitions and bookkeeping lemmas only, with no security theorem; Spec/Relation.lean:137 has the placeholder `CommittedInstanceZero : Prop := True`.
- Canonicality on deserialisation matches: scalars are rejected unless < r (`frOfRepr?` vs `Fq::from_repr`); G1 compressed reads in Rust check on-curve and torsion-free (curves/src/bls12_381/g1.rs:369-371), consistent with decoding into a prime-order carrier. The five D-P36a axioms (formal/Midfall/Assumptions/CurvePairing.lean:69-101) are jointly consistent: three cyclic groups of order r admit a bilinear non-degenerate map.

### E — not covered

- No Lean, lake or cargo command was run. Every claim about sorry-freedom, KAT values and the proposed Lean checks is by reading; the needs_lean_check snippets are untested and may need namespace or instance adjustments.
- The working tree moved from 6481731 to f2fe99b during the audit (five commits touching Instances/Poseidon*.lean, DeployedProfile.lean, docs, cs.json, conformance_export.rs). The in-scope Exec, LoweringV2, Lowering, Conformance, Statements, Assumptions, Common and Rust proofs/src files are unchanged between the two. The new PoseidonFixture.lean was only skimmed.
- formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Accumulator.lean (2738 lines; accumulator mode is outside the first-headline scope) was not read.
- formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/TranscriptLog.lean proofs (3503 lines) were not read line by line; only the absence of sorry, the imports and the top-level proof structure were checked.
- formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Poly.lean, formal/Midfall/Halo2/Spec/Degrees.lean (1169 lines) and the selector-conversion path in Exec/Circuit.lean (replaceSelectors, directlyConvertSelectorsToFixed) were not audited.
- EvaluationDomain.new was not compared with domain.rs:37-142 beyond the verifier-relevant fields (omega, omegaInv, n, k, barycentricWeight, quotientPolyDegree).
- The concrete BLS12-381 codec in Instances/Bls12_381.lean (on-curve only, no subgroup check; used by the conformance replay) and the Keccak implementation in Common/Keccak.lean were not audited against blst / the Keccak specification.
- Rust MSM and pairing back ends (blst multi_exp, msm_best, multi_miller_loop), including their handling of identity bases, are modelled mathematically in Lean and were not examined.
- Whether the generated contract handles the E-1 and E-4 cases as Rust does (per-slot MSM terms, DELTA literal) was inferred from trace.json labels and BASELINE.md, not from the templates.
- proofs/src/plonk/evaluation.rs, keygen and prover code (out of the verifier path) were not read.


## F — fidelity of the Lean generator port

**Reviewer summary.** Verdict: the Lean port is a careful, line-cited transcription, and every function I compared line by line with the Rust is faithful. But the tie between the theorem's left-hand side and the real contract is much weaker than PLAN-V2's "golden-tested" suggests, and one frozen clause mis-states the generator.

1. Frozen `Valid.circuitMeta` is wrong. Rust's plan meta is `ConstraintSystemMeta::new` followed by `set_num_dummy_evals` and `set_num_point_sets`; the clause forces `numPointSets = 0`. So `circuitMeta` and `layout` together exclude every real plan (poseidon: 4224 against 4320 proof bytes, VK_MPTR 0x28a0 against 0x2900).
2. The headline's program is the hand-written AST emitter. At 6481731 it omits PCS, the quotient numerator and the accumulator helpers, so the stated per-deployment headline is false today, `codegenAgrees` is false for every (pv, m), and the freeze does not pin the emitter.
3. Fidelity evidence is one fixture in one profile. The VK payload, quotient program and blocks, PCS blocks and layout numbers are parsed or sliced from the Rust output, so roughly half of the executable Yul in the byte-exact gate is the artifact compared with itself. The Kzg and quotient emitters are never compared with Rust output.
4. `Valid` ties only the digest word of the payload to the verifier's VK, so the generic hinge becomes false once `Valid` is inhabited.
5. No CI job regenerates the dump or runs the Rust crate's tests. The fixture is not deployed anywhere; the Sepolia deployment is a different, out-of-scope artifact from a superseded generator.

The working tree advanced from 6481731 to 2ec80db during the review (F.1 landed). Findings are stated at 6481731, with HEAD differences noted. Nothing was built or run, so every Lean check below is still to be executed; please verify these findings before relying on them. All paths are under .

### F-1 — Frozen Valid.circuitMeta forces numPointSets = 0, so no faithful plan satisfies circuitMeta and layout together

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (Only after the audit baseline: the header and `deciding_layout_rootCause` docstring of formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonValid.lean at commit 2041977 report 4224 against 4320 and attribute it to un-ported post-constructor sizing. Nothing records that the frozen clause is incompatible with the generat […]) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:186-190; formal/Midfall/Generator/Encoding.lean:473-474; formal/Midfall/Generator/Vk.lean:1026,1037-1038; proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/encoding/mod.rs:160-161; proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/vk.rs:511,519; proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/plan.rs:238-244
- **Claim (reviewer):** `Valid.circuitMeta` requires `R.plan.meta_` to equal the raw output of `ConstraintSystemMeta.new`, whose `numPointSets` and `numDummyEvals` are the literal 0. The Rust plan's meta is that output after `set_num_dummy_evals` and `set_num_point_sets(kzg::num_point_sets(..))`, and `LoweringPlan::new` builds the proof layout from that later meta. So for any circuit (at least one point set) the real plan violates `circuitMeta`. Conversely, any render satisfying `circuitMeta` has, by `layout`, a proof layout with zero q_eval words, which is not the contract's. `poseidon_valid` is therefore unprovable for a plan that matches the Rust output, even after D-P47, WP-G1 and WP-G4.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Statements.lean:186-187 `circuitMeta : ConstraintSystemMeta.new Features.solidity R.vk.cs R.cfg.numCommittedInstances = .ok R.plan.meta_`; :189-190 `layout : ProofCalldataLayout.fromProtocol R.plan.meta_.protocol abiVerifyProofProofCptr R.plan.meta_.numEvals R.plan.meta_.numPointSets = .ok R.plan.proofLayout`. Encoding.lean:473-474 `numDummyEvals := 0` / `numPointSets := 0`. encoding/mod.rs:160-161 `num_dummy_evals: 0, num_point_sets: 0,`. vk.rs:519 `meta.set_num_point_sets(kzg::num_point_sets(&meta, &data));`. plan.rs:238 `let (vk_mptr, meta, data, _) = inputs.meta_data_for_stable_static_layout(&vk);` then :239-244 `ProofCalldataLayout::from_protocol(&meta.protocol, ..., meta.num_evals, meta.num_point_sets)`. The Lean port of the same step agrees with Rust: Vk.lean:1037-1038 `let nSets ← numPointSets meta1 data` / `let meta2 := meta1.setNumPointSets nSets`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Poseidon fixture. The real plan has num_point_sets = 3: the dump checks `eq(0x10e0, calldataload(PROOF_LEN_CPTR))` (target/poseidon-fixture-dump/Halo2Verifier.sol:1164) and has VK_MPTR = 0x2900 (:115) = 0x1000 + 200*32, where 200 is the transcript word bound computed with 3 point sets (32 + 21*128 + 48*32 + 3*32 + 1024 + 1024 = 6400 bytes). With numPointSets = 0, as the clause demands: proofLen = 19*128 + 48*32 + 2*128 = 4224 (0x1080), and the transcript bound is 197 words, giving VK_MPTR 0x28a0.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Frozen `Valid.circuitMeta` equates `R.plan.meta_` with the raw output of `ConstraintSystemMeta.new`, whose `numPointSets` and `numDummyEvals` are literally 0. Rust's `plan.meta` is that output after `set_num_dummy_evals` and `set_num_point_sets(kzg::num_point_sets(..))`, and the proof layout is built from the later meta. So no plan equal to the Rust plan satisfies `circuitMeta`, and any plan satisfying both `circuitMeta` and `layout` has zero q_eval words (poseidon: 4224 proof bytes instead of 4320). Under Features.solidity only `numPointSets` conflicts, since the dummy-eval feature is off. At HEAD clause 1 is counted PROVED for a plan carrying the raw meta, while clause 2 is refuted by exactly the 96 bytes; the two cannot both hold for the generator's plan. PLAN-V2 WP-G4's step to restate `circuitMeta` as a lemma of the ported constructor would therefore produce a false lemma. The repair is a frozen-surface edit under sign-off.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:186-190 `circuitMeta : ConstraintSystemMeta.new Features.solidity R.vk.cs R.cfg.numCommittedInstances = .ok R.plan.meta_` and `layout : ProofCalldataLayout.fromProtocol R.plan.meta_.protocol abiVerifyProofProofCptr R.plan.meta_.numEvals R.plan.meta_.numPointSets = .ok R.plan.proofLayout`. formal/Midfall/Generator/Encoding.lean:472-474 `numEvals := protocol.numMainEvals / numDummyEvals := 0 / numPointSets := 0`. proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/encoding/mod.rs:160-161 `num_dummy_evals: 0, num_point_sets: 0,`. vk.rs:511 `meta.set_num_dummy_evals(n_dummy);`, :519 `meta.set_num_point_sets(kzg::num_point_sets(&meta, &data));`. plan.rs:238-244 `let (vk_mptr, meta, data, _) = inputs.meta_data_for_stable_static_layout(&vk);` then `ProofCalldataLayout::from_protocol(&meta.protocol, ..., meta.num_evals, meta.num_point_sets)`, and the plan stores that `meta`. formal/Midfall/Generator/Abi.lean:392 `let (qEvals, cursor9) := ProofCalldataLayout.wordSection cursor8 numPointSets`. Lean port of the same step: Vk.lean:1037-1038 `let nSets ← numPointSets meta1 data` / `let meta2 := meta1.setNumPointSets nSets`; Vk.lean:1006-1007 says the dummy feature is FALSE in Features.solidity. Dump target/poseidon-fixture-dump/Halo2Verifier.sol:1164 `eq(0x10e0, calldataload(PROOF_LEN_CPTR))` (4320), :115 `VK_MPTR = 0x2900`. I re-derived the transcript bound from Abi.lean:530-566 with cushion 32 words (Abi.lean:117): 32 + 21*128 + 1536 + 96 + 1024 + 1024 = 6400 bytes = 200 words, giving 0x2900; with 0 point sets it is 197 words, giving 0x28a0. HEAD record: formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonValid.lean:13-21 lists clause 1 PROVED and clause 2 REFUTED, 'the 96-byte gap is the `num_point_sets` / `num_dummy_evals` that `LoweringPlan::new` sets AFTER `from_constraint_system`'. formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:251-252 'Then restate `Valid.circuitMeta/layout/invariants` as lemmas of the constructor.' No doc records the clause conflict (grep of STATUS, QUEUE, DECISIONS-PENDING, design).
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
At HEAD: `#eval (match Midfall.Generator.ConstraintSystemMeta.new Midfall.Halo2.Features.solidity Midfall.Instances.PoseidonFixture.poseidonCs 1 with | .ok m => (m.numPointSets, m.numDummyEvals) | .error _ => (999, 999))` expecting (0, 0); `#eval Midfall.Generator.poseidonProofLayout.qEvals.itemCount` expecting 3.
```

- **Suggested fix:** Change frozen section 2 under sign-off. Either state `∃ m0, ConstraintSystemMeta.new .. = .ok m0 ∧ R.plan.meta_ = (m0.setNumDummyEvals d).setNumPointSets n` with d and n fixed by the ported `numDummyQueries` and `numPointSets` on (meta, plan.data), or replace clauses 1-3 by `metaDataForStableStaticLayout .. = .ok (R.plan.vkMptr, R.plan.meta_, R.plan.data, _)`. Add a KAT that the derived VK_MPTR is 0x2900.

### F-2 — The stated per-deployment headline is false today: its program omits PCS, the quotient numerator and the accumulator helpers, and codegenAgrees is false for every (pv, m)

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** partially-correct → high · **Kind:** statement-false · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (Program.lean:92-97 (CodegenAgrees false until WP-G1); STATUS.md C.4 table (codegen refuted); QUEUE.md held pcs row ("REFUTABLE"). - **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Program.lean:92-99,190-194; formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAst.lean:579-581,840-868; formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAstDispatch.lean:131-135; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonV2.lean:247-250; proofs/solidity-verifier/templates/partials/verifier/AccumulatorHelpers.yul:22-167
- **Claim (reviewer):** (a) `LoweringEquivalence poseidonRender product` has no `Valid` hypothesis and is about `asmOf`, the hand-written emitter. At 6481731 that program has an empty PCS slice and a `quotientLin` slice that only stores x_split and 1 - x^n, so FinalPairing pairs the never-written PAIRING_LHS/RHS slots and soundness conjunct 1 is false. At HEAD the PCS slice is emitted, but QUOTIENT_EVAL and the selector accumulators are still never written, so completeness conjunct 2 fails instead. The sorried `poseidon_quotient_block` and `poseidon_rejects` are therefore false as stated until emitter definitions change. (b) Five accumulator helper functions render unconditionally in the text and are never emitted as AST, so `codegenAgrees pv m = false` for every pv and m. `Render.Valid` is uninhabited for every render, and `lowering_equivalence_of_blocks`, `blockContracts_of_valid` and `end_to_end`, which all take `hR : R.Valid`, are vacuous today.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Program.lean:98-99 `def mainAsmRaw (pv : PlanView) : YulBlock := mainGuardBlock ++ GenAst.emitHelpers pv ++ mainLocals ++ GenAst.emitSlices pv`; :96 "`CodegenAgrees` below is FALSE until they are". GenAst.lean:868 `def emitQuotientLin (pv : PlanView) : Block Op := emitQuotientLinAt pv.k`, whose body (:840-864) ends with the two `mstore`s to QUOTIENT_MPTR. GenAstDispatch.lean:134-135 `| .ok blocks => blocks.flatten | .error _ => []`. DeployedProfile.lean@6481731:729 gates `(emitPartial pv .pcs).isEmpty`; STATUS.md:41 "pcs empty at plan level". AccumulatorHelpers.yul:55 `function is_bls_p_minus_one(hi, lo) -> yes {` precedes the `{%- if self.expected_has_accumulator %}` at :167; the name occurs twice in the poseidon dump and in GenAst*.lean only in a comment (GenAst.lean:574).
- **Counterexample / witness:** At 6481731: calldata = selector, canonical ABI head, 0x10e0 zero proof bytes, one zero instance. Every G1 word is the canonical identity encoding and every scalar is canonical, so vkLoading, transcriptParser and lagrange pass unless x lands on the domain. The pairing inputs are two identity points, the honest pairing check returns 1, and the program returns ABI true. The Lean verifier rejects, since with v = 0 and pi = O the left pairing input is a non-trivial combination of VK commitments.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed: (i) `LoweringEquivalence poseidonRender product` has no `Valid` hypothesis and is about `asmOf`, whose main block is `mainGuardBlock ++ emitHelpers ++ mainLocals ++ emitSlices`. The emitter writes neither QUOTIENT_EVAL_MPTR nor the selector accumulators, and at 6481731 its PCS slice is empty. (ii) At 6481731 PAIRING_LHS/RHS are never written, so `ec_pairing` pairs two zero G1 slots with the payload's G2 points and the program returns ABI true for every calldata that passes the format checks. Soundness conjunct 1 and the sorried `poseidon_rejects` are therefore false as stated there, unless the Lean verifier accepts every well-formed calldata, which I did not compute. (iii) `codegenAgrees pv m = false` for every (pv, m): the AST defines exactly eight functions while the text always defines at least thirteen, five accumulator helpers being unconditional. So `Render.Valid` is uninhabited for every render, and `blockContracts_of_valid`, `lowering_equivalence_of_blocks` and `end_to_end` are vacuous today. Not established: (a) that completeness fails at HEAD, which needs a calldata the Lean verifier accepts at `poseidonRender` and none is known; (b) that `poseidon_quotient_block` is false. `snapshotOf` always sets `lin := none`, so `Inv .quotient` adds only the two QUOTIENT_MPTR words, which the emitted slice does write; that contract is too weak, not false. The emitter gap is recorded (Program.lean:94-97, WP-G1); what is new is that the stated headline is false at the audited commit and that `Valid` is empty for all renders.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Program.lean:98-99 `def mainAsmRaw (pv : PlanView) : YulBlock := mainGuardBlock ++ GenAst.emitHelpers pv ++ mainLocals ++ GenAst.emitSlices pv`. formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAst.lean:579-581 `emitHelpers := emitFail ++ emitScalarInvAt pv ++ emitTranscriptHelpers ++ emitBatchInvertAt ++ emitEcPairingAt pv`; the only `yfun` definitions in GenAst*.lean are fail, ec_pairing, transcript_init, common_word, common_uncompressed_g1, squeeze_to, scalar_inv, batch_invert. GenAstDispatch.lean:131-135 pcs error or empty arm `| .error _ => []`; DeployedProfile.lean@6481731:729 gates `(emitPartial pv .pcs).isEmpty`. Template AssemblyHelpers.yul:79-83: `common_uncompressed_g1` 'does not run an independent curve/subgroup check'; :268-306 `ec_pairing` mcopies lhs, G2_BASE_MPTR, rhs, NEG_S_G2_BASE_MPTR and requires `eq(mload(scratch), 1)`. ### F-4 — Port fidelity rests on one fixture in one profile, with the circuit-specific half of the contract parsed or sliced from the Rust output

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** partially-correct → medium · **Kind:** trusted-base-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (DeployedProfile.lean gate 8 `derivedRender_residualChannels_ok` names the three borrowed channels; VerifierPartials.lean:88-108 and the Kzg.lean header say what is not pinned. PLAN-V2 section 0 still describes the port as golden-tested without these qualifications.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonInputs.lean:76-111,283-292; formal/Midfall/Instances/DeployedProfile.lean (at 6481731: 81-110,142-152,230-244,535-569); formal/Midfall/Generator/VerifierPartials.lean:88-108,350-369,1068-1087,1162-1173,4295-4302; formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAstPcs.lean:654-718; formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:24-26
- **Claim (reviewer):** The only comparison of Lean generator output with Rust generator output is the poseidon fixture (VM lowering, separate VK, no accumulator, no trace, no gas checkpoints, truncated challenges). In it the VK payload is parsed back from the Rust-rendered Halo2VerifyingKey.sol, and the quotient program model, proof layout and layout sizes are typed in from the dump. The five generated quotient regions and all eight PCS blocks are sliced from the Rust-rendered Halo2Verifier.sol and re-inserted before the byte comparison. By my measurement those slices are about 54 kB of 175,918 bytes; I estimate them at roughly half of the executable Yul tokens (all of PCS at 3,623 tokens, plus the generated identity code of the numerator). The ported emitters for exactly those regions (`Kzg.computations`, `compactQuotientComputationBlocks`, the VM builder) are checked only against transcribed Rust unit-test assertions and against snapshots of their own output on synthetic circuits. Template arms the fixture does not take are gated for selection only, although tracked dumps from the current generator exist for them.

They are ported so another profile can be rendered, and gated only for SELECTION". GenAstPcs.lean:680 "Block 2 of the Kzg fixture" (a snapshot of the port's own output). Measured slice sizes in the dump: lines 1750-1849, 1974-2056, 2075-2118, 2136-2252, 2316-2508 total 32,333 bytes; PCS lines 2593-3000 are 23,706 bytes. The ported `Kzg.computations` and `compactQuotientComputationBlocks` are exercised only on synthetic fixtures; at HEAD the PCS AST is emitted for poseidon but explicitly not compared with the artifact. Four other tracked dumps from the current generator, plus the poseidon `.direct.sol`, are read by no Lean gate. ### F-5 — The golden dump is a committed file that nothing regenerates; no CI job runs the Rust crate's tests, and the formal workflow ignores generator changes

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → medium · **Kind:** tooling-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (BASELINE.md:249 records that the fixture tests write into the tree and that the dumps reproduced byte for byte once by hand; REVIEW.md R-38 covers pin-against-file only.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** .github/workflows/formal.yml:4-9; .github/workflows/ci.yaml; proofs/solidity-verifier/tests/poseidon_fixture.rs:184-191; proofs/solidity-verifier/scripts/check_baseline_pins.py; formal/scripts/check_golden_provenance.py:1-21; formal/BASELINE.md:249
- **Claim (reviewer):** The golden text is `proofs/solidity-verifier/target/poseidon-fixture-dump/*.sol`, tracked in git and hash-pinned in BASELINE.md. `cargo test --test poseidon_fixture` rewrites it in place, but no CI job runs `halo2_solidity_verifier` tests, nothing regenerates and diffs the dump, and formal.yml triggers only on `formal/**`. A change to `proofs/solidity-verifier/src` or `templates` can merge with every Lean gate green against the old dump. `tests/template_digest.rs` is not run in CI either. By history the dump and the Rust source are currently in step: the last commit touching both is 9822446.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** formal.yml:6 `paths: ['formal/**', '.github/workflows/formal.yml']`. ci.yaml tests midnight-curves, midnight-circuits, midnight-zk-stdlib, midnight-proofs, midnight-aggregation and midnight-zkir; the string `solidity` does not occur in `.github/`, and the only golden diff step is `git diff --quiet -- zk_stdlib/goldenfiles/examples`. poseidon_fixture.rs:185-190 `let dump_dir = format!("{}/target/poseidon-fixture-dump", ...)` then `std::fs::write(format!("{dump_dir}/Halo2Verifier.sol"), &verifier_solidity).ok();`. check_golden_provenance.py and check_baseline_pins.py compare pins with the file on disk, not with generator output.
- **Counterexample / witness:** A pull request that edits only templates/partials/verifier/Lagrange.yul: the formal job is not triggered, ci.yaml runs only clippy on that crate, and the Lean KATs keep comparing against the unchanged dump.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** No CI job runs `halo2_solidity_verifier` tests (including `tests/template_digest.rs`) or regenerates and diffs the tracked dumps; formal.yml triggers only on `formal/**`; and the provenance scripts compare pins with the file on disk, not with generator output. Correction: the reviewer's statement that dump and source are 'currently in step' is wrong. The project's own post-baseline regeneration (QUEUE R.5 at HEAD) found the committed Halo2Verifier.sol differs from a fresh render in BUILD_ID, and that `proof.bin`/`trace.json` are not reproducible. This strengthens the finding; the staleness itself is recorded at HEAD, the CI gap is not.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** .github/workflows/formal.yml:5-9 `paths: ['formal/**', '.github/workflows/formal.yml']` for both pull_request and push. .github/workflows/ci.yaml test jobs use packages midnight-curves, midnight-circuits, midnight-zk-stdlib, midnight-proofs, midnight-aggregation, midnight-zkir; the only golden diff is :109 `git diff --quiet -- zk_stdlib/goldenfiles/examples`; `rg -i solidity .github/workflows` returns nothing. Root Cargo.toml lists `proofs/solidity-verifier` as a workspace member, so only clippy, doc and fmt reach it. proofs/solidity-verifier/tests/poseidon_fixture.rs:185-191 `let dump_dir = format!("{}/target/poseidon-fixture-dump", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))` then `std::fs::write(format!("{dump_dir}/Halo2Verifier.sol"), &verifier_solidity).ok();`. formal/scripts/check_golden_provenance.py:1-21 describes the chain 'bytes on disk --(this script)--> pinned string'. formal/BASELINE.md:249: the fixture test 'rewrites `proofs/solidity-verifier/target/poseidon-fixture-dump/`'. Contradicting 'in step': formal/docs/QUEUE.md:630-633 'The regenerated `Halo2Verifier.sol` differs from the committed one only in `BUILD_ID` (a stale tracked dump).' Git history: the main dump was last committed in 5312cb7 (2026-10-23); 9822446 (2026-10-28) changed src/api.rs and added only the two `.direct.sol` dumps.
- **Suggested fix:** Add a CI job that renders the tracked dumps with the pinned toolchain and runs `git diff --exit-code -- proofs/solidity-verifier/target/*-dump/*.sol` (the .sol files are deterministic; the proof is not needed). Trigger formal.yml on `proofs/solidity-verifier/**` as well.

### F-6 — Valid does not make the render the generator's output: payload content, plan.data, the quotient identity list and delta are free, so the generic hinge is false once Valid is inhabited

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** partially-correct → high · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (STATUS.md "section 6 systematically under-pins the memory layout"; DECISIONS-PENDING.md items 4 and 8 (three base pins, Gap B); design.md D-P50. The scalar header words, the commitment words, plan.data, the identity list, delta and validate_layout are not recorded.) · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:182-217; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:508-510,533-579; formal/Midfall/Generator/Artifacts.lean:495-499,568; formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAst.lean:695-717; proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/artifacts.rs:275,337-339
- **Claim (reviewer):** `Valid` never states `plan = LoweringPlan::new(inputs)`, and its substitutes leave these generator facts free. (1) Payload words 1-30 (num_instances, k, n_inv, omega, omega_inv, omega_inv_to_l, G1 and G2 bases), the quotient constant and program words, and the fixed and permutation commitment words are tied to `R.vk` and `R.params` by nothing; `EnvAgrees` reads word 0 only. (2) `plan.data`, whose pointers the PCS and quotient emitters read, is tied to `plan.memory` by nothing. (3) `plan.quotient.plan.items` is tied to `R.vk.cs` by nothing. (4) `inputs.frDelta` is tied to `E.delta` by nothing; Rust computes it from the field constant. (5) `Valid.model` omits the `validate_layout` check on which Rust panics. Once `Valid` is inhabited, `blockContracts_of_valid` is false as stated.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Inv.lean:579 `vkDigestWord : (PV.vk.constants.getD 0 ("", 0)).2 = frWord vk.transcriptRepr` is the only `EnvAgrees` field that reads the payload; :508-509 "vkDigestWord is kept: it is the one header fact the payload copy does not already give". Statements.lean:203-205 claims of `slots`: "The payload words the contract loads (VK header, field constants, domain, G1/G2 bases) agree with the environment and VK". GenAst.lean:695 `ylet "omega" (yop Op.mload [yvar "OMEGA_MPTR"])`. Artifacts.lean:496-497 "`validate_layout().unwrap_or_else(panic)` is NOT folded in here"; :568 `frDelta := inputs.frDelta`; artifacts.rs:275 `fr_delta: fr_delta_literal(),`. Statements.lean:196-198 `inputs` ties numInstances, accEncoding, k and truncatedChallenges only.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take any Valid R (none exists today, see F-2). Replace payload word 4 (omega) in `plan.vk.constants` and recompute `model` from the new plan. All 13 clauses still hold: the codehash and length constants are recomputed from the plan, `EnvAgrees` reads word 0 only, and quotient certification re-derives its seed. But the Lagrange slice now computes with `mload(OMEGA_MPTR)` different from `vk.domain.omega`, so `BlockSound R' .lagrange` fails.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** The core is confirmed. `Valid` never states that the plan is the generator's output for `R.vk`, `R.params` and `R.E`. `EnvAgrees` reads payload word 0 only, although the frozen docstring of `slots` claims the VK header, domain and G1/G2 bases agree; so payload words 1-30 and the 27 commitment words are free. `E.delta` is tied to nothing. Once some render satisfies `Valid`, changing payload word 4 (omega) and recomputing the model keeps all 13 clauses and makes `BlockSound .lagrange` false, so `blockContracts_of_valid` is false as stated. Corrections: (a) payload words 1, 2 and 7-10 are checked at run time by vkLoading against plan-view literals, so a mismatch there breaks the progress half (the sorried `vk_progress`), not soundness; (b) `plan.data` is not wholly free, since `Valid.invariants` runs the absorbed-G1 coverage check, `memoryRequirements` and the quotient operand model on it, but nothing equates it with `Data.new`; (c) the quotient identity list only matters for the per-deployment quotient row, which the generic hinge excludes; (d) the delta gap is recorded in v1 material (Inv.lean dropped-list, docs/plans/P-DELTA-GAP.md) though absent from V2 registers; (e) PLAN-V2 intends a constructor clause after WP-G4, which would tie payload and data but not `E.delta`. Today the finding is conditional, because `Valid` is uninhabited.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:533-579: the ten `EnvAgrees` fields; the only one reading the payload is :579 `vkDigestWord : (PV.vk.constants.getD 0 ("", 0)).2 = frWord vk.transcriptRepr`; :473 'delta : E.delta = (Nat.cast blsDeltaLiteral : Fr p). STILL DROPPED'. formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:203-205 docstring of `slots`: 'The payload words the contract loads (VK header, field constants, domain, G1/G2 bases) agree with the environment and VK the Lean verifier uses.'; :147-168 `vkPins`/`vkProgressPins` pin table constants, payload length, instance count and codehash, no payload word; :196-198 `inputs` ties numInstances, accEncoding, k, truncatedChallenges only. formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAst.lean:695 `ylet "omega" (yop Op.mload [yvar "OMEGA_MPTR"])`, :700 `mload [yvar "OMEGA_INV_TO_L_MPTR"]`, :717 `mload [yvar "N_INV_MPTR"]`. Blocks.lean:92-101: `BlockSound` allows only revert, `exitOk` (Clean at the next checkpoint) or normal-and-Poisoned; Inv.lean:779-787 `Inv .lagrange` pins lLast, lBlind, l0 computed by Lean from `vk.domain.omega`. `LoweringPlan.validateGeneratorInvariants` (LoweringPlan.lean:534-618) has no payload-header check; it does use `L.data` at :540, :545, :599. formal/Midfall/Generator/Artifacts.lean:496-499 '`validate_layout().unwrap_or_else(panic)` is NOT folded in here'; :568 `frDelta := inputs.frDelta`; Rust artifacts.rs:275 `fr_delta: fr_delta_literal(),`, :337-339 `verifier.validate_layout().unwrap_or_else(...)`. vkLoading run-time header checks: GenAst.lean:941-954 `eq(mload(NUM_INSTANCES_MPTR), numInstances)`, `eq(mload(K_MPTR), k)` and four accumulator words. Program.lean:46-49 `planViewOf` passes `L.data` unchanged. Recorded part: STATUS.md:280-289 (section 6 under-pins); DECISIONS-PENDING.md section 4 (three base pins) and section 8 (Gap B).
- **Suggested fix:** End WP-G4 with a single clause `plan = LoweringPlan.new (inputsOf R)` and `qblocks = compactBlocks plan`, where `inputsOf` is built from R.vk, R.params and R.cfg. Until then add explicit payload pins for words 1-30 and both commitment lists, and a `Halo2Verifier.validateLayout R.model = .ok ()` clause.

### F-7 — Typed-in deployment plan inputs contradict what Rust derives, and WP-G4's acceptance test compares the ported constructor against them

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → medium · **Kind:** fidelity-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (The PCS sizes only, and only after the baseline (PoseidonValid.lean clause 3 at commit 2041977). (4) WP-G4's acceptance is equality with this typed-in plan.

It therefore fails WP-G4's field-by-field KAT against `poseidonPlan` on `memory.map`, `pcsMemoryRequirements` and `quotient.build`.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** (1) `poseidonLayoutConfig.transcriptWords := 128` contradicts the Rust derivation, which sets it to the transcript bound, 200 for this circuit; VK_MPTR = 0x1000 + 200*32 = 0x2900 is that bound, so the docstring's claim that the value is unobservable is wrong. `poseidonVkMptr` is typed in although `staticWorkingMemorySizeForMeta` is ported. (2) The typed PCS sizes (28, 65, 0, 0, no trace MSM) differ from what the ported `memoryRequirements` computes on the derived meta and Data (3, 33, 6, 33, 39 terms). This is the project's own HEAD measurement and an inference through the Lean port, not a Rust observation. (4) WP-G4's acceptance compares the ported constructor field by field with this typed-in plan, so a faithful port fails it on `memory.map`, `pcsMemoryRequirements` and `quotient.plan/build`. The missing quotient callees are covered only by WP-G4's inventory step and the G.5 probe.

### F-8 — Stale inventory: compact_quotient_computation_blocks is described as unported in the frozen surface and in WP-G3, but it has been ported since 2026-10-27

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → low · **Kind:** doc-inaccuracy · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:66-70; formal/Midfall/Generator/Quotient/QuotientBlocks.lean:856; formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:256-258; formal/Midfall/Generator/LoweringPlan.lean:49-51,75-77; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonV2.lean:193-197
- **Claim (reviewer):** The frozen docstring of `Render`, PLAN-V2 WP-G3 (budgeted at 2-4 tickets to port it), the LoweringPlan.lean header and PoseidonV2.lean all call QRS:2000 unported. `compactQuotientComputationBlocks` exists (commit 3b8aaea, P-10c) with the Evaluator it needs. What is actually missing for the quotient side is the glue that `LoweringPlan::new` calls: `quotient_program_plan` (quotient.rs:426), `build_quotient_program_items` (:2102, :2120), `compact_quotient_program_for` (:166), `quotient_template_program` (:189), `native_callback_scratch_words` (:1448) and `quotient_stack_words_for_build` (:314). None of these has a Lean definition; `generateVk` runs only over the `VkBuildEnv` stubs. The Lean port of QRS:2000 has itself never been compared with Rust output.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Statements.lean:68-69 "`qblocks` is the output of the unported QRS:2000 `compact_quotient_computation_blocks` (GAP G3)". QuotientBlocks.lean:856 `def compactQuotientComputationBlocks {F : Type} [BEq F] (field : FieldCtx F)`, and its header lists `compact_quotient_computation_blocks (Q:2000-2099)` among the ported items. PLAN-V2.md:256 "**WP-G3 Port QRS:2000 `compact_quotient_computation_blocks`** (2-4)." A search of formal/Midfall/Generator finds no definition named quotientProgramPlan, buildQuotientProgramItems, quotientTemplateProgram, compactQuotientProgramFor, nativeCallbackScratchWords or quotientStackWordsForBuild.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** `compact_quotient_computation_blocks` (QRS:2000) has been ported since commit 3b8aaea (2026-10-27) as `compactQuotientComputationBlocks`, together with `quotient_identity_parts`. The frozen `Render` docstring, PLAN-V2 WP-G3, the LoweringPlan.lean header, the DeployedProfile.lean header and PoseidonV2.lean still call it unported. The port is used only in its own KATs on a synthetic fixture. ### F-10 — CodegenAgrees is token-stream equality, not parse equality, and two helper parsers fail silently

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** statement-weaker-than-claimed · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (The builtin-versus-call blindness is recorded in the YulGolden.lean and GenAst.lean headers. The lenient constant parser, the missing literal KAT and the guard block's absence from `mainClosed` are not.) · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Program.lean:58-60,128-134,186-194; formal/Midfall/Generator/YulGolden.lean:7-21; formal/Midfall/Lowering/InlineConstants.lean:96-112; formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAstDispatch.lean:131-135
- **Claim (reviewer):** The tie between the AST and the text is equality of `yulTokens` streams. No parser exists, so "the AST is the parse of the text" is not established: `.builtin` and `.call` print alike, as the project documents. `constTableOf` uses `filterMap`, so a constant line it cannot split is dropped, and `natOfLiteral` reads a non-literal right-hand side by keeping its digits. `emitPcsFromPlan` maps a generator fault to the empty block. Each is caught only indirectly, by `mainClosed`, `Valid.model` or a completeness proof, and `mainClosed` does not cover the ABI guard block, which reads NUM_INSTANCE_CPTR and ERR_BAD_CALLDATA_SHAPE. The KAT that the frozen docstring requires (every constant line parses to a literal) does not exist.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Program.lean:191-192 `yulTokens (YulSemantics.EVM.print (abiGuardBlock m)) == abiGuardTokens m && yulTokens (YulSemantics.EVM.print (mainAsmRaw pv)) == mainAssemblyTokens m`; :56-57 "until then a KAT must check that every `uint256 internal constant` line of `renderConstants` parses to a literal"; :133 `mainClosed` scans `mainAsmRaw pv` only. YulGolden.lean:16-18 "a port that emits '.call' where the Rust means the built-in passes every token gate". InlineConstants.lean:100 `s.toList.foldl (fun acc c => if c.isDigit then acc * 10 + (c.toNat - '0'.toNat) else acc) 0`. GenAstDispatch.lean:135 `| .error _ => []`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** A constant line `uint256 internal constant X = A + 1;` enters the table as (X, 1). A constant whose trailing comment contains `=` or `;` is dropped from the table without an error.
- **Suggested fix:** Make `solConstantLine?` return an error on any right-hand side that is not a literal, and add the literal KAT. Extend `mainClosed` to `abiGuardBlock`. Make `emitPcsFromPlan` total by stating `computationsAst .. = .ok _` as a `Valid` clause.

No clause of `Valid` or `EnvAgrees` detects it: the delta clause was dropped and only payload word 0 is tied. Partially recorded: Gap B in DECISIONS-PENDING section 8; the decoder placeholder only as 'blocks `end_to_end`'; the delta gap only in v1 material, not in STATUS, QUEUE, PLAN-V2 or DECISIONS-PENDING.

Permutation.lean:408 `let mut currentDelta := (beta * x) * powVartime delta (chunkIndex * chunkLen)`; Native.lean:182 `Verifier.prepare ... E.g1 E.delta F`. Inv.lean:487-489 'the fixture's VerifierEnv.delta is 2 ..., a placeholder no exhibit ties to anything, so the clause would be false at the only Surface that could check it'. Vk.lean:359-366: `VerifierBuildInputs` carries `vk : VerifyingKey (Fr p) G₁`, the Exec VK. DECISIONS-PENDING.md:111 'Decide `g1CodecZ.ofCompressed?` (same discrete-log class, blocks `end_to_end`)'. ### F+2 — Snapshot.lin is always none, so no block contract constrains QUOTIENT_EVAL or the selector accumulators; BlockSound .pcs is false as stated for any faithful PCS slice (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Lowering/Snapshot.lean:30-40,236-249; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:788-813; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:92-101,152-154; proofs/solidity-verifier/target/poseidon-fixture-dump/Halo2Verifier.sol:2537,2670
- **Claim:** A planned obligation is unprovable as stated. `snapshotOf` sets `lin := none` on every path, so the `Inv .quotient` clauses for QUOTIENT_EVAL_MPTR and the selector buckets are `true`. Two consequences. (1) The quotient row is satisfied by the present two-store slice, so the contract layer cannot tell a program with the quotient numerator from one without it; this is why F-2's claim that `poseidon_quotient_block` is false does not hold. (2) `BlockSound R .pcs` has precondition `Clean R .quotient`, which leaves the memory at QUOTIENT_EVAL_MPTR and SELECTOR_ACC_MPTR unconstrained, while the real PCS region reads both and its postcondition pins PAIRING_LHS/RHS to the Lean snapshot. A state with arbitrary words there satisfies the precondition and violates the postcondition under honest precompiles. So the `.pcs` component of `blockContracts_of_valid` is false for any render whose PCS slice is the generator's, independently of what `Valid` says. The same holds for the REVERSED_EVALS window (source records `gap_reversedEvalsWindow`). The hole is recorded in v1 source as 'D6 limit 1' but appears in no V2 plan, queue or decision document; QUEUE S.2 covers a different pcs/Inv issue (the empty slice). Section 6 and `Inv` are refinable, so the repair needs no frozen edit, but it needs the Exec `linearize` widened or the values recomputed.
- **Evidence:** Snapshot.lean:249 `pure { toSnapLagrange := s3, queries := queries, lin := none }`; header :37-40 'Snapshot.lin is an Option snapshotOf sets to none, and the Inv clauses that read it are inert until the hole is filled'. Inv.lean:792-802 `(if Checkpoint.quotient ≤ k then (holdsFr st (ptrAddr M.quotientMptr) S.alg.splittingFactor) && (holdsFr st (ptrAddr M.quotientMptr + 32) (1 - S.alg.xn)) && (match S.lin with | none => true | some L => (holdsFr st (ptrAddr M.quotientEvalMptr) (linComEval L.linCom)) && ...`; :807-813 `Inv .pcs` requires `holdsG1 E st (ptrAddr M.pairingLhsMptr) S.c.dual.left.eval` and the RHS twin. Checkpoint.lean:87-93 `Partial.pre .pcs = .quotient`. Blocks.lean:94-97: `BlockSound` precondition `Clean R q.pre Snap ...`, postcondition revert, `exitOk` or normal-and-Poisoned. Dump: :2537 `mstore(QUOTIENT_EVAL_MPTR, linearization_expected_eval)` (numerator region); inside the PCS region (2593-3000) `mstore(0x6a60, QUOTIENT_EVAL_MPTR)` (:2670) and `mstore(0x7cc0, mulmod(lin_query_scalar_31, mload(add(SELECTOR_ACC_MPTR, 0x0)), r))`. `rg -i 'linearization hole|D6 limit|Snapshot.lin'` over STATUS.md, QUEUE.md, DECISIONS-PENDING.md, PLAN-V2.md, EXECUTION-V2.md, SORRY.md and design.md returns nothing.

### F+3 — The sorried vk_progress is false as stated for Valid renders: D-P50's six pins do not cover the six payload header words vkLoading checks (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks/VkLoading.lean:751-756; formal/Midfall/Lowering/GenAst.lean:941-954; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:147-168
- **Claim:** `vk_progress` claims `BlockProgress R .vkLoading` from `R.Valid`, `vkPins` and `vkProgressPins`. The block ANDs six run-time header comparisons into `success` and then calls `fail(ERR_VK_MISMATCH)` if it is zero. The payload is whatever `R.plan.vk` carries (`Frame.vkCode`), and no clause of `Valid` relates payload words 1, 2 and 7-10 to `pv.numInstances`, `pv.k` and the four accumulator words. Take a Valid render (none exists today), change payload word 2 and recompute the model: all 13 clauses still hold, every run of the block reverts, and total progress fails. The statement is vacuous today and false once `Valid` is inhabited, provided some calldata passes the snapshot and `DualMSM.check` gates. The pins are true by computation at the poseidon payload, so the repair is six more decidable pins or the WP-G4 constructor clause. This is a concrete instance of F-6 on an existing registered sorry, and it shows D-P50's claim that the row needs no further pin hypothesis is incomplete.
- **Evidence:** VkLoading.lean:751-755 `theorem vk_progress (R : Render p G₁ G₂ Gₜ) (product : PairingProduct) (hR : R.Valid) (hpins : vkPins R) (hppins : vkProgressPins R) ... : G1CodecLaws R.E.g1 → ChallengesGood R.vk Snap → DualMSM.check Snap.c.dual R.params = true → BlockProgress R Partial.vkLoading Snap H vkAddr product cd := by sorry`; :760-762 'SINCE D-P50 IT CARRIES NO PIN HYPOTHESES AT ALL'. GenAst.lean:941-954 `yset "success" (yop Op.and [yvar "success", yop Op.eq [yop Op.mload [yvar "NUM_INSTANCES_MPTR"], ylit numInstances]])`, likewise K_MPTR, HAS_ACCUMULATOR_MPTR, ACC_OFFSET_MPTR, NUM_ACC_LIMBS_MPTR, NUM_ACC_LIMB_BITS_MPTR, then `yif (yop Op.iszero [yvar "success"]) [yexpr (ycall "fail" [yvar "ERR_VK_MISMATCH"])]`. Statements.lean:147-168: the six pins are the table's VK_MPTR, EXPECTED_VK_PAYLOAD_LENGTH, `R.pv.numInstances = R.cfg.numInstances`, window disjointness, EXPECTED_VK_LENGTH and EXPECTED_VK_CODEHASH_WORD. Blocks.lean:61 `vkCode : st.env.extCodeOf vkAddr = R.plan.vk.runtimeBytes.data.toList`; :106-111 `BlockProgress` is `StmtsTotal` with postcondition `exitOk`. Inv.lean:579: the only payload-content clause is word 0.

### F — errors the skeptic found in the reviewer's report

- F-2: the claim that the sorried `poseidon_quotient_block` is false as stated is not supported. `snapshotOf` always sets `lin := none` (formal/Midfall/Lowering/Snapshot.lean:249), so `Inv .quotient` adds only the two QUOTIENT_MPTR words (Inv.lean:792-797), which the emitted slice writes. The contract is too weak to notice the missing numerator, not false.
- F-2: 'At HEAD ... completeness conjunct 2 fails instead' is not established. It needs a calldata with `LeanAccepts` at `poseidonRender`; none is known (DECISIONS-PENDING.md section 3), and with identity VK commitments, delta = 2 and sG2 = g₂ the honest fixture proof is unlikely to be one. Falsity of the headline is demonstrated structurally only at 6481731, where the PCS slice is empty.
- F-2: the all-zero-proof counterexample is not checked against the Lean side. That the Lean verifier rejects it at `poseidonRender` is plausible (v is generically non-zero and the G1 generator is non-trivial) but was neither computed nor derived.
- F-5: 'By history the dump and the Rust source are currently in step: the last commit touching both is 9822446' is wrong. 9822446 changed src/api.rs and added only the two `.direct.sol` dumps; the main dump was last committed in 5312cb7. The project's own regeneration found the committed Halo2Verifier.sol stale in BUILD_ID (formal/docs/QUEUE.md:630-633, R.5).
- F-5: `already_recorded` understates the HEAD record. QUEUE R.5 (commit f2fe99b) records that the committed fixture is not reproducible and that the tracked dump is stale; only the CI gap is unrecorded.
- F-6: 'plan.data ... is tied to plan.memory by nothing' is overstated. `Valid.invariants` runs `validateAbsorbedG1PrecompileCoverage L.meta_ L.data L.memory L.proofLayout`, compares `memoryRequirements L.meta_ L.data` with the planned sizes, and builds the quotient operand model from `L.data` (LoweringPlan.lean:540, :545, :599). It is not equated with `Data.new`, but it is not free.
- F-6: listing payload words 1 (num_instances) and 2 (k) as tied by nothing omits that vkLoading compares them at run time with plan-view literals (GenAst.lean:941-944). A mismatch makes the contract always revert, which breaks the progress half, not soundness.
- F-6: `recorded_where` says the delta gap is not recorded. It is recorded in v1 material: the dropped-list in formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:473-490 and formal/docs/plans/P-DELTA-GAP.md. It is absent from the V2 registers. The reviewer also missed that `poseidonE.delta := 2` at the deployment (PoseidonEnv.lean:230).
- F-7: the title says the typed-in PCS sizes 'contradict what Rust derives'. For the PCS sizes the evidence is the Lean port's `memoryRequirements` on the derived meta and Data, not a Rust observation; only `transcriptWords` is pinned directly by Rust text plus VK_MPTR. 'No card exports or ports' the quotient plan builders is also slightly too strong: WP-G4's inventory step and probe card G.5 are meant to spawn those ports.
- F-4: the stated total 'about 54 kB' does not match the reviewer's own figures, which sum to 56,039 bytes (32,333 + 23,706). The severity also ignores that PLAN-V2 WP-G3 and WP-G4 already make the PCS and quotient-block comparisons acceptance criteria at the fixture (PLAN-V2.md:253-258).
- F-8: severity medium is too high for a stale inventory. WP-G4 already says to inventory callees and port the missing ones, and probe cards G.5/G.6 (QUEUE.md:1105-1106) exist to re-measure by `#check`.

### F — checked and found correct

- ProofCalldataLayout.fromProtocol and takeCommitmentSection are a faithful port, including both assert_eq panic sites and the cursor order: formal/Midfall/Generator/Abi.lean:292-417 against proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/abi/proof.rs:95-245.
- TranscriptBufferLayout.fromProofLayout is faithful, including add-before-break and the two cushion spellings: Abi.lean:530-567 against abi/proof.rs:318-368.
- VerifierMemoryLayout.new and its three helpers match let for let, with the same registration order, alignment and the 23-term trace bound: formal/Midfall/Generator/LayoutMemory.lean:103-414 against proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/layout/memory.rs:591-971. The layout constants in Layout.lean:76-133 and 728-749 match layout/mod.rs. I did not compare the arena allocator bodies.
- ConstraintSystemMeta.new, validateAgainstProtocol, setNumPointSets, setNumDummyEvals and numMainEvals are faithful: formal/Midfall/Generator/Encoding.lean:454-527 against encoding/mod.rs:141-218. The defect in F-1 is in the frozen clause, not in this port.
- ProtocolPlan.fromConstraintSystem and the remapping closure are faithful, including the eval-stream versus PCS-stream order difference, the I-6 assert and the validate panic: formal/Midfall/Generator/Protocol.lean:566-802 against protocol/mod.rs:327-644.

## G — assumptions, axioms, trusted base

**Reviewer summary.** Read-only audit; no Lean was run, so every claim below rests on reading the source and should be verified before it is relied on. HEAD moved from 6481731 to f2fe99b during the audit; the changed in-scope files (PoseidonEnv, PoseidonValid) were re-checked and the findings hold at both commits.

**What is sound.** The trusted base is consistent. There are exactly five axioms, all in Assumptions/CurvePairing.lean, and none in vendor. They have a model, and they are true of the real pairing on the subgroups generated by H1 and H2. G1 is a real curve group over Fp; G2 is a discrete-log carrier (ZMod r) with a real-bytes encoder. Both primes are kernel-certified. native_decide is confined to Instances/. keccak256 is pure Lean, not @[extern]. The sorry register matches my independent count (60 of 60) at 6481731. Eip2537.Holds is satisfiable and assumes nothing beyond real EIP-2537 behaviour.

**What is not.** The deployment instance is not the deployed verifier, in ways the project has only partly recorded:

- **G-1 (critical):** LeanAccepts is false for every calldata at poseidonRender, because verifyMsgs re-reads G1 points through `ofCompressed?`, which is `fun _ => none` there. The headline then says the contract never returns; poseidon_rejects is false once the program is complete.
- **G-2 (high):** the five axioms are derivable, so unnecessary, and leave the generators opaque, so every G1/G2 base pin is unprovable.
- **G-3 (high):** the recommended Gap B fix needs a G2 torsion theorem or a new assumption; "no new axiom" is wrong.
- **G-4, G-5:** Valid does not pin the VK commitments or E.delta. The deployment uses identity commitments and delta := 2.
- **G-6, G-7:** `permissive` excludes the real "missing precompile succeeds with empty returndata" behaviour, and the real-world precompile assumption is stated nowhere.

Three low findings cover documentation and gates.

### G-1 — LeanAccepts is identically false at poseidonRender: verifyMsgs round-trips through a placeholder compressed codec

- **Severity (reviewer):** critical · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → critical · **Kind:** statement-vacuous · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (DECISIONS-PENDING.md §8 last bullet and design.md:1820 (ofCompressed? placeholder, said to block end_to_end only); REVIEW.md R-72 ("no accepted calldata at any model in the tree")) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Native.lean:164-170,269-292; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Transcript.lean:69,145-151; formal/Midfall/Instances/G1Agreement.lean:2002-2006; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:379-380 (at 6481731; 229-230 at f2fe99b); formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:278,306; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:263-267
- **Claim (reviewer):** verifyMsgs (hence LeanAccepts, snapshotOf, verifyNative and calldataOf) reads every G1 point through E.g1.ofCompressed?. The deployment codec g1CodecZ has `ofCompressed? := fun _ => none` and a flagless x-only toCompressed. So for every H and cd, `LeanAccepts poseidonRender H cd` is false and `snapshotOf poseidonE ...` is never `.ok`. Consequences at the deployment: (a) headline conjunct 2 and every BlockSound/BlockProgress row are vacuous; (b) conjunct 1 and `poseidon_rejects` assert that the generated contract never returns, which is false once the emitted program is the real contract; (c) EndToEnd is `False <-> False`; (d) the D-P45 repair of HypothesesInhabited, which adds LeanAccepts, is provably false at this render. The project records ofCompressed? only as blocking end_to_end.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Native.lean:170 `| Msg.g1 P => E.g1.toCompressed P`. Native.lean:278-281 `let bytes := ms.foldl (init := Bytes.empty) (fun acc m => ByteArray.append acc (msgWire E m))` followed by `(prepare (Gₜ := Gₜ) E F vk [[1]] [[inst.toList]]).run (Transcript.initWithHash H bytes)`. Transcript.lean:69 `readRepr? := c.ofCompressed?`. Transcript.lean:150-151 `match h.readRepr? repr with | none => .error (Fault.error (Error.transcript IoErrorKind.other))`. MultiOpen.lean:278 `let fCom ← tmRead (hashableG1 g1)` (unconditional in multiPrepare.phaseB). G1Agreement.lean:2005-2006 `toCompressed := fun Q => bytesNatBE (g1zPair Q).1 fpBytes` / `ofCompressed? := fun _ => none`. PoseidonEnv.lean:380 `{ g1 := g1CodecZ, field := poseidonFc, delta := 2, fewerPointSetsRuntime := false }`. Snapshot.lean:166-167 uses the same msgWire/initWithHash route. DECISIONS-PENDING.md:115 (6481731) records only: "Decide `g1CodecZ.ofCompressed?` (same discrete-log class, blocks `end_to_end`)"; :50-51 expects the accepting-calldata witness to "be proved, not evaluated".
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take any H, cd, ms, inst. `verifyMsgs poseidonE H Features.solidity poseidonParams poseidonVkExec inst ms` binds prepareMsgs, which must pass multiPrepare.phaseB's `tmRead (hashableG1 g1CodecZ)`. Transcript.read returns `.error` because `g1CodecZ.ofCompressed? _ = none`. So verifyMsgs is never `.ok ()`, and ¬LeanAccepts holds for all cd. SoundSide holds for all cd (the error is a transcript error, not the logup panic). With the product from holds_exhibited, Rejects therefore demands that every run on an honest valid proof reverts.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed, and it is wider than stated. For EVERY render whose E.g1 is g1CodecZ (so poseidonRender), every vk, inst, ms and H: prepareMsgs/verifyMsgs/verifyNative/snapshotOf are never .ok, because Verifier.prepare cannot return .ok without passing multiPrepare.phaseB's unconditional `tmRead (hashableG1 g1)`, and Transcript.read fails when `readRepr? = ofCompressed? = fun _ => none`. No try/catch exists on the path (the only match on the multiPrepare result re-throws both error arms). Hence at poseidonRender: (1) LeanAccepts is false for all H, cd; (2) SoundSide and CompleteSide are true for all cd (the first G1 read, in parseTrace or Logup.readMultiplicities, precedes linearize where the logup.rs:460 panic lives; CompleteSide is vacuous because snapshotOf is never .ok); (3) Render.BlockContractsAt is vacuously true for every Partial, so poseidon_quotient_block and the hinge AT THIS RENDER are provable in a few lines with no block proof, which makes the sorry-cone count a misleading progress measure here; (4) the headline reduces to 'no run of poseidonRender.prog ever returns', i.e. poseidon_rejects plus rejects_of_not_accepts; (5) D-P45's proposed gate (adds LeanAccepts) is unsatisfiable at this render; (6) EndToEnd is False<->False. Point (4) is false in the standard model once the emitted program is the real contract and a valid proof exists; that part is a semantic claim, not something refutable in Lean without exhibiting a run. Also: g1CodecZ.toCompressed is x-only with no flag/sign byte, so it is not injective (Q and Q^-1 collide) and must be replaced together with ofCompressed?. The project's recorded reason for the placeholder (a real decoder needs a discrete log / an r-torsion-to-zpowers route the tree lacks) is wrong for G1: G1PointCount.lean already proves that route.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** G1Agreement.lean:2002-2006 `noncomputable def g1CodecZ : G1Codec G₁z where ... toCompressed := fun Q => bytesNatBE (g1zPair Q).1 fpBytes / ofCompressed? := fun _ => none`. PoseidonEnv.lean:229-230 `noncomputable def poseidonE ... := { g1 := g1CodecZ, field := poseidonFc, delta := 2, fewerPointSetsRuntime := false }`; PoseidonV2.lean:153 `E := poseidonE`, :169 `poseidonRender.E = poseidonE := rfl`. Transcript.lean:69 `readRepr? := c.ofCompressed?`; :150-151 `match h.readRepr? repr with | none => .error (Fault.error (Error.transcript IoErrorKind.other))`. MultiOpen.lean:278 `let fCom ← tmRead (hashableG1 g1)`, :338 `let b ← phaseB g1 fc feat a`. Verifier.lean:1061-1066 `match (pcs.multiPrepare queries).run t with | .ok ... | .error (Fault.panic site) => throw (Fault.panic site) | .error (Fault.error _) => throw (Fault.error Error.opening)`; :1084-1086 prepare = parseTrace then verifyAlgebraicConstraints. Native.lean:278-283 prepareMsgs re-serialises with msgWire (:170 `| Msg.g1 P => E.g1.toCompressed P`) and runs `prepare` on `Transcript.initWithHash H bytes`; :291-292 verifyMsgs binds it. Snapshot.lean:163-167 transcriptOf uses the same msgWire route, :260 `let (b, t4) ← (phaseB E.g1 E.field feat a).run t3`, :353 snapshotOf binds snapshotPcsRun. Statements.lean:263-267 LeanAccepts requires `verifyMsgs ... = .ok ()`. Blocks.lean:116-127 BlockContractsAt is under `snapshotOf R.E H R.vk inst ms none = .ok Snap`; :134-139 Rejects. SoundSide: Verifier.lean:182 `let m ← Logup.readMultiplicities chunked (hashableG1 g1)` and Logup.lean:145 `let multiplicities ← tmRead h` run in parseTrace, before linearize (Logup.lean:279 is the panic site). Recorded only as: DECISIONS-PENDING.md:111 `Decide g1CodecZ.ofCompressed? (same discrete-log class, blocks end_to_end)`; :50-52 expects the D-P45 witness to 'be proved, not evaluated'. Fix is available in-tree: G1PointCount.lean:238 `theorem torsion_eq_zpowers_gen (X : W.Point) (hX : blsR • X = 0) : ∃ k : ℤ, k • emb genFrame = X`, :502 `g1zToEip_surjective_on_accepted`.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
import Midfall.Instances.PoseidonV2
open Midfall Midfall.Halo2 Midfall.Instances
theorem read_g1CodecZ_never_ok (t : Transcript) (v : G₁z × Transcript) :
    Transcript.read (hashableG1 g1CodecZ) t ≠ .ok v := by
  unfold Transcript.read
  cases bytesTake t.buffer t.pos (hashableG1 g1CodecZ).width with
  | none => simp
  | some repr => simp [hashableG1, g1CodecZ]
-- then the target: theorem leanAccepts_never (H : Hash) (cd : Bytes) : ¬ Midfall.LoweringV2.LeanAccepts Midfall.Instances.PoseidonV2.poseidonRender H cd
-- and the vacuity corollary: example (product) : Midfall.Instances.PoseidonV2.poseidonRender.BlockContractsAt product (fun _ => True)  -- expected provable from snapshotOf never .ok
```
- **Suggested fix:** Install a real compressed codec on G₁z before any further block work. toCompressed must be the real 48-byte flagged encoding (as G1Affine.toCompressed in Instances/Bls12_381.lean). ofCompressed? can be Classical.choose over its image, with injectivity from ptPair_injective plus the sign bit. Then add `¬ (∀ cd, ¬ LeanAccepts R H cd)` (or D-P45's LeanAccepts conjunct) as a gate that is actually discharged at the deployment. Until then, record that the headline at poseidonRender is degenerate, not merely that end_to_end is blocked.

### G-2 — The five D-P36a axioms are derivable (unnecessary) and leave the generators opaque, so every G1/G2 base pin is unprovable at the deployment

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (Gap B (DECISIONS-PENDING §8, QUEUE R.4) covers only sG2 := g₂; QUEUE F.2 lists `g1BaseWords` as a row to decide ("NOT-COMPUTABLE (depends on an axiom ...)"). Neither the independence nor the eliminability of the axioms is recorded.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Assumptions/CurvePairing.lean:69-103; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:311; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:573,617,623; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks/FinalPairing.lean:234-236; formal/docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:64-71,107-110 (6481731); formal/docs/design.md:1359-1375
- **Claim (reviewer):** (i) `bls12_381Pairing : AdmissiblePairing G₁z G2fold blsGT` and its four companions add no mathematical content. An admissible pairing on these carriers is constructible with the standard three axioms: G₁z is a 1-dimensional ZMod r-space and G2fold is literally Multiplicative (ZMod r), so `e x y := f x • y` with `f` a linear functional sending H1 to 1 works, with Gₜ := Exp blsR. D-P36's dichotomy (assume, or build Fp12 and the ate pairing) missed this. (ii) The axiom does not say which elements its `g₁` and `g₂` are. The Lean verifier uses `P.g₁⁻¹` as an MSM base and EnvAgrees forces `params.nG2Prepared = P.g₂⁻¹`, while the contract uses the payload bytes of H1 and H2. Hence `g1CodecZ.toEip bls12_381Pairing.g₁ = bytes(H1)` and `g2foldToEip bls12_381Pairing.g₂ = bytes(H2)` are independent of the axioms. The D-P46 draft `baseSlots`, v1's `g1BaseWords`/`g2BaseWords`, and FP.0's `fpG2Slots` cannot be proved at poseidonRender. The recommended Gap B repair (a) (redefine nG2Prepared/sG2Prepared from bytes) would instead make the currently proved `EnvAgrees.nG2` unprovable.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** CurvePairing.lean:101 `axiom bls12_381Pairing : AdmissiblePairing G₁z G2fold blsGT`; :98-100 "Nothing here says the pairing is the BLS12-381 ate pairing ... or that its generators are the published ones". MultiOpen.lean:311 `{ scalars := #[b.x3, v], bases := #[pi, P.g₁⁻¹],`. Inv.lean:573 `nG2 : params.nG2Prepared = P.g₂⁻¹`; :617 `g1BaseWords : vkG1Words PV vkG1BaseSlot = E.g1.toEip P.g₁`. PoseidonEnv.lean:386 `noncomputable def poseidonSG2 : G2fold := bls12_381Pairing.g₂`. DECISIONS-PENDING.md:68 `baseSlots : vkG1Words R.pv vkG1BaseSlot = R.E.g1.toEip P.g₁ ∧`; :110 "No new axiom." design.md D-P36: "(b) Construct Fp12 and a real ate pairing in Lean ... genuinely research-grade". HoldsFinal.lean:71-73 says expAdmissible "is NOT a model of the assumption itself".
- **Counterexample / witness:** Two models of the five axioms: M1 with g₁ := g1zGen, g₂ := g2foldExp 1; M2 with g₁ := g1zGen², g₂ := g2foldExp 2 (same e up to scalar). In M2 the Lean verifier's `-v·g₁` term and `nG2Prepared` differ from the contract's G1_BASE and G2_BASE slots. So the pcs and finalPairing block contracts fail in M2, and no proof from the five axioms can establish them or the base-slot pins.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed with one wording correction: the five axioms are ELIMINABLE rather than literally derivable (blsGT is an opaque constant; what is provable is that the same structure exists by construction). (i) AdmissiblePairing only asks for a bilinear map, two non-identity elements and e(g₁,g₂) ≠ 0. G2fold is Multiplicative (ZMod blsR) and Additive G₁z is a ZMod blsR vector space with g1zGen ≠ 1, so e x y := f x • y with f a linear functional sending g1zGen to 1 and Gₜ := Exp blsR is an axiom-free instance; holds_staged is generic in Gₜ, so holds_exhibited goes through without D-P36a. D-P36 considered only 'assume' versus 'build Fp12 and the ate pairing'. (ii) Because bls12_381Pairing is opaque, the equations `g1CodecZ.toEip bls12_381Pairing.g₁ = <G1_BASE payload bytes>` and `g2foldToEip bls12_381Pairing.g₂ = <G2_BASE payload bytes>` are independent of the axioms (two models: g₁ = g1zGen, g₂ = g2foldExp 1, and g₁ = g1zGen², g₂ = g2foldExp 2; toEip is injective). So D-P46's draft baseSlots (G1 half and the nG2 half), FP.0's fpG2Slots first conjunct and v1's g1BaseWords cannot be proved at poseidonRender even after Gap B is resolved for sG2; and Gap B option (a), which re-derives nG2Prepared from payload bytes, would make the currently rfl-proved EnvAgrees.nG2 (`params.nG2Prepared = P.g₂⁻¹`) unprovable. The .pcs row is affected the same way, since the Lean verifier uses P.g₁⁻¹ as an MSM base while the contract uses the G1_BASE slot. Today these rows are vacuously true at poseidonRender only because of G-1.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** CurvePairing.lean:101 `axiom bls12_381Pairing : AdmissiblePairing G₁z G2fold blsGT`; :98-100 'Nothing here says ... that its generators are the published ones'. Common/Curve.lean:44-55 class fields `e`, `g₁`, `g₂`, `hG₁ : g₁ ≠ 1`, `hG₂ : g₂ ≠ 1`, `hNonDeg : e (Additive.ofMul g₁) (Additive.ofMul g₂) ≠ 0`. ExponentModel.lean:127 `abbrev Exp (p : ℕ) := Multiplicative (ZMod p)`, :144 expModule, :217 expPrimeOrderWith; G2Codec.lean:245 `abbrev G2fold : Type := Exp blsR`. G1Agreement.lean:1839 moduleZ, :2009 g1zGen. HoldsStaging.lean:99-101 `theorem holds_staged {Gₜ : Type} [Group Gₜ] [PrimeOrderWith Gₜ blsR] [Module (ZMod blsR) (Additive Gₜ)] [P : AdmissiblePairing G₁z G2fold Gₜ] : ∃ product ...`. HoldsFinal.lean:71-72 'It is NOT a model of the assumption itself'. design.md:1365-1368 lists only options (a) Assume and (b) Construct Fp12 and a real ate pairing. MultiOpen.lean:311 `bases := #[pi, P.g₁⁻¹]`. Inv.lean:573 `nG2 : params.nG2Prepared = P.g₂⁻¹`; :617 `g1BaseWords : vkG1Words PV vkG1BaseSlot = E.g1.toEip P.g₁`. PoseidonEnv.lean:236 `noncomputable def poseidonSG2 : G2fold := bls12_381Pairing.g₂`, :242 params built from it; PoseidonValid.lean:364-368 nG2/sG2 discharged by rfl. FinalPairing.lean:234-236 fpG2Slots. DECISIONS-PENDING.md:69 `baseSlots : vkG1Words R.pv vkG1BaseSlot = R.E.g1.toEip P.g₁ ∧`, :103-106 option (a) 'name both G2 bases from their bytes ... No new axiom'. QUEUE.md:573-576 expects fpG2Slots 'REFUTED or NOT-COMPUTABLE' for the Gap B reason only.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
The reviewer's `exists_admissible_no_axiom` (construct `AdmissiblePairing G₁z G2fold G2fold` with g₁ = g1zGen, g₂ = g2foldExp 1 via LinearMap.exists_leftInverse_of_injective on LinearMap.toSpanSingleton, then `#print axioms` expecting only propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound). Not run by me; the mathematics is sound, lemma names may need adjusting.
```
- **Suggested fix:** Delete the five axioms and define the deployment pairing by construction, with g₁ := g1zGen and g₂ := g2foldExp 1 and Gₜ := Exp blsR. The headline's axiom list then shrinks to the standard three plus native_decide, and the G1_BASE and G2_BASE pins become provable facts about H1 and H2. If the axiom is kept, add the two generator equations to it and to the trusted-base table. Correct D-P36's rationale.

### G-3 — Gap B option (a) is not executable: membership of the deployed s·G₂ in the image of g2foldToEip is a discrete-log existence fact the tree cannot prove

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** partially-correct → medium · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (DECISIONS-PENDING §8 and QUEUE R.4 record Gap B and call it "same discrete-log class"; the feasibility of the recommended option is not assessed.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:102-115 (6481731); formal/Midfall/Instances/G2Codec.lean:245,285,372-374; formal/Midfall/Instances/G1PointCount.lean:176,181,238
- **Claim (reviewer):** DECISIONS-PENDING §8(a) recommends naming s·G₂ by `Classical.choose` on `∃ x, encG2 x = <bytes>`, with "No new axiom. It does need proofs that the bytes lie in the encoder's image." For G2fold = Exp blsR, that proof is `∃ k : ZMod r, bytes([k]·H2) = s_g2 bytes`. It cannot be obtained by computation (k is the SRS secret), and the tree has no theorem that E'(Fp2)[r] equals the subgroup generated by H2. The G1 argument used for torsion_eq_zpowers_gen does not transfer: it needs r² to exceed the trivial point-count bound, which over Fp2 is 2p²+1, larger than r². So option (a) requires a point count or structure theorem for the twist (not in mathlib) or a new assumption. The same holds for any G2 carrier with PrimeOrderWith.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** DECISIONS-PENDING.md:107-110 "`Classical.choose` on `∃ x, encG2 x = <bytes>` ... No new axiom. It does need proofs that the bytes lie in the encoder's image." G2Codec.lean:372-374 `noncomputable def g2foldOfEip? ... if h : ∃ R : G2fold, g2foldToEip R = b then some (Classical.choose h) else none`; header :49-51 "What it is NOT is a claim that every accepted G2 frame decodes into the carrier - that is the discrete log, and 'Holds' has no g2Decodes clause". G1PointCount.lean:176 `theorem two_p_add_one_lt_r_sq : 2 * blsP + 1 < blsR * blsR := by decide`; :181 `theorem naive_bound_is_too_weak : blsP * blsP + 1 > blsR * blsR := by decide`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** The lemma needed is `∃ X : G2fold, g2foldToEip X = <EIP bytes of params.json s_g2>`. The only in-tree technique is the pigeonhole of G1PointCount (an r-torsion point outside the generated subgroup forces r² ≤ #E). Over Fp2 the elementary bound is #E'(Fp2) ≤ 2p²+1, and the tree itself proves p²+1 > r², so no contradiction arises.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** The core is right: option (a) of Gap B needs `∃ X : G2fold, g2foldToEip X = <s_g2 bytes>`, i.e. that the deployed s·G₂ lies in the subgroup generated by H2. This cannot be obtained by evaluation (the exponent is the SRS secret), the tree has no theorem that the r-torsion of the twist over Fp2 is cyclic, and the G1 pigeonhole (r² > 2p+1) does not transfer because the elementary bound over Fp2 is 2p²+1 > r². The decision text therefore understates the cost: 'It does need proofs that the bytes lie in the encoder's image' hides a missing, uncarded piece of mathematics. The overclaim to correct: 'no new axiom' is not wrong in principle. An elementary route in the style of G1PointCount exists: bound #E'(Fp2) ≤ 2p²+1, exhibit a twist point whose order is divisible by the large (about 448-bit) prime factor ℓ of the cofactor, and conclude r² ∤ #E'(Fp2) since r²ℓ > 2p²+1; that needs a kernel primality certificate for ℓ and two long scalar multiplications over Fp2. So the correct statement is: option (a) is not executable as scoped; it requires either a new twist-torsion package (days, not a choose_spec one-liner) or an explicit SRS well-formedness assumption.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** DECISIONS-PENDING.md:103-106 '(a) Recommended: name both G2 bases from their bytes ... Classical.choose on ∃ x, encG2 x = <bytes> ... No new axiom. It does need proofs that the bytes lie in the encoder's image.' G2Codec.lean:245 `abbrev G2fold : Type := Exp blsR`; :285 `def g2foldToEip (R : G2fold) : Bytes := g2PointBytes (g2foldFrame R).1`; :372-374 `g2foldOfEip? ... if h : ∃ R : G2fold, g2foldToEip R = b then some (Classical.choose h) else none`; :49-51 'What it is NOT is a claim that every accepted G2 frame decodes into the carrier - that is the discrete log, and Holds has no g2Decodes clause'. G1PointCount.lean:176 `theorem two_p_add_one_lt_r_sq : 2 * blsP + 1 < blsR * blsR := by decide`; :181 `theorem naive_bound_is_too_weak : blsP * blsP + 1 > blsR * blsR := by decide`; :236 'THE INEQUALITY IS CURVE-SPECIFIC: what does the work is r² > 2p+1'. rg for torsion/zpowers/Nat.card in Instances/G2Codec.lean and G2Agreement.lean finds no G2 membership or count theorem (only comments, e.g. G2Codec.lean:279 'No point count and no Fp2 pigeonhole').
- **Suggested fix:** Treat SRS well-formedness as an explicit, named deployment hypothesis rather than something to compute. For example, quantify the deployment theorem over `s : ZMod r` with hypothesis `vkG2Words pv vkNegSG2BaseSlot = encG2 (ofAdd s)⁻¹` (or one named assumption stating exactly that for the fixture bytes), and set sG2Prepared := ofAdd s. This is option (b)/(c) stated honestly. Record that option (a) needs #E'(Fp2) or the cyclicity of its r-torsion.

### G-4 — Valid does not tie the payload's commitment words or G1 base to the Lean VK; the deployment's Lean VK has all-identity commitments and still passes vkPoints

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (STATUS.md "STRUCTURAL FINDING - §6 systematically under-pins the memory layout" records the class (fpG2Slots, RETURN_MPTR, vkPins) and that the hinge "may be unprovable as stated". PoseidonEnv's header records the identity commitments as a "fallback shape". The missing commitments pin and its conseq […]) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:118-134,182-217; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:540-579,741,810-812; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:345-350,367-373 (6481731; 195-200 at f2fe99b); formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonValid.lean:411-415 (6481731; 441-445 at f2fe99b); formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:152-154
- **Claim (reviewer):** No clause of Render.Valid, SupportedV2 or EnvAgrees relates `R.plan.vk.fixedComms/permutationComms` (the bytes the contract MSMs with) to `R.vk.fixedCommitments/permutation.commitments` (what verifyMsgs uses), nor the G1_BASE header slot to `P.g₁`. Valid constrains the Lean commitments only by count (WellFormed) and `ptReadable (toEip C)`. So Valid, R.prog and R.pv are invariant under replacing `R.vk.fixedCommitments`, while LeanAccepts is not. For any render where the headline is non-vacuous, the sorried hinge `blockContracts_of_valid` is false at `.pcs`: `Inv .pcs` pins memory to `Snap.c.finalCom.eval`, computed from the Lean commitments. The deployment exercises this: poseidonVkExec's 19+8 commitments are `Array.replicate n (1 : G₁z)`, and `SupportedV2.vkPoints` is PROVED there because they are all the identity.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Statements.lean:124-125 `vkPoints : ∀ C ∈ R.vk.fixedCommitments.toList ++ R.vk.permutation.commitments.toList, ptReadable (R.E.g1.toEip C)` (the only Valid clause mentioning them besides sizes). Inv.lean:741 `(bytesAt st (ptrAddr M.vkMptr) PV.vk.len == PV.vk.bytes)`; :810 `(holdsG1 E st (ptrAddr M.finalComMptr) S.c.finalCom.eval)`. PoseidonEnv.lean:345-346 `def poseidonFixedCommitments : Array G₁z := Array.replicate poseidonVkData.numFixedColumns (1 : G₁z)`. PoseidonValid.lean:414 `have h1 : ∀ x ∈ poseidonVkExec.fixedCommitments.toList, x = (1 : G₁z) := by native_decide`. Verifier.lean:795 `let com ← Permutation.atIndex vk.fixedCommitments q.2.1.index` (the Lean verifier does use them).
- **Counterexample / witness:** Let R be Valid and let R' := { R with vk := { R.vk with fixedCommitments := R.vk.fixedCommitments.map (fun C => C * g) } } with g ≠ 1. R'.Valid holds (same sizes; ptReadable by Holds.g1Valid), and R'.prog = R.prog, R'.pv = R.pv. If cd is an honest proof for R (LeanAccepts R, CompleteSide R), conjunct 2 for R gives a returning honest run. Honest runs are permissive runs, so conjunct 1 for R' would give LeanAccepts R' H cd, which fails generically. Hence the hinge or Rejects is false for R'. Concretely, poseidonRender is such an R'.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed. I read Render.Valid (13 clauses), SupportedV2, vkPins, vkProgressPins, EnvAgrees (10 fields) and VerifyingKey.WellFormed in full: the Lean VK's commitments are constrained only by array size (WellFormed.fixedCommitmentsMatch / permutationCommitmentsMatch) and by ptReadable of their encoding (vkPoints). Nothing relates them to R.plan.vk.fixedComms / permutationComms, and nothing pins the G1_BASE slot to P.g₁. R.pv and R.prog do not depend on R.vk, and the transcript absorbs only the imported vk.transcriptRepr, so replacing R.vk.fixedCommitments changes neither Valid nor the program nor any snapshot field up to .quotient, while it changes Snap.c.finalCom.eval, which Inv pins at .pcs. Consequences, stated precisely: for a non-degenerate Valid R and R' := R with perturbed commitments, (a) Rejects R' is directly false (the honest run of the identical program returns while LeanAccepts R' fails), and (b) BlockSound R' .pcs is false at the state the honest run passes through, so blockContracts_of_valid is false as a statement over all Valid renders. Two corrections of scope: the deployment exhibits the mismatch (all 27 Lean commitments are the identity while the payload carries the real ones) but the hinge is not refuted AT poseidonRender by this argument, because Valid is false there and BlockContractsAt is vacuous (G-1); and with all commitments equal, the Lean dedup in constructIntermediateSets (kzgCommitBeq) merges queries the contract keeps apart, so the identity placeholders are wrong structurally, not only by value. The proposed deployment fix is feasible with in-tree theorems (g1zToEip_surjective_on_accepted).
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Statements.lean:124-125 `vkPoints : ∀ C ∈ R.vk.fixedCommitments.toList ++ R.vk.permutation.commitments.toList, ptReadable (R.E.g1.toEip C)`; :182-217 Valid (no other clause mentions the commitment values); :95-99 `pv := planViewOf R.plan R.model R.cfg.accumulator`, `prog ... := asmOf R.pv R.model vkAddr qAddr`. Inv.lean:533-579 EnvAgrees (no commitment or G1-base field; :579 pins only `vk.transcriptRepr`); :741 `(bytesAt st (ptrAddr M.vkMptr) PV.vk.len == PV.vk.bytes)`; :810 `(holdsG1 E st (ptrAddr M.finalComMptr) S.c.finalCom.eval)`. Verifier.lean:795 `let com ← Permutation.atIndex vk.fixedCommitments q.2.1.index`, :801 `permutationsCommon.queries (p := p) vk.permutation x`. PoseidonEnv.lean:195-196 `def poseidonFixedCommitments : Array G₁z := Array.replicate poseidonVkData.numFixedColumns (1 : G₁z)`, :199-200 same for permutation. PoseidonValid.lean:441-454 vkPoints proved from `h1 : ∀ x ∈ poseidonVkExec.fixedCommitments.toList, x = (1 : G₁z) := by native_decide`; its own docstring :438-439 'This is NOT evidence that the deployment's real commitments are readable; they are not even in the carrier'. MultiOpen.lean:257-259 constructIntermediateSets with kzgCommitBeq. Blocks.lean:152-154 the hinge. STATUS.md:280-289 records the class (fpG2Slots, trRetMptr, vkPins) but no commitments pin. G1PointCount.lean:502-504 `g1zToEip_surjective_on_accepted`.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
variable as in LoweringV2/Statements.lean:
example (R : Render p G₁ G₂ Gₜ) (cs : Array G₁) (a b : U256) :
    ({ R with vk := { R.vk with fixedCommitments := cs } } : Render p G₁ G₂ Gₜ).prog a b = R.prog a b := rfl
example (R : Render p G₁ G₂ Gₜ) (cs : Array G₁) (hsz : cs.size = R.vk.fixedCommitments.size)
    (hpt : ∀ C ∈ cs.toList, ptReadable (R.E.g1.toEip C)) (hR : R.Valid) :
    ({ R with vk := { R.vk with fixedCommitments := cs } } : Render p G₁ G₂ Gₜ).Valid := by sorry -- expected provable field by field
```
- **Suggested fix:** Add Valid clauses pinning each payload commitment word group to `R.E.g1.toEip` of the corresponding Lean VK commitment (fixed and permutation), and the G1_BASE slot to `R.E.g1.toEip P.g₁`. At the deployment, define the VK commitments as `g1zOfEip?` of the payload bytes. Membership is provable per point by the r-torsion `decide` plus `torsion_eq_zpowers_gen`, so the identity placeholders can be retired.
