- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → medium · **Kind:** fidelity-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (v1 only: Inv.lean:473-490 and docs/plans/P-DELTA-GAP.md ("E.delta is unconstrained by the specs while the contract hard-codes a value"). STILL DROPPED"; :485 "the constant is a BASE-field DELTA (Fq::DELTA) while E.delta : Fr p". PoseidonEnv.lean:380 `delta := 2`. At poseidonRender, delta = 2 against the contract's 0x08634d0a...
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Confirmed. VerifierEnv.delta is threaded into the Lean permutation argument; the contract hard-codes fr_delta (Fq::DELTA of the SCALAR field, computed by fr_delta_literal). EnvAgrees has no delta field and no other Valid clause reads R.E.delta (Valid reads E only through E.field.numBits and E.g1.toEip), so Valid, pv and prog are invariant under changing R.E.delta while the Lean verifier's verdict is not. poseidonE.delta is the placeholder 2. The Inv.lean note that keeps the clause dropped misreads the field: in midnight-curves Fq is the scalar field, so no field crossing is involved. ### G-6 — `permissive` excludes the real EVM behaviour of a missing precompile (success with empty returndata); the contract's returndatasize guards are unverified and the docstring claims otherwise

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** partially-correct → low · **Kind:** trusted-base-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Yul/Bridge.lean:694-732,1431; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:224-231,251-252,302-304; proofs/solidity-verifier/templates/partials/verifier/FinalPairing.yul:39-40; proofs/solidity-verifier/templates/partials/verifier/AssemblyHelpers.yul:33-34
- **Claim (reviewer):** Soundness is stated under `permissive (callsOf product)`, which admits a response only if it fails or is exactly the honest model answer. On a chain where 0x0b/0x0c/0x0f (or 0x05) is not a precompile, STATICCALL to that code-less address succeeds with returndatasize = 0 and leaves the output buffer untouched. That response is not in `permissive`, so the headline says nothing about such chains. The docstring's claim that failure covers "a precompile may be missing on a different deployment" is wrong. The generated contract does guard every call with `eq(returndatasize(), ...)`, but under `permissive` a successful call always has the right size, so those guards are never exercised by the proof. DeploymentV2 states no precompile-availability assumption and the PLAN-V2 §6 English statement does not name one.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Bridge.lean:725-727 "The soundness relation: any FAILURE is permitted (gas is unmodelled, a precompile may be missing on a different deployment), a SUCCESS must be the honest one". Bridge.lean:731-732 `def permissive (c : ExternalCalls) : ExternalCalls := ⟨fun req st resp => resp.world = CallWorld.ofState st ∧ (resp.success = true -> c.Call req st resp)⟩`. FinalPairing.yul:39-40 `success := staticcall(G1MSM_GAS_1PAIR, ...)` / `success := and(success, eq(returndatasize(), {{ template_constants.g1_bytes|hex() }}))`. Statements.lean:231 `disjoint : accountKey vkAddr ∉ ([0x0b, 0x0c, 0x0f, 0x05] : List Nat)` (the only mention of those addresses in DeploymentV2).
- **Counterexample / witness:** Request: STATICCALL to 0x0f with a valid 0x300-byte frame on a pre-Prague chain. Real response: success = true, returndata = []. `permissive (callsOf product)` rejects it, because `honestOf` gives returndata = pairingOutputWord (32 bytes). The theorem is therefore silent, although the contract would revert through its returndatasize check.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** The technical observation is right and the scope claim is overstated. Right: `permissive c` admits a response only if it reports no world change and, when successful, is exactly the honest model answer; with c = callsOf product a successful response to a precompile address must equal honestOf's output, so the real-EVM behaviour of a STATICCALL to a code-less, non-precompile address (success = 1, empty returndata, output buffer untouched) is NOT in the relation. The docstrings at Bridge.lean:725-727 and :1430-1432 wrongly present a missing precompile as covered by the failure case. The generated contract does guard every precompile call with a returndatasize check, and under `permissive` those guards are never exercised by the proof, so the theorem would not notice their removal. Overstated: the precompile assumption is not absent from the trusted base; PLAN.md section 1 lists EIP-2537 and modexp as 'assumption, stated as a yul-semantics ExternalCalls relation; validated on revm', and the English headline's 'precompiles that may fail but never lie' is consistent with the Lean statement. What remains is a documentation inaccuracy in two docstrings, the omission of the ExternalCalls/precompile-availability row from PLAN-V2 section 6's list of what the English statement must name (it lists Eip2537.Holds only), and a cheap strengthening opportunity (allow successes with a wrong returndata length so the guards become load-bearing).
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Bridge.lean:725-727 'The soundness relation: any FAILURE is permitted (gas is unmodelled, a precompile may be missing on a different deployment), a SUCCESS must be the honest one'; :731-732 `def permissive (c : ExternalCalls) : ExternalCalls := ⟨fun req st resp => resp.world = CallWorld.ofState st ∧ (resp.success = true -> c.Call req st resp)⟩`; :705-711 honestOf returns `ofOutcome st o` or `failResp st`; :718-723 honestCalls; :1430-1432 same parenthetical. Statements.lean:251-252 `callsOf product := honestCalls product 0 (fun _ _ _ => False)`; :224-231 DeploymentV2 (only `disjoint` mentions 0x0b/0x0c/0x0f/0x05). Templates: FinalPairing.yul:39-40 `success := staticcall(G1MSM_GAS_1PAIR, ...)` / `success := and(success, eq(returndatasize(), ...))`; AssemblyHelpers.yul:33-34, :187-188, :238-239, :294-295 same pattern. docs/PLAN.md:72 '| EIP-2537 (0x0b G1ADD, 0x0c G1MSM, 0x0f PAIRING_CHECK), modexp 0x05 (EIP-198/7883) | assumption, stated as a yul-semantics ExternalCalls relation; validated on revm |'. PLAN-V2.md section 6 (lines 355-358) names SoundSide, CompleteSide, EntryFrame, the D-P36a axioms, Eip2537.Holds, scope and gas. EVM rule relied on: a message call to an account with empty code that is not an active precompile executes no code and returns success with empty return data.
- **Suggested fix:** Either add "EIP-2537 precompiles and MODEXP are active and behave as Midfall/Yul/Externals.lean" to the trusted-base table and the English headline, or weaken `permissive` so that success with a returndata length other than the honest length is also allowed. The existing returndatasize guards then become load-bearing and the theorem covers chains without the precompiles. Correct the docstrings at Bridge.lean:725-727 and :1431.

### G-7 — The real-world precompile assumption is stated nowhere: holds_exhibited exhibits a product defined from the assumed pairing, and the file header says nothing about the precompile is assumed

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** partially-correct → low · **Kind:** trusted-base-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Assumptions/CurvePairing.lean:20-25; formal/Midfall/Assumptions/Eip2537.lean:130-164,1275-1280; formal/Midfall/Instances/HoldsFinal.lean:57-58; formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:68-72
- **Claim (reviewer):** `Eip2537.Holds c c₂ product` constrains a free oracle `product : Bytes → Bool`; five of its six clauses concern the Lean byte model. `holds_exhibited` proves `∃ product, Holds ...` with witness `pairingProductOf`, a noncomputable function defined to equal the assumed `P.e` after Classical.choose decoding. That shows satisfiability. It is not evidence that the EVM's PAIRING_CHECK is such a product, nor that Externals.lean's computed G1ADD/G1MSM/MODEXP match the real precompiles. To apply the headline to a chain one must assume both. CurvePairing.lean's header says "Nothing about the PRECOMPILE is assumed", and PLAN-V2 names a term `poseidonProduct` that does not exist in the tree. The needed assumption is true: on prime-order cyclic groups every admissible pairing gives the same truth value for `e(P₁,Q₁)+e(P₂,Q₂)=0`, namely a₁b₁+a₂b₂ ≡ 0 mod r, so the real precompile satisfies pairing2 for any choice of e.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** CurvePairing.lean:20-23 "Nothing about the PRECOMPILE is assumed. Every one of the six 'Holds' clauses is a theorem of this tree once the pairing exists ... 'pairing2' from 'pairingProductOf_pairing2', which agrees with the precompile by DEFINITION of the product". Eip2537.lean:1275-1280 `def pairingProductOf ... fun b => match frameDecode c d₂ b with | some (p₁, q₁, p₂, q₂) => isZeroTargetWith P.decEqGₜ (P.e ... + P.e ...) | none => false`. HoldsFinal.lean:57-58 `theorem holds_exhibited : ∃ product : PairingProduct, Eip2537.Holds g1CodecZ g2CodecFold product := holds_staged`. PLAN-V2.md:71 "The one concrete product, `HoldsFinal`'s exhibited `poseidonProduct`" (rg finds `poseidonProduct` only in a comment at Blocks.lean:613).
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** Partly right. Right: Eip2537.Holds constrains a free oracle `product` only through pairing2 (the other five clauses are facts about the tree's own byte model), and holds_exhibited proves satisfiability with a product defined from the assumed P.e after Classical.choose decoding; it is not evidence about the EVM. To apply the headline to a chain one additionally assumes that the real G1ADD/G1MSM/MODEXP agree with Externals.lean and that the real PAIRING_CHECK is a product satisfying pairing2 (which is true for any admissible pairing on prime-order groups, since the truth value of e(P₁,Q₁)+e(P₂,Q₂)=0 does not depend on the choice of e). CurvePairing.lean:20-25 'Nothing about the PRECOMPILE is assumed' reads as a claim about the trusted base although it is only true of the exhibit, and PLAN-V2/design.md/QUEUE name a term `poseidonProduct` that does not exist. Wrong: 'stated nowhere'. The assumption is a row of the trusted-base table (PLAN.md:72), which PLAN-V2 section 2 amends rather than replaces, and CurvePairing.lean:55-57 itself points to it. The real defect is imprecision (the row does not say WHICH property of the real precompiles is assumed, and PLAN-V2 section 6 does not require the English headline to name it), not absence.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Eip2537.lean:130-164 Holds: g1Valid, g2Valid, pairing2, g1Add, g1Msm1, g1Decodes; only pairing2 (:137-140) mentions `product`. :1275-1280 `def pairingProductOf ... fun b => match frameDecode c d₂ b with | some (p₁, q₁, p₂, q₂) => isZeroTargetWith P.decEqGₜ (P.e ... + P.e ...) | none => false`. HoldsFinal.lean:57-58 `theorem holds_exhibited : ∃ product : PairingProduct, Eip2537.Holds g1CodecZ g2CodecFold product := holds_staged`. CurvePairing.lean:20-23 'Nothing about the PRECOMPILE is assumed ... pairing2 from pairingProductOf_pairing2, which agrees with the precompile by DEFINITION of the product'; :55-57 'TRUSTED BASE: docs/PLAN.md section 1 lists this alongside the EIP-2537 semantics'. docs/PLAN.md:72 the EIP-2537/modexp assumption row. PLAN-V2.md:71 'The one concrete product, HoldsFinal's exhibited poseidonProduct'; rg finds `poseidonProduct` in no Lean declaration (only the comment at Blocks.lean:613, plus design.md:1742, QUEUE.md:1127, REVIEW.md:3023).
- **Suggested fix:** State the trusted assumption explicitly in the trusted-base table and the English headline: (T1) the real G1ADD, G1MSM and MODEXP equal `g1AddOutput`, `g1MsmOutput` and `modexpOutput` on valid inputs and fail otherwise; (T2) the real PAIRING_CHECK on a valid two-pair frame returns 1 iff a₁b₁+a₂b₂ ≡ 0 (mod r). Optionally prove in Lean that `Pairing2` is independent of the choice of AdmissiblePairing, so (T2) no longer mentions an assumed e. Reword CurvePairing.lean:20-25 and remove `poseidonProduct` from the plan or define it.

### G+1 — The recorded reason for the G1 placeholder codec is contradicted by the tree: the r-torsion-to-zpowers theorem exists (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** medium

(and placing the deployment's commitments in the carrier) as a discrete-log problem that 'may be unfixable'. For G1 that is false: G1PointCount.lean proves every r-torsion point of E(Fp) is a multiple of H1 and that every frame the precompile accepts is the encoding of a carrier element. A noncomputable decoder (Classical.choose over the image of a real flagged 48-byte encoder) is therefore definable and its decodability on valid points is provable with in-tree theorems. The identity-commitment fallback (G-4) and the placeholder codec (G-1) are not forced; only the G2 side (G-3) is a genuine missing theorem. The pending decision bundles the two as 'same discrete-log class', which is wrong.

DECISIONS-PENDING.md:111 'Decide g1CodecZ.ofCompressed? (same discrete-log class, blocks end_to_end)'. G1PointCount.lean:238-239 `theorem torsion_eq_zpowers_gen (X : W.Point) (hX : blsR • X = 0) : ∃ k : ℤ, k • emb genFrame = X`; :502-504 `theorem g1zToEip_surjective_on_accepted (b : Bytes) (hb : b.size == g1Bytes) (h : ∃ pt, g1Of? b = some pt ∧ g1PointMsmValid pt = true) : ∃ Q : G₁z, g1zToEip Q = b`; :527 `theorem g1CodecZ_g1DecodesWidth : g1DecodesWidth g1CodecZ`.

### G+2 — The .quotient boundary cannot carry the quotient block's result: Snapshot.lin is always none, so the .pcs row is not derivable from Clean .quotient (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** high

- **Claim:** Obligation unprovable as stated (outside my assigned dimension; found while tracing where delta and the VK commitments enter; confidence medium-high, I did not read the Lean PCS emitter in full). Inv at .quotient pins only the two QUOTIENT_MPTR words; the clauses for QUOTIENT_EVAL_MPTR and the selector accumulators are guarded by `match S.lin with | none => true`, and snapshotOf always sets `lin := none` (v1's 'D6 limit 1'). The PCS code reads exactly those slots (expected eval and `mload(add(SELECTOR_ACC_MPTR, ...))`) to build the final MSM, and Inv at .pcs pins the result (fEval, v, finalCom, pairing inputs) to Lean values. So from `Clean R .quotient Snap` the inputs of the pcs slice are unconstrained while its post is fully constrained: BlockSound R .pcs is false for any state with wrong accumulator words (the MSM still succeeds with success = 1), for any render with simple selectors and a real pcs slice. This is independent of any Valid pin, so adding clauses to Valid cannot repair it; it needs Exec's linearize to expose the linearization (or a boundary predicate that can state it). Symmetrically, the quotient block's post asserts almost nothing about the 6.5k-token numerator computation. The V2 plan documents carry no item for it.

Inv.lean:795-801 `(match S.lin with | none => true | some L => (holdsFr st (ptrAddr M.quotientEvalMptr) (linComEval L.linCom)) && ...)`; :808-812 the .pcs clauses. QuotientAndLinearization.yul:89-97 'Prepare linearization scalars for the final PCS MSM ... PCS block 5 expands the linearized commitment into its quotient and selector pairs inside the already-fused final MSM'. kzg/mod.rs:1373 and :1785 `format!("mload(add(SELECTOR_ACC_MPTR, {:#x}))", sel_idx * WORD_BYTES)`; :287 `Query::new(data.computed_quotient_comm, 0, data.computed_quotient_eval)`. rg for 'D6 limit', 'linearization hole', 'S.lin', 'lin := none' in QUEUE.md, STATUS.md, PLAN-V2.md, EXECUTION-V2.md, DECISIONS-PENDING.md and SORRY.md returns nothing; QUEUE.md:1140 lists only v1 `quotientFold_matches_linearization` as a reuse candidate.

### G — errors the skeptic found in the reviewer's report

- G-7 (and the scope sentence of G-6): 'the real-world precompile assumption is stated nowhere' is wrong. docs/PLAN.md:72 has the trusted-base row 'EIP-2537 (0x0b G1ADD, 0x0c G1MSM, 0x0f PAIRING_CHECK), modexp 0x05 | assumption, stated as a yul-semantics ExternalCalls relation; validated on revm', PLAN-V2 section 2 amends that table rather than replacing it, and CurvePairing.lean:55-57 points to it. The defect is imprecision, not absence; both findings drop to low.
- G-3 and the summary: 'no new axiom is wrong' is an overclaim. The membership fact is not provable by the in-tree technique or by evaluation, but an elementary proof route exists (bound #E'(Fp2) by 2p²+1 and exhibit a twist point whose order is divisible by the large prime factor of the cofactor, so r² cannot divide the group order). The accurate statement is 'not executable as scoped; needs a new uncarded package or an explicit assumption'.
- G-2 title: the axioms are not 'derivable' as stated (blsGT is an opaque constant about which nothing beyond the axioms can be proved); they are eliminable, i.e. replaceable by an in-tree construction. The body of the finding says this correctly.
- G-4: 'Concretely, poseidonRender is such an R'' overstates what the deployment shows. At poseidonRender Valid is provably false and, by G-1, BlockContractsAt is vacuous, so the hinge is not refuted there; the deployment exhibits the mismatch, the false-hinge counterexample is a generic R'. The finding also does not notice that Rejects R' is directly false, which is the cleaner half of the argument.
- G-5 location: DeployedProfile.lean:125 is the line at 6481731. At f2fe99b the frDelta literal is at :154; DeployedProfile.lean also changed between the two commits, although the summary says only PoseidonEnv and PoseidonValid did.
- G-1: line references for DECISIONS-PENDING.md (:115) are the 6481731 positions; at f2fe99b the ofCompressed? bullet is at :111. The finding also repeats the project's framing that a real ofCompressed? needs injectivity work only; it misses that the project's stated blocker (no r-torsion-to-zpowers route) is already refuted by G1PointCount.lean:238 and :502, and that g1CodecZ.toCompressed is x-only and non-injective.
- G-1 consequence (b) is a statement about the standard model ('false once the emitted program is the real contract'); it is not refutable in Lean without exhibiting a returning run, and the reviewer did not check whether the current, incomplete emitted program ever returns.
- G-5 says the delta gap has 'the same false-hinge shape as G-4' without noting that the quotient-eval boundary clause through which delta would be compared is currently inert (Snapshot.lin = none), so the mismatch can only surface at .pcs.

### G — checked and found correct

- AXIOMS (complete list, formal/Midfall and formal/vendor): exactly five, all in formal/Midfall/Assumptions/CurvePairing.lean - :69 `axiom blsGT : Type`; :74 `axiom blsGT_group : Group blsGT`; :81 `axiom blsGT_primeOrder : PrimeOrderWith blsGT blsR`; :88 `axiom blsGT_module : Module (ZMod blsR) (Additive blsGT)`; :101 `axiom bls12_381Pairing : AdmissiblePairing G₁z G2fold blsGT`. A comment- and string-aware scan finds no other `axiom`, and no `unsafe`, `@[extern]`, `@[implemented_by]`, `opaque` or `decreasing_by` in formal/Midfall.
- The five axioms are jointly consistent relative to Lean and mathlib, and cannot contradict the concrete G1/G2 definitions: G₁z and G2fold are sorry-free prime-order-r groups with ZMod r-module instances (Instances/G1Agreement.lean:1814-1843 primeOrderZ/moduleZ; Instances/ExponentModel.lean:144-218), so a model exists with blsGT := Exp blsR. They are also true of the real BLS12-381 pairing restricted to the subgroups generated by H1 and H2.
- G1 is a real curve group: `G₁z := Subgroup.zpowers (Multiplicative.ofAdd (emb genFrame))` in mathlib's WeierstrassCurve.Affine.Point over ZMod blsP for y² = x³ + 4 (G1Agreement.lean:1814). The encoder uses canonical ZMod.val coordinates and infinity is 128 zero bytes (:1848-1851, :1989). E(Fp)[r] equals the subgroup generated by H1, proved from r² > 2p+1 (Instances/G1PointCount.lean:176, :238). The files contain no sorry.
- G2 is a discrete-log carrier with real bytes: `G2fold := Exp blsR` (Instances/G2Codec.lean:245) and the encoder is `g2PointBytes (smulFrame2 R.toAdd.val h2Frame).1` (:263, :285). Fp2 = AdjoinRoot (X²+1) over ZMod blsP (Instances/Fp2.lean:157-189). H2's four coordinates equal EIP-2537's (Instances/G2Kernel.lean:33-46), with on-curve and [r]H2 = O by kernel `decide` (:54, :68). The encoder is injective (G2Codec.lean:329).
- Moduli and primality: blsP/pBase and blsR/r are the BLS12-381 values and agree by rfl (Assumptions/Eip2537.lean:71, :76). `pBase_prime` is a kernel-checked 10-node Pratt certificate (Assumptions/BasePrime.lean:210-230). r's primality is CompPoly's Pratt certificate (CompPoly/Fields/BLS12_381/Basic.lean:27-46). No native_decide and no sorry-built Fact instances; every `Fact (Nat.Prime _)` instance was enumerated.
- Eip2537.Holds (Assumptions/Eip2537.lean:130-164) is satisfiable: `holds_exhibited` (Instances/HoldsFinal.lean:57-58) is `holds_staged` (Instances/HoldsStaging.lean:99-106) supplying all six clauses at g1CodecZ/g2CodecFold with product := pairingProductOf. The R-63 repair is in place (width hypothesis at :162) and the old clause is pinned and refuted (:1014-1035).
- The byte model in Midfall/Yul/Externals.lean assumes no more than real EIP-2537/EIP-198: G1ADD has no subgroup check (:505, :812-816); G1MSM and PAIRING_CHECK require on-curve and r-torsion (:512-518, :865-868, :924-927); coordinates must be canonical over the full 64 bytes (:466-470); infinity is all zeros; lengths are 0x100, 0xa0·k and 0x180·k with k > 0 (:813, :847, :907); pairing output is one 32-byte 0/1 word (:948); invalid input gives a failed call with empty returndata (Bridge.lean:694-695). MODEXP is computed, and `modexpInv_correct` is proved (Assumptions/Eip2537.lean:825-843).
- Holds.pairing2 forces `product` to agree with the same typeclass pairing `P.e` that DualMSM.check uses (Halo2/Exec/Kzg/Msm.lean:222-232). The sorried `fp_pairing_algebra` (LoweringV2/Blocks/FinalPairing.lean:278-281) is a true statement that follows from pairing2 and map_neg.
- G1CodecLaws (Assumptions/Codec.lean:75-90) is discharged at the real codec by `g1CodecZ_laws` (Instances/CurveSurface.lean:540-547). Its compressedRoundtrip field holds only vacuously; see G-1.
- FieldConsts.IsWellFormed is discharged at the real scalar field by kernel `decide` (Assumptions/TwoAdicity.lean:374-384 `frFc_isWellFormed`). It is not a hypothesis of any V2 statement; V2 uses only `orderExactly` through EnvAgrees.omegaOrder.
- native_decide confinement: 139 code-position occurrences in formal/Midfall, all under Midfall/Instances/ (SurfaceFixture 57, PoseidonValid 36, NativeE2E 14, StatementsRepair 8, ExponentModel 7, PoseidonPins 6, FrameWalkWitness 5, ObligationsGates 2, and one each in PoseidonV2, PoseidonCoherence, InvFixture, ImportedIR). The checks.sh gate strips comments before matching (scripts/checks.sh:41-52). In the headline cone today: `poseidonModel_isOk` (Instances/PoseidonV2.lean:101), plus the six PoseidonPins lemmas once poseidon_valid consumes them.
- keccak256 is not @[extern]: its kernel-visible definition is VCVio's pure-Lean Keccak-f[1600] (Common/Keccak.lean:15-16; VCVio/Algorithms/Keccak.lean:53-127). It is known-answer tested through Transcript.lean:293-338, Templates.lean:478-981, Evm.lean:423 and the PoseidonPins codehash pin.
- Sorry census: 60 code-position sorries with file and line identical to SORRY.md's 60 rows at 6481731. Exactly one is a sorried definition (Midfall/Halo2/Spec/Piop.lean:67, `Spec.accepts : Prop := sorry`); the other 59 are theorem proofs. No sorried instance or structure field.

## H — the deployment instance

**Reviewer summary.** Anchored at commit 6481731 (line numbers for PoseidonEnv/PoseidonValid/DeployedProfile/STATUS/QUEUE/DECISIONS-PENDING/design.md are that commit's). NOTE: HEAD moved to f2fe99b during the audit (F.1 landed cs.json; C.4 table is now 8 proved / 4 refuted / 1 sorry); I re-checked that every finding below still holds there. Nothing was built or run; all Lean claims are by reading, with checks supplied.

Verdict: `lowering_equivalence_poseidon` is not merely unproved. As the instance is modelled, its statement is FALSE, and it stays false after every repair the plan currently schedules (R.1-R.4, WP-G1):
1. The Lean verifier at the deployment rejects EVERYTHING: `verifyMsgs` round-trips each G1 message through `toCompressed`/`ofCompressed?`, and `g1CodecZ.ofCompressed? = fun _ => none`. So `LeanAccepts` is uninhabited, conjunct 2 and all block contracts are vacuous, and conjunct 1 says "the contract never returns".
2. The D-P36a pairing axiom leaves its generators opaque, yet the Lean verifier computes with `P.g₁` and the proposed slot pins mention `P.g₁`/`P.g₂`: unprovable at any deployment under the DONE axiom list (the five axioms are in fact eliminable).
3. `Render.Valid` ties the VK payload to the Lean VK only through the digest word: omega words, G1/G2 bases and all 27 commitments are free (the instance carries 27 identity commitments and delta := 2, undetected). `blockContracts_of_valid` is false as stated.
4. Gap B option (a) needs cyclicity of E'(Fp2)[r], which the G1 counting trick cannot give; and the D-P45 non-vacuity witness needs a pairing equation under an unknown SRS trapdoor.
5. "The deployment" is the k=6 PoseidonExample test fixture; the only on-chain contract (Sepolia moonlight-wrap, k=20, accumulator mode, older generator) is outside scope.

Positive: clause table, pins, codehash, fixture cross-consistency and the Holds exhibit check out as documented.

Please review these findings for accuracy and completeness before relying on them.

### H-1 — LeanAccepts is uninhabited at the deployment: verifyMsgs routes every G1 message through a placeholder compressed codec

- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Native.lean:164-170,269-292; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Transcript.lean:65-69,145-151; formal/Midfall/Instances/G1Agreement.lean:2002-2006; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:379-380; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Snapshot.lean:163-167; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:263-267,302-304

Consequences at poseidonRender: (a) headline conjunct 1 reduces to 'no permissive run ever returns', which is false for any program that accepts a proof, including the Rust-generated contract; (b) conjunct 2, `prefix_run_clean`, `BlockContractsAt` and therefore `poseidon_quotient_block` are VACUOUSLY true (a registered 'open' sorry that could be closed with zero content, moving the cone); (c) `poseidon_rejects` is false as stated; (d) the D-P45 repaired `HypothesesInhabited` (adds `LeanAccepts`) is refutable; (e) `SoundSide` holds for every cd because the fault is a transcript error, not the logup panic. This holds for ANY render over g1CodecZ, the only real-curve G1 codec in the tree, and is untouched by R.1-R.4/WP-G1. The project records the placeholder only as blocking `end_to_end`.

At 6481731 the pcs slice is empty (DeployedProfile.lean:729 `(GenAst.emitPartial pv Partial.pcs).isEmpty`) and PAIRING_LHS/RHS are written only in the PCS region (poseidon-fixture-dump/Halo2Verifier.sol:2954,2976), so finalPairing pairs two all-zero (infinity) G1 points and returns ABI true for well-formed calldata, while LeanAccepts is false: conjunct 1 fails. - **Claim after the skeptic's check:** CONFIRMED CORE: for every H and cd, `LeanAccepts poseidonRender H cd` is false and `snapshotOf poseidonE H poseidonVkExec inst ms none` is never `.ok`. The reason is data-independent: `multiPrepare` always runs `phaseB`, whose first statement is an unconditional `tmRead (hashableG1 g1)`, and at `g1CodecZ` every such read is an error. So at poseidonRender (and at ANY render over g1CodecZ): conjunct 2, `prefix_run_clean`, `BlockContractsAt` (hence `poseidon_quotient_block` and the poseidon instance of `blockContracts_of_valid`) are vacuously true; `SoundSide` holds for every cd (the failure is a transcript error at the 8 advice reads, before any logup code); conjunct 1 and `poseidon_rejects` are both equivalent to 'no permissive run of the modelled program returns'; and D-P45's added `LeanAccepts` conjunct is refutable. import Midfall.Instances.PoseidonV2

- **Suggested fix:** Give G₁z a real compressed codec and put it on the HEADLINE critical path. It is definable today, contrary to PoseidonEnv.lean:27-29 ('a route the tree does not have'): `torsion_eq_zpowers_gen` (G1PointCount.lean:238) and `g1zToEip_surjective_on_accepted` (:502) already give r-torsion -> zpowers, so `ofCompressed? b := (G1Affine.ofCompressed? b) >>= subgroup check >>= g1zOfEip? ∘ eip-bytes` and a flag-carrying `toCompressed` work noncomputably. Add the encoder-first law `ofCompressed? (toCompressed Q) = some Q` to `G1CodecLaws` (or to `SupportedV2`) so a codec that decodes nothing cannot satisfy the hypotheses. Alternatively make `prepareMsgs` consume messages directly instead of re-parsing bytes (a change to the Lean verifier).

### H-2 — The D-P36a pairing axiom leaves g₁/g₂ opaque, so the deployment headline is unprovable under the DONE axiom list (and the five axioms are eliminable)

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** unsound-or-unrealistic-assumption · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Assumptions/CurvePairing.lean:92-103; formal/Midfall/Common/Curve.lean:44-55; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:308-312; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:573; formal/docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:64-71; formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:340-346
- **Claim (reviewer):** `bls12_381Pairing : AdmissiblePairing G₁z G2fold blsGT` is an axiom whose fields `g₁ : G₁z` and `g₂ : G2fold` are unspecified (only `≠ 1` and `e g₁ g₂ ≠ 0`). The Lean verifier computes the `-v·G` term with `P.g₁⁻¹`, `EnvAgrees.nG2` (a clause of frozen `Valid.slots`) demands `nG2Prepared = P.g₂⁻¹`, and every proposed base-slot pin is stated against them (`vkG1Words … = E.g1.toEip P.g₁`, `vkG2Words … = encG2 nG2Prepared⁻¹`). Nothing proves `bls12_381Pairing.g₁ = g1zGen` or `bls12_381Pairing.g₂ = g2foldExp 1`, and it is independent of the axioms (replacing g₁ by g₁² gives another model). So at the deployment (i) the G1 base pin, (ii) BOTH conjuncts of `fpG2Slots` separately (not only the Gap-B one), and hence (iii) the pcs and finalPairing rows, are unprovable with only the axioms PLAN-V2 §6 allows. The contract's G1_BASE bytes are the published H1 and its G2_BASE bytes the published H2 (verified against the VK dump), i.e. the bytes of `g1zGen` and `g2foldExp 1`.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** CurvePairing.lean:98-101 `Nothing here says the pairing is the BLS12-381 ate pairing ... or that its generators are the published ones` / `axiom bls12_381Pairing : AdmissiblePairing G₁z G2fold blsGT`; Curve.lean:46,48 `g₁ : G₁` / `g₂ : G₂`; MultiOpen.lean:311 `{ scalars := #[b.x3, v], bases := #[pi, P.g₁⁻¹],`; Inv.lean:573 `nG2 : params.nG2Prepared = P.g₂⁻¹`; DECISIONS-PENDING.md:68 `baseSlots : vkG1Words R.pv vkG1BaseSlot = R.E.g1.toEip P.g₁ ∧`; PoseidonEnv.lean:386 `noncomputable def poseidonSG2 : G2fold := bls12_381Pairing.g₂`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Model of the axioms with g₁ := g1zGen ^ 2 (still non-identity, still non-degenerate): Lean's `-v·G` is -2v·H1 while the contract MSMs with the payload's H1, so the two sides disagree on every proof with v ≠ 0. The headline at the deployment is therefore not derivable from the five axioms.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** As stated. `bls12_381Pairing` is an opaque axiom of type `AdmissiblePairing G₁z G2fold blsGT`; its fields `g₁`, `g₂` are constrained only by `≠ 1` and `e g₁ g₂ ≠ 0`, and no theorem in the tree relates them to `g1zGen` or `g2foldExp 1`. The Lean verifier computes with `P.g₁⁻¹`; frozen `EnvAgrees.nG2` forces `nG2Prepared = P.g₂⁻¹`; the proposed base pins and both conjuncts of `fpG2Slots` equate concrete payload bytes (the published H1 and H2, which I checked in the VK dump) with encodings of those opaque generators. Each such equation is independent of the five axioms, so the `.pcs` and `.finalPairing` rows cannot be proved at this deployment under PLAN-V2 §6's axiom list. FinalPairing.lean's own remark that `fpG2Slots` can be exhibited 'by computation' is therefore wrong. The ported generator has the same dependency (`generateBaseVk` asserts against `P.g₁` and `P.g₂`). The sub-claim that the five axioms are eliminable (Gₜ := G₁z, e(P,k) := k•P) is mathematically sound by my derivation (`Holds.pairing2` reduces to `a•P₁ + b•P₂ = 0` for every non-degenerate bilinear e over the exponent carrier) but is not verified in Lean.

Payload: poseidon-fixture-dump/Halo2VerifyingKey.sol:79-80 g1_x = 0x17f1d3a7…c6bb, :83-84 g2_x_c0 = 0x024aa2b2…bdb8 (the standard generators). Eip2537.lean:138-141 pairing2.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
-- does an axiom-free instance with pinned generators elaborate?
import Midfall.Instances.G2Codec
import Midfall.Instances.G1Agreement
open Midfall Midfall.Instances
noncomputable example : AdmissiblePairing G₁z G2fold G₁z where
  e := LinearMap.mk₂ (ZMod blsR) (fun (x : Additive G₁z) (k : Additive G2fold) => (Multiplicative.toAdd (Additive.toMul k) : ZMod blsR) • x) (by intros; simp [smul_add]) (by intros; simp [smul_comm]) (by intros; simp [add_smul]) (by intros; simp [mul_smul])
```

- **Suggested fix:** Replace the five axioms by a constructed instance on (G₁z, G2fold, Gₜ := G₁z) with e(P, k) := k • P, g₁ := g1zGen, g₂ := g2foldExp 1. This removes D-P36a from the trusted base entirely (the only real trust is `Eip2537.Holds` for the EVM's product, which is the same proposition for every non-degenerate bilinear e) and makes the G1/G2 base pins computable facts. If the axiom is kept, strengthen it to fix both generators, and update PLAN-V2 §6 accordingly.

### H-3 — Render.Valid ties the VK payload to the Lean VK only through the digest word; blockContracts_of_valid is false as stated

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (STATUS.md:282-293 ('§6 systematically under-pins the memory layout', three pin families) and QUEUE.md:568 (v1's g1BaseWords as an F.2 row); the omega/n_inv words and the 27 commitments are recorded nowhere, and the identity placeholders appear only in PoseidonEnv/PoseidonValid docstrings) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:124-125,182-217; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:533-579,779-813; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:152-154; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:341-350; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonValid.lean:404-424; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonInputs.lean:76-89; formal/Midfall/Generator/Vk.lean:1093
- **Claim (reviewer):** `R.plan.vk` (35 header words + 27 commitment points, the bytes the contract extcodecopies) is a free field relative to `R.vk`/`R.E`/`R.params` except for word 0 (`EnvAgrees.vkDigestWord`) and its length/hash (`vkPins`). No clause of the 13 relates the payload's omega, omega_inv, omega_inv_to_l, n_inv words, the G1 base, the two G2 bases, or the fixed/permutation commitments to the Lean verifier's values; `baryWeight`/`omegaInvMul`/`omegaOrder` are facts about `R.vk.domain` alone and `vkPoints` asks only that `toEip` of R.vk's commitments be readable. The contract reads all of these from VK memory (dump lines 1513, 1534, 2599-2600, 2955, 911-913). Therefore `blockContracts_of_valid : R.Valid → R.BlockContractsAt product (· ≠ .quotientLin)` is false as stated for `.lagrange` and `.pcs` (in addition to the recorded `.finalPairing` G2 gap). The deployment exhibits it: `poseidonVkExec` carries 27 identity commitments against 27 real points in the payload, yet C.4 marks `vkPoints` holding and `slots` refuted only by `negLagranges` (at HEAD f2fe99b `slots` is fully PROVED with the identities still in place). A ported `generateVk` exists but `Valid` does not use it; at the deployment `plan.vk` is parsed back out of the Rust artifact. D-P47's 'export instead of port' route never establishes this link.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Statements.lean:124-125 `vkPoints : ∀ C ∈ R.vk.fixedCommitments.toList ++ R.vk.permutation.commitments.toList, ptReadable (R.E.g1.toEip C)`; Inv.lean:579 `vkDigestWord : (PV.vk.constants.getD 0 ("", 0)).2 = frWord vk.transcriptRepr` (the only payload-content clause); Inv.lean:811-812 `holdsG1 E st (ptrAddr M.pairingLhsMptr) S.c.dual.left.eval` / `... pairingRhsMptr) S.c.dual.right.eval` (snapshot computed from R.vk); PoseidonEnv.lean:345-346 `def poseidonFixedCommitments : Array G₁z := Array.replicate poseidonVkData.numFixedColumns (1 : G₁z)`; PoseidonValid.lean:406-409 `D.2 could only put 1 : G₁z in every commitment slot ... This is NOT evidence that the deployment's real commitments are readable; they are not even in the carrier`; PoseidonInputs.lean:76 `def poseidonVkGen? : Option Halo2VerifyingKey := vkModelFromArtifact poseidonVkDump`; Statements.lean:17-18 claims `Valid ties the plan to the VERIFIER'S OWN verifying key`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take any valid R with an accepting snapshot and set R' := { R with vk := { R.vk with domain := { R.vk.domain with omega := ω^3, omegaInv := (ω^3)⁻¹ } } }. All 13 clauses still hold (circuitMeta reads vk.cs; domainN, baryWeight unchanged; omegaInvMul holds; orderExactly (ω^3) n holds since n = 2^k; vkDigestWord reads the imported transcriptRepr). The program is unchanged and reads ω from the payload, but Lean now rotates by ω^3, so `Clean .lagrange` (lLast/lBlind/l0 from the snapshot) fails after a normal exit with success = 1: `BlockSound R' .lagrange` is false. Same construction with `fixedCommitments := Array.replicate n 1` refutes `.pcs`.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** As stated, with two precisions. (1) I checked every one of the 13 `Valid` clauses: the only one relating the content of the payload (`R.plan.vk` / `R.pv.vk`) to the Lean VK is `EnvAgrees.vkDigestWord`. `InvAt` does not take `vk` at all, `baryWeight` / `omegaInvMul` / `omegaOrder` speak about `R.vk.domain` alone, `vkPoints` asks only readability of `toEip` of R.vk's own commitments, and `WellFormed` has no omega or commitment-content clause. The contract reads omega, omega_inv_to_l and n_inv from VK memory and the G1 base and commitments in the PCS region. (2) The omega^3 counterexample goes through: R' satisfies all 13 clauses whenever R does, the transcript is unchanged (`hashInto` absorbs only `transcriptRepr`), and `lagrangeTriple` uses `vk.domain.lIRange`, so `Inv .lagrange` (lLast, lBlind) fails after a normal exit with success = 1 for generic x. So `blockContracts_of_valid` cannot hold at both R and R'; it is unprovable from `R.Valid`, and false as soon as one Valid render with a non-degenerate snapshot exists, which DONE requires. No Valid render exists in the tree today, so there is no closed-form refutation yet. At poseidonRender itself `BlockContractsAt` is vacuous (H-1), so what the deployment exhibits is the missing tie (27 identity commitments with `slots` and `vkPoints` PROVED), not a failing block contract. Not recorded in STATUS / QUEUE / DECISIONS-PENDING / PLAN-V2 (no hit for omega, n_inv or the identity commitments there).

### H-4 — Gap B, worked out: the modelled verifier has trapdoor 1, and option (a) needs a G2 cyclicity theorem the tree cannot prove with its G1 method

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (DECISIONS-PENDING.md §8 and QUEUE R.4 record that the Lean side pairs with g₂ and that the pins cannot hold; they do not record falsity in both directions, the forgeability, the G2 cyclicity requirement, or the clash with EnvAgrees.nG2) · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:382-392; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/Msm.lean:222-233; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:300-318; formal/Midfall/Instances/G1PointCount.lean:20-30; formal/Midfall/Instances/G2Codec.lean:285,372-374; formal/docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:102-115
- **Claim (reviewer):** (1) With `sG2Prepared = g₂` and `nG2Prepared = g₂⁻¹`, `DualMSM.check` is `e(left,g₂) + e(right,g₂⁻¹) = 0`, i.e. `left.eval = right.eval`. Since left = π and right = finalCom + x3·π − v·g₁ with π read after x3, x4 and v are fixed, the equation is solvable for π for ANY transcript prefix: once H-1 is repaired the Lean verifier at this render accepts forgeries for false statements and rejects every honest proof, so the headline would be false in BOTH directions (contract true / Lean false on honest proofs; Lean true / contract revert on forgeries). (2) `fpG2Slots` at this render is refutable as a conjunction (its two halves force g₂ = exp 1 and g₂ = exp s; params.json has s_g2 ≠ g2). (3) Option (a) is recorded as 'No new axiom. It does need proofs that the bytes lie in the encoder's image'. The image of `g2foldToEip` is ⟨H2⟩, so the existence statement for the −s·G2 slot is exactly 'every r-torsion point of E'(Fp2) is a multiple of H2'. The tree's G1 proof of the analogous fact rests on r² > 2p+1; over Fp2 the bound is #E' ≤ 2p²+1 ≈ 2^763 > r² ≈ 2^510, so that argument does not transfer, and mathlib has neither a Weil pairing nor point counting. Option (a) therefore needs a new axiom/hypothesis after all. (4) Option (a) as drafted ('set nG2Prepared/sG2Prepared from them') makes the frozen clause `EnvAgrees.nG2 : nG2Prepared = P.g₂⁻¹` unprovable while P.g₂ is opaque (H-2). (5) Confirmed: the EQUIVALENCE needs no SRS well-formedness beyond the two payload G2 points lying in the encoder's image (subgroup membership); it needs no relation to the G1 powers of tau.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Msm.lean:230-233 `decideBool (P.decEqGₜ (Additive.toMul (P.e (Additive.ofMul left) (Additive.ofMul params.sG2Prepared) + P.e (Additive.ofMul right) (Additive.ofMul params.nG2Prepared))) 1)`; PoseidonEnv.lean:391-392 `{ sG2 := poseidonSG2, nG2Prepared := poseidonSG2⁻¹, sG2Prepared := poseidonSG2 }`; MultiOpen.lean:306-318 `let pi ← tmRead (hashableG1 g1)` after `let x4 ← tmSqueezeChallenge` and `let v ← …`; G1PointCount.lean:20-24 `WHAT REPLACES BOTH IS ONE INEQUALITY: r^2 > 2p + 1 ... (2) r^2 > 2p + 1 for THIS curve: p is 381 bits and r^2 is 510 bits`; G2Codec.lean:285 `def g2foldToEip (R : G2fold) : Bytes := g2PointBytes (g2foldFrame R).1`; DECISIONS-PENDING.md:110 `No new axiom. It does need proofs that the bytes lie in the encoder's image.`
- **Counterexample / witness:** Forgery at the modelled params (codec repaired): for any prefix with x3 ≠ 1 choose π := (1 − x3)⁻¹ • (finalCom.eval · g₁^(−v)); then left = right and Lean accepts, while the contract checks against −s·G2 and reverts.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** As stated; items (1), (2), (4), (5) reproduced from the definitions, item (3) with medium confidence. (1) At `poseidonParams` (`sG2Prepared = g₂`, `nG2Prepared = g₂⁻¹`) `DualMSM.check` is `e(left − right, g₂) = 0`, i.e. `left.eval = right.eval`; `left = π`, `right = finalCom·π^x3·g₁^(−v)`, and π is the last read with no squeeze after it, so π := (finalCom.eval·g₁^(−v))^((1−x3)⁻¹) is accepted for any prefix. This is conditional on the codec repair (today nothing is accepted, H-1). After that repair the modelled verifier accepts forgeries and rejects honest proofs, so the headline would fail in both directions. (2) The payload's NEG_S_G2 x-coordinate differs from the G2 x-coordinate, so the two halves of `fpG2Slots` cannot both hold. (3) Option (a)'s 'No new axiom' is unsupported: the image of `g2foldToEip` is ⟨H2⟩, the −s·G2 bytes come from the Filecoin SRS (trapdoor unknown), so membership needs 'E'(Fp2)[r] ⊆ ⟨H2⟩'. The tree's only method (if non-cyclic then r² ≤ #E, contradicted by an elementary size bound) does not transfer because #E'(Fp2) ≈ p² ≈ 2^762 exceeds r² ≈ 2^510 even under Hasse. Precisely: this needs a fact the tree cannot prove with its current method or with anything it cites from mathlib, i.e. in practice a new named assumption or a known-trapdoor fixture. (4) Setting `nG2Prepared` from bytes makes frozen `EnvAgrees.nG2` require `P.g₂ = g2foldExp 1`, unprovable under H-2. Options (b) and (c) of DECISIONS-PENDING §8 are unaffected.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Msm.lean:230-233 `decideBool (P.decEqGₜ (Additive.toMul (P.e (Additive.ofMul left) (Additive.ofMul params.sG2Prepared) + P.e (Additive.ofMul right) (Additive.ofMul params.nG2Prepared))) 1)`. open Midfall Midfall.Halo2 Midfall.Instances Midfall.Instances.PoseidonEnv in

### H-5 — The D-P45 non-vacuity witness cannot be produced at this deployment even after the data repairs

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** partially-correct → medium · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (DECISIONS-PENDING.md §3 'Cost to know' and QUEUE Q0.5 note that an accepting calldata is needed and must be proved; the in-principle obstruction (unknown trapdoor under an abstract pairing) is not recorded) · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:30-52; formal/docs/QUEUE.md:173-196; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:317-320; proofs/solidity-verifier/tests/m0_poseidon_regen.rs:22-48; formal/fixtures/conformance/poseidon/trace.json
- **Claim (reviewer):** The repaired gate adds `LeanAccepts R H cd`. (a) Today it is refutable at poseidonRender (H-1). (b) After the codec, VK and Gap B are repaired, proving `LeanAccepts` for an honest proof requires `DualMSM.check = true`, a pairing equation between concrete G1 points under an abstract pairing and an SRS whose trapdoor is unknown (the fixture uses the Filecoin `bls_filecoin_2p6` SRS). In the exponent model it reduces to `s • left = right` in G₁z with s the (Classical.choose'd) exponent of the payload point: not provable without either a pairing implementation, the trapdoor, or a new trusted fact. The plan's note says only that the witness 'will be proved, not evaluated'. (c) No accepted calldata is tracked for the bundle the Lean side reads: there are three different 2640-byte proofs (conformance bundle, committed fixture, untracked dump), the prover is not reproducible (OsRng blinding), and `calldataOf` cannot build calldata at g1CodecZ. So PLAN-V2 §6's DONE item '`HypothesesInhabited` ... proved' has no route.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** DECISIONS-PENDING.md:49-52 `Cost to know. Discharging the gate at the deployment needs an accepting calldata. REVIEW.md:3019 records none at any model in the tree, and the decoder is Classical.choose ..., so it will be proved, not evaluated.`; m0_poseidon_regen.rs:48 `| SRS | zk_stdlib/examples/assets/bls_filecoin_2p6 via srs_for_test(_, Some(6)) |` and :24-27 `blind_quotient_limbs ... draws its blinding scalars from OsRng`; trace.json `"fixture_match": {"committed_proof_len": 4752, "exported_proof_len": 2640, "instance": false, "proof": false}`, `"pairing_check": true`; sha256 of proof.bin: conformance 0f3c50f8…, fixtures/poseidon 21205450…, target dump 68621a29….
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** (a) Confirmed: today the repaired gate is refutable at poseidonRender (H-1). (c) Confirmed, but now recorded: three different 2640-byte poseidon proofs exist (sha256 0f3c50f8…, 21205450…, 68621a29…), the prover is OsRng-blinded, and trace.json reports `fixture_match.proof = false`; QUEUE R.5 (added at HEAD) records the non-reproducibility. (b) Overclaimed. It is right that acceptance of an HONEST keccak proof reduces to `s • left = right` for the unknown Filecoin trapdoor, which the tree cannot prove without a pairing implementation, the trapdoor, or a trusted SRS-consistency fact. But the conclusion 'the DONE item has no route' does not follow, because the gate (frozen and D-P45 text alike) quantifies `H : Hash` existentially and `DeploymentV2` pins H only at the VK runtime bytes. With H constant elsewhere every challenge is independent of the proof, so one can take π := 1 and solve for the last free commitment, fCom := (g₁^v · (∏ qᵢ^{cᵢ})⁻¹)^{c_m⁻¹}; then `left.eval = right.eval = 1` and `DualMSM.check` holds by linearity for ANY params, with no trapdoor (after the codec repair; only `xⁿ ≠ 1` is needed for `ChallengesGood`). So a witness exists in principle, at the cost of a large symbolic proof. The corrected finding is therefore twofold: the D-P45 gate still does not certify that any keccak-honest proof is accepted, and a gate that did (H := keccak256, as `EndToEnd` fixes) would hit the trapdoor obstruction the reviewer describes.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Statements.lean:317-320 `∃ (W : ExecEnv) (Lyt : EVM.Layout) (H : Hash) (vkAddr : U256) (cd : Bytes) (st₀ : EvmState), DeploymentV2 R W H vkAddr ∧ …`; :229 `vkHash : H R.plan.vk.runtimeBytes = keccak256 R.plan.vk.runtimeBytes` (the only constraint on H besides `keccak : W.keccakOf = hashOfBytes H`, with W also existential). DECISIONS-PENDING.md:33-37 D-P45 text keeps `∃ … (H : Hash) …` and adds `LeanAccepts R H cd ∧ G1CodecLaws R.E.g1`; :50-52 'so it will be proved, not evaluated'. Transcript.lean:127-129 `let d := wordToBytesBE (t.hash t.state)`. MultiOpen.lean:278-286 (fCom read, then x3, qEvalsOnX3, fEval), :300-318 (x4, finalCom, v, π, dual); Msm.lean:228-233 check; Inv.lean:692-696 `ChallengesGood` has the single field `xNotRoot`. shasum: formal/fixtures/conformance/poseidon/proof.bin 0f3c50f8…, proofs/solidity-verifier/fixtures/poseidon/proof.bin 21205450…, target/poseidon-fixture-dump/proof.bin 68621a29… (all 2640 bytes; the last is git-ignored). trace.json: `fixture_match {'committed_proof_len': 4752, 'exported_proof_len': 2640, 'instance': False, 'proof': False}`, `pairing_check True`. m0_poseidon_regen.rs:26,48 (OsRng; `bls_filecoin_2p6`). QUEUE.md:627-640 R.5. PoseidonCoherence.lean:55-62 (`repackPushG1` refuses at offset 0, so `calldataOf` cannot build calldata at g1CodecZ).
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
-- the pairing-free half of the degenerate witness, for any params
open Midfall Midfall.Halo2 in
example {p : ℕ} [Fact (Nat.Prime p)] {G₁ G₂ Gₜ : Type} [Group G₁] [Group G₂] [Group Gₜ]
    [PrimeOrderWith G₁ p] [PrimeOrderWith G₂ p] [PrimeOrderWith Gₜ p]
    [Module (ZMod p) (Additive G₁)] [Module (ZMod p) (Additive G₂)] [Module (ZMod p) (Additive Gₜ)]
    [P : AdmissiblePairing G₁ G₂ Gₜ] (g : DualMSM (Fr p) G₁ G₂ Gₜ) (params : ParamsVerifierKZG G₁ G₂)
    (hl : g.left.eval = 1) (hr : g.right.eval = 1) : DualMSM.check g params = true := by
  simp [DualMSM.check, hl, hr, decideBool]
-- the full witness (constant-H transcript with π = 1) is a long unfolding, not a small check.
```
- **Suggested fix:** Choose explicitly, and record it in the trusted base: (i) regenerate the non-vacuity fixture with a known-trapdoor test SRS so acceptance is a G1 computation (s • left = right via the byte fold plus the existing agreement lemmas); or (ii) import the one pairing fact from the Rust run (trace.json dual_msm left/right, pairing_check) as a named, fixture-specific axiom and say so in the English headline. Track `HypothesesInhabited poseidonRender …` as a stated (sorried) theorem so it appears in SORRY.md and in a cone.

### H-7 — poseidonE.delta is the placeholder 2; the contract hard-codes Fr::DELTA; no Valid clause relates them, and the recorded reason for dropping the clause is wrong

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → medium · **Kind:** fidelity-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (v1-era docs/plans/P-DELTA-GAP.md and the Inv.lean comment ('E.delta is unconstrained by the specs while the contract hard-codes a value'); absent from the V2 plan and from F.2's row list) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:379-380; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:472-490; formal/Midfall/Instances/DeployedProfile.lean:122-125; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:195-198; curves/src/bls12_381/fq.rs:492; proofs/solidity-verifier/target/poseidon-fixture-dump/Halo2Verifier.sol:1975
- **Claim (reviewer):** The Lean verifier's permutation argument uses `E.delta`; at the deployment it is 2, while the generated contract uses 0x08634d0a…189d7. `EnvAgrees` has no delta clause and `Valid.inputs` constrains only numInstances, accEncoding, k and truncatedChallenges, so `R.E.delta` and `R.inputs.frDelta` (which the program text depends on) are free fields, contrary to the 'NO FREE FIELDS' claim. The gap is masked at 6481731 only because the placeholder CS has no permutation columns; with the real CS (cs.json at HEAD) the two verifiers evaluate different permutation identities. Inv.lean justifies the omission partly by saying the literal is 'a BASE-field DELTA (Fq::DELTA) while E.delta : Fr p'; in midnight-curves `Fq` is the scalar field (`Fq(blst_fr {…})`), so that reason is false. The gap is not carried into any V2 card (no 'delta' in STATUS/QUEUE/DECISIONS-PENDING/PLAN-V2).

At HEAD the CS has 8 permutation columns, so (once anything is accepted at all, H-1) the Lean verifier and the contract evaluate different permutation identities. The Inv.lean justification that the literal is 'a BASE-field DELTA (Fq::DELTA)' is wrong: midnight-curves' `Fq` is the BLS12-381 scalar field. No V2 plan document carries the gap.

### H-9 — Valid.model does not need a human decision: a third route stays inside Instances/

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonV2.lean:101-114; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonValid.lean:193-227; formal/docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:119-127
- **Claim (reviewer):** DECISIONS-PENDING §9 and QUEUE R.3 offer only (a) `deriving Inhabited` on the v1 record plus a `getD` restatement, or (b) a frozen change to `Render.model`. The obstruction is only the dependent motive of `poseidonModelVal`'s own `match h : poseidonModel with`. Defining the projection through a generic total function on `Except` whose proof argument is a Bool equation removes the motive problem with no v1 edit and no frozen change: only PoseidonV2.lean (C.1's refinable file) changes.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** PoseidonV2.lean:111-114 `def poseidonModelVal : Model := match h : poseidonModel with | .ok v => v | .error e => False.elim (poseidonModel_notError e h)`; PoseidonValid.lean:204-209 `poseidonModelVal is a match h : poseidonModel with whose ERROR BRANCH mentions poseidonModel in the type of its proof argument, so abstracting poseidonModel to case on it is not type-correct`; DECISIONS-PENDING.md:123-125.
- **Lean check proposed (reviewer):**

```lean
def Except.getOk {ε α} : (x : Except ε α) → x.isOk = true → α
  | .ok a, _ => a
  | .error _, h => by simp [Except.isOk, Except.toBool] at h
theorem Except.eq_ok_getOk {ε α} (x : Except ε α) (h : x.isOk = true) : x = .ok (x.getOk h) := by
  cases x with
  | ok a => rfl
  | error e => simp [Except.isOk, Except.toBool] at h
-- then: def poseidonModelVal : Model := poseidonModel.getOk poseidonModel_isOk
-- and Valid.model at poseidonRender is `Except.eq_ok_getOk _ _` (the render fields unfold by rfl)
```
- **Suggested fix:** Replace `poseidonModelVal` by `poseidonModel.getOk poseidonModel_isOk` and discharge `poseidonValid_model` with the generic lemma; delete item 9 from DECISIONS-PENDING if the check elaborates.

### H+1 — The D-P45 non-vacuity gate leaves the hash existential, so it can be met by an artificial hash and a forged transcript (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:229,317-320; formal/docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:33-52; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:278-318
- **Claim:** STATEMENT weaker than the English claim (the gate, not the headline). In `HypothesesInhabited`, frozen and in the D-P45 draft, `H` is existentially quantified and constrained only by `H runtimeBytes = keccak256 runtimeBytes`. Choosing H constant elsewhere makes every challenge independent of the proof. Then π := 1 and fCom := (g₁^v · (∏ qᵢ^{cᵢ})⁻¹)^{c_m⁻¹} give `left.eval = right.eval = 1`, and `DualMSM.check` is true by linearity for any params. So once the codec is repaired the gate has a witness needing no trapdoor and no honest proof; it would not show that any keccak-honest proof is accepted. Confidence medium: derived by reading the definitions, not Lean-checked; I verified the challenge order and that `ChallengesGood` has only `xNotRoot`. Suggested repair: fix `H := keccak256` in the gate (as `EndToEnd` does) and decide explicitly how the pairing fact for the honest fixture proof enters the trusted base.
- **Evidence:** Statements.lean:318 `∃ (W : ExecEnv) (Lyt : EVM.Layout) (H : Hash) (vkAddr : U256) (cd : Bytes) (st₀ : EvmState),`; :229 `vkHash : H R.plan.vk.runtimeBytes = keccak256 R.plan.vk.runtimeBytes`. Transcript.lean:127-129 squeeze = `t.hash t.state`. MultiOpen.lean:278 fCom read before x3/x4; :306 π read last; :317-318 `{ left := piMsm, right := finalCom }` plus scaledPi. Msm.lean:230-233 check. Inv.lean:692-696 ChallengesGood.

### H+2 — No plan item repairs WellFormed.permutationCommitmentsMatch, a clause reachable from frozen SupportedV2 that is false for the real VK (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/VerifyingKey.lean:99-102,114-116; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:128; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonValid.lean:38-43,399-413; proofs/src/plonk/permutation.rs:123; formal/docs/STATUS.md:279
- **Claim:** PLAN incomplete. `SupportedV2.wf` requires `vk.permutation.commitments.size = divCeil vk.cs.permutation.columns.size (vk.csDegree - 2)`. The Rust VK holds one sigma commitment per permutation COLUMN, and the Lean port itself reads `vk.commitments.size` evaluations, so the clause equates a per-column count with the per-chunk count of the proof's product commitments. It fails at the real poseidon VK (8 vs 3), and for any VK with more than one column unless csDegree = 3. HEAD records this as a measurement (clause 9 refuted, 'a v1 clause defect'), but QUEUE and DECISIONS-PENDING contain no card or decision for it, although the clause sits behind the frozen surface and `poseidon_valid` is on the critical path. R.1-R.5 and WP-G1 do not touch it, so `poseidonRender.Valid` stays refuted after every scheduled repair.
- **Evidence:** VerifyingKey.lean:114-116 `permutationCommitmentsMatch : vk.permutation.commitments.size = divCeil vk.cs.permutation.columns.size (vk.csDegree - 2)`; docstring :99-102 'read_product_commitments reads one commitment per CHUNK'. permutation.rs:123 `let commitments = (0..argument.columns.len())`. Permutation.lean:209 `let permutationEvals ← tmReadN hashableFr vk.commitments.size`. PoseidonValid.lean:399-402 `poseidonVkExec.permutation.commitments.size ≠ divCeil …`, :413 `exact deciding_supported_perm h.wf.permutationCommitmentsMatch`. STATUS.md:279 lists '9 permutationCommitmentsMatch' among the four remaining refuted clauses; `rg permutationCommitmentsMatch docs/QUEUE.md docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md` returns nothing.

### H — errors the skeptic found in the reviewer's report

- H-1 counterexample is stale at HEAD although the summary says every finding was re-checked there: it cites DeployedProfile.lean:729 `(GenAst.emitPartial pv Partial.pcs).isEmpty`. That text is correct at 6481731, but at f2fe99b the gate is `let g3 := !(…emitPartial pv …Partial.pcs).isEmpty` (DeployedProfile.lean:767) and PoseidonValid.lean:32-33 records 'the pcs arm emits 225 statements where it used to emit none'. The 'finalPairing pairs two all-zero G1 points and returns ABI true' witness is therefore not established at HEAD, and the unconditional sub-claims '(a) conjunct 1 fails' and '(c) poseidon_rejects is false as stated' are conditional there on the modelled program returning on some calldata.
- H-5(b) overclaims: 'PLAN-V2 §6's DONE item HypothesesInhabited … proved has no route'. The gate quantifies `H : Hash` existentially (Statements.lean:318; D-P45 draft, DECISIONS-PENDING.md:33-37) and pins it only at the VK runtime bytes (Statements.lean:229), so a degenerate witness with an artificial hash and π = identity exists in principle without the trapdoor. The trapdoor obstruction applies to an honest keccak witness only.
- H-5(c) is listed as 'partially recorded' citing DECISIONS-PENDING §3 and Q0.5 only; at HEAD the non-reproducibility of proof.bin / trace.json is recorded as its own card, QUEUE.md:627-640 (R.5).
- H-3: 'The deployment exhibits it' is imprecise. At poseidonRender `BlockContractsAt` is vacuous (no snapshot exists, H-1), so the deployment exhibits that `Valid`'s clauses pass with 27 identity commitments, not a failing block contract. 'False as stated' for `blockContracts_of_valid` also presupposes a Valid render with a snapshot, and none exists in the tree today.
- H-1: 'g1CodecZ, the only real-curve G1 codec in the tree' is imprecise. `g1AffineCodec` (Instances/Bls12_381.lean:147) is a real-curve codec with a working `ofCompressed?`, but on a carrier without the Group / PrimeOrderWith structure a `Render` needs; g1CodecZ is the only one usable in a `Render`.
- H-6: 'absent from the V2 plan' is slightly too broad. PLAN-V2 §5 lists 'X1 accumulator mode' as a later phase; what is absent is the Moonlight render and older-generator artifact, and any statement that poseidon is a test fixture.
- H-4(3): 'Option (a) therefore needs a new axiom/hypothesis after all' is an inference, not a proof of impossibility. What reading establishes is that the tree's counting method cannot give cyclicity of E'(Fp2)[r] and that the tree itself says mathlib has no Weil pairing or point count.
- Line references for PoseidonEnv.lean (379-380, 382-392, 341-350), PoseidonValid.lean (404-424) and DECISIONS-PENDING.md (115, 110) are those of 6481731 (the reviewer disclosed this). At HEAD they are PoseidonEnv.lean:229-230, 232-242, 191-200; PoseidonValid.lean:434-454; DECISIONS-PENDING.md:111, 106. The quoted text is unchanged.

### H — checked and found correct

- Headline statement and proof term (formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonV2.lean:247-250): `lowering_equivalence_poseidon (product) : LoweringEquivalence poseidonRender product := lowering_equivalence_of_blocks poseidonRender product poseidon_valid (poseidon_block_contracts product poseidon_valid) (poseidon_rejects product)`; quantified over every product, no Valid hypothesis in the statement. Reading the composition body (formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:493-608) gives exactly 11 sorried declarations in its cone, matching STATUS: poseidon_valid, poseidon_quotient_block, poseidon_rejects, blockContracts_of_valid, prefix_run_clean, runBody_iff_slices, slices_seq_sound, slices_seq_progress, noWriters_prog, rejects_of_not_accepts, Bridge.Run_worldEq_of_noWriters. (`prog_slices_decomp` is sorried but not cited by the composition, although its docstring lists it as step 1.)

So 6 proved / 6 refuted / 1 sorry of 13 at 6481731 (the STATUS table still shows the 11-clause count).

With `poseidon_valid` sorried, `False` is derivable from sorryAx in the environment, as the project records.

All six Holds clauses are discharged in-tree, including `g1Decodes` via the G1 point count.
