## I — the plan

**Reviewer summary.** Verdict: the plan, executed to its DONE test as written, cannot reach DONE, and what DONE would certify is narrower than the section 0 sentence. Read-only review; nothing was built or run, so please verify these findings before relying on them.

No new critical defect beyond the recorded R-72 vacuity. Six high findings:

1. The headline cone contains a false sorried lemma. `rejects_of_not_accepts` contradicts the tree's own Inv.lean:698-703, and the deployment has one lookup.
2. `BlockSound` carries none of the soundness direction. In conjunct 1, `LeanAccepts` is discharged by the case hypothesis, so all soundness sits in the undesigned monolithic `Rejects`.
3. `Render.Valid` never ties the Lean verifier's VK commitments to the payload. The generic hinge is therefore false for PCS, and the deployment's Lean VK holds 27 identity placeholders.
4. The D-P36a pairing axiom leaves g1 and g2 unspecified, yet the Lean verifier uses `P.g₁` as the KZG base. Base-point pins are undischargeable, and Gap B option (a) needs G2 torsion structure the tree lacks.
5. The D-P45 gate needs a Lean proof that a concrete proof passes a pairing check, under an axiomatised pairing with unknown s. No card exists.
6. Hand proofs cost about 43 Lean lines per Yul token against roughly 13k remaining tokens. WP-T was declined, and generic-in-R rows keep the program symbolic.

Further gaps:
- The reject side is forall-runs only.
- The revm differential (M4) exists nowhere.
- The byte-exact golden gate splices PCS and quotient text from the artifact.
- The axiom whitelist is by name.

Governance:
- D-P49 is applied in code while PROPOSED.
- D-P50 edited the frozen surface under a delegation that design.md says was never given.
- STATUS.md is a 330-line contradictory log.

Decisions: section 2 (reject hR) is right. Sections 4, 8 and 9 are wrong or unnecessary as drafted. Section 3 is sound as a vacuity fix but likely undischargeable.

Finding I-17 lists the top five changes.

### I-1 — `rejects_of_not_accepts` (headline cone, conjunct 3) is false as stated; deleting SoundSide from Rejects would make `poseidon_rejects` false

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (Audit list item 4; design.md D-P47 'Not decided' paragraph; DECISIONS-PENDING 'Also open'. None records that the lemma is false by Inv.lean:698-703, or that the correct repair is neither offered option.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:344-363, 606-608; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:698-703; formal/docs/STATUS.md:25-32; formal/docs/QUEUE.md:414-423; formal/docs/design.md:1816-1819
- **Claim (reviewer):** The sorried lemma asserts: not LeanAccepts implies every permissive run reverts, with no SoundSide hypothesis and no R.Valid. The tree's own analysis says the contract ACCEPTS on the logup-panic set while Lean rejects, and the deployment has one lookup. So the lemma is false, not merely unproved, and the cone cannot be closed as planned. Its docstring justification (SoundSide is 'a COMPLETENESS side condition ... spurious') is backwards. Frozen conjunct 3 itself stays true there (the left disjunct holds); only the proof route is wrong. The STATUS option 'delete SoundSide from Rejects' would make `poseidon_rejects` false for the same reason. design.md says this 'has not been checked'; it is already decided by Inv.lean.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Blocks.lean:350-352: "`SoundSide` excludes the logup-panic bad set, which is a COMPLETENESS side condition: nothing in the \"Lean rejects, so the contract reverts\" direction needs it, so the hypothesis is spurious for this use." Blocks.lean:360-363: "(hna : ¬ LeanAccepts R H cd) (hobs : RunBody R vkAddr 0 (permissive (callsOf product)) g st₀ obs) : obs.isRevertOrHalt := by sorry". Inv.lean:700-703: "the contract computes the lookup helper identity with prefix and suffix products and never inverts ... while Lean Logup.expressions panics when f_j(x) + beta = 0 ... On that set the contract accepts an honest proof and Lean rejects". proofs/src/plonk/logup.rs:460: ".map(|f| product * f.invert().unwrap())". proofs/solidity-verifier/src/lowering/quotient_numerator/yul_emit.rs:509-512: "Build partial products via prefix * suffix". formal/Midfall/Instances/DeployedProfile.lean:83: "numLookups := 1" (vk.json num_lookups = 1). STATUS.md:28-29: "Deleting `SoundSide` from `Rejects` makes `rejects_of_not_accepts` a one-liner and **drops the cone by 1**".
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take any valid render with at least one logup argument (the deployment, once valid). H is universally quantified, so program it. The honest prover commits advice and receives theta. It picks x* off the domain and sets beta* := -f_j(x*), where f_j is the compressed lookup input polynomial (beta* is not -f_j(omega^i) for any row with overwhelming probability). It finishes the proof honestly with beta = beta*. Define H pointwise to return beta* and x* at those two squeezes. Lean: Logup panics (logup.rs:460), so verifyMsgs is not ok and LeanAccepts fails. Contract: the helper identity is evaluated without inversion and holds for an honest proof, so the run returns abiTrue. Then obs.isReturn holds and obs.isRevertOrHalt fails.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** `rejects_of_not_accepts` (Blocks.lean:356-363) is false as stated, for two independent reasons. (a) It has no `SoundSide` hypothesis, yet the tree's own frozen text says the contract accepts where Lean panics at logup.rs:460; `H` is universally quantified and constrained only at the VK runtime bytes, so that set is reachable. (b) It has no `R.Valid` hypothesis, so the Lean-side fields of R need not relate to the program at all. The docstring calling SoundSide 'a COMPLETENESS side condition' is backwards. Frozen conjunct 3 stays true on the bad set (left disjunct). Deleting SoundSide from `Rejects` would not be unsound (it strengthens a hypothesis of the composition) but would make `poseidon_rejects` false, hence unprovable, at any correctly modelled render with a lookup. The counterexample is an argument, not an executed run: nothing was built.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Blocks.lean:356-363: hypotheses are hdep, hentry, hpc, `(hna : ¬ LeanAccepts R H cd)`, hobs, conclusion `obs.isRevertOrHalt`; no SoundSide, no Valid. Blocks.lean:350-352: "`SoundSide` excludes the logup-panic bad set, which is a COMPLETENESS side condition ... the hypothesis is spurious for this use". Statements.lean:269-270 (frozen): "Lean panics where `f_j(x) + β = 0`, the contract never inverts there". Inv.lean:697-703: "On that set the contract accepts an honest proof and Lean rejects, so verifier_sound needs this for every H". Halo2/Exec/Logup.lean:278-279: `match invert f with | none => .error (Fault.panic "proofs/src/plonk/logup.rs:460")`. proofs/src/plonk/logup.rs:460: `.map(|f| product * f.invert().unwrap())`. yul_emit.rs:509: "Build partial products via prefix * suffix" (mulmod only). Native.lean:50 `abbrev Hash := Bytes → H256`; Statements.lean:229 is the only constraint on H. DeployedProfile.lean:84 `numLookups := 1`, :116 `lookupChunks [1]`. Use site Blocks.lean:606-608. STATUS.md:25-31 still offers 'Deleting `SoundSide` from `Rejects`'; design.md:1816-1819 says the bad set 'has not been checked'. Step check of the counterexample: with a one-input chunk the contract evaluates h*(f+beta)-1, the true value of the constraint polynomial at x, so an honest proof passes; Lean panics in prepareMsgs, so LeanAccepts fails; every hypothesis of the lemma is met.
- **Suggested fix:** Prove conjunct 3 without any reference to the Lean verifier. (1) Keep NoWriters for the world. (2) Add a syntactic `NoCommittingHalt` scan, in the style of noWritersScan, over the prefix, helpers and slices 1-6 (no return/stop/selfdestruct), with one generic lemma: such code halts only non-committingly. (3) Use the snapshot-free fact already proved in TraceReturn.lean (`tr_sound_clean_noarm` / `tr_poison_walk_noarm`): from any state with `success` bound, traceReturn reverts or returns abiTrue. Delete `rejects_of_not_accepts`. Keep SoundSide in `Rejects`, or drop `Rejects` per I-2. Correct the docstrings at Blocks.lean:344-355 and QUEUE.md:417-419.

### I-2 — BlockSound carries none of the soundness direction; all of 'returns implies Lean accepts' is in the undesigned monolithic `Rejects`

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** partially-correct → medium · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:541-560, 587-608, 129-139; formal/docs/QUEUE.md:991-999; formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:283; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Snapshot.lean:321-349
- **Claim (reviewer):** In the composition, the seven BlockSound contracts are used only on calldata Lean already accepts, and only to derive `returnData = abiTrue` or the conjunct-3 disjunction. That fact is available far more cheaply and snapshot-free (see I-1). The entire implication 'the contract returns, so Lean accepts' is `hrej : R.Rejects product`. `Rejects` is a program-level statement with no decomposition, no invariant and no fault table: one 2-hour skeleton card (C.3) and one sentence in WP-M0. BlockSound assumes the whole-run snapshot exists up front, so it cannot be reused when Lean fails mid-way. Every block would need a second, differently shaped proof. The work done so far on BlockSound rows (TraceReturn, VkLoading, FinalPairing, D-P49) therefore does not advance soundness.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Blocks.lean:543-557: "by_cases hacc : LeanAccepts R H cd ... exact ⟨returnData_of_committed_ret st₀ st' hh, hacc⟩" (LeanAccepts in the conclusion is the case hypothesis). Blocks.lean:558-560: "REJECTION: `Rejects` applies here ... exact absurd (hrej W Lyt H vkAddr cd st₀ gg obs hdep hentry hpc hside hacc hobs)". Blocks.lean:129-133: "This is the half of soundness the block contracts do not cover (they assume a snapshot exists). WP-M0 decomposes it into STAGED per-block rejection contracts". QUEUE.md:994-995: "**Goal.** `Render.Rejects` from per-block rejection lemmas, modulo sorries, with each block's invariant and fault class." The Lean port has over 30 distinct `Fault.panic` sites under formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec (for example kzg/mod.rs:349, verifier.rs:366, trash.rs:70, logup.rs:422); SoundSide names one.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** The observation about the composition is right: in `lowering_equivalence_of_blocks` the per-block contracts are applied only under `LeanAccepts`, and the whole implication 'returns, so Lean accepts' is supplied by `hrej : R.Rejects product`, which is monolithic and sorried (`poseidon_rejects`), with only WP-M0 item 2 and the 2-hour skeleton card C.3 behind it. The claim that BlockSound 'carries none of the soundness direction' and that the BlockSound rows 'do not advance soundness' is wrong. `BlockContractsAt` gives BlockSound for every snapshot that exists, with no DualMSM.check hypothesis, and `BlockSound .finalPairing` forces DualMSM.check on any clean exit. So BlockSound covers the central case of `Rejects` (snapshot exists, pairing fails). What it cannot cover is decode failure, a non-canonical instance, or a Lean fault before the snapshot exists; those need staged contracts and a fault table that do not exist. No lemma yet connects BlockSound to Rejects: `prefix_run_clean` demands LeanAccepts and `slices_seq_sound`'s post drops the check fact. The project already records that Rejects is 'the half of soundness the block contracts do not cover', so this is partially recorded, not unrecorded.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Blocks.lean:543-557: `by_cases hacc : LeanAccepts R H cd` ... `exact ⟨returnData_of_committed_ret st₀ st' hh, hacc⟩`; :558-560 the negative branch is `hrej`. Blocks.lean:116-127: the sound half needs only decode, instOfWords? and `snapshotOf ... = .ok Snap`; `DualMSM.check ... = true` gates progress only. Inv.lean (the `.pairing` arm): `(PV.hasAccumulator == true || DualMSM.check S.c.dual params)`, so `Clean R .pairing` entails the check. FinalPairing.lean:45-52: "the SOUNDNESS half ... has to show: if the slice finishes with `success = 1` then `DualMSM.check` holds". Snapshot.lean:380-386: snapshotOf agrees with prepareMsgs, so a snapshot exists whenever prepare succeeds, check or no check. Blocks.lean:246-250 (`hacc` required), :307-313 (post is revert or exitOk only). Blocks.lean:129-133 docstring records the staged-rejection need. QUEUE.md:1050-1058 (C.3, 'SEM · SKELETON · box 2 h'); PLAN-V2.md:283. Distinct panic site strings under Halo2/Exec: about 65 (rg | sort -u), SoundSide names one; most I sampled are structural (index misses), kzg/mod.rs:479 is input-dependent and claimed 'both sides reject' (Inv.lean, ChallengesGood docstring) - not verified here.
- **Suggested fix:** Replace BlockSound plus Rejects by one staged forward-simulation contract per block, in the permissive dialect. From a state related to Lean's partial state after stage k (the staged functions snapshotOfTrace .. snapshotOfPcs already exist), every run reverts, or exits normally and then Lean's stage k+1 is `.ok` with the related state (under SoundSide for the linearize stage), or is poisoned. Chaining gives conjunct 1 directly, and `Rejects` becomes its contrapositive. Before any proof, write the fault table: each reachable Lean fault site mapped to the contract check that reverts. Keep BlockProgress for conjunct 2.

### I-3 — `Render.Valid` does not tie the Lean verifier's VK commitments to the payload; the generic hinge is false for PCS and the deployment's Lean VK holds identity placeholders

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:340-349; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:124-125, 205; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:533-579, 741; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/VerifyingKey.lean:109-119; formal/Midfall/Generator/Models.lean:390-393; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:152-154
- **Claim (reviewer):** No clause of `Valid`, `SupportedV2`, `EnvAgrees`, `WellFormed`, `Frame` or `Inv` states that the payload's fixed and permutation commitment words equal `E.g1.toEip` of `R.vk.fixedCommitments` and `R.vk.permutation.commitments`. The contract MSMs over the payload; the Lean verifier MSMs over R.vk. So `blockContracts_of_valid` (for all R with R.Valid) is false at `.pcs` for any render whose PCS reads a VK commitment. v1's `pcs_spec` had the same hole. Independently, `poseidonVkExec` carries 27 copies of the identity as commitments. Even after D-P47, WP-G1 and Gap B, `LoweringEquivalence poseidonRender` would be false as modelled: the contract accepts the fixture proof and Lean rejects it. No card, decision or F.2 row covers this. The docs record the `ofCompressed?` placeholder only as blocking `end_to_end`.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** PoseidonEnv.lean:344-345: "def poseidonFixedCommitments : Array G₁z := Array.replicate poseidonVkData.numFixedColumns (1 : G₁z)"; :348-349 likewise for the permutation commitments. Statements.lean:124-125: "vkPoints : ∀ C ∈ R.vk.fixedCommitments.toList ++ R.vk.permutation.commitments.toList, ptReadable (R.E.g1.toEip C)" (the only clause reading them, besides the two size checks in WellFormed). Inv.lean:741: "(bytesAt st (ptrAddr M.vkMptr) PV.vk.len == PV.vk.bytes)" pins memory to the payload, not the payload to the VK. Inv.lean:579: "vkDigestWord : (PV.vk.constants.getD 0 (\"\", 0)).2 = frWord vk.transcriptRepr" is the only VK-content link. Lowering/Partials.lean:683-696 (`pcs_spec` hypotheses: hblk, hsup, henv, hperm, hh, hcd, hinst, hsnap; none about commitment words).
- **Counterexample / witness:** Let R be any Valid render whose PCS reads a fixed commitment. Set R' := { R with vk := { R.vk with fixedCommitments := R.vk.fixedCommitments.map (· * g) } } with g ≠ 1 chosen so the encodings stay ptReadable. All 13 Valid clauses still hold: they read vk.cs, vk.domain, vk.transcriptRepr and array sizes only. R'.prog = R.prog, since the program depends on plan and model only. Take an honest proof for R under any H with SoundSide. The contract returns abiTrue. Lean, for R', computes the final MSM with shifted commitments, so DualMSM.check fails and LeanAccepts fails. Hence conjunct 1 of LoweringEquivalence R' fails, and BlockSound R' .pcs is false: after the block, memory at pairingLhsMptr is the payload-based point, not toEip Snap'.c.dual.left.eval.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** As stated. No clause of `Render.Valid`, `SupportedV2`, `EnvAgrees`, `VerifyingKey.WellFormed`, `Frame` or `Inv` relates the payload's commitment words (`R.plan.vk`) to `R.vk.fixedCommitments` / `R.vk.permutation.commitments`; only readability and two size equalities read them. The Lean verifier MSMs over `R.vk` (Linearization.lean:100) and the program depends on (plan, model) only, so the generic hinge `blockContracts_of_valid` is false at `.pcs` for any Valid render with a non-trivial PCS, and `∀ R, R.Valid → LoweringEquivalence R` (X4) is false. At the deployment the Lean VK still holds 27 identity placeholders at HEAD. Correction to 'already recorded: no': the placeholders themselves are recorded in two source docstrings; what is unrecorded is the missing Valid clause and the consequence for the hinge and headline. 'The contract accepts the fixture proof and Lean rejects it' is the expected consequence of the model, not something I could execute.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Statements.lean:124-125 `vkPoints : ∀ C ∈ R.vk.fixedCommitments.toList ++ R.vk.permutation.commitments.toList, ptReadable (R.E.g1.toEip C)`. VerifyingKey.lean:109-119: WellFormed reads commitments only through `.size`. Inv.lean EnvAgrees: the only VK-content link is `vkDigestWord : (PV.vk.constants.getD 0 ("", 0)).2 = frWord vk.transcriptRepr`; `transcriptRepr` is 'imported and unchecked' (VerifyingKey.lean:73-77). Inv `.vk` arm: `bytesAt st (ptrAddr M.vkMptr) PV.vk.len == PV.vk.bytes` pins memory to the payload. Statements.lean:99 `prog := asmOf R.pv R.model`, :93 `pv := planViewOf R.plan R.model ...`. rg for fixedComms/fixedCommitments in Lowering/ and LoweringV2/ finds no binding clause. PoseidonEnv.lean:195-200 (HEAD): `Array.replicate poseidonVkData.numFixedColumns (1 : G₁z)` and the same for permutation; :191-194 'NOT a claim that the deployment's fixed commitments are the identity'. PoseidonValid.lean:441-444 discharges `vkPoints` by `∀ x ∈ ..., x = (1 : G₁z) := by native_decide`, with the docstring 'they are not even in the carrier'. Counterexample re-derived: mapping the commitments preserves all 13 clauses (only vkPoints and wf.fixedCommitmentsMatch mention them), leaves `prog`, `DeploymentV2` and SoundSide's panic test unchanged, and changes Lean's final MSM.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
In a scratch file importing Midfall.LoweringV2.Statements, with the file's variable block: `example (R : Render p G₁ G₂ Gₜ) (hR : R.Valid) (f : G₁ → G₁) (hf : ∀ C, ptReadable (R.E.g1.toEip (f C))) : Render.Valid ({ R with vk := { R.vk with fixedCommitments := R.vk.fixedCommitments.map f } }) := by obtain ⟨h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,h7,h8,h9,h10,h11,h12,h13⟩ := hR; obtain ⟨s1,s2,s3,s4,s5,s6,s7,s8,s9,s10,s11,s12⟩ := h9; refine ⟨h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,h7,h8,⟨s1,s2,s3,s4,s5,?_,s7,s8,?_,s10,s11,s12⟩,h10,h11,h12,h13⟩; · intro C hC; rcases List.mem_append.mp hC with hC | hC; · obtain ⟨C', -, rfl⟩ := by simpa using hC; exact hf C'; · exact s6 C (List.mem_append_right _ hC); · exact ⟨s9.1, s9.2, s9.3, s9.4, by simpa using s9.5, s9.6, s9.7⟩`. If it elaborates, Valid is blind to commitment content.
```
- **Suggested fix:** Add one Valid clause: for every i, the payload's i-th fixed (resp. permutation) G1 slot equals `R.E.g1.toEip` of the i-th VK commitment, with matching lengths. At the deployment, define the 27 commitments from the payload bytes through `g1zOfEip?` and prove each is in the image; the G1 torsion theorem exists, and each point needs an on-curve and r-torsion check. Add both to F.2 and the critical path. This is a fifth root cause behind not-Valid that C.4's four-cause analysis and D-P47 do not cover.

### I-4 — The pairing axiom leaves g1 and g2 unspecified while the Lean verifier uses `P.g₁` as the KZG base; base-point pins are undischargeable and Gap B option (a) is not 'no new axiom'

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (Gap B (s·G₂ modelled as g₂) is recorded (DECISIONS-PENDING section 8, QUEUE R.4). The opacity of P.g₁ / P.g₂, the infeasibility of option (a), and its clash with EnvAgrees.nG2 are not.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Assumptions/CurvePairing.lean:92-101; formal/Midfall/Halo2/Exec/Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:309-311; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:573, 617, 623; formal/Midfall/Instances/G2Codec.lean:1-56 (header); formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonEnv.lean:386-392; formal/docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:56-71, 102-115
- **Claim (reviewer):** (1) The Lean verifier's G1 base is `AdmissiblePairing.g₁`. At the deployment that is a field of the axiom `bls12_381Pairing`, whose docstring says its generators are not claimed to be the published ones. Any pin of the payload's G1_BASE or G2_BASE bytes to `P.g₁` / `P.g₂` (v1 `g1BaseWords` / `g2BaseWords`, D-P46's first clause, DECISIONS section 4's `baseSlots`) is therefore unprovable from D-P36a. Without such a pin the PCS and FinalPairing rows are false. (2) Section 8 option (a) needs `∃ x : G2fold, g2foldToEip x = <s·G₂ bytes>`. G2fold is the exponent carrier, so this says the SRS point is a multiple of H2. That needs cyclicity of the r-torsion of the twist over Fp2 (Weil pairing or a point count; the G1 trick 2p+1 < r² fails over Fp2), or knowledge of s. (3) After (a), `EnvAgrees.nG2 : params.nG2Prepared = P.g₂⁻¹`, which holds today by rfl, would require the byte-named generator to equal the axiom's `g₂`; that is again unprovable. So the claim 'No new axiom. It does need proofs that the bytes lie in the encoder's image' is wrong. Recommending D-P46 'after #8' is insufficient.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** CurvePairing.lean:97-101: "Nothing here says the pairing is the BLS12-381 ate pairing, or that 'blsGT' is the r-th roots of unity in Fp12, or that its generators are the published ones ... axiom bls12_381Pairing : AdmissiblePairing G₁z G2fold blsGT". MultiOpen.lean:309-311: "generator is AdmissiblePairing.g₁ (D-P1); the Rust writes -E::G1::generator(). ... bases := #[pi, P.g₁⁻¹]". G2Codec.lean header: "What it is NOT is a claim that every accepted G2 frame decodes into the carrier - that is the discrete log, and 'Holds' has no g2Decodes clause". ETA.md R5 (round 120): the G1 count works because "#E(F_p) <= 2p+1 ... and 2p+1 < r^2"; mathlib has "no Weil pairing; no point-counting theorem". DECISIONS-PENDING.md:107-110: "`Classical.choose` on `∃ x, encG2 x = <bytes>`. ... No new axiom."
- **Counterexample / witness:** A model of D-P36a with `bls12_381Pairing.g₁ := g1zGen ^ 2` satisfies every stated axiom (g₁ ≠ 1, bilinear, non-degenerate on the generators). In it, `E.g1.toEip P.g₁` is not the payload's G1_BASE bytes, so the pin is not derivable from the axioms.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** As stated, with two refinements. (1) Confirmed: the Lean verifier's KZG base is `P.g₁⁻¹` where Rust uses `-E::G1::generator()`; at the deployment `P` is the axiom `bls12_381Pairing`, whose `g₁`/`g₂` fields are constrained only by `≠ 1` and non-degeneracy, and nothing in the tree equates them to `g1zGen` / `g2foldExp 1`. So v1's `g1BaseWords`, and all three conjuncts of the DECISIONS section 4 draft, are underivable from D-P36a. This holds for the G2_BASE pin too, independently of Gap B. (2) Section 8 option (a) needs `∃ x : G2fold, g2foldToEip x = <s·G₂ bytes>`; G2fold is the exponent carrier, so this is membership of the SRS point in the cyclic group generated by H2. It is not impossible in principle, but it needs either s or a proof that the r-torsion of the twist over Fp2 is cyclic, which the tree does not have; the G1 counting trick does not transfer (r² is about 2^510, below p² at about 2^762). 'No new axiom' is therefore unsupported, not strictly refuted. (3) Confirmed, and it would regress a clause that is PROVED at HEAD: after (a), `EnvAgrees.nG2` would require the byte-named generator to equal the axiom's `g₂`.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** CurvePairing.lean:92-101: 'Nothing here says ... that its generators are the published ones' and `axiom bls12_381Pairing : AdmissiblePairing G₁z G2fold blsGT`. Common/Curve.lean:45-55: fields `g₁ : G₁`, `g₂ : G₂`, `hG₁ : g₁ ≠ 1`, `hG₂`, `hNonDeg`. Kzg/MultiOpen.lean:308-312: 'The generator is AdmissiblePairing.g₁ (D-P1); the Rust writes -E::G1::generator()' and `bases := #[pi, P.g₁⁻¹]`; proofs/src/poly/kzg/mod.rs:546 `bases: vec![pi, -E::G1::generator()]`. rg for `bls12_381Pairing.g₁`/`.g₂` finds uses only in HoldsFinal, PoseidonEnv and PoseidonValid, none equating them to named points. Inv.lean: `nG2 : params.nG2Prepared = P.g₂⁻¹`, `g1BaseWords : vkG1Words PV vkG1BaseSlot = E.g1.toEip P.g₁`. DECISIONS-PENDING.md:69-71 (baseSlots draft), :103-106 ('`Classical.choose` on `∃ x, encG2 x = <bytes>` ... No new axiom'). G2Codec.lean:245 `abbrev G2fold : Type := Exp blsR`, :46-51 'What it is NOT is a claim that every accepted G2 frame decodes into the carrier - that is the discrete log'. ETA.md:956 (`#E(F_p) <= 2p+1` and `2p+1 < r^2`), :957 'no Weil pairing; no point-counting theorem'. PoseidonEnv.lean:236-242 (HEAD): `poseidonSG2 := bls12_381Pairing.g₂`, `nG2Prepared := poseidonSG2⁻¹`. The model with `g₁ := g1zGen ^ 2` satisfies every field of the class (r is an odd prime), so the pin is independent of the axioms.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
Sanity only: `example : Midfall.Assumptions.bls12_381Pairing.g₁ = Midfall.Instances.g1zGen := rfl` must FAIL to elaborate, confirming the axiom's generator is opaque. Independence itself cannot be shown inside Lean.
```
- **Suggested fix:** Make the curve assumption explicit and sufficient, as a human decision that changes PLAN-V2 section 6's axiom list. Either strengthen D-P36a to state `bls12_381Pairing.g₁ = g1zGen ∧ bls12_381Pairing.g₂ = g2foldExp 1`, plus a named assumption that the SRS point is `g2foldToEip (g2foldExp s)` for some s; or use a test SRS with exported s. Pin the axiom STATEMENTS in the DONE gate, not just their names: a strengthened `bls12_381Pairing` keeps its name and would pass `check_frozen_axioms.py` and the section 6 list unnoticed.

### I-5 — The D-P45 gate (LeanAccepts at the deployment) has no feasible discharge route in the current trusted base, and leaves H existential

- **Severity (reviewer):** high · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → high · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (DECISIONS-PENDING section 3 'Cost to know' and QUEUE Q0.5 note the witness must be proved. Not recorded: that it requires a concrete pairing equation with unknown s, and that H stays existential.) · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:30-52; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:224-231, 263-267, 317-320; formal/Midfall/Yul/Externals.lean:932-938; formal/Midfall/Instances/G1Agreement.lean:1995-1997; formal/docs/QUEUE.md:186-192, 1068

But DONE then needs a Lean PROOF that some calldata is accepted, which includes `DualMSM.check = true`. That is a pairing-product equation on concrete points under an axiomatised pairing, with no pairing implementation in Lean. It is provable only as s·L = R, which needs s (unknown for a real SRS; today modelled as 1 by Gap B) plus verified G1 arithmetic for the whole final MSM. It also needs every proof point decoded through a `Classical.choose` decoder and keccak through an extern. There is no card or design: Q0.5 excludes it, and I.7 is a one-line 'later epic' written before R-72. Separately, the gate quantifies H existentially. DeploymentV2 constrains H only on the VK runtime bytes, so a witness need not use keccak256 or the fixture proof.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** DECISIONS-PENDING.md:49-51: "Discharging the gate at the deployment needs an accepting calldata. REVIEW.md:3019 records none at any model in the tree, and the decoder is `Classical.choose` (G1Agreement.lean:1995), so it will be proved, not evaluated." Externals.lean:934-937: "Bridge.lean supplies it (from the revm replay, or from a real pairing once one exists in Lean - docs/ecosystem.md gap 2 says none does)". Statements.lean:229: "vkHash : H R.plan.vk.runtimeBytes = keccak256 R.plan.vk.runtimeBytes" (the only constraint tying H to keccak). QUEUE.md:189-192: "Also not this card: discharging the new gate at the deployment." PoseidonEnv.lean:386: "noncomputable def poseidonSG2 : G2fold := bls12_381Pairing.g₂".
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** As stated, with one correction and one sharpening. Adding `LeanAccepts R H cd ∧ G1CodecLaws R.E.g1` to the gate does remove the no-runs vacuity, but discharging it at the deployment needs a Lean proof of `DualMSM.check = true`, an equation in the axiomatised target group that can only be reached through bilinearity (s·left = right), plus point decoding through `Classical.choose`. Sharpening: in the model as it stands (identity VK commitments, sG2 = g₂, i.e. s = 1) the real fixture calldata is not accepted by the Lean side at all, so the gate at the fixture is false today, not merely hard. `H` is existential and constrained only at the VK runtime bytes. Correction: 'no card exists' is slightly overstated: READY probe F.2 has a row 'the D-P45 witness (an accepting calldata)' with a NOT-COMPUTABLE verdict option; there is no card that discharges it.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Halo2/Exec/Kzg/Msm.lean:222-233: `decideBool (P.decEqGₜ (Additive.toMul (P.e (Additive.ofMul left) (Additive.ofMul params.sG2Prepared) + P.e (Additive.ofMul right) (Additive.ofMul params.nG2Prepared))) 1)`. Externals.lean:941 `abbrev PairingProduct := Bytes -> Bool` with the note that no real pairing exists in Lean. PoseidonEnv.lean:236-242 (HEAD) for s = 1; :195-200 for the placeholder VK. G1Agreement.lean:1995-1997: `g1zOfEip?` is `Classical.choose`. Statements.lean:224-231 (DeploymentV2: `vkHash` is the only H constraint), :317-320 (gate binds H existentially). DECISIONS-PENDING.md:36-41 (draft text), :50-53 ('it will be proved, not evaluated'). QUEUE.md:189-193 ('Also not this card: discharging the new gate at the deployment'), :561-575 (F.2 rows), :1127 (I.6 / I.7 one-line epic). PLAN-V2.md section 6: '`HypothesesInhabited` and `entryFrame_inhabited` are proved'.
- **Suggested fix:** Decide the evidence class before signing D-P45. (a) Use a test SRS with exported s, so sG2 := g2foldExp s, and prove s·L = R by verified G1 evaluation; this is a new work package. (b) Add a named axiom stating the fixture's pairing equation, listed in section 6. (c) Split the gate: prove 'the fixture calldata decodes and the program has a run on it', and report acceptance as EXHIBITED (the executable verifier on a computable curve instance plus revm), saying so in the English statement. In all cases fix `H := keccak256` and `cd := calldataOf fixture` in the gate, as `EndToEnd` already does.

### I-6 — The reject side is forall-runs only and the model's adequacy is unvalidated: the cited 'revm differential (M4)' exists nowhere

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → medium · **Kind:** statement-weaker-than-claimed · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (R-72 (`headline_of_no_runs`) records the no-runs vacuity for the gate. The reject-side totality gap, the weakness of `closed`, and the non-existence of M4 are not recorded.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:302-311; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Program.lean:128-134; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Shared.lean:113-190; formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:54-59, 337-359; formal/scripts/
- **Claim (reviewer):** 'Otherwise reverts without touching the world' is formalised as 'every run reverts'. Run existence is asserted only in conjunct 2, under LeanAccepts. On rejected calldata the headline holds vacuously if the model has no run: a stuck name, an op without a rule, or a non-terminating loop. No totality lemma is planned. `Valid.closed` does not rule this out: `freeNames` ignores called function names and counts a name bound anywhere in the block as bound. So the real-EVM reading rests entirely on the adequacy of the vendored Yul/EVM dialect and of `EntryFrame`. D-V2 says these are 'validated by the revm differential (M4)'. No such harness exists under formal/ (the only revm code is the Rust generator's own tests), no QUEUE card creates one, and V2 has no executable run of its program at all. Section 6's English-statement list also omits the Yul semantics, the wrapper model in `asmOf`, the VK import and the native_decide axioms from the trusted base.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Statements.lean:302-304: "(∀ (g : ExternalGas) (obs : Obs), RunBody R vkAddr 0 (permissive (callsOf product)) g st₀ obs → obs.isReturn → SoundSide R H cd → ..."; :305-306: "(LeanAccepts R H cd → CompleteSide R H cd → G1CodecLaws R.E.g1 → (∃ obs, RunBody ...". Shared.lean:160: "| Expr.call _ args => args.flatMap refE" (the callee name is not a reference); :113-116: "Deliberately generous to the program - a name bound anywhere at all counts as bound". PLAN-V2.md:57-58: "Trusted; validated by the revm differential (M4), not in Lean." A search for `revm` in formal/scripts and in QUEUE.md, STATUS.md and DECISIONS-PENDING.md returns nothing. The executable Yul runs in the tree are v1 files on the AVOID list (RunEmitted, RunShellInhabited, RunLegRouted).
- **Counterexample / witness:** A hypothetical semantics or program in which a rejected input's path reaches a call to an undefined helper has no `Step` derivation. Conjuncts 1 and 3 then hold for that input with zero runs, whatever the real EVM does.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** As stated. Conjuncts 1 and 3 of `LoweringEquivalence` are forall-runs; run existence appears only in conjunct 2 under LeanAccepts, CompleteSide and G1CodecLaws. On rejected calldata the headline says nothing if the model has no run, and no totality lemma or card exists. `mainClosed` does not exclude a call to an undefined helper. The 'revm differential (M4)' that PLAN-V2 cites as validation has no script, harness or QUEUE card under formal/, and no file runs the V2 program through the interpreter. Minor: the callee-name line is Shared.lean:155, not :160.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Statements.lean:302-304 and :309-311 (`∀ (g : ExternalGas) (obs : Obs), RunBody ... →`), :305-306 (`∃ obs` only inside conjunct 2). Shared.lean:155 `| Expr.call _ args => args.flatMap refE`; :114-117 'Deliberately generous to the program - a name bound anywhere at all counts as bound'. PLAN-V2.md:57-58: 'Trusted; validated by the revm differential (M4), not in Lean.' rg -i revm over docs/QUEUE.md, STATUS.md, DECISIONS-PENDING.md, EXECUTION-V2.md and scripts/ returns nothing; Lean hits are comments in Externals.lean, Pairing.lean and Generator/Evm.lean. `Interp.run` is used only in RunLegRouted, RunShellInversion, RunShellInhabited, RunEmitted and Yul/Builtins; the first four are v1 files (three are on the AVOID list in Tools/ConeReport.lean:39). PLAN-V2 section 6's English-statement list names SoundSide, CompleteSide, EntryFrame, D-P36a, Eip2537.Holds, scope and gas; it does not name the vendored Yul semantics, the `asmOf` wrapper model or the imported VK.
- **Suggested fix:** Add to DONE either (a) a totality theorem: for every EntryFrame and total gas oracle there exists obs with RunBody under the permissive calls (once blocks are proved through the functional interpreter this is nearly free); or (b) at minimum a landed executable differential: the fuel interpreter on the closed program against revm, on the fixture and on byte-flipped mutants, comparing the outcome and the `Inv` checkpoints (`InvAt` is Bool-valued by design). Either create the M4 card or delete the 'validated by' wording. Extend `mainClosed` to check that called function names are defined.

- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** The facts are confirmed: TraceReturn (23 tokens) is the only closed block; VkLoading (223 tokens) and FinalPairing (22 tokens) each still carry three sorries; T.2 declined the tactic; P.1, T.3, T.4, T.5 and C.5 have not run; WP-T's accept line was never met although PLAN-V2 places it 'BEFORE M1' and says hand proofs 'are infeasible'; the Inv chain already exceeds default heartbeats. The quantitative extrapolation is not supportable: '43 lines per token' divides a file that includes docstrings, reusable inversion lemmas and duplicated armed/arm-free walks by the smallest block, and PLAN-V2 section 9.1 itself warns that 'One proved block is a sample of one'. 'About 500k lines' should be replaced by 'no demonstrated method for loops, staticcall, MSM or the QVM'. Also, generic-in-R rows do not contradict D-P24: PLAN-V2 itself specifies 'WP-M1 ... Generic in `R`' and scopes D-P24 to the headline; the tension with symbolic constant tables (trap S1, the pins) is real but is the plan's own choice.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** wc -l: TraceReturn.lean 1053, VkLoading.lean 779, FinalPairing.lean 484. Sorries at VkLoading.lean:728, :743, :756 and FinalPairing.lean:281, :328, :358; TraceReturn.lean has none. PLAN-V2.md WP-T: '(4–12, BEFORE M1; added after measurement)', 'hand proofs at that density are infeasible', '*Accept:* re-prove `traceReturn_spec` on the closed slice in under 100 lines'. PLAN-V2.md section 9.1: 'Token counts are a size proxy, not a difficulty measure. One proved block is a sample of one.'; section 9.2 prior 15–30 tickets to calibration point 1. STATUS.md:182-185: 'NOT DONE, DELIBERATELY: no `yul_step` macro'; :141-143 trap S1. QUEUE.md headers: P.1, T.3, T.4, T.5, C.5 carry no MERGED tag; S.4 'PARTIAL: row NOT closed'. Blocks.lean:688-689 `def BlockRow ... := (R.Valid) → ...`. FinalPairing.lean:196-204: '`InvAt` is a thirty-clause `&&` chain ... exceed the 200 000-heartbeat default'. vendor/yul-semantics/YulSemantics/Adequacy.lean:772 `run_adequacy` exists with no sorry in the file.
- **Suggested fix:** Change the proof architecture, not just add a tactic. (1) Prove blocks per deployment on the concrete closed AST: obtain `sliceOf poseidonPv poseidonM q = <literal>` once by computation; this removes S1 and all pins, and generic rows become WP-X4. (2) Use the vendored fuel interpreter as the proof interface. `Interp.run_adequacy` is sorry-free for lawful ExecDialects. Instantiate one for the honest calls (functional by P-DET) and one per failure schedule for the permissive calls. A block contract then becomes one equation `Interp.execStmts N funs V slice st = .ok (...)`, giving existence and uniqueness together, with loop lemmas per emitted loop schema. (3) Insert a reference-model layer: Yul block equals a pure word-level Lean function (mechanical), then that function equals the verifier stage (algebra, no Yul). For the 6.4k-token quotient, prove the QVM interpreter once against the ported `vm/reference.rs` and discharge the per-deployment program by a reflective polynomial-identity check. (4) Do not start S.5, S.6 or S.7 until one loop-bearing block is closed this way and lines per token are re-measured.

### I-9 — Pin-by-pin growth of frozen `Valid` is the wrong repair: about 90 constants need coherence with the plan view

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** partially-correct → medium · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (STATUS.md 'STRUCTURAL FINDING - section 6 systematically under-pins the memory layout' records the pattern. It proposes no structural fix and does not note the missing decode hypothesis.) · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Statements.lean:136-168, 213-217; formal/docs/STATUS.md:282-293; formal/Midfall/Lowering/Inv.lean:723-724; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks/VkLoading.lean:706-728, 768-771; formal/docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:56-91
- **Claim (reviewer):** `Inv` reads every address from `PV.memory`; the program reads the inlined constant table. Every generic row therefore needs 'table constant X equals plan-view address X' for each constant it touches. Three consecutive cards found nine such pins: fpG2Slots (2), trRetMptr, vkPins (4), vkProgressPins (2). Each one enters the FROZEN `Valid` by a separate decision and re-hash. The table has 90 constants and five blocks are untouched, so this recurs dozens of times. The remaining pins from DECISIONS section 4 and I-3 are of the same kind. Likely one more is already missing: `vk_sound_clean` must establish Inv .vk's calldata-length clause, and its own header says this comes from 'decodeCalldata's own guard', but the lemma takes no decode hypothesis (the row drops `hdec`), and no pin relates INSTANCE_CPTR to `proofLayout.proofEnd`.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** STATUS.md:284-287: "**Three consecutive cards each found a missing pin in the same place** ... **Without S.4's first four, `BlockSound R .vkLoading` is FALSE, not merely unproved**". Inv.lean:723-724: "The addresses are all read out of PV.memory, never hard-coded". VkLoading.lean:707-708: "calldata length via `decodeCalldata`'s own guard plus `vkPins.numInst`" versus the signature at :718-720 "(hR : R.Valid) (hpins : vkPins R) (Snap ...) (H : Hash) (vkAddr : U256) (cd : Bytes) (g : ExternalGas)" and :768-769 "intro hR _hpc H vkAddr cd ms instW inst Snap hdec hinst hsnap; refine ⟨fun g => ⟨vk_sound_clean R product hR hR.vkPins Snap H vkAddr cd g" (hdec unused). PLAN-V2.md:110: "the pinned 90-constant poseidon table".
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** The pattern is real: `Inv` reads addresses from `PV.memory` while the program reads the inlined constant table, and successive cards each found render facts that `Valid` did not supply. Corrections. The nine pins are not all 'table constant equals plan-view address': `fpG2Slots` relates payload bytes to params through a noncomputable codec, `vkProgressPins` relate table entries to the runtime bytes, `trRetMptr` is bare existence; so a single decidable `consts = constsOfPlanView pv model` clause would not subsume the G2 or base-point pins. Six of the nine entered `Valid` by one decision (D-P50), not one each. The missing-hypothesis observation is confirmed: `vk_sound_clean` must establish the calldata-length clause of `Inv .vk`, its header says this comes from 'decodeCalldata's own guard', yet it takes no decode hypothesis and `vk_blockRow` binds `hdec` without passing it. The alternative route through the block's own `calldatasize()` check would need INSTANCE_CPTR tied to `proofLayout.proofEnd`, which no pin gives. So the lemma is unprovable from its stated hypotheses with existing lemmas, though not false for a truly generated render.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Inv.lean:723-724: 'The addresses are all read out of PV.memory, never hard-coded'. Program.lean:114-116 `asmOf ... := Inline.inlineContractConstants (constTableOf m) (...)`. Statements.lean:147-158 (vkPins: vkMptr, payloadLen, numInst, disjoint), :165-168 (vkProgressPins). Inv `.vk` arm: `st.env.calldata.length == PV.proofLayout.proofEnd + 32 + 32 * PV.numInstances`. VkLoading.lean:707-708: 'calldata length via `decodeCalldata`'s own guard plus `vkPins.numInst`'; :718-720 signature `(hR : R.Valid) (hpins : vkPins R) (Snap ...) (H : Hash) (vkAddr : U256) (cd : Bytes) (g : ExternalGas)`; :768-771 `intro hR _hpc H vkAddr cd ms instW inst Snap hdec hinst hsnap` then `vk_sound_clean R product hR hR.vkPins Snap H vkAddr cd g`. Calldata.lean:341 `guard (cd.size == instCptr + 32 * numInstances)` is the guard that would supply it. templates/partials/verifier/VkLoading.yul:108 `eq(calldatasize(), add(INSTANCE_CPTR, ...))`. STATUS.md 'STRUCTURAL FINDING - §6 systematically under-pins the memory layout'. PLAN-V2.md section 3 table: 'the pinned 90-constant poseidon table'.
- **Suggested fix:** Replace the individual pins by ONE decidable clause: a function `constsOfPlanView : PlanView → Model → List (String × Nat)` with `R.consts = constsOfPlanView R.pv R.model`, or a Bool `constsAgree` that checks every *_MPTR / *_CPTR / EXPECTED_* entry against the plan view and layout. Decide it once per deployment. Or go per-deployment (I-8), where no pin is needed. Pass `hdec` to `vk_sound_clean`. Do not sign D-P46 or D-P48 as separate frozen edits; fold them into the single clause.

QUEUE.md:1127: 'I.6 / I.7 | `entryFrame_inhabited`; `HypothesesInhabited poseidonRender poseidonProduct`'. Bridge.lean:1221-1223 sorry.
- **Suggested fix:** Write one card per cone sorry and per required out-of-cone sorry, in the standard format, and fix `gen_sorry.sh` to print the real card ID and a status of OPEN, REFUTED or FALSE-AS-STATED. Prove all remaining glue (`runBody_iff_slices`, `noWriters_prog`, `prog_slices_decomp`, `inline_append`) before any further row; for the first, add a determinism lemma for call-free, gas-free code. Critical-path order: object fixes (WP-G1, R.1 or G4, I-3, I-4), then the executable end-to-end exhibit, then the architecture decisions (I-1, I-2, I-8), then blocks.

### I-12 — `ec_pairing_contract` (T.4's target) quantifies its first conjunct over an arbitrary call relation and is false as stated

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → medium · **Kind:** obligation-false-or-unprovable · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks/FinalPairing.lean:159-171, 205-214, 314-328
- **Claim (reviewer):** The first conjunct holds for EVERY `c : ExternalCalls`, yet its postcondition fixes `success` to `fpWord (fpInBit V && product (fpFrame R Snap))` and concludes `Inv .pairing` (hence DualMSM.check) when success = 1. Nothing relates an arbitrary `c` to `product`. The intended instance is `c = permissive (callsOf product)`. So the planned obligation is false, not merely open. Three false-as-stated sorried lemmas were found in this review (I-1, I-3's hinge, this one). The W2 rule 'witness and falsifier next to every new statement' is not being applied to skeleton leaves; it cannot be at the deployment while Valid is refuted there.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** FinalPairing.lean:317-320: "(H : Hash) (vkAddr : U256) (cd : Bytes) (Snap : Snapshot p G₁ G₂ Gₜ) (c : ExternalCalls) (g : ExternalGas) : StmtsContractAll (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (R.scope c g) (R.slice Partial.finalPairing) (fpPre R Snap H vkAddr cd) (fpPost R product Snap H vkAddr cd) ∧ (c = callsOf product → ...". :211-214: "VEnv.get V' \"success\" = some (fpWord (fpOutBit R product Snap V)) ∧ ... (VEnv.get V' \"success\" = some (1 : U256) → Inv ... Checkpoint.pairing ...)". :161-163: "def fpAnsBit ... : Bool := product (fpFrame R Snap)".
- **Counterexample / witness:** Take R Valid with fpG2Slots, product with Holds, and a Snap with DualMSM.check false, so product (fpFrame R Snap) = false by `fp_pairing_algebra`. Let c be a call relation whose PAIRING_CHECK always answers success with output word 1 (a lying precompile). From a state satisfying fpPre with success = 1, the slice runs to normal with success = 1. fpPost requires success = fpWord false = 0, and requires Inv .pairing. Both fail.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** As stated. The first conjunct of `ec_pairing_contract` is quantified over an arbitrary `c : ExternalCalls` (a bare ternary relation), but its postcondition fixes `success` to `fpWord (fpInBit V && product (fpFrame R Snap))` and concludes `Inv .pairing` when success = 1. For a relation whose PAIRING_CHECK answers success with word 1 regardless, and a `Snap` whose check fails (Snap carries no `snapshotOf` hypothesis here), the slice exits normally with success = 1 and the post fails. So the obligation is false wherever its hypotheses (`R.Valid`, `fpG2Slots R`, Holds) are satisfiable. The only use of `.1` is at `permissive (callsOf product)`, so restating the conjunct there is a contained fix; the lemma is outside the headline cone today.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** FinalPairing.lean:317-320: `(c : ExternalCalls) (g : ExternalGas) : StmtsContractAll (D := Bridge.Dialect c g) (R.scope c g) (R.slice Partial.finalPairing) (fpPre R Snap H vkAddr cd) (fpPost R product Snap H vkAddr cd) ∧ (c = callsOf product → ...`. :209-214 fpPost: `VEnv.get V' "success" = some (fpWord (fpOutBit R product Snap V))` and `(VEnv.get V' "success" = some (1 : U256) → Inv ... Checkpoint.pairing ...)`. :161-163 `fpAnsBit ... := product (fpFrame R Snap)`. Use sites: :389-390 `(ec_pairing_contract ... (permissive (callsOf product)) g).1`; :429-430 and :442-443 `.2 rfl` at `callsOf product`. Bridge.lean:84-85 treats `c.Call` as an unconstrained relation; honesty is imposed only by `honestCalls` / `permissive` (:718-732). Helper behaviour per the file header :30-34: on a successful call it returns `and(success, eq(mload(scratch), 1))`.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
Change the first conjunct's dialect to `permissive (callsOf product)` (drop the free `c` there) and confirm `fp_finalPairing_contracts` still elaborates with only the `.1` call site adjusted.
```
- **Suggested fix:** Restate the first conjunct at `c = permissive (callsOf product)`, or under a hypothesis that c's PAIRING_CHECK answers are honest-or-fail for `product`. Require every sorried leaf to carry a falsifier and, where the deployment cannot supply a witness, a small synthetic render as satisfiability witness.

### I-14 — DECISIONS-PENDING section 9 is unnecessary: `Valid.model` should be provable by generalising the scrutinee, with no v1 edit and no frozen change

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonValid.lean:219-252; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonV2.lean:101-114; formal/docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:119-127; formal/docs/QUEUE.md:610-616
- **Claim (reviewer):** The clause is `x = .ok (proj x)` with x the closed constant `poseidonModel`. The recorded obstruction (cases, rw, simp fail on the motive) is a tactic artefact of case-splitting on a constant whose equation appears in the error arm's proof type. Proving the statement first for a VARIABLE x, with `hne : ∀ e, x = .error e → False`, makes `cases x` type-correct. Instantiating at `poseidonModel` with `poseidonModel_notError` yields a term definitionally equal to `poseidonModelVal`, with no evaluation of the generator. The proposed 'recorded exception to do-not-edit-v1' (deriving Inhabited plus a getD restatement) and the frozen change to `Render.model` are then both unneeded.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** PoseidonValid.lean:230-233: "`poseidonModelVal` is a `match h : poseidonModel with` whose ERROR BRANCH mentions `poseidonModel` in the type of its proof argument, so abstracting `poseidonModel` to case on it is not type-correct: `cases h : poseidonModel`, `rw [h]`, `conv`, `simp [h]` and `h ▸ rfl` all fail on the motive". PoseidonV2.lean:111-114: "def poseidonModelVal : Model := match h : poseidonModel with | .ok v => v | .error e => False.elim (poseidonModel_notError e h)". DECISIONS-PENDING.md:123-124: "**(a) Recommended:** a one-line, recorded exception to \"do not edit v1 .lean files\": `deriving Inhabited` on `Halo2Verifier`, plus a `getD` restatement."
- **Lean check proposed (reviewer):**

```lean
theorem Except.ok_of_noError {ε α : Type} (x : Except ε α) (hne : ∀ e, x = .error e → False) : x = .ok (match h : x with | .ok v => v | .error e => False.elim (hne e h)) := by
  cases x with
  | ok v => rfl
  | error e => exact (hne e rfl).elim

example : Midfall.Instances.PoseidonInputs.poseidonModel = .ok Midfall.Instances.PoseidonV2.poseidonModelVal :=
  Except.ok_of_noError _ Midfall.Instances.PoseidonV2.poseidonModel_notError
-- then: theorem poseidonValid_model : ... := by show poseidonModel = .ok poseidonModelVal; exact ...
-- If the two matcher auxiliaries do not unify by `exact`, use `unfold poseidonModelVal` first, or define poseidonModelVal through the lemma's match.
```
- **Suggested fix:** Run the snippet. If it elaborates, close `poseidonValid_model` with it and delete DECISIONS-PENDING section 9 and QUEUE R.3. If the matcher auxiliaries do not unify, restate `poseidonModelVal` in the non-frozen PoseidonV2.lean as `Classical.choose` of `∃ v, poseidonModel = .ok v` (the render is already noncomputable); `Valid.model` is then `choose_spec`, and the computable proxy `poseidonM` is tied to it by the same lemma.

### I-15 — The progress measure gives no signal during the block phase and rewards statement changes; a better measure exists

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** tooling-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (STATUS records the non-monotonicity and the citation problem; no replacement is proposed.) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/docs/plans/EXECUTION-V2.md:300-316; formal/docs/STATUS.md:75-78, 128-132, 265-268; formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks/Compose.lean:24-31; formal/Midfall/Tools/ConeReport.lean
- **Claim (reviewer):** `#cone_sorries` counts sorried declarations CITED by the root. By construction it stays at 11 until all six non-quotient rows exist, and then drops by one. It does not count uncited sorries (`prog_slices_decomp`, `inline_append`, `snapshotOf_prepareMsgs`, the three vk and three fp leaves) or unstated obligations (three block rows, the quotient contract, the staged rejection). It cannot distinguish OPEN from REFUTED or FALSE-AS-STATED. Its only cheap moves are statement edits; the recorded temptation is that deleting SoundSide 'drops the cone by 1'. Eight roots are now reported per card.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** STATUS.md:77-78: "**A card can refute a cone member and leave the cone unchanged — the cone measures what is cited, not what is known.**" Compose.lean:28-31: "`cone_sorries lowering_equivalence_poseidon` stays 11 with this file in the tree, and stays 11 until ALL SIX non-quotient rows exist — at which point it drops to 10". STATUS.md:265: "**ALL EIGHT CONES UNCHANGED**".
- **Suggested fix:** Report four numbers generated by tools. (1) Valid clauses proved at the deployment, out of N. (2) Yul statements or tokens of the deployed program covered by block contracts that are sorry-free AND instantiated at the deployment, out of the total. (3) An obligation ledger over ALL sorries reachable from a `Done` conjunction, including not-yet-cited leaves, each OPEN, REFUTED, FALSE-AS-STATED or BLOCKED, with a card ID. (4) Executable evidence: fixture and mutant runs of the fuel interpreter agreeing with revm, with `InvAt` true at each checkpoint. Keep the cone only as the final DONE check.

### I-17 — Overall: the top five changes to the plan

- **Severity (reviewer):** info · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:22-29, 337-359; formal/docs/QUEUE.md:555-629
- **Claim (reviewer):** As written, the plan cannot reach its DONE test: I-1 puts a false lemma in the cone; I-3 and I-4 are uncarded blockers on the object; I-5 is likely unprovable; I-8 rules out the proof method. If DONE were reached, it would establish this: a specific closed Yul AST, token-equal to text the Lean templates print for an artifact-derived plan, is equivalent, in a trusted and unvalidated Yul/EVM model from a trusted entry frame, to the Lean verifier at a trusted-imported VK, outside two bad sets, for every hash. That is a meaningful translation-validation result for the user's question, but it is narrower than section 0's wording. Five changes are proposed.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** See I-1 to I-16. PLAN-V2.md:24-29 (objective); :351-359 (DONE bullets).
- **Suggested fix:** 1. OBJECT FIRST. Stop all block rows. Make the render the real program and the real VK: WP-G1 emission; a real plan and meta (R.1, then G4 or G3, or an explicit translation-validation scope); payload-named G1 commitments with the missing Valid clause; the curve-assumption decision on generators and the SRS point. Gate this with an executable end-to-end exhibit before any proof: the fuel interpreter runs the fixture calldata to return(abiTrue), agrees with revm, `InvAt` is true at every checkpoint, and byte-flip mutants revert.
2. RESTRUCTURE SOUNDNESS. Prove conjunct 3 by a snapshot-free exit-shape scan plus NoWriters. Replace BlockSound and Rejects by staged forward-simulation contracts with a fault-site table. Keep BlockProgress.
3. CHANGE THE PROOF TECHNOLOGY. Per-deployment concrete ASTs; the functional interpreter with adequacy as the interface; a word-level reference-model layer; the QVM interpreter proved once plus a reflective per-program check; one constants-coherence clause instead of pins.
4. REPAIR DONE. Fix H := keccak256 and the fixture calldata in the gate, with a chosen evidence class. Add reject-side totality or the landed differential. Add the artifact-equality gate without spliced parts. Use a mechanised `Done` conjunction with an axiom whitelist by statement. Give `EndToEnd` its own non-vacuity item. Write the English statement with the full trusted base (Yul model, wrapper model, VK import, port fidelity, native_decide list).
5. GOVERNANCE. Ratify or revert D-P49 and D-P50. Regenerate STATUS and the register from tools, with per-leaf status and card. Require a falsifier and a satisfiability witness for every sorried leaf before it is briefed; three false-as-stated leaves were found here. Replace the cone count by the four measures of I-15.

### I — errors the skeptic found in the reviewer's report

- Baseline drift: the review was written against 6481731, but HEAD is f2fe99b. Line references for PoseidonEnv.lean (340-349, 386-392), DeployedProfile.lean (83, 227-244), QUEUE.md (991-999, 631-656) and STATUS.md (262, 284-287) are for the old commit; the quoted text is otherwise accurate.
- I-11 items (1) and (2) no longer hold at HEAD: commit 1206fb7 records human sign-off of D-P49 and D-P50 in design.md, and QUEUE S.3 is marked MERGED. The summary line 'D-P49 is applied in code while PROPOSED; D-P50 edited the frozen surface under a delegation ... never given' describes 6481731 only.
- I-2 overclaims: 'BlockSound carries none of the soundness direction' and 'the work done so far on BlockSound rows ... does not advance soundness' are wrong. BlockContractsAt supplies BlockSound for any existing snapshot without a DualMSM.check hypothesis, and BlockSound .finalPairing forces the check on a clean exit, which is the pairing-fails case of Rejects. Its 'already recorded: no' is also wrong: Blocks.lean:129-133 records that Rejects is the half the block contracts do not cover.
- I-3 'already recorded: no' is too strong: the identity placeholders are recorded in PoseidonEnv.lean:191-194 and in the docstring of poseidonValid_supported_vkPoints (PoseidonValid.lean:433-440). The missing Valid clause and its consequences are the unrecorded part.
- I-5 'There is no card' is slightly overstated: READY probe F.2 (QUEUE.md:561-575) has a row for 'the D-P45 witness (an accepting calldata)' and for g1BaseWords, with a NOT-COMPUTABLE verdict option. There is no card that discharges either.
- I-6 cites Shared.lean:160 for `| Expr.call _ args => args.flatMap refE`; it is line 155.
- I-7's 'circular for about 10k of about 13k main-block tokens' is an unverified upper bound: the QVM interpreter template text is rendered by the Lean port, and only the PCS lines, compact quotient blocks and sliced render context come from the artifact.
- I-8's '43 Lean lines per Yul token' and 'on the order of 500k lines' extrapolate from one 23-token block whose file includes docstrings, reusable lemmas and duplicated walks; PLAN-V2 section 9.1 explicitly warns against this. 'This contradicts per-deployment first (D-P24)' is also overstated: PLAN-V2 itself specifies generic-in-R rows for WP-M1..M4.
- I-9 says each pin 'enters the FROZEN Valid by a separate decision and re-hash'; six of the nine entered together under D-P50, and two (D-P46, D-P48) have not entered at all. The nine are not all of one kind, so the proposed single decidable coherence clause would not cover fpG2Slots or the base-point pins.
- I-1's suggested fix calls tr_sound_clean_noarm 'snapshot-free'; its statement takes `Clean R Checkpoint.pairing Snap ...`, though the proof uses only `success = 1`.
- I-16 calls deleting SoundSide from Rejects 'unsound'. It strengthens a hypothesis of the composition, so it cannot prove a false headline; it makes poseidon_rejects false and therefore unprovable. I-16 also leans on I-14 and I-17, which were not among the findings supplied for verification.
- I-11 item (4) lists T.1, T.2, M.1, S.3, S.9 as the false-premise cards; the ledger text I could find records false premises for FP.0, T.1 and T.2. The trigger still fired, but the list is not reproducible as given.

### I — checked and found correct

- The composition `lowering_equivalence_of_blocks` (formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Blocks.lean:493-608) is wired consistently with the frozen three conjuncts (Statements.lean:298-311). Conjunct 1 uses `hrej` only where SoundSide is available (:558-560). Conjunct 2 derives both the existence and the forall halves from `slices_seq_progress` and `runBody_iff_slices` (:561-586). Its own body contains no `sorry`; the gaps are the named lemmas of section 7.1.

### I — not covered

- Nothing was built or run (per the rules). Every claim about what elaborates comes from reading. That covers the Valid-transfer check in I-3, the generalisation lemma in I-14, the restatement in I-12, and the reported cone counts and `#print axioms` outputs.
- Whether SoundSide and CompleteSide are the ONLY divergence sets between the Lean verifier and the contract. Examples not checked: x = 0 or x3 collisions in the PCS denominators, and the other 30-plus `Fault.panic` sites. I-2 only notes that no fault table exists.
- Realism of `EntryFrame` against solc output (free-memory pointer, zero memory above 0x80, internal calls from a derived contract). Also soundness of `Run_worldEq_of_noWriters` for arbitrary call relations, and the flattening of the two assembly blocks plus the immutable read in `asmOf`. These belong to the trusted-base and Yul dimensions.
- Fidelity of the Lean generator port to the Rust beyond the deployment golden gate, and whether `CodegenAgrees` token equality is a sound link between AST and text.
- formal/docs/REVIEW.md R-58 to R-71 were not read in full; only the headings and R-72 were read. ETA.md was read only for its last 150 lines. design.md was read from D-P43's tail to D-P50 only.
- The sorry-free interface lemmas of Blocks/VkLoading.lean sections 1-4 (lines 1-640) and Blocks/TraceReturn.lean sections 1-3 were not read line by line; only their declaration lists, the sorried statements and the assembly of the rows were read.
- Whether `prog_slices_decomp` and `inline_append` hold for every Render without Valid (the top-binder versus constant-name side condition for arbitrary models). Suspected to need a Valid clause or a per-deployment proof; not established.
- Whether a degenerate accepting witness (for example all-identity points under a contrived H) could discharge the D-P45 gate at the current render. Raised in I-5 only as the reason to fix H and cd.
- Feasibility estimates for the recommended architecture (interpreter-based evaluation, reference-model layer, reflective polynomial check for the quotient) are judgment, not measurement.


## J — tooling and gates

**Reviewer summary.** Verdict: the gates are honest tripwires at the audited commit, but they do not mechanically establish the V2 claim and several can be fooled. No critical finding; eight medium, six low.

At 6481731 everything I could run without Lean is green: both frozen hashes, the sorry register (60 = 60), module reachability (149), the KAT pins (102 files, 1393), and the five pure-Python gates. I did not run lake or lean, so Lean behaviour is argued from source, including the v4.35.0 toolchain source.

Main weaknesses:
- **The done test is not executable.** No script runs `#print axioms` on any V2 theorem or asserts a cone count; `verify.sh` exits 0 with the headline on `sorryAx`.
- **Sorry detection is a regex.** `admit`, `stop`, `sorryAx`, user macros, `axiom` and `decide +native` all escape it, and no gate enumerates axioms.
- **`#cone_sorries` is weaker than `#print axioms`.** It ignores non-sorry axioms and never visits constructors. It is also gameable: `poseidon_valid` is sorried while its negation is proved, so any goal closes ex falso.
- **`check_frozen_axioms.py` has a real bug.** A statement declared PROVED may regain `sorryAx` and the gate still prints that it is proved (demonstrated).
- **"Frozen" covers the text of four files only.** About 40 meaning-bearing definitions, the root theorem's own statement and `poseidonRender` are unpinned; the three statement gates read v1 only.
- **CI is not a backstop.** `formal.yml` does not trigger on pushes to this branch and, as written, most likely fails inside lean-action before reaching the build.

One observation outside the pinned commit: HEAD moved to f2fe99b during the audit (scope files byte-identical). From 2041977 onward the sorry register and the KAT pin are both red, yet those commits are on `origin/formal/baseline`.

Repository root: <repo>. Please review these findings for accuracy and completeness before relying on them.
