### J-3 — #cone_sorries is strictly weaker than #print axioms: non-sorry axioms are invisible and inductive constructors are never traversed

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → low · **Kind:** tooling-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Tools/ConeSorries.lean:20-36
- **Claim (reviewer):** `coneGo` follows the constants used in a declaration's type and value and records only those that mention `sorryAx`. Two consequences. (a) A user axiom (say one asserting False, or a false curve axiom) and the generated native_decide axioms are in `all` but never reported, so `cone_sorries = 0` is compatible with an unsound cone. (b) For an inductive or structure it reads only the type; Lean's own CollectAxioms also visits the constructors. A sorried definition reachable only through a field of a hypothesis structure is therefore missed whenever no projection of that field is used. The traversal is otherwise sound: it covers definition, theorem and opaque bodies (`allowOpaque := true` is needed and present) and auxiliary constants.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** ConeSorries.lean:29-33 `let used : Array Name := ci.type.getUsedConstants ++ ((ci.value? (allowOpaque := true)).map Expr.getUsedConstants).getD #[]` ... `sorries := if used.contains ``sorryAx then acc.sorries.push n else acc.sorries`. Toolchain v4.35.0 Lean/Util/CollectAxioms.lean: `| some (.inductInfo v) => collectExpr v.type *> v.ctors.forM (collect extFind?)` and `| some (.axiomInfo v) => modify fun s => { s with axioms := s.axioms.insert c }`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** `def Hole : Prop := sorry`; `structure Hyp : Prop where h : Hole`; `theorem t2 (x : Hyp) : True := trivial`. The cone of t2 is {t2, Hyp, True, trivial}; `Hyp.mk` is never visited, so the tool prints 0, while `#print axioms t2` lists sorryAx. No in-tree instance exists today: the only sorried definition is Spec.accepts at Halo2/Spec/Piop.lean:64-67, which the V2 headline does not use.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** `#cone_sorries` does what its docstring says: it lists cone members whose own type or value mentions `sorryAx`. It is weaker than `#print axioms` in two ways. (a) Non-sorry axioms, including generated native_decide axioms, are traversed but never reported. (b) For an inductive or structure only the type is read; constructors are not visited, whereas Lean's CollectAxioms does visit them. A sorried definition reachable only through a structure field whose projection or recursor is never used is therefore missed. There is no in-tree instance of (b): the only sorried `def` is `Spec.accepts`, which no V2 file references. Severity is lowered because PLAN-V2 section 6 and EXECUTION-V2 W7 both require `#print axioms` alongside the count; the residual risk is that nothing enforces it, which is J-1.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** formal/Midfall/Tools/ConeSorries.lean:
- :6 '`#cone_sorries foo` — declarations in `foo`'s dependency cone whose own type or value uses `sorryAx`'.
- :29-33 `let used : Array Name := ci.type.getUsedConstants ++ ((ci.value? (allowOpaque := true)).map Expr.getUsedConstants).getD #[]` ... `sorries := if used.contains ``sorryAx then acc.sorries.push n else acc.sorries`.

Toolchain v4.35.0:
- Lean/Declaration.lean:483-488: `value?` returns a value only for defnInfo, and for thmInfo/opaqueInfo when allowOpaque; `none` for inductInfo, ctorInfo, recInfo, axiomInfo.
- Lean/Util/CollectAxioms.lean: `| some (.inductInfo v) => collectExpr v.type *> v.ctors.forM (collect extFind?)` and `| some (.axiomInfo v) => modify fun s => { s with axioms := s.axioms.insert c }`.

Counterexample re-derived: the cone of t2 is {t2, Hyp, True, trivial and their dependencies}. `Hyp` is an inductInfo of type Prop, so `Hyp.mk : Hole → Hyp` and `Hole` are never visited and the tool prints 0. CollectAxioms goes Hyp → Hyp.mk → Hole → sorryAx.

In-tree check: mapping all 60 registered sorries to their enclosing declaration gives 59 theorems and one def, Midfall/Halo2/Spec/Piop.lean:64-67 `def accepts ... : Prop := sorry`. It is referenced only from Midfall/Statements.lean:95 and comments in StatementsRepair.lean.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
Optional confirmation, expected `cone_sorries t2: 0` and `[sorryAx]`:
import Midfall.Tools.ConeSorries
def Hole : Prop := sorry
structure Hyp : Prop where
  h : Hole
theorem t2 (x : Hyp) : True := trivial
#cone_sorries t2
#print axioms t2
```
- **Suggested fix:** In coneGo, for `.inductInfo v` push `v.ctors` (and optionally the recursor); add a second accumulator for every `.axiomInfo` reached and print it. Better: implement the done test with `Lean.collectAxioms` directly.

### J-4 — The cone count is gameable while a refuted sorry is in the environment: any goal closes ex falso and the headline cone drops from 11 to 1

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** partially-correct → low · **Kind:** tooling-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (Recorded item 2; PoseidonValid.lean header at 6481731 lines 56-59 notes that swapping poseidon_valid for its refutation would make the headline vacuous, but not that the cone measure is gameable) · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonV2.lean:190-191,247-250; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonValid.lean:468-469 (at 6481731; 501-502 at f2fe99b); formal/Midfall/Tools/ConeReport.lean:51
- **Claim (reviewer):** `poseidon_valid : poseidonRender.Valid` is a registered sorry and `poseidonRender_notValid : ¬ poseidonRender.Valid` is proved without sorry. Any module importing both has a proof of False modulo sorryAx. The register (one line per sorry) and `#cone_sorries` (one node per sorried constant) cannot tell a real proof from `absurd poseidon_valid poseidonRender_notValid`. The deployment obligations `poseidon_quotient_block`, `poseidon_rejects` and the headline itself can therefore be closed with no mathematical progress, taking the progress measure from 11 to 1. No gate rejects a sorried statement whose negation is proved in-tree. This deepens recorded item 2: the project records that Valid is refuted, but not that the measure it steers by is unsound while that sorry stands.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** PoseidonV2.lean:190-191 `theorem poseidon_valid : poseidonRender.Valid := by` / `sorry`. PoseidonValid.lean:468-469 at 6481731 `theorem poseidonRender_notValid : ¬ poseidonRender.Valid :=` / `fun h => poseidonValid_circuitMeta_not h.circuitMeta`. STATUS.md at 6481731, line 193: "`cone_sorries lowering_equivalence_poseidon: 11`"; EXECUTION-V2.md:255 reserves "Headline proved" for "`#cone_sorries` = 0 and clean axioms".
- **Counterexample / witness:** In a file importing Midfall.Instances.PoseidonValid: `theorem headline_ex_falso (product) : LoweringEquivalence poseidonRender product := absurd poseidon_valid poseidonRender_notValid`. Expected `#cone_sorries headline_ex_falso` = 1 (just poseidon_valid).
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** The technical core is right. `poseidon_valid : poseidonRender.Valid` is a registered sorry and `poseidonRender_notValid : ¬ poseidonRender.Valid` is proved, so any goal closes by `absurd` in a module that sees both. Neither the register nor `#cone_sorries` distinguishes that from real progress; an ex-falso headline would report cone 1. No gate forbids a registered sorry whose negation is proved in-tree.

Three corrections:
1. The project does record the consequence in process text, more than the reviewer credits. EXECUTION-V2 classifies such a node REFUTED, says the root is unreachable by the current route, and says downstream tickets are not READY. The vacuity and empty-cone trap is also written down in PoseidonValid.lean and DECISIONS-PENDING.md.
2. The final done test cannot be met this way: the ex-falso proof keeps `poseidon_valid`'s sorryAx, so the cone stays at least 1 and `#print axioms` still shows sorryAx. Only intermediate cone numbers are gameable.
3. In the current import order (PoseidonValid imports PoseidonV2) the trick needs a new downstream theorem or an import refactor; `poseidon_quotient_block` and `poseidon_rejects` cannot be closed in place.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** Paths under formal.
- Midfall/Instances/PoseidonV2.lean:190-191 `theorem poseidon_valid : poseidonRender.Valid := by` / `sorry`.
- Midfall/Instances/PoseidonValid.lean at 6481731 :468-469 `theorem poseidonRender_notValid : ¬ poseidonRender.Valid :=` / `fun h => poseidonValid_circuitMeta_not h.circuitMeta`; at f2fe99b :501-502 via `poseidonValid_layout_not h.layout`. Both routes end in native_decide lemmas with no sorry. The file's one sorry (`poseidonValid_model`, :227 at 6481731, :252 at HEAD) is not on that path.
- PoseidonValid.lean imports Midfall.Instances.PoseidonV2 (import block after the header), so the reviewer's snippet is well-formed.
- Expected cone of the ex-falso theorem is 1: the sorried theorems of the present cone live in LoweringV2/Blocks.lean and PoseidonV2.lean, downstream of the statement definitions, so none can sit in the type's cone.

Already recorded by the project:
- docs/plans/EXECUTION-V2.md:312-315 'REFUTED (a proved negation exists, e.g. `poseidonRender_notValid`) ... A REFUTED node means the root is unreachable by the current route ... tickets whose only effect is downstream of it are not READY'.
- PoseidonValid.lean:58-62 (6481731) 'Replacing it with `fun h => poseidonRender_notValid h |>.elim` would close the hole by proving its negation ... the headline would then be vacuous rather than open'.
- docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md:24-25 (6481731) 'with `R.Valid` as a hypothesis the headline is provable by contradiction with an empty cone'.

No current abuse: `poseidonRender_notValid` is cited only in docs and comments, and `poseidon_valid` only by the headline at PoseidonV2.lean:249-250.
- **Lean check proposed (skeptic):**

```lean
Optional confirmation, expected `cone_sorries headline_ex_falso: 1` listing only Midfall.Instances.PoseidonV2.poseidon_valid:
import Midfall.Instances.PoseidonValid
import Midfall.Tools.ConeSorries
open Midfall.Instances.PoseidonV2 Midfall.Instances.PoseidonValid Midfall.LoweringV2 Midfall.Yul in
theorem headline_ex_falso (product : PairingProduct) : LoweringEquivalence poseidonRender product :=
  absurd poseidon_valid poseidonRender_notValid
#cone_sorries headline_ex_falso
```
- **Suggested fix:** Do not keep a sorry whose statement is refuted in-tree. Make `poseidonRender.Valid` an explicit hypothesis of the deployment theorem until it is provable, or delete `poseidon_valid`. Add a rule (and ideally a Lean check) that no registered sorry has a proved negation.

### J-5 — check_frozen_axioms.py passes, and prints that the statement is proved, when the PROVED frozen statement regains sorryAx

- **Severity (reviewer):** medium · **Skeptic verdict:** confirmed → medium · **Kind:** tooling-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/scripts/check_frozen_axioms.py:58-65,252-270,329-334
- **Claim (reviewer):** EXPECTED contains sorryAx. The only branch that treats a PROVED declaration specially is `if "sorryAx" not in ax`. If `Midfall.Statements.verifyNative_iff_msgs` (declared PROVED under D-P33) depends on sorryAx again, no problem is appended and the gate prints "1 PROVED on exactly ['Classical.choice', 'Quot.sound', 'propext']". The gate is asymmetric: it reddens when a sorry statement is filled, not when a proved one regresses.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** check_frozen_axioms.py:58 `EXPECTED = {"propext", "sorryAx", "Classical.choice", "Quot.sound"}`; :252 `for a in sorted(ax - EXPECTED):`; :256-261 `if "sorryAx" not in ax:` / `if d in PROVED:` ... `continue`; comment :62-63 "a declared-proved statement must depend on exactly the other three". Demonstrated with `--input`: a captured-output file giving verifyNative_iff_msgs the four axioms including sorryAx returns rc 0 with the "1 PROVED on exactly [three]" line; the control where verifyMsgs_iff_spec loses sorryAx returns rc 1.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Replace the proof of `Midfall.Lowering.verifyNative_iff_msgs_codec` (Lowering/Obligations.lean, unhashed; the frozen theorem at Midfall/Statements.lean:70 is a one-line re-export of it) by `sorry` and regenerate SORRY.md. The frozen hash is unchanged, the register is in sync, and this gate stays green and still reports the statement as proved.
- **Claim after the skeptic's check:** As stated. If the declared-PROVED frozen statement `Midfall.Statements.verifyNative_iff_msgs` regains sorryAx, check_frozen_axioms.py appends no problem and prints '1 PROVED on exactly [Classical.choice, Quot.sound, propext]'. The only PROVED-specific branch sits under `if "sorryAx" not in ax`. Mitigation: a literal `sorry` in the unhashed proof it re-exports must also appear as a new SORRY.md row. Aggravation: combined with J-2, a regression through `admit` or a macro would be silent in both the register and this gate.
- **Evidence (skeptic):** formal/scripts/check_frozen_axioms.py:
- :58 `EXPECTED = {"propext", "sorryAx", "Classical.choice", "Quot.sound"}`; :65 `PROVED = {"Midfall.Statements.verifyNative_iff_msgs"}`.
- :252 `for a in sorted(ax - EXPECTED):`; :256-261 `if "sorryAx" not in ax:` / `if d in PROVED:` ... `continue`; :266-270 the PINNED check subtracts sorryAx.
- :329-334 the success message counts `len([d for d in decls if d in PROVED])` without looking at the axiom sets.
- :62-63 states the intended rule: 'a declared-proved statement must depend on exactly the other three'.

Reproduced with `--input` on crafted captured output (files in my scratchpad, nothing written to the repo):
- honest input: rc 0;
- verifyNative_iff_msgs given [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound, sorryAx]: rc 0 and the same '1 PROVED on exactly [...]' line;
- control with verifyMsgs_iff_spec losing sorryAx: rc 1, 'sorryAx GONE'.

Counterexample path: Midfall/Statements.lean:66-70 is a one-line re-export, `Midfall.Lowering.verifyNative_iff_msgs_codec E F params vk inst π hc`, of a theorem in unhashed Midfall/Lowering/Obligations.lean:832. No script pins that proof (`grep verifyNative_iff_msgs_codec scripts/*.py` is empty).
- **Suggested fix:** After the unexpected-axiom loop add `if d in PROVED and "sorryAx" in ax: problems.append(...)`, and require the exact three-axiom set for PROVED declarations in both directions.

### J-9 — kat_gate.py tracks only `/--` as a comment opener, so a KAT inside a plain block comment still counts toward the pinned total and can be disabled with every pin intact

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** tooling-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (R-53 (kat_gate.py:147-151) fixed the `/--` case only) · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/scripts/kat_gate.py:153-170,251-263
- **Claim (reviewer):** `gate_lines` increments comment depth only when `/-` is followed by `-`. A line `#eval X -- expect true` inside `/- ... -/` or `/-! ... -/` is therefore still counted as an annotated gate in the static pass, so the totals pinned at lines 113-114 do not move. Values are matched positionally, and only lines starting with `#eval ` count as gates. If the commented KAT is replaced by a command that prints `true` without being such a line (`#eval! true`, or `#eval` with the term on the next line), the per-file value count also matches and the gate passes with the KAT never evaluated. An accidental comment-out without that replacement fails closed on a count mismatch.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** kat_gate.py:160 `if line.startswith("/-", i) and i + 2 < len(line) and line[i + 2] == "-":`; :157 `is_gate = depth == 0 and stripped.startswith("#eval ")`. Calling `kat_gate.gate_lines` on "/-\n#eval foo_ok -- expect true\n-/\n#eval! true\n" returns ['#eval foo_ok -- expect true']; the `/-!` variant behaves the same; the `/--` variant returns [].
- **Counterexample / witness:** Replace a failing `#eval foo_ok -- expect true` by the three lines `/-`, `#eval foo_ok -- expect true`, `-/` followed by `#eval! true`. The static total stays 1393, the file prints the same number of values, and the comparison is true against true. (Static pass executed; the Lean run was not.)
- **Suggested fix:** Use `lean_grep.code_only`, which handles `/-`, `/-!`, `/--`, nesting and strings, to strip comments before collecting gates. Count every `#eval`-family command, or require value lines to equal all `#eval*` occurrences.

### J-10 — The sorry register is positional only: statement, declaration name and ownership are not checked, and its stated scope is wider than what is scanned

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** tooling-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/scripts/checks.sh:32-39; formal/scripts/gen_sorry.sh:10-26,41-44; formal/scripts/sorries.sh:10; formal/scripts/lean_grep.py:115-119; formal/SORRY.md:3-6
- **Claim (reviewer):** The gate compares the set of `file:line` pairs and nothing else. (1) The declaration column is the first 44 characters of the sorry line itself, which is the literal word `sorry` in 57 of 60 rows, so the register does not say which declaration is open. The statement of a sorried theorem can be weakened, strengthened or made false, or a different declaration can take over the same line, with the gate green. (2) Owners are assigned by path glob in gen_sorry.sh, so a new sorry under Midfall/Lowering/ automatically gets an owner; the `UNOWNED` fallback is not a failure in checks.sh. (3) SORRY.md says every sorry in formal/ is listed, but the scan covers Midfall/** minus Midfall/Probe/*. (4) lean_grep.py skips unreadable files silently with rc 0. (5) There is no ceiling: gen_sorry.sh followed by checks.sh is green for any number of new sorries, by design.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** checks.sh:33 `listed=$(sed -nE 's/^\| ([^ |]+) \| ([0-9]+) \|.*/\1:\2/p' SORRY.md | sort ...)`. gen_sorry.sh:24 `*) echo 'UNOWNED - assign a work package' ;;`; :42 `rest=$(sed -n "${line}p" "$file" | ... [:44])`. SORRY.md:3 "Every `sorry` in `formal/` is listed here". lean_grep.py:118-119 `except OSError:` / `continue`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Change `theorem poseidon_rejects ... : poseidonRender.Rejects product := by sorry` to state `True` on the same lines: the register is unchanged and the gate is green.
- **Suggested fix:** Record the declaration name (from the Lean-side sweep proposed in J-2) and a hash of its pretty-printed type in the register; fail on UNOWNED; make an unreadable file an error; correct the scope sentence or extend the scan.

### J-11 — Negative controls: two harnesses cannot run off the original machine, one mutates tracked files in place, none is run by any gate, and the hash, register, cone and axiom gates have no falsifier

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** tooling-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/scripts/perturb_import_ir.py:16-18; formal/scripts/perturb_dialect.py:11-24; formal/scripts/gate_negctl.py:66-82; v2-bundle/tools/ConeSorriesTest.lean
- **Claim (reviewer):** Which gates have falsifiers:
- kat_gate.py and check_golden_provenance.py, through gate_negctl.py (four controls each).
- The vendored EVM dialect KATs and the v1 solc-IR import gates, through perturb_dialect.py and perturb_import_ir.py. Both hard-code `/Users/<user>/midfall` and a toolchain path and execute at import time, so they fail with FileNotFoundError on any other checkout; perturb_dialect.py's usage line still points at a /tmp copy.
- The three text gates accept `--root DIR`, but no perturbation battery is stored.
No falsifier exists for the frozen hash, sorries.sh and lean_grep.py (the R-66 char-literal fix has no regression test), check_frozen_axioms.py (see J-5), check_orphans.py, the two confinement gates, verify.sh's scan, or the cone tool. The cone tool's test file lives outside formal/, is built by nothing, and has no case for an axiom in the cone, a constructor field, or `admit`. None of the controls is called from checks.sh, verify.sh or CI.
gate_negctl.py writes mutations into four tracked files (including the tracked .sol artifact) and restores them with `git checkout -- <file>`. That discards any uncommitted edits to those files, and a SIGKILL leaves the mutation in the tree; ram_guard kills process trees, and the perturb scripts' own docstrings cite an earlier incident of this kind.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** perturb_import_ir.py:16 `FORMAL = pathlib.Path("/Users/<user>/midfall/formal")`. perturb_dialect.py:21 `LEAN = "/Users/<user>/.elan/toolchains/leanprover--lean4---v4.35.0/bin/lean"`. gate_negctl.py:67 `subprocess.run(["git", "checkout", "--", rel], cwd=REPO, check=True)`; :76 `path.write_text(new)`; docstring :20 still says "elaborates 29 files" (now 102). `/Users/<user>/midfall` does not exist on this host. QUEUE.md:102-103 runs the cone test once by hand from `../v2-bundle/tools/`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** Running `python3 scripts/perturb_import_ir.py` on this checkout fails at line 17 before any mutation runs.
- **Suggested fix:** Derive paths from `__file__`; make gate_negctl.py operate on a copied tree via the existing `--root` options instead of the working tree; move ConeSorriesTest.lean under formal/ as a non-library `lake env lean` step in verify.sh and add the missing cases; add a small controls stage to verify.sh.

### J-12 — checks.sh control flow: the native_decide scan failure is masked by `|| true`, its verdict is tested after an unrelated section, and the Instances-import confinement regex is narrow

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** tooling-gap · **Already recorded by the project:** no · **Reviewer confidence:** high
- **Where:** formal/scripts/checks.sh:41-52,61-79; formal/Midfall/Assumptions/CurvePairing.lean:60-101
- **Claim (reviewer):** (1) `bad=$(python3 scripts/lean_grep.py ... | grep -v 'Midfall/Instances/' || true)`: the `|| true` needed for grep's exit 1 also swallows a crash of the scanner (for example a UnicodeDecodeError, which lean_grep.py does not catch), leaving `bad` empty and the gate green. The sorry scan fails closed on the same crash, except for Midfall/Probe, which only this scan reads.
(2) `bad` is computed under the native_decide heading and tested at line 52, after the module-reachability section; no ok line is printed. This is cosmetic, but an orphan failure hides the native_decide verdict.
(3) The confinement test is `grep -q '^import Midfall\.Instances\.'` together with `grep -qE '^(theorem|lemma) '`. It sees direct imports only and theorems that start at column 0 with no modifier. `private theorem`, `protected theorem`, an attribute on the same line, instances with proof bodies, and `axiom` all pass. CurvePairing.lean, outside Instances/, imports Instances.G2Codec and declares the five trusted axioms and four instances.
(4) The memory section cannot fail, by design. Every other section fails closed when python3, lake or sha256sum is missing.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** checks.sh:44-45 as quoted; :50 `python3 scripts/check_orphans.py || exit 1`; :52 `if [ -n "$bad" ]; then echo 'native_decide outside Instances/:'; ...`; :70 `if grep -q '^import Midfall\.Instances\.' "$f" && grep -qE '^(theorem|lemma) ' "$f"; then`. CurvePairing.lean:60 `import Midfall.Instances.G2Codec`, :69 `axiom blsGT : Type`.
- **Counterexample / witness:** A file outside Instances/ that imports Midfall.Assumptions.CurvePairing (which imports Instances) and states `private theorem foo : ... := by decide +native` passes both confinement gates.
- **Suggested fix:** Capture the scanner's exit status separately from grep's; test `bad` in its own section; decide confinement from the import closure (the cone tool's `#imports_avoid` can do it) and from declaration kinds in the environment rather than a column-0 regex.

### J-14 — The "clean axioms" criterion is under-specified for native_decide: one generated axiom per use, identified by name only, and no gate guards the compiled code those proofs trust

- **Severity (reviewer):** low · **Skeptic verdict:** not re-verified (low/info) · **Kind:** plan-defect · **Already recorded by the project:** partially (Compiler trust is recorded (D-V2 clause 5, D-P17, STATUS line 262); the forgeability and the missing guard on attributes are not) · **Reviewer confidence:** medium
- **Where:** formal/docs/plans/PLAN-V2.md:346-348; formal/scripts/check_frozen_axioms.py:66-77; formal/Midfall/Instances/PoseidonV2.lean:101
- **Claim (reviewer):** In Lean v4.35.0 each `native_decide` adds a fresh axiom `<decl>._native.native_decide.ax_N : e = true`. The headline's `#print axioms` will therefore list one axiom per use in its cone: one at C.1, seven after S.9 per STATUS, more as Valid clauses are discharged, with 122 uses in Instances/ today. PLAN section 6 speaks of the compiler-trust axioms "recorded by Q0.3" as if the list were fixed. A reviewer sees only names, and a name or substring allowlist (the approach of check_frozen_axioms.py's BANNED list) is forgeable, because a hand-written `axiom Foo._native.native_decide.ax_1_1 : False` has the same shape. Separately, these proofs trust the compiled code of every function they evaluate, wherever it is defined. No gate scans for `@[implemented_by]`, `@[extern]` or `unsafe` on project functions, which would make such proofs unsound; none exists today.
- **Evidence (reviewer):** Toolchain v4.35.0 Lean/Meta/Native.lean: `let auxAxiomName ← mkAuxDeclName <| `_native ++ tacticName ++ `ax` followed by `Declaration.axiomDecl { ... type := mkApp3 (mkConst ``Eq [1]) (mkConst ``Bool) e (mkConst ``Bool.true) ...}`. STATUS.md at 6481731, line 262: "`native_decide` axioms in the deployment path: 1 → 7 — §6 permits this". check_frozen_axioms.py:66-68 "Matched as SUBSTRINGS, not keys". My grep found no implemented_by, extern, unsafe or csimp under Midfall/.
- **Counterexample / witness:** `axiom Midfall.Instances.X._native.native_decide.ax_1_1 : False` used in a proof would read as a compiler-trust axiom in a name-based review.
- **Suggested fix:** Audit axioms in Lean: accept an axiom as compiler-trust only if its type is `(closed Bool term) = true`, its name was produced by the auxiliary-name generator under a declaration whose module is in Midfall.Instances, and it is not hand-declared. Print the count and the decided propositions. Add a scan forbidding implemented_by, extern and unsafe under Midfall/. The upstream ArkLib checkout ships an axiom-sweep fixture for exactly this forged-name case, and lean-action offers an axiom-audit step.

### J+3 — Every `#cone_avoids ... ok` line in ConeReport is implied by `#imports_avoid: ok` in the same file (found by the skeptic)

- **Severity:** info
- **Where:** formal/Midfall/Tools/ConeSorries.lean:39-41,63-76; formal/Midfall/Tools/ConeReport.lean:39-80
- **Claim:** `#imports_avoid` passes exactly when none of the 14 retired modules is in the file's transitive import list. `#cone_avoids` flags a module only if some cone constant's defining module equals it, and that module name is looked up in the same import list. So once `#imports_avoid` is ok in ConeReport, the eight `#cone_avoids` commands there cannot fail. Reports that cite 'cone_avoids ok (14 modules)' per root as separate evidence are restating one file-level fact. This is not a soundness problem; the file-level check is the stronger one.
- **Evidence:** - ConeSorries.lean:39-41 `moduleOf?` reads `env.header.moduleNames[i.toNat]?`.
- :68 `let hit := avoid.filter fun m => c.all.any fun d => moduleOf? env d == some m`.
- :73-74 `let mods := (← getEnv).header.moduleNames` / `ms.getElems.map (·.getId) |>.filter mods.contains`.
- ConeReport.lean:39 runs `#imports_avoid` on the same 14 modules that each `#cone_avoids` at :41,43,49,62,63,71,80 lists.
- STATUS.md already notes that `#imports_avoid` is file-level, but not that it makes the per-root checks redundant.

### J — errors the skeptic found in the reviewer's report

- J-1 says verify.sh, checks.sh and CI are all green with the headline on sorryAx. The CI part is unverified and at odds with the reviewer's own J-7: the workflow does not trigger on this branch and most likely fails inside lean-action.
- J-4 says the project does not record that its measure is unsound while the refuted sorry stands. EXECUTION-V2.md:312-315 does: a REFUTED node means the root is unreachable by the current route and downstream tickets are not READY. DECISIONS-PENDING.md:24-25 (at 6481731) and PoseidonValid.lean:58-62 also record the empty-cone and vacuity trap. What is missing is a mechanical gate, not the analysis.
- J-4 overstates reach in two ways. `poseidon_quotient_block` and `poseidon_rejects` cannot be closed ex falso in place, because PoseidonValid.lean imports PoseidonV2.lean and not the reverse; it needs a downstream theorem (as in the reviewer's own snippet) or an import refactor. And the final done test cannot be gamed this way, since the ex-falso proof keeps poseidon_valid's sorryAx.
- J-6 counterexamples (1) and (2) are described as leaving every gate green, but neither builds as written. `permissive c := c` breaks `honest_le_permissive` (Yul/Bridge.lean:738-741) and other lemmas across about 59 use sites. Restating the headline as `... ∨ True` breaks `lowering_equivalence_poseidon_is_the_root` (PoseidonV2.lean:255-256). Both are repairable in unhashed files, so the point stands, but extra edits are needed.
- J-6's list of what check_statement_fingerprints.py pins is incomplete. It also tracks `VkSlotsAgree` (:1183) and pins `G1Codec.compressedRoundtrip` and `Spec.accepts` through FROZEN_CITED_DEFS (:1024-1043); the first of these is a hypothesis of V2 `end_to_end`.
- J-7(e) lists the absence of `continue-on-error` as a weakness. Its absence means failures are not swallowed; only the missing `timeout-minutes` is a minor point.
- J-3 is rated medium, but the tool matches its own docstring and both PLAN-V2 section 6 and EXECUTION-V2 W7 require `#print axioms` alongside the cone count. The exposure is the unenforced done test, which is J-1; there is no in-tree instance of the constructor gap.
- J-8 misses that the same commit introduced two `-- expect TRUE` annotations (DeployedProfile.lean:857, PoseidonCoherence.lean:502) that kat_gate.py cannot match, so the KAT gate is red at the tip for a second reason that moving the pin would not fix.
- J-2 does not say that none of the listed escapes is used in the tree today (verified: no admit, no stop tactic, no sorryAx use, no `+native`, five axioms, no sorry in Probe, root, Conformance or vendor). It also misses that checks.sh:33 compares file:line only, and that 57 of 60 register rows carry `sorry` instead of a declaration name, so the register cannot be audited by name.

### J — checked and found correct

- Frozen hashes match at 6481731 and at f2fe99b: `shasum -a 256 -c` reports OK for formal/Midfall/Statements.lean, LoweringV2/Program.lean, LoweringV2/Shared.lean and LoweringV2/Statements.lean (formal/Midfall/Statements.sha256, formal/Midfall/LoweringV2/Frozen.sha256).
- Sorry register in sync at 6481731: replaying formal/scripts/checks.sh:32-38 gives 60 code-level sorry lines equal to the 60 rows of formal/SORRY.md; no line carries two sorries; no sorry or admit in formal/Midfall.lean, formal/Conformance.lean, formal/Midfall/Probe/ or formal/vendor/yul-semantics.
- No hidden proof holes at 6481731 by comment-stripped scan of formal/Midfall: no `admit` tactic (the one hit, Generator/VerifierPartials.lean:731, is inside a string literal), no direct sorryAx use, no `stop`, no user macro/elab outside Tools/ConeSorries.lean, no opaque, unsafe, implemented_by, extern or csimp, no debug.skipKernelTC, no `decide +native`, ofReduceBool or trustCompiler.
- Exactly five `axiom` declarations exist, all in formal/Midfall/Assumptions/CurvePairing.lean:69,74,81,88,101 (the D-P36a constants). All 122 code lines using native_decide are under formal/Midfall/Instances/. The vendored yul-semantics has no axiom and no sorry; its native_decide uses are in `example`s only.
- Only one of the eleven registered sorries I classified is a sorried definition: Spec.accepts at formal/Midfall/Halo2/Spec/Piop.lean:64-67. The sorries in Yul/Bridge.lean (472, 479, 1009, 1223), Lowering/Snapshot.lean:387, Lowering/Calldata.lean (743, 795, 1288), LoweringV2/ProgramLemmas.lean:31 and Lowering/VkDigest.lean:72 are theorem proofs, so the definitions the frozen V2 statements cite (RunCommitted, decodeCalldata, snapshotOf, honestCalls) are not defined by sorry.
- Module reachability: formal/scripts/check_orphans.py reports all 149 modules reachable from formal/Midfall.lean at 6481731 (150 at f2fe99b); no comment line is miscounted as an import. Midfall.lean:137 imports Midfall.Tools.ConeReport, so `#cone_avoids` and `#imports_avoid` violations (logged as errors, formal/Midfall/Tools/ConeSorries.lean:70,76) fail `lake build`.
- The cone traversal itself is sound for definitions, theorems and opaques: formal/Midfall/Tools/ConeSorries.lean:29-30 uses `value? (allowOpaque := true)`, which toolchain Lean/Declaration.lean:483-487 shows is required to read theorem bodies. No Midfall or vendor file uses the `module` keyword, so imported bodies are available.
- KAT pins are consistent at 6481731: the static pass of formal/scripts/kat_gate.py finds 102 files, 1393 annotated and 0 unannotated `#eval` gates, equal to EXPECTED_KAT_FILES and EXPECTED_KAT_TOTAL (:113-114). No gate has a literal expression and no gate sits inside a comment. The script fails closed on a non-zero lean exit (:244-249), a value-count mismatch (:252-255), an unparsable line (:200-202) and skipped files (:268-271).
- These gates exit 0 at 6481731 when run read-only without Lean: check_statement_fingerprints.py (375 fragments), check_duplicate_facts.py (12 families), check_hypothesis_bundles.py (8 bundles, 23 spec bundles), check_golden_provenance.py (9 pins, 5 anchors, 15 includes), and proofs/solidity-verifier/scripts/check_baseline_pins.py (30 tracked pins).
- check_frozen_axioms.py is correct in the directions it tests: a crafted `--input` in which a still-sorry frozen statement loses sorryAx returns rc 1 ("sorryAx GONE"). The exact-set pin for holds_exhibited (:309-321) and the wrapped-line parser (:149) read correctly.
- checks.sh fails closed on missing tools and gate errors: `set -euo pipefail` (:4), `found=$(bash scripts/sorries.sh)` propagates failure, every Python gate uses `|| exit 1`, and `sha256sum -c` is unguarded. The only section that cannot fail is the memory report (:24), by design.
- formal/scripts/ram_guard.sh wrapper mode returns the wrapped command's own exit status (:316, :339) or 137 when it kills, so verify.sh:41 cannot treat a failed `lake build` as success.
- formal/lakefile.toml sets `autoImplicit = false` package-wide and builds both the Midfall library and the midfall-conformance executable by default (:3-7). A file that compiles under the build elaborates identically under the option-less `lake env lean` runs used by kat_gate.py, verify.sh and check_frozen_axioms.py, so the option difference weakens nothing while every module is in the build.
- No script commits, pushes or otherwise changes the remote. The only state-changing git call is `git checkout -- <file>` in formal/scripts/gate_negctl.py:67. gen_sorry.sh rewrites SORRY.md as intended; the generator scripts gen_import_solc_ir.py and extract_ir_builtins.py write tracked v1 artifacts only when invoked by hand. formal.yml has no continue-on-error.
